### 1. [Where and Why is Grounded Reasoning Useful?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08485v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08485

**作者：** David Yan、Le Xue、Forrest Huang、Qiuyi Zhang、Dustin Tran、Andrew M. Dai、Yinfei Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08485v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08485v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P0

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文系统考察带坐标指代的 grounded reasoning 在何处、为何有益：提出衡量思维链是否真正使用输出坐标的分析方法，对训练配方做消融，并构建需要不可描述视觉指代的 BrickVision 基准。

**相关性简评：** 直接关联视觉证据筛选与 LOOK 步骤的收益和成本；其「主要在计数上有效、多数空间与感知基准无显著增益」的结论，可供核查坐标是否被实际使用及基准可描述性造成的混淆。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Where and Why is Grounded Reasoning Useful?

**abstract/A1：** Grounded reasoning, where a model outputs text coordinates of relevant locations in chain-of-thought reasoning, is a common strategy for solving complex visual reasoning problems. However, the benefits and limitations of grounded reasoning are understudied. We study where and why grounded reasoning is most useful. To do so, we introduce a chain-of-thought analysis method to measure how much a model uses its outputted grounding in its thinking trace and perform a detailed ablation study of a grounded reasoning training recipe. We find that grounded reasoning improves counting but shows no significant gain over baselines without coordinate grounding across a wide range of spatial reasoning and perception benchmarks. Using several synthetic tasks, we show that grounded reasoning is most useful when a task requires undescribable references, which are visual concepts that are difficult to describe in language. However, we observe that many existing visual reasoning benchmarks are solvable with only describable text references. To close this gap, we introduce BrickVision, a new benchmark that requires undescribable visual references to solve.

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://elorian.ai/blog/why-grounding

</details>

<a id="paper-2"></a>

### 2. [MammoSeek: A Mammography Agent with selective zooming for grounded reports](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07188v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07188

**作者：** Haoyan Ding、Qian Wang、Lixia Wang、Debiao Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07188v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07188v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MammoSeek 乳腺钼靶 agent：先对四视图形成整体印象，再自主判断哪些候选病灶区域值得放大，仅在原分辨率视图上细看这些区域，随后在 BI-RADS 技能引导下给出分级和结构化、带定位的报告，并与图像模型分数在决策层融合以决定召回；在 EMBED 与 VinDr-Mammo 上取得 0.824/0.818 的检查级 AUC。

**相关性简评：** 其「自主选择性放大」是模型自决的视觉证据获取环节，与 LOOK 类问题直接相关；文中 no-zoom / forced-zoom 对比可供核查动态证据筛选的收益与成本，但手段是外部裁剪而非内部 latent 状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MammoSeek: A Mammography Agent with selective zooming for grounded reports

**abstract/A1：** Radiologists read a screening mammogram by first forming a global impression of the four views and then selectively magnifying suspicious regions before assigning a BI-RADS assessment. MLLMs, in contrast, see the whole image at a fixed, reduced resolution, which makes sub-millimeter calcifications invisible, and they perform close to chance on mammography. Therefore, we present MammoSeek, a mammography agent that reproduces this reading process. After forming an impression of each breast, the agent performs selective zooming: it decides by itself which detector candidates deserve a closer look and inspects only those on full-resolution views. Guided by a BI-RADS assessment skill, it then assigns a category and generates a localized, lexicon-structured report for each breast; its assessment is combined with image-model scores in a decision layer to decide recall. On an internal (EMBED) and an external (VinDr-Mammo) dataset, \\ours reaches exam-level AUCs of 0.824 and 0.818, far above other MLLMs, mammography agent and CNN classifiers. Selective zooming also outperforms both no zooming and forced zooming in localization, structured-report F1 and recall decisions at low cost.

**comment/C1：** 5 pages, 2 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-3"></a>

### 3. [Where Do Reported Long-Video QA Gains Come From? A Controlled Audit of Frame Selection, Evidence Injection, and Expert Routing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07329v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07329

**作者：** Shih-Yao Lin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07329v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07329v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文对长视频问答中的「帧选择」增益做受控审计，固定骨干、帧预算、解码、提示与评分器，逐一变动帧选择、非视觉证据注入与换作答模型三种决策，并在 Video-MME 全量长视频桶与小样本子集上比较效应大小。

**相关性简评：** 其结果直接质疑多模态推理中「筛选视觉证据」与测试规模带来的收益虚高：多数选择器与均匀采样在 ±5 pt 内等价，小样本子集效应不稳定甚至反转，且转录文本的位置（前置/后置）大幅改变结果；可为 LVR 的视觉证据筛选、证据注入与评测可靠性核查提供负结果与偏差估计参照。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Where Do Reported Long-Video QA Gains Come From? A Controlled Audit of Frame Selection, Evidence Injection, and Expert Routing

**abstract/A1：** Long-video question answering is usually studied as frame selection: which few frames should a vision-language model see? We introduce VideoRouteAudit, a controlled audit that separates this choice from two alternatives, injecting non-visual evidence and routing to another answering model. It holds the backbone, frame budget, decoding, prompt and grader fixed while varying one decision at a time. On the full Video-MME long bucket (n=900, K=8), no tested selector significantly improves over uniform sampling. The largest selection-only estimate is +1.9 pt (95% CI \[-1.2,+5.0\]) and our cascade is +1.2 pt \[-1.7,+4.1\]; all are equivalent to uniform within +/-5 pt. The same contrasts are unstable at the n=200 subset scale commonly used: three of four are up to 3.8x larger, one reverses sign, and resampled 200-item subsets reverse the full-bucket effect's sign in 35.8% of draws. The audit also corrects our own first results. The anomaly-domain "+ASR" arms injected no text, so the pooled +4.7 pt gain was selection plus a development prefix. With transcripts, ASR lowers XD-Violence accuracy by 10.0 pt when appended after the question and not at all when placed before it, and lowers UCF-Crime by 13.0 pt either way; on Video-MME, placing it first adds about 4 pt (7 on Qwen2-VL) to uniform sampling and to the cascade alike (post hoc, unadjusted). Swapping the answering model gives the largest contrast (+15.1 pt on MUSIC-AVQA, +33.3 pt on its worst type), but the same audio-visual expert with its soundtrack silenced keeps 92% of the gain (69% with a mismatched soundtrack), so the contrast is mostly a model swap. Code, per-item predictions and scripts that recompute every statistic are available at https://github.com/mikeslin-ir/long-video-qa-audit.

</details>

<a id="paper-4"></a>

### 4. [Vis2State: Visual Reasoning through Executable State Abstractions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07397v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07397

**作者：** Sheng Jin、Wentao Liu、Chen Qian

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07397v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07397v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出免训练 agentic 框架 Vis2State，将视觉谜题抽象为可检查、可修订的显式问题状态，由视觉检查指导状态构建，再由生成程序执行并借助反馈分别修订表示与求解过程。

**相关性简评：** 其显式状态与独立修订机制可作为 latent 推理工作区的对照，状态干预和错误分析可供核查视觉证据筛选、表示修订及执行反馈对推理状态与停止决策的影响。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Vis2State: Visual Reasoning through Executable State Abstractions

**abstract/A1：** Solving visual puzzles requires both accurate interpretation of visual evidence and reliable computation under task-specific rules. Embedding visual assumptions and task constraints directly in solving code makes the represented problem difficult to inspect and revise independently. We introduce Vis2State, a training-free agentic framework that abstracts visual puzzles into explicit, revisable problem states linking visual inspection and execution. Visual inspection guides state construction and refinement, while generated programs solve the represented problem. The model constructs both the state and the program without predefined puzzle schemas or solving procedures. Inspection and execution feedback allow it to revise the problem representation and solving procedure independently. Vis2State achieves the state-of-the-art performance on four benchmarks. Cross-model evaluation further demonstrates consistent gains. Ablations, error analyses, and fixed-program state interventions further support the effectiveness of the state-centered design. The code will be made publicly available to facilitate future research.

</details>

<a id="paper-5"></a>

### 5. [CST-Bench: Benchmarking Counterfactual Spatial Transition Reasoning with Causal Transition On-Policy Self-Distillation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07541v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07541

**作者：** Ying Li、Yongle Cheng、Jing Li、Xingquan Cai、Yawei Luo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07541v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07541v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 CST-Bench，一个含 7,082 个样本、覆盖 6 类干预与 12 种变换算子的反事实空间推理基准，并给出利用因果标注的 CT-OPSD 自蒸馏训练方法。

**相关性简评：** 其关于干预来源、受影响关系与不变关系的密集标注，可供核查 VLM 是否在内部保留实体关系并完成因果状态转移；「常规空间训练在反事实干预下失效」也是直接相关的失败诊断线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CST-Bench: Benchmarking Counterfactual Spatial Transition Reasoning with Causal Transition On-Policy Self-Distillation

**abstract/A1：** Spatial reasoning is a fundamental capability of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), yet existing benchmarks mainly focus on static scene understanding. When objects are moved or swapped, models must infer not only spatial relations but also how interventions change the scene state. To address this problem, we introduce CST-Bench, a benchmark for counterfactual spatial reasoning. CST-Bench contains 7,082 examples covering 6 intervention types and 12 transformation operators, with dense causal annotations including intervention sources, affected relations, expected effects, invariant relations, and metric changes. We benchmark general-purpose MLLMs on CST-Bench and further compare post-training strategies on CST-Bench and standard spatial benchmarks. Results show that methods effective for ordinary spatial reasoning can still fail under counterfactual interventions. We further formulate counterfactual spatial reasoning as causal state-transition modeling and propose CT-OPSD, which uses CST-Bench annotations to guide a teacher model in learning counterfactual state transitions and causal reasoning chains. These chains are then distilled into a student model that reasons only from real observations. Overall, experiment results establish CST-Bench as a challenging benchmark and reusable supervision resource for counterfactual spatial reasoning. CT-OPSD further provides an initial demonstration of how its annotations can support model improvement.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 5 figures, 8 tables; includes technical supplementary material

</details>

<a id="paper-6"></a>

### 6. [The Geometry of Simulated Perspectives: Dissociating Representation and Behaviour in a Multimodal Language Model](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08262v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08262

**作者：** Mathis Immertreu、Fitim Abdullahu、Thomas Kinfe、Claus Metzner、Helmut Grabner、Patrick Krauss、Achim Schilling

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08262v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08262v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 在 Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct 上，以 100 张图像、53 种提示条件系统性改变 persona 内容与语言框架，同时记录行为评分与逐层隐状态，考察 persona 条件化视觉趣味判断的表征几何，并发现表征变化与行为判断出现分离。

**相关性简评：** 直接触及「内部状态是否被实际使用」这一核心问题：分层结果显示早期层偏图像身份、后层才出现 persona 结构，且框架对表征的影响强于 persona 身份、对行为的影响却相反，可供核查 latent 相似度解读中深度、内容与功能因果性的混淆。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Geometry of Simulated Perspectives: Dissociating Representation and Behaviour in a Multimodal Language Model

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal language models can simulate different observers, yet it remains unclear how assigned perspectives reshape their internal representations and whether these changes govern their judgements. Here, we investigate the representational geometry of persona-conditioned visual interestingness in Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct. Across 100 images and 53 prompting conditions, we systematically vary persona content and the linguistic framing used to introduce it, while recording behavioural ratings and layer-wise hidden states. We observe a systematic reorganisation of representational geometry across processing depth: early layers primarily encode image identity, whereas persona-specific structures emerge in later layers. Strikingly, prompt framing reshapes internal representations more strongly than persona identity, whereas persona content has a greater impact on behavioural judgements. This dissociation is further illustrated by minimal identity statements that alter representations even without changing responses. These findings demonstrate that representational distance does not necessarily reflect behavioural relevance. More broadly, they reveal how simulated perspectives emerge as distinct structures within the representational space of artificial cognitive systems, highlighting a fundamental distinction between the geometry of internal representations and their functional consequences.

**comment/C1：** 30 pages, 10 figures, 2 tables. Code: https://github.com/MathisImm/persona-vlm-representations; data: https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.23664820

</details>

<a id="paper-7"></a>

### 7. [Octopus: One Brain, Many Branches for Latent-KV Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06956v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06956

**作者：** Tianming Sha

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06956v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06956v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Octopus 单模型多智能体 RL 框架，用 GPU 常驻的 KV 状态 DAG 和 fork、read、wait、rewind、commit 五个原语协调并行分支，并配合零拷贝 radix 分叉、仅注意力层 KV 挂载与打包 DAG-GRPO 训练。

**相关性简评：** 以 KV 而非文本摘要作为跨分支共享状态，加上分支回滚与「留一分支」反事实信用分配，可能为 LVR 的 latent 状态复用、证据汇合与贡献分配提供可核查线索；但实验为纯文本检索任务，迁移到视觉或多模态推理仍需判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Octopus: One Brain, Many Branches for Latent-KV Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Multi-agent LLM systems explore in parallel by spawning sub-agents with isolated context windows, whereas single-agent systems append every search and verification step into one growing trajectory. However, existing multi-agent frameworks duplicate prefix KV caches across branches, exchange findings by re-prefilling text summaries, and flatten or drop cross-branch dependencies during reinforcement learning (RL). We introduce Octopus, a single-model multi-agent RL framework in which all threads share one backbone and coordinate over a GPU-resident directed acyclic graph (DAG) of KV states through five primitives (fork, read\_thread, wait\_threads, rewind, commit). Octopus combines three co-designed mechanisms: (1) Zero-Copy Radix Forking, which spawns branches as $\\mathcal{O}(1)$ cursor views over shared physical KV pages; (2) Attention-Only KV Mounting, which mounts a sibling thread's terminal KV blocks directly into full-attention layers while bypassing linear-recurrent states (Gated DeltaNet / Mamba-2), cutting off-task hijacking by $21\\times$ ($57.9\\% \\to 2.7\\%$) while retaining $92.2\\%$ of full-text mutual information at zero extra KV allocation; and (3) a Packed DAG-GRPO Trainer, which packs all episode branches into one block-sparse attention pass and assigns counterfactual branch credit via leave-one-branch-out marginal rewards. Trained end-to-end with GRPO on Qwen3.5-4B over a sparse BM25 corpus, Octopus reaches $49.6\\%$ accuracy on BrowseComp-Plus ($+17.5$ points over single-agent GRPO at 143K context and $+12.5$ points over text-summary multi-agent RL) and $51.2\\%$ on OpenResearcher, outperforming dense-retrieval agents up to $250\\times$ larger while increasing training throughput $3.3\\times$ and cutting peak KV memory $2.8\\times$.

**comment/C1：** 19 pages, 12 figures, 7 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-8"></a>

### 8. [Shaping &amp; Monitoring Models through their Internal Representations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07071v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07071

**作者：** Thomas Fel、Matthew Kowal、Matt Feiszli、Satchel Grant、Andrew Lee、Chris Earls、Fatih Dinc、Vasudev Shyam、Usha Bhalla、Leon Bergen、Siddharth Boppana、Ching Fang、Michael Byun、Tristan Gallent、Nicholas Wang、Tal Haklay、Dron Hazra、Neehar Kondapaneni、Connor Watts、Ryan Panwar、Thomas Dooms、Jack Merullo、Atticus Geiger、Ekdeep Singh Lubana、Thomas McGrath、Owen Lewis

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07071v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07071v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Mechanistic Shaping 训练框架，通过输出或内部表示约束模型表达及依赖的概念，在视觉、医学影像、语言、最高 32B 及分类/SFT/DPO/GRPO 上验证；并给出监控器层级与概念规避的三种几何机制（漂移、擦除、非线性高阶编码）。

**相关性简评：** 其区分「逃出某个探针」与「概念真正消失」的层级、重拟合探针与梯度隔离实验，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 是否真实保留视觉内容、线性探针读数的可信度及信息坍缩判定，迁移到多模态推理尚需较多验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Shaping &amp; Monitoring Models through their Internal Representations

**abstract/A1：** We propose Mechanistic Shaping, a unified framework for shaping models through their internal representations. Mechanistic Shaping is applied during training so that models follow specifications (rules) about which concepts they express, measured via their outputs or internal representations (e.g., no harmful content), and also which concepts they rely on, i.e., what they use to make their decisions (e.g., not a candidate's gender when screening a résumé). Across multiple domains (vision, medical imaging, language), scales (up to 32B parameters), and training paradigms (classification, SFT, DPO, GRPO), we find that Mechanistic Shaping substantially reduces targeted behaviors while preserving task performance. Since training against a representation can also modify it, we ask whether shaped concepts remain monitorable, a question that depends on the class of monitors considered. We therefore introduce a hierarchy of such classes, which separates a concept that escapes one probe from a concept that is truly gone. We characterize three geometric mechanisms of circumvention: the concept can drift away from the monitor, be erased from the layer, or persist in higher-order statistics as a nonlinear encoding, which we explain through a product-code construction. Although other mechanisms may arise in different settings or at larger scales, these three account for all of our observations, and each can be detected by a matching monitor. In practice, we find that shaped concepts largely remain monitorable: refitted linear probes recover the concept in most settings, and the original probe remains reliable whenever gradients do not flow through it. Taken together, these results point to the potential of using interpretability as a tool for training, and call for a correspondingly rigorous science of interpretability-based training.

</details>

<a id="paper-9"></a>

### 9. [Compositional Unalignment: Auditing Safety in Large Vision-Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07216v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07216

**作者：** Hong kyu Lee、Kai Shu、Li Xiong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07216v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07216v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文审计 LVLM 组合式安全对齐的稳健性：发现最小监督微调即可达到多阶段 RL 流水线的评判分数；九种方向发现方法虽能以最高 AUC=0.99 识别组合危害，但消融所恢复方向要么不影响拒答、要么破坏连贯性；据此提出免训练的 CMaG，用拒答—顺从对数概率差的梯度在中层激活上构造方向，并以部分良性配对对比隔离跨模态交互项，单次反向即得到可单调把拒答转为顺从的引导方向。

**相关性简评：** 其「线性可解码 ≠ 被因果使用」的分离，以及用良性配对隔离跨模态交互项、在中间层激活上定位可干预方向的做法，可能为 LVR 中 latent 内容是否真被答案使用、贡献分配与视觉证据因果作用的核查提供方法线索，值得人工核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Compositional Unalignment: Auditing Safety in Large Vision-Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Large vision-language models (LVLMs) are vulnerable to compositional harm: an image and a prompt that are benign in isolation become harmful through the model's cross-modal reasoning. Recent alignment methods report strong scores on compositional safety benchmarks, but an LLM judge only measures observed behavior and cannot reveal how robustly safety is integrated into the model. We argue that un-alignment provides a complementary audit of alignment robustness. We first show that minimal supervised fine-tuning reaches an LLM-judge score comparable to a multi-stage reinforcement-learning pipeline, so similar benchmark scores cannot establish robustness by themselves. We then show that recognition and refusal of compositional harm are decoupled: nine standard direction-finding methods detect compositional harm with up to AUC=0.99, yet ablating the recovered directions either leaves refusal intact or destroys model coherence. We therefore propose CMaG, a training-free un-alignment method for compositional safety. CMaG measures refusal intention with a refusal-versus-compliance log-probability margin, takes its gradient with respect to mid-layer activations, and contrasts it against partially benign pairs to isolate the cross-modal interaction term. A single backward pass over 80 examples yields a direction along which steering monotonically converts refusal into compliance. On Qwen2.5-VL-7B and LLaVA-1.5-7B, CMaG reduces compositional safety scores by up to 65 points while over 98% of outputs remain coherent, outperforming gradient-based baselines; the direction also transfers to text-explicit harm and un-aligns models hardened by defensive training. Compositional safety can thus appear behaviorally strong while remaining fragile to targeted internal intervention. Warning: this paper contains unsafe model outputs, including references to self-harm.

</details>

<a id="paper-10"></a>

### 10. [Test-Time Self-Play: Scaling without a Verifier](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07367v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07367

**作者：** Akira Yoshiyama、Akmal Ashirmatov、Jonas Hübotter、Ido Hakimi、Andreas Krause

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07367v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07367v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Test-Time Self-Play（TTSP），在缺少可靠验证器时用非对称自博弈进行测试时扩展，并在 LiveCodeBench v6 的难度校准子集上验证其效果随验证器减弱而更突出。

**相关性简评：** 无验证器下的测试时扩展与自博弈式内部监督，可能为 LVR 的视觉证据筛选、latent 轨迹贡献分配及停止决策提供方法线索，但原文未涉及多模态状态，角色分工与选择机制能否迁移尚待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Test-Time Self-Play: Scaling without a Verifier

**abstract/A1：** Test-time scaling methods spend additional compute at deployment to improve a large language model's performance on a hard target problem. Most methods rely on a verifier that scores candidate solutions for correctness, using it to select among or refine the model's outputs. In many deployment regimes, however, a strong verifier on the target problem is unavailable. We introduce Test-Time Self-Play (TTSP), an approach to test-time scaling using asymmetric self-play without reliance on a verifier. On a difficulty-calibrated subset of LiveCodeBench v6, TTSP is the best-performing method at every verifier strength we evaluate, and the gap to the strongest baseline widens as the verifier weakens; in the no-verifier limit, TTSP yields a 35\\% absolute improvement in mean@8. The gains hold across a range of verifier strengths, except when the base policy has near-zero coverage of the correct answer.

</details>

<a id="paper-11"></a>

### 11. [Localization Does Not Imply Steerability: A Mechanistic Study of Sign Errors in LLM Mathematics](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07478v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07478

**作者：** Shradha Agarwal、Tariq J.、Deepak Rajbhar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07478v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07478v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文用逐层读出与残差流方向消减，定位 LLM 多步数学中错误符号的写入层与因果回路，并发现部分模型虽同样定位清晰却因跨层冗余编码而难以干预。

**相关性简评：** “定位不等于可操控”及方向性因果干预可供核查 LVR 中视觉细节或 latent 状态究竟被答案使用，还是仅可解码；其冗余编码分析也可能启发 latent 内容贡献分配的研究。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Localization Does Not Imply Steerability: A Mechanistic Study of Sign Errors in LLM Mathematics

**abstract/A1：** When large language models do multi-step mathematics, a characteristic failure recurs: the model writes one wrong sign token mid-computation -- a cofactor sign, the minus of a recursive integration-by-parts step -- then executes the rest of the arithmetic correctly, and the answer comes out wrong. We ask where inside the network that sign is written. Across six open models spanning 14B to 675B parameters, a per-layer readout shows the wrong sign is committed late -- by MLP layers in the last quarter of the network (70-100% per model and task), a pattern present, where tested (Qwen), in the base model before instruction tuning. The mechanism varies: some models carry fixed "minus-stamp" layers that push a minus regardless of the right answer, others carry amplifiers of whichever sign was already chosen, and Qwen adds a downstream rescue layer that opposes the stamp. The circuit is causal: subtracting a single sign direction from the residual stream flips 13 of 14 held-out minus-written integration-by-parts errors on Llama-3.3-70B, with flips replicating on Qwen, Gemma, and Mistral-Small (24-70% per cell), and with representation-level specificity -- the same dose breaks correctly-minus answers while sparing correct-plus answers where the direction is surgical (0/72 on Llama), and matched random-direction and control-layer interventions are null. Core claims were preregistered with pass criteria frozen in git before held-out data was seen: 23 of 29 checks passed, all misses reported. But causality is not uniform: Phi-4 implements an equally well-localized circuit at the same late depth, yet the identical intervention flips almost nothing -- the sign is written redundantly across ~15 layers with margins several times larger (median 17.8 logits). Localization, we conclude, does not imply steerability: two models can share a circuit location yet differ enormously in how overturnable it is.

**comment/C1：** 61 pages. Code, per-case results and preregistration: https://github.com/signcircuits-experiments/sign-circuit-scaling

</details>

<a id="paper-12"></a>

### 12. [ReferClaw: Disambiguating User Intent for Egocentric Assistance](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07683v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07683

**作者：** Hangyu Qin、Junbin Xiao、Angela Yao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.MA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07683v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07683v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ReferClaw，将第一视角流式视频问答建模为交互澄清过程：多个协作代理产生候选解释、提出澄清问题并基于确认意图与视觉证据作答，并用有界、成本感知的策略平衡信息增益与用户负担，结合过程奖励与代理级贡献分配进行训练。

**相关性简评：** 其「何时停止追问」的成本感知决策和 agent 级贡献分配，可能为 LVR 的 STOP 决策、视觉证据获取与训练贡献分配提供可核查线索，但论文不涉及 latent 推理状态本身。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ReferClaw: Disambiguating User Intent for Egocentric Assistance

**abstract/A1：** Users of egocentric AI assistants often ask short, ambiguous questions whose intended referents cannot be uniquely resolved from video and dialogue history. For example, "Where did I leave it?" may refer to several previously handled objects. Existing VideoQA systems typically commit to a single interpretation, risking plausible but intention-misaligned answers. We introduce ReferClaw, an agentic framework that formulates egocentric streaming QA as an interactive process: the assistant asks clarifying questions to resolve user intent before answering. Three cooperating agents identify candidate interpretations, request clarification, and generate answers grounded in the resolved intent and visual evidence. A bounded, cost-aware policy balances expected information gain against user effort while limiting clarification turns and question complexity. Referclaw learns from interaction through intent-specific skill updates and process-reward-guided policy optimization with agent-level credit assignment. Evaluations on augmented egocentric QA benchmarks demonstrate improved answer accuracy and joint answer-and-grounding performance over existing baselines, alongside zero-shot transfer across datasets. These findings support interactive clarification as a practical approach to resolving online user intent in egocentric streaming assistance.

</details>

<a id="paper-13"></a>

### 13. [The Intrusion Pathway: How Non-Evidential Context Degrades Language Model Performance](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07778v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07778

**作者：** Dikshant Kukreja、Gautam Gupta、Avni Mittal、Avinash Anand、Kritarth Prasad、Zhengkui Wang、Timothy Liu、Rajiv Ratn Shah、Aik Beng Ng、Simon See、Erik Cambria

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07778v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07778v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文追踪语言模型在提示含无关非证据上下文时准确率下降的机制：MLP 富化查询主体与无关 token、parroting heads 搬运前缀内容，并把干扰部分定位到稀疏的「intrusion heads」，用约十个头的掩码 knockout 与干净末位残差替换进行干预。

**相关性简评：** 其注意力头级定位、类别条件干预和与证据整合头的权重可分性分析，可能为 VLM 中视觉干扰与证据筛选的机制诊断提供可核查线索，例如无关视觉 token 是否经类似通路污染末位 latent 状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Intrusion Pathway: How Non-Evidential Context Degrades Language Model Performance

**abstract/A1：** Language models lose accuracy when a prompt contains non-evidential context: irrelevant statements from unrelated domains, or random off-distribution tokens, with no competing answer and no semantic overlap with the query. Such noise pervades retrieval-augmented, tool-using, and long-context deployments, yet the failure has lacked a mechanistic account. We trace the corruption to the Intrusion Pathway, which starts with MLPs enriching both query-subject and irrelevant-token representations, parroting heads carry prefix-token content to the prediction position, and relational readers largely avoid unrelated sources. Nevertheless, preserved subject enrichment and selective reading do not prevent intrusion in the broader computation. We then localize part of this interference to a sparse set of attention heads, which we call intrusion heads. Their prefix-derived writes promote distractor-related content, while their reduced support for the correct answer arises primarily from weaker non-prefix contributions rather than direct suppression by the prefix. The same heads transfer across tasks and distractor types and are largely weight-disjoint from the heads that integrate relevant contextual evidence, enabling a class-conditional intervention. Replacing the corrupted last-token residual with its clean counterpart, an oracle probe that requires a matched clean forward pass, recovers 97.6% on average of the distractor-induced drop across four downstream tasks. A deployable mask level knockout of about ten heads, roughly 1% of the model's attention heads, recovers 57% at bounded cost to helpful context.

**comment/C1：** 32 pages, 25 figures, 29 tables. Dikshant Kukreja and Gautam Gupta contributed equally. Website: https://dk4248.github.io/the-intrusion-pathway/ Code: https://github.com/dk4248/the-intrusion-pathway

</details>

<a id="paper-14"></a>

### 14. [Same Answer, Different Future: Matched Predictions Do Not Imply Matched Belief Updating in Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07913v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07913

**作者：** Weihan Li、Junhao Wu、Xinlei Chen、Tianshi Zheng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07913v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07913v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在 Bernoulli 推断任务中，用四种文本路径和两种激活干预构造模型状态，并以「有效证据权重」量化初始信念的持续性，发现当前预测一致并不代表后续证据更新一致。

**相关性简评：** 「相同预测≠相同信念状态」的配对读出与有效证据权重，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 是否真正承载视觉证据，以及同答案不同内部状态下的更新差异，但需迁移到多模态并检验估计有效性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Same Answer, Different Future: Matched Predictions Do Not Imply Matched Belief Updating in Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Language models can produce similar probabilistic predictions after observing outcomes, reading summaries, receiving instructions, or undergoing activation interventions. Whether these routes induce equivalent belief states remains unclear: agreement in current predictions does not establish how a model will respond to subsequent evidence. We investigate this distinction in a Bernoulli inference task with an analytic Bayesian reference. Across three open-weight language models, we prepare states through four textual routes and two activation-level interventions, measure both next-outcome continuations and explicit probabilistic estimates, and append identical new evidence from each prepared state. We quantify the persistence of the initial belief through its effective evidence weight, the number of new observations it is worth. We find that the format of evidence decides which readouts express its content: outcome sequences primarily affect continuations, whereas summaries and stated rates primarily affect explicit estimates. Moreover, effective weights differ substantially across routes, with summaries receiving weights that scale weakly with their stated observation counts and elicitation questions changing inferred weights by orders of magnitude. In a one-shot steering setting, an intervention that reproduces a natural record's immediate prediction loses most of its effect under subsequent evidence. These results show that agreement in current readouts is insufficient to establish equivalence in belief updating: how a belief is acquired and how it is read jointly determine its subsequent dynamics.

**comment/C1：** 24 pages, 13 figures, 7 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-15"></a>

### 15. [QRAE: Query-Relevant Attention Entropy for Context Compression in DeepSearch Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07976v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07976

**作者：** Yingjie Tan、Haiquan Qiu、Quanming Yao、Manyi Zhong、Xianzhi Yu、Yaqing Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07976v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07976v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 QRAE，用稀疏证据评估头结合配对 prefill，以归一化注意力熵为检索块打分，高分块进入持久证据白名单、不确定块进入候选缓存待重评，实现无需生成式压缩的在线上下文压缩。

**相关性简评：** 其「无查询对照抑制共享注意力响应＋熵度量证据广度」的打分思路和不确定证据缓存重评机制，可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选、证据持续保留与是否继续补充证据的决策提供可供核查的线索；但工作对象是纯文本 DeepSearch agent，未涉及多模态 latent 状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** QRAE: Query-Relevant Attention Entropy for Context Compression in DeepSearch Agents

**abstract/A1：** DeepSearch LLM agents repeatedly search and carry retrieved sources into subsequent reasoning steps. This expanding context increases token consumption and latency, although many results do not contribute to later reasoning. We propose Query-Relevant Attention Entropy (QRAE), an online context compression method that uses sparse evidence-assessment heads to decide which intact retrieved chunks should persist across reasoning steps. It scores bounded candidate batches through paired prefills: a query-free control suppresses shared attention responses, while normalized entropy measures the breadth of the positive response within each chunk. High-scoring chunks enter a persistent evidence whitelist and are included in the reasoning context. Uncertain chunks remain outside the reasoning context but are retained in a candidate cache, where they can be retrieved for later reevaluation. QRAE requires neither an additional generative compression pass nor modification of the attention operator. Across multiple DeepSearch benchmarks and reasoning models, QRAE improves task success by an average of 2.35 percentage points and reduces end-to-end latency and reported token usage by an average of 30% and 45%, respectively. These benefits generalize to a model with nearly 100B parameters.

**comment/C1：** 29 pages, including appendices

</details>

<a id="paper-16"></a>

### 16. [SwarmEvolve: Latent Agent Swarms for Evolutionary Discovery and Optimization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08483v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08483

**作者：** Qile Jiang、George Em Karniadakis

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.NE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08483v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08483v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SwarmEvolve：每轮以数百个低成本 agent 组成群体，在 latent space 中推理而不解码成文本，通过传递 KV cache 共享信息，并用隐状态向量距离做新颖性过滤以保留差异想法，在 21 个数学发现与科学计算问题上快速取得高分。

**相关性简评：** 不解码的 latent 推理、KV cache 拼接式信息交换与基于隐状态距离的筛选，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 内容的传递、选择与相似度度量；但任务无视觉输入，隐状态距离是否反映内容而非深度或缓存结构尚不清楚。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SwarmEvolve: Latent Agent Swarms for Evolutionary Discovery and Optimization

**abstract/A1：** LLM-guided evolutionary search uses AI agents to improve programs for mathematical and computational problems by iteratively refining solutions and combining high-scoring methods. However, the process is often slow because the LLM generates a long textual chain of thought for each round and must repeat this generation many times to maintain population diversity. We present SwarmEvolve, a framework in which hundreds of low-cost agents per round work in swarms, reason in latent space without decoding to text, and share information by passing KV caches. Each swarm studies the problem from a different angle. A novelty filter keeps the most distinct agent ideas by directly measuring the distances between the agents' hidden state vectors. Crossover of ideas happens naturally in latent space, as a summarizer agent receives the combined KV caches of all swarm members. On 21 problems in mathematical discovery and scientific computing, SwarmEvolve gets the highest average score and needs a median of only 3 to 8 minutes to reach the best scores that other state-of-the-art frameworks reach in 60 minutes. On several problems, SwarmEvolve with Qwen3 8B improves on the solutions that AlphaEvolve first found with frontier LLMs. Our work shows that agent swarms that communicate in latent space offer a fast, low-cost approach to program search for scientific problems.

</details>

<a id="paper-17"></a>

### 17. [When Does Test-Time Search Help World Action Models? An Empirical Audit of Candidate Selection and Simulator Integrity](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41970v2>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.41970

**作者：** Jun Liu、Dajie Sun、Zhenglun Kong、Zhenjiang Fan、Peiyan Dong、Fan Yang、Wenbin Zhang、Geng Yuan、Xue Lin、Yanzhi Wang、Dong Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41970v2>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.41970v2>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在 Cosmos Policy 与 RoboCasa 上以预注册方式审计世界动作模型测试时搜索的三项前提：候选可选择性、计算节省是否转化为实际加速、评估是否可靠；发现学习选择器不如随机、82% 状态候选结果相同、token 剪枝端到端加速远低于目标，并发现与修复一个沿重计算路径加剧的静默渲染损坏。

**相关性简评：** 其「决策信号—实际效率—评估完整性」前提链审计与 LVR 中 THINK/LOOK/STOP 收益成本核验、latent 候选信息是否可用及视觉证据/渲染可靠性问题可能直接呼应，负结果与审计设计可供核查借鉴。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Does Test-Time Search Help World Action Models? An Empirical Audit of Candidate Selection and Simulator Integrity

**abstract/A1：** World-action models increasingly apply test-time scaling, spending extra inference computation on candidate selection and adaptive allocation to improve control. Such gains are credible only when candidates carry exploitable signal, nominal compute savings become real speedup, and evaluation stays reliable. We audit all three on Cosmos Policy and RoboCasa with a premise-first, pre-registered methodology. On clean data, a learned value selector is 1.66 pp worse than random; a hindsight oracle gains only 1.40 pp, with an episode-level interval spanning zero; and 82% of states show identical candidate outcomes, leaving little selection headroom. Token pruning cuts per-forward FLOPs by up to 47% but yields at most a measured 1.15$\\times$ end-to-end speedup on the deployed path (1.25$\\times$ on a decode-excluded path), short of a 1.5$\\times$ target, and our adaptive-allocation experiment is inconclusive under a power gate. We further identify and fix a silent rendering corruption whose rate rises sharply along compute-heavy planning paths while leaving the official direct-policy result unaffected, and release a detector, fix, and scripts. These findings show why test-time scaling should be audited as a chain of premises (decision signal, realized efficiency, evaluationintegrity) rather than by success rate alone. Conclusions are scoped to the tested configuration.

</details>

<a id="paper-18"></a>

### 18. [Action-Conditioned VLM Monitoring for Autonomous Vehicle Semantic Anomalies](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07540v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07540

**作者：** Daniel Adejumo、Omkar Ankalkope、Kunal Runwal、Aliasghar Arab

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07540v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07540v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究自动驾驶中的「动作条件化」语义异常监控：VLM 先从结果隐藏的视频窗口预测场景所要求的动作，再与真实自车轨迹按显式兼容规则比对；同时给出 112 个配对场景的六字段分类体系与视频语料，两阶段协议取得 83.0% 准确率、异常召回 0.773（单阶段视频基线为 74.5%/0.536，但特异度下降）。

**相关性简评：** 以「场景应有动作」作显式中间表示再与观测行为核验，可供核查 VLM 视觉理解是否真正进入判定、以及该中间假设在召回与特异度上的代价，与 LVR 中视觉证据使用和评价设计有具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Action-Conditioned VLM Monitoring for Autonomous Vehicle Semantic Anomalies

**abstract/A1：** We study action-conditioned monitoring of autonomous-vehicle behavior in semantically unusual driving scenes. The target is whether the executed ego action is appropriate for the scene: the same scene can admit both normal and anomalous behavior, so visual unusualness alone does not determine the monitoring label. A Vision-Language Model (VLM) first predicts the scene-required action from an outcome-hidden video window; the monitor then compares that expectation with the realized ego-motion trajectory under an explicit compatibility rule. To study this monitoring task, we contribute a six-field taxonomy of 112 paired scenarios spanning both failure polarities (42 derived from documented incidents), a generated video corpus with estimated trajectories, and an exploratory two-stage monitor on that corpus. On 235 clips from 15 scenario families, the two-stage protocol with a fixed T-2.5 s window obtains 83.0% accuracy and anomaly recall 0.773, versus 74.5% and 0.536 for a single-stage video-only baseline; specificity is 0.880 versus 0.928.

**comment/C1：** 7 pages. Submitted to IEEE ICRA 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-19"></a>

### 19. [THG-Omni: Traceable Human-Centric Graph Supervision for Video Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07836v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07836

**作者：** Jisheng Dang、Kai Wei、Ronghao Lin、Bimei Wang、Hong Peng、Wei-Shi Zheng、Bin Hu、Qi Tian、Tat-Seng Chua

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07836v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07836v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作提出 THG-Omni，用人物中心图结构监督全模态视频推理：把参与者轨迹与带时间定位的标注连成图，构建可核查的训练问答，并在推理时使用预测图，使答案可追溯到具体参与者、事件与时间区间。

**相关性简评：** 其图结构是显式证据载体而非 latent 状态，但其「仅凭预测图仍保留答题所需证据」的诊断可供核查视觉细节与实体关系在内部表示中的保留与坍缩问题，并关联证据筛选与可追溯性评价。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** THG-Omni: Traceable Human-Centric Graph Supervision for Video Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Omni-modal models can answer questions about video, but their predictions rarely show which participant, event, or interval supports an answer. Moreover, answer-level supervision alone does not teach a model to interpret structured video evidence. To systematically mitigate this issue, we present THG-Omni, a human-centric graph-supervision framework that links Segment Anything Model~3 participant tracks with temporally grounded Qwen3-VL annotations. Candidate semantic claims are checked against the source video and available audio before they are used to construct training questions, while track geometry provides explicit spatial and temporal supervision. The resulting questions teach an omni-modal reasoning model to interpret participant attributes, actions, affect, interactions, and their timing from a structured graph. At inference, THG-Omni uses a predicted graph constructed from the evaluation video. Experiments on IntentBench show consistent improvements over the unadapted backbone across social reasoning, emotion understanding, and deception perception. On NExT-QA, THG-Omni achieves 89.54\\% accuracy with the matched graph, compared with 85.10\\% without graph input. Further validation experiments show that compact graph input achieves 78.22\\% accuracy, compared with 78.02\\% using the full graph and 77.58\\% without graph input. A complementary diagnostic demonstrates that the predicted graph preserves evidence useful for answering questions even without the source video and audio. Together, these findings show that THG-Omni improves human-centric video reasoning while grounding its evidence in identifiable participants and events. Our codes are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/review-n94hhfcwp5666/

</details>

### 20. [NPCBench: A Clinical Apprenticeship Benchmark for Multimodal Care-Pathway Reasoning in Nasopharyngeal Carcinoma](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08369v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08369

**作者：** Pengkai Wang、Wei-Wei Zhang、Yan Li、Min Tang、Zhitian Hou、Zeyu Liu、Guanghao Zhu、Yuanyi Wang、Yanggan Gu、Wenjun Wang、Minheng Ni、Congkai Xie、Zhijie Sang、Jianmin Wu、Ying Sun、Hongxia Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08369v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08369v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出鼻咽癌临床「学徒制」基准 NPCBench，含 4,777 个知识与病例题及 100 名患者的 CarePath 队列（417 个计分任务），按整条诊疗路径联合判定诊断与照护答案是否正确，并报告单题高分不等于路径联合成功的「组合差距」，21 个多模态模型严格成功率最高 23%。

**相关性简评：** 其 episode 级联合打分、配对对照与纵向复评，可供核查 VLM 是否真正依据视觉证据并在多步中更新患者状态、是否出现视觉旁路或信息坍缩；但属专科临床基准，线索向通用 LVR 的迁移仍需核查任务设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** NPCBench: A Clinical Apprenticeship Benchmark for Multimodal Care-Pathway Reasoning in Nasopharyngeal Carcinoma

**abstract/A1：** Current medical benchmarks typically break down clinical competence into isolated factual questions, single-image tasks, or static case vignettes. These tests score individual answers, but reveal less about whether a model can integrate subspecialty knowledge with multimodal evidence and track a patient's condition throughout a course of care. To close this gap, we introduce NPCBench, a clinical apprenticeship benchmark for nasopharyngeal carcinoma (NPC). NPCBench mirrors specialist training, from guideline and foundational knowledge to case planning, longitudinal review, CarePath integration, and open-ended consultation. It contains 4,777 component and consultation items and a 100-patient CarePath cohort with 417 scored diagnostic and care tasks. The episode-level evaluation tests whether diagnostic and care answers are jointly correct within a care pathway. By linking stepwise tasks to patient-level care trajectories, NPCBench evaluates whether models ground decisions in visual evidence, interpret changes in patient state, and meet clinical requirements in consultation. Across 30 evaluated models, we observe a composition gap: strong performance on individual tasks does not ensure jointly correct diagnostic and care answers for the same patient. Among 21 multimodal models, strict CarePath success reaches at most 23%. Paired controls and physician calibration further examine evidence grounding, longitudinal revision, and consultation judging. Overall, NPCBench broadens the evaluation of medical artificial intelligence beyond static question-answering paradigms to an apprenticeship-oriented assessment of clinical workflows, thereby offering a testbed for evidence-based and temporally coherent reasoning in oncology care.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the 40th Conference on Neural Information Processing Systems (NeurIPS 2026), Evaluations and Datasets Track

</details>

### 21. [KDASO: Knowledge-Data Dual-Driven Automated Skill Optimization for Liability Adjudication Tasks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06972v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06972

**作者：** Zi-Jian Cheng、Weiming Wu、Jie Meng、Zhen Peng、Shan Huang、Qun Li、Guobing Wu、Lan-Zhe Guo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06972v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06972v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 KDASO，用近期判例数据做邻域条件化的技能归纳、依据 LLM 执行轨迹对齐技能描述，并在上下文预算内路由技能，使技能库随场景演化而持续更新，用于责任判定类任务。

**相关性简评：** 其「依轨迹分析修正技能描述」与「预算约束下的选择性路由」可供核查是否迁移为 LVR 中的轨迹级监督与证据/状态筛选线索；但对象是文本法律技能库，与视觉 latent 状态无直接关系，联系较弱。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** KDASO: Knowledge-Data Dual-Driven Automated Skill Optimization for Liability Adjudication Tasks

**abstract/A1：** Liability adjudication tasks (e.g., legal charge determination) require analyzing event descriptions against domain-specific skills to determine whether a party should bear liability. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promise in this area by leveraging executable skills for interpretable reasoning, existing approaches face three critical limitations: skill libraries become stale as real-world scenarios dynamically evolve, skill descriptions often suffer from a semantic execution gap where LLMs deviate from intended expert logic, and growing skill collections exceed context budgets causing critical skill omission. To address these challenges, we propose Knowledge-Data Dual-Driven Automated Skill Optimization (KDASO), which continuously evolves a skill library through neighborhood-conditioned induction from recent adjudication data, execution-aligned refinement based on LLM trajectory analysis, and context-budgeted routing for scalable skill selection. Experiments across ride-hailing adjudication, legal liability determination, and logical reasoning judgment demonstrate that KDASO significantly outperforms current general-purpose and domain-specific baselines. KDASO further achieves 8.96% improvement when transferring skills to concept-similar scenarios and maintains consistent performance gains through continuous optimization, underscoring the high transferability and stability of generated skills.

</details>

### 22. [Big Models, Small Gains: Frozen Forecasters as Classifiers on MONSTER](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06988v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06988

**作者：** Rishit Jain、Rishit Kar、Chetashri Bhadane、Nilesh Patil

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06988v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06988v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 在 MONSTER 的六个大规模时序分类数据集上，以 1%、10%、100% 标签比例比较两个 forecasting 基础模型（Reverso、Chronos-Bolt）的冻结表示与四种成熟分类器及同架构随机权重版本，发现预训练相对随机权重多数有效，但仍稳定弱于最强非预训练基线。

**相关性简评：** 其「随机权重对照＋强任务基线」的表示评估范式可能可供核查：用于判断 VLM latent 状态中保留的任务信息究竟来自预训练还是架构本身；但论文属时序领域，迁移到视觉多模态推理需较多验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Big Models, Small Gains: Frozen Forecasters as Classifiers on MONSTER

**abstract/A1：** Forecasting foundation models are increasingly used as frozen feature extractors for time series classification, but it remains unclear whether their pretrained representations offer an advantage over strong classifiers that do not require pretraining. This raises an important question: do these representations provide useful information beyond what the model architecture alone can capture, particularly when labelled data are scarce? We present a controlled benchmarking of two forecasting models, Reverso and Chronos-Bolt, on six large datasets from the MONSTER benchmark, using 1%, 10%, and 100% of the available training labels. We compare their frozen representations against four established time series classification methods and random-weight versions of the same forecasting models. Our results show that pretraining improves classification accuracy over random-weight counterparts on five of the six datasets, with gains of up to 23 percentage points. However, the frozen representations do not reliably outperform the strongest non-pretrained baseline in any setting: they perform worse in 15 of 17 settings and tie in the remaining two. Further experiments show that restoring amplitude information recovers part of the performance gap, while fine-tuning and forecast-error features do not close it. These findings highlight the importance of strong task specific baselines and random weight controls when evaluating forecasting foundation models for classification. Our code is available at https://github.com/Rachet2006/frozen-forecasters-monster.

**comment/C1：** Asian Conference on Machine Learning, FMLTS Workshop, 2026

</details>

### 23. [The Two-Ledger Model: Transaction-Grain Cost Accounting for Multi-Agent AI Systems Across Clouds, Models, and Systems of Record](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06989v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06989

**作者：** Vivek Acharya

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.DC、cs.MA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06989v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06989v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出「双账本模型」，把结算粒度的 FOCUS 账单与基于 OpenTelemetry GenAI 遥测的调用粒度用量记录关联起来，通过 trace 上下文、A2A/MCP 元数据与业务键等多层关联把每条调用归属到具体业务事务，并用版本化价目表实时定价、周期对账与残余项识别未计量开销。

**相关性简评：** 其调用粒度的用量归属与 trace 级成本核算机制，可能可供核查用于度量 LVR 中 THINK/LOOK/STOP 的实际计算成本与动态停止的性价比；但论文本身不涉及视觉推理状态或证据筛选，属方法线索而非竞争工作。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Two-Ledger Model: Transaction-Grain Cost Accounting for Multi-Agent AI Systems Across Clouds, Models, and Systems of Record

**abstract/A1：** Enterprises run business transactions through multi-agent AI systems spanning clouds, large language models (LLMs), orchestration harnesses, and systems of record. Finance receives AI cost only at settlement grain, aggregated by SKU and charge period. The business question (what did this order, claim, or case cost in AI?) cannot be answered from billing data, because providers never see enterprise transaction boundaries. The FinOps Open Cost and Usage Specification (FOCUS) gives billing data one schema across providers but remains settlement-grain even as it adds AI token and requester attribution. We formalize this grain mismatch, derive seven requirements, and propose the Two-Ledger Model: a settlement ledger of FOCUS-conformant billing data joined to an attribution ledger of invocation-grain usage records built on OpenTelemetry GenAI telemetry. A layered correlation spine (trace context, A2A/MCP metadata, business-key correlation, probabilistic matching) assigns each record to its business transaction with recorded method and confidence; a versioned rate card prices usage in near real time; period reconciliation ties both ledgers to the invoice, and the unexplained residual identifies shadow AI. Costs are reported per order, claim, or outcome with agentic cost metrics. A simulation with known ground truth shows the spine sustaining cost coverage above 99% as trace propagation falls to zero, the reconciliation residual recovering planted unmetered spend within 0.24 percentage points while flagging provider billing errors, and mid-flight gating cutting p99 transaction cost by up to 61% at a 2-4% false-pause rate. We propose the attribution ledger as an invocation-grain FOCUS profile for standardization.

**comment/C1：** 16 pages, 5 figures, 12 tables. Code and synthetic data: https://github.com/curiosityexplorer/two-ledger-model

</details>

<a id="paper-24"></a>

### 24. [Batch-Conditioned Semantic Anchors for Robust Transductive Adaptation of Vision--Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06993v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06993

**作者：** Mohammed Rahman Sherif Khan Mohammad、Ardhendu Behera、Sandip Pradhan、Swagat Kumar、Amr Ahmed

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06993v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06993v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 JPTA，把 VLM 的 transductive 测试时适配表述为「批条件语义参考估计」，使 CLIP 文本原型仅在可靠度校准的证据下向图像侧批结构偏移，并在稀疏、先验偏移批次上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其按可靠度缩放视觉证据对语义锚点的修正、以及锚点漂移与有效类支撑诊断，可能与 LVR 中视觉证据筛选和推理状态更新的门控设计相关，可供核查；但论文本身针对分类适配，不涉及 latent 推理轨迹。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Batch-Conditioned Semantic Anchors for Robust Transductive Adaptation of Vision--Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Transductive adaptation improves vision-language models at test time by refining predictions on an unlabeled batch, but current methods face a key trade-off. Aggressive approaches exploit batch structure but can drift when batches are sparse, have prior shift, or cover little of the label space. Conservative methods better preserve CLIP text semantics, but fixed semantic references can under-adapt even when the batch provides strong corrective evidence. We propose JPTA (Joint Prior and Transport Adaptation), a prior-aware framework that casts transductive VLM adaptation as batch-conditioned semantic reference estimation. Instead of treating CLIP text prototypes as fixed priors or freely replaceable, JPTA views them as semantic references that shift toward image-side batch structure only under calibrated evidence. It estimates batch priors and soft image-side prototypes to form transported semantic references whose impact is scaled by batch reliability. Across standard benchmarks, low effective-class regimes, all-class evaluation, and online streams, JPTA consistently outperforms TransCLIP and StatA, with the largest gains on sparse and prior-shifted batches. Anchor-drift, active-support, and absent-class-mass diagnostics show that JPTA fixes effective-class mismatch without unconstrained transductive drift, indicating that robust transductive VLM adaptation should recalibrate semantic references using reliable batch evidence rather than choose between unrestricted transduction and fixed prototypes. Code is available at https://github.com/MR-Sherif/JPTA.git.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-25"></a>

### 25. [LowRAM: Low-Rank Keys and Amortised Values for Composable KV Cache Compression at Test-Time](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07025v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07025

**作者：** Teresa Delgado、Hugo Hazard、Martin Benfeghoul、Jun Wang、Zafeirios Fountas

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07025v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07025v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 LowRAM：利用每个注意力头内 value 可由 key 较好预测这一点，在测试时拟合轻量 per-head 预测器、只存重建误差最大的 token 残差，并与跨层低秩 key 压缩组合，实现约 4 倍近无损的 KV cache 压缩。

**相关性简评：** 其「由 key 预测 value、仅保留高残差」的状态压缩与选择性存储思路，可能为 latent 推理状态的缓存压缩及重建误差分配提供线索，但原论文只面向解码效率，与视觉推理状态语义无关，需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LowRAM: Low-Rank Keys and Amortised Values for Composable KV Cache Compression at Test-Time

**abstract/A1：** Autoregressive decoding relies on the KV cache, whose memory footprint grows linearly with context length and caps batch size and throughput. Existing post-training methods exploit redundancy across tokens, layers, and feature dimensions, or lower numerical precision. We identify a complementary axis: within each attention head, values are largely predictable from their corresponding keys. We exploit this with value cache amortisation, which fits a lightweight per-head predictor at test time and stores residuals only for the tokens it reconstructs worst, giving $4\\times$ near-lossless value compression. The predictor can be initialised by meta-learning for substantially reduced prefill overhead, or fitted from scratch in settings that allow a one-off cost, such as multi-query inference. We pair value amortisation with cross-layer low-rank key compression to produce LowRAM, an asymmetric compression pipeline that stays close to lossless across three model families. By reconstructing only a selected subset of KV pairs at each decoding step, LowRAM can keep decoding overhead minimal and achieve up to $5\\times$ higher peak decode throughput at $4\\times$ compression. LowRAM further composes with quantisation and eviction, reaching $26.9\\times$ compression at $94.5\\%$ of baseline performance on long contexts. These results show that treating keys and values as structurally distinct enables efficient, aggressive, and composable KV cache compression.

</details>

<a id="paper-26"></a>

### 26. [Expanding the Quality-Coverage Frontier with Competitive Flow Matching](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07055v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07055

**作者：** Wenxuan Peng、Richard Zhang、Haitian Zheng、Jaskirat Singh、Tsai-Shien Chen、Eli Shechtman、Bharath Hariharan、Hadar Averbuch-Elor、Zongze Wu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07055v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07055v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出在 flow matching 训练中为预测器加入随机低维条件码 z，每个样本采样 K 个候选速度并以 winner-take-all 只对最匹配目标者计损失，从而避免多模态被平均为单一预测；另引入 auxiliary conditioning scaling 作为新的引导方式，在 ImageNet-256 上改善质量-覆盖前沿。

**相关性简评：** 其「多候选竞争、只监督最佳匹配」的目标可能可供核查 LVR 中多种 latent 假设的区分、避免表示坍缩及轨迹贡献分配；但论文属图像生成，与 VLM 推理无直接联系，迁移需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Expanding the Quality-Coverage Frontier with Competitive Flow Matching

**abstract/A1：** Flow matching (FM) generators often face a quality-coverage tradeoff. Inference-time techniques such as classifier-free guidance raise quality, but at the cost of coverage. We hypothesize this limitation stems from a basic property of the objective: an intermediate noisy image could correspond to many clean images, yet the model can only predict a single velocity, at best averaging over the multiple possibilities. To mitigate this, we introduce competition among predictions during training. We achieve this by augmenting the predictor with a randomly drawn low-dimensional conditioning code $z$. For each training example, we sample $K$ values of $z$ to produce $K$ candidate velocities that compete to explain the target, and apply the loss only to the best-matching candidate. This winner-take-all objective allows different modes to shape different predictions, rather than being averaged into a single compromise. Furthermore, this term naturally enables a new form of guidance, auxiliary conditioning scaling, which complements traditional guidance methods. Together, these two guidance mechanisms show that our method substantially improves the quality-coverage frontier. We validate on ImageNet-256, using two different testbeds, SiT and RAEv2. Given less than 3% of the original SiT pretraining compute for post-training, at the 67% Recall point, our method reduces $\\mathrm{FD}\_{r,6}$ from 19.22 to 5.91. On RAEv2, at the 70% Recall point, it reduces $\\mathrm{FD}\_{r,6}$ from 16.83 to 3.18.

**comment/C1：** 40 pages, 24 figures. Project page: https://lilydaytoy.github.io/CFM/

</details>

<a id="paper-27"></a>

### 27. [Segment What It Can Draw: Querying a Text-to-Image Generator with Its Own Images for Skin Lesion Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07061v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07061

**作者：** Hongyu Cao、Guangfu Guo、Jinghan Zhang、Dakshak Keerthi Chandra、Ying Zhang、Kunpeng Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07061v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07061v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 为免除皮肤病变分割对标注掩码的依赖，作者发现 T2I 扩散 Transformer 中由生成图像得到的「皮肤—病变」方向比概念词注意力方向更对齐，据此提出免训练方法 GENIQ：由生成图构造初始原型，用无标注皮肤镜图像中最像皮肤/病变的 patch 精炼，再沿该方向打分并用门控阈值抑制小病变周围溢出。

**相关性简评：** 虽属分割应用而非推理，但其在扩散模型内部注意力—输出空间用合成数据构造方向原型、再以真实 patch 精炼并做门控筛选的做法，可能与 LVR 中视觉证据筛选、内部状态读出的具体线索相关，值得核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Segment What It Can Draw: Querying a Text-to-Image Generator with Its Own Images for Skin Lesion Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Skin lesion segmentation without annotated masks requires the target to be specified through other cues. Existing approaches rely on dermoscopy-specific rules, annotation-derived model priors, or user-selected regions. A general text-to-image diffusion transformer offers a text interface to the target, but its concept-word attention is unreliable on dermoscopy images: in the model's attention-output space, the word-derived skin-to-lesion direction is poorly aligned with the direction separating real skin from real lesions. We show that the direction derived from generated healthy-skin and lesion images is substantially better aligned. Based on this finding, we propose GENIQ, a training-free method that constructs initial skin and lesion prototypes from generated images and refines them using the most skin-like and lesion-like patches from unlabelled dermoscopy images. It scores test patches along the refined direction and applies a gated threshold to suppress contextual spill-over around small lesions. Without inference-time annotations or additional models, GENIQ achieves Dice scores of 0.85-0.88 on three dermoscopy test sets. It outperforms zero-shot and word-based attention methods on all three sets, and a hand-designed dermoscopy pipeline in Dice and IoU on two.

</details>

<a id="paper-28"></a>

### 28. [SD-LoRA: Training-Time Structural Distillation into LoRA for Few-Shot Vision-Language Adaptation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07063v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07063

**作者：** Mohammed Rahman Sherif Khan Mohammad、Ardhendu Behera、Sandip Pradhan、Swagat Kumar、Yonghuai Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07063v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07063v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SD-LoRA：训练阶段用异构图教师建模 patch-patch、全局-局部与视觉-文本关系，再通过隐式蒸馏和原型对齐把这种结构偏置写入 CLIP-LoRA 主干，推理时丢弃图教师，仅需单次前向与缓存分类器，在 11 个小样本基准上超过 CLIP-LoRA 基线。

**相关性简评：** 把训练期的 token 级结构推理压缩进轻量参数、测试时不再付出额外计算的思路，可供核查 LVR 中「结构性 latent 监督如何转入推理期状态」以及思考成本与收益的分配问题，但论文本身面向分类适配而非多模态推理，联系需较多验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SD-LoRA: Training-Time Structural Distillation into LoRA for Few-Shot Vision-Language Adaptation

**abstract/A1：** Few-shot adaptation of vision-language models must balance two competing goals: learning fine-grained visual distinctions from very limited supervision and preserving the efficiency that makes frozen CLIP-like models attractive for deployment. Existing lightweight adaptation methods, including prompt tuning, LoRA, and cache-based adapters, typically supervise global image embeddings and therefore underuse token-level structure. Conversely, structure-aware methods exploit local evidence but often introduce additional inference-time computation. We propose SD-LoRA, a structural distillation framework that uses token-level graph reasoning only during training and transfers its effects into a lightweight LoRA-adapted CLIP model. During training, a heterogeneous graph teacher models patch-patch, global-local, and visual-text relations over high-resolution ViT tokens. Rather than retaining this graph at test time, SD-LoRA transfers its structural bias into the shared CLIP-LoRA backbone through implicit distillation and Prototype Predictive Alignment, which aligns graph-conditioned representations with support-derived class prototypes. At inference, the graph teacher is discarded; prediction requires only a single CLIP-LoRA forward pass and a cache-based classifier. Across 11 few-shot benchmarks, SD-LoRA improves over a strong CLIP-LoRA baseline by 2.4/1.8/3.0/2.4/2.0 percentage points in the 1/2/4/8/16-shot settings, respectively, without additional graph computation at test time. Results on base-to-novel generalization, cross-dataset transfer, out-of-distribution robustness, and calibration further show that training-time structural supervision improves both accuracy and transfer reliability. Code is available at https://github.com/MR-Sherif/SD-LoRA.git.

**comment/C1：** Accepted in NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-29"></a>

### 29. [When Verification Is Not Agreement: Cross-Agent LLM-to-Dafny Formalization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07073v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07073

**作者：** Priyanath Maji

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07073v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07073v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出一种跨智能体形式化协议：Specifier 模型把临床试验标准转写为 Dafny 谓词，Implementer 模型写出需通过该谓词校验的方法，并在 96 份文档和多种模型配置下测量二者独立提取的一致率，同时报告弱 Implementer 直接调用 Specifier 谓词即满足后置条件的验证伪影。

**相关性简评：** 「验证通过不等于独立一致」以及直接借用对方谓词通过校验的伪影，可能为 LVR 中判断答案是否真正使用 latent 内容、识别视觉旁路式捷径与贡献分配提供可核查的检验思路；但该工作为纯文本形式化，与视觉推理无直接联系，线索需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Verification Is Not Agreement: Cross-Agent LLM-to-Dafny Formalization

**abstract/A1：** A verified Dafny postcondition certifies that a formula holds, but it does not guarantee that the result was reached through genuine independent reasoning. We introduce a cross-agent formalization protocol where a Specifier model translates clinical trial criteria into a Dafny predicate, and an Implementer model writes an operational method checked against it. A same-model control establishes that even with no capability gap, independent re-extraction from identical text agrees only 59.3% of the time. Evaluating four model configurations on 96 clinical trial documents, we find that agreement is highly sensitive to how model capability is allocated across the two roles: the GPT-4.1-nano/GPT-4.1-mini configuration achieves 53.0% agreement when the stronger model is the Implementer, but only 14.3% when the roles are reversed. The strongest tested configuration, GPT-5.6-luna/GPT-5.4-mini, improves agreement to 34.4% but remains below the same-model baseline and is dominated by direct contradiction (42.7%). We also detect a critical verification artifact in which a weaker Implementer satisfies the postcondition by invoking the Specifier's predicate directly; after structural auditing, corrected agreement falls from 14.3% to 13.2%. Our results show that verification success alone is insufficient evidence of independent cross-agent agreement, and that model capability does not guarantee agreement when formalizing the same source independently.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on AI for Verifiable Coding

</details>

### 30. [Optimistic Hedge in General-Sum Games: Fundamental Limits and Regret Bounds via Temporal Approximation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07078v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07078

**作者：** Yuval Dagan、Nadav Eisen、Liad Erez、Tomer Koren

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.GT

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07078v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07078v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究 general-sum 博弈中 Optimistic Hedge（带一步外推的乘法权重更新）的 regret 极限：证明两人博弈可选取学习率使 regret 为 O(n log n) 且与时间跨度 T 无关，而三名及以上玩家时对足够大的 T 存在构造博弈使某玩家 regret 达 2^{Ω(mn)}、在学习率较大时甚至随 T 线性增长；技术工具是基于时间局部窗口损失近似的时间近似框架。

**相关性简评：** 其「一步外推/乐观预测更新」加「时间窗口损失近似、以总变差与累积方差比较」的分析框架，可能可供核查用于刻画循环式 latent 状态更新中误差与方差随时间的累积、以及预测式更新对长期稳定性的影响；但该文属博弈学习理论，与 VLM 推理相距较远，迁移性尚未验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Optimistic Hedge in General-Sum Games: Fundamental Limits and Regret Bounds via Temporal Approximation

**abstract/A1：** Optimistic Hedge, a multiplicative weight update with one step of extrapolation, is one of the most natural regret-minimizing dynamics for games, and characterizing its regret has been a longstanding open question. Daskalakis, Fishelson and Golowich proved that if all $m$ players in a finite general-sum game follow Optimistic Hedge with a fixed learning rate, each player's regret is polylogarithmic in the horizon $T$. Subsequently, Ha showed that an appropriate choice of learning rate makes each player's regret bounded independently of $T$. This bound, however, is non-constructive and its dependence on the number of actions $n$ is not explicit, leaving open whether Optimistic Hedge can achieve a constant regret bound which is polynomial in the size of the game. We show that the answer hinges on the number of players. In two-player games, there is a learning rate for which Optimistic Hedge guarantees $O(n\\log n)$ regret independent of the horizon. In three-player games, however, no such bound is possible: for any learning rate up to an absolute constant and for sufficiently large horizon $T$, there is a game with $n$ actions per player in which some player's regret is $2^{Ω(n)}$, and more generally, for every $m\\ge3$ we construct $m$-player games in which some player's regret is $2^{Ω(mn)}$; for learning rates above $2^{-Ω(mn)}$, that player's regret even grows linearly in $T$. Our upper bound is based on a temporal approximation framework for the analysis of Optimistic Hedge. Starting from the variance-based RVU bound of Daskalakis et al., we approximate the losses over time-localized windows by well-behaved functions, whose total variation is a small fraction of their cumulative variance. In $m$-player games, the same framework gives a significantly simplified proof of the recent $O(\\sqrt{m}\\log n\\log T)$ regret bound due to Ha.

</details>

<a id="paper-31"></a>

### 31. [Synthesizing symbolic models of human and machine reasoning at scale](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07088v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07088

**作者：** Solim LeGris、Philipp Mondorf、Jinran Jin、Akshay K. Jagadish、Brenden M. Lake、Todd M. Gureckis

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07088v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07088v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Behavior-Conditioned Symbolic Synthesis，通过自动搜索可解释的确定性符号程序来复现人类与机器在数百个推理问题上的行为，并据此诊断两者共有的过度简化、局部规则与错误依赖推断等模式。

**相关性简评：** 该工作不涉及视觉或 latent 状态，但其“由行为反推可解释规则”的框架可能为核查 VLM 推理是否依赖视觉证据、抑或采用语言先验式局部规则提供诊断线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Synthesizing symbolic models of human and machine reasoning at scale

**abstract/A1：** Symbolic models offer a powerful account of cognition, but their scope has historically been limited by the need for hand-engineering and computational tractability. We show how advances in artificial intelligence (AI) can scale symbolic modeling and help overcome these engineering limitations. We introduce Behavior-Conditioned Symbolic Synthesis (BCSS)---an approach for automated search of interpretable, deterministic symbolic programs that verifiably reproduce behavior---and apply it to model both human and machine behavior across hundreds of challenging reasoning problems. Analyzing these programs reveals that both people and machines tend to oversimplify, adopt rules that are partial explanations for their observations, and misinfer dependencies between problem features. These shared signatures are modulated by available resources, with reasoning effort predicting the extent to which machines simplify and explain their observations. Together, these results point to underlying mechanisms reminiscent of satisficing in both people and machines, and illustrate how BCSS offers a general approach for interpreting and comparing rule-based reasoning of humans and black-box AI systems.

</details>

<a id="paper-32"></a>

### 32. [A Night at the LLM Zoo: Data Conditioned Flow Matching for Generating Pretrained LLM Weights](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07103v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07103

**作者：** Scott Biggs、Bedionita Soro、Saumya Gupta、Bruno Andreis、Sung Ju Hwang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07103v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07103v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文系统改变网页文本、书籍、数学、代码与法语的混合比例，训练了 51M/124M/355M 三档、每档 100 个 GPT-2 模型构成权重动物园，分析其参数空间结构，并以数据混合嵌入为条件，在 Gram-PCA 压缩权重空间中用 Flow Matching 生成完整预训练权重。

**相关性简评：** 其「以连续条件嵌入驱动 flow matching 在压缩子空间中采样整体参数」的建模方式，可能与 LVR 中按输入条件生成或初始化 latent 推理状态的参数化思路有方法类比，但条件对象是数据配比而非视觉证据，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Night at the LLM Zoo: Data Conditioned Flow Matching for Generating Pretrained LLM Weights

**abstract/A1：** A challenge to Weight Space Learning as it expands from task specific and vision models to Large Language Models (LLMs) is the absence of a well curated and analyzed LLM population. To address this, we train such a model zoo for application to Weight Space Generation by systematically varying the data mixture composition between web text, books, mathematics, coding, and French across populations of 51M, 124M, and 355M parameter GPT-2 Models, each tier with count N = 100. We analyze these populations by cosine similarity, functional diversity, Leave-One-Mixture-Out modeling, and variance decomposition. We show that the zoo contains diverse models whose variation is structured by training mixture composition. We embed the training mixture composition to condition a Flow Matching model of complete pretrained LLM weights in Gram-PCA compressed weight space. By demonstrating zero-shot generalization to held out mixtures, measuring Continuous Ranked Probability Score skill, as well as finding minimal changes in downstream test-set perplexity and general capability benchmark scores, we show that our generator generalizes on the simplex of data mixtures to produce models that perform similarly to those in the zoo ex nihilo. Finally, we produce all source code and artifacts to support the continued expansion of Weight Space Learning and Generation into the LLM domain.

**comment/C1：** Preprint

</details>
