### 33. [Teaching Agents the "Principle", Not the "Reward"](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07130v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07130

**作者：** Shan Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07130v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07130v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 prescriptive policy learning（PPL）：用设计者给定的「行为原则」替代奖励信号，通过选取关键量与参考值，在每一步直接判断智能体是否遵循原则及遵循程度，从而在无奖励条件下学到样本效率更高、可迁移的策略。

**相关性简评：** 其逐步原则符合度信号本质上是由先验知识构造的密集过程监督，可能为 LVR 中中间推理步骤的贡献分配与监督设计提供线索；但论文属 RL 控制任务，无视觉或 latent 状态研究，需核查迁移可行性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Teaching Agents the "Principle", Not the "Reward"

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement learning has achieved strong performance in many tasks by letting agents learn from nothing more than a reward signal. However, in more complex tasks, agents still struggle to learn good, generalizable policies, even with carefully designed rewards and abundant samples. We argue that this difficulty stems from the reward decoding burden: designers encode their knowledge of the desired behavior into rewards and penalties, and agents must infer this knowledge from long sequences of noisy scalars. To address this burden, we propose prescriptive policy learning (PPL), which directly teaches agents higher-level knowledge about the tasks, termed principles of behavior, such as how to maintain balance, how to accumulate energy, and how to land smoothly. Through appropriately selected key quantities and references, PPL can directly indicate at every step whether the agent follows the principles and to what extent. Our experiments show that agents can learn good, generalizable policies without any reward. Taught only simple heuristic principles, PPL agents outperform reward-based methods with far fewer samples. Their policies also transfer directly to harder settings and keep performing well, demonstrating their strong generalization ability. In summary, PPL provides a new approach in which rough knowledge of the desired behavior is enough for agents to learn good, generalizable policies, even when the system dynamics cannot be accurately modeled.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages, 9 figures, 5 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-34"></a>

### 34. [Structural Conditional Independence Test via Neural Quantile Resolution](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07165v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07165

**作者：** Khoa Nguyen、Bao Duong、Thin Nguyen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07165v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07165v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 QRCIT：用共享的残差分位流模型先学习 P(X\|Z) 的基线条件分位律，再学习 Y 引入的额外形变，从而比较 P(X\|Z) 与 P(X\|Y,Z)，给出全局预测增益统计量、条件随机化校准以及可检视的形变场；同时构建 SDB 基准，覆盖上下文局部、仅尾部、符号反转等结构化依赖。

**相关性简评：** 其“条件于上下文后是否仍依赖某变量”的检验与局部化形变诊断，可能可供核查 latent 状态中视觉证据的贡献是否集中在特定区域或分位段，以及弱依赖、局部依赖的检测思路；尚需确认能否迁移到高维内部表示。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Structural Conditional Independence Test via Neural Quantile Resolution

**abstract/A1：** Conditional independence testing is difficult when dependence is weak, localized, or expressed through distributional changes rather than a global mean shift. Existing tests usually return a scalar statistic or p-value, which summarizes evidence against independence but does not describe where the conditional law changes. We propose QRCIT, a quantile-resolution conditional independence test that compares P(X\|Z) and P(X\|Y,Z) through a quantile deformation learned by a shared model. A lightweight residual quantile-flow model first learns the baseline conditional quantile law and then learns the additional deformation induced by Y, yielding a global predictive-gain statistic with conditional-randomization calibration and an inspectable deformation field over context and quantile level. We also introduce the Structural Dependency Benchmark (SDB), which evaluates dependence structures motivated by real applications, such as context-local, tail-only, sign-reversing, moving-ridge, and mean-neutral patterns. Across synthetic post-nonlinear (PNL) and SDB benchmarks, QRCIT improves detection of structured alternatives, with empirical Type-I behavior evaluated across settings. Higher quantile resolution improves merged-SDB power at moderate sample sizes, while diagnostics restricted to known affected regions improve detection of localized dependence. On Sachs, METABRIC breast cancer, and CCLE/PLX4720, QRCIT provides competitive real-data performance and interpretable deformation patterns. These results support quantile-resolved conditional-law deformation as a practical framework for testing and diagnosing structured conditional dependence.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at IEEE ICDM 2026. Code: https://github.com/khoangdadk/qrcit

</details>

<a id="paper-35"></a>

### 35. [TrustMix: Graceful Degradation for Pretrained Models under Distribution Shift](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07206v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07206

**作者：** Juliusz Ziomek、Maike Osborne、Yee Whye Teh

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07206v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07206v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出轻量后处理方法 TrustMix：将预训练模型的预测分布与用户指定的回退分布按 OOD 偏离统计量混合，随分布偏移增大逐步收敛到回退分布，无需重训，并在表格、图像与自然语言任务上验证其校准与似然表现。

**相关性简评：** 其「随 OOD 信号增强而平滑退化为回退分布」的机制，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据失配时降低对外部输入的依赖、以及 STOP 与弃答决策提供可核查的连续置信度调控线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TrustMix: Graceful Degradation for Pretrained Models under Distribution Shift

**abstract/A1：** Predicting beyond the training domain necessarily requires assumptions about how the ground truth extrapolates, yet in most neural networks these assumptions are made only implicit through the architecture. We propose TrustMix, a lightweight post-hoc method that makes a pretrained model's extrapolative behaviour explicit and controllable. TrustMix combines the predictive distribution produced by a pretrained model with a user-specified fallback distribution $p\_0(y\|x)$, such that as the out-of-distribution (OOD) deviation statistic grows, we collapse towards $p\_0(y\|x)$. TrustMix provides graceful degradation: the predictive distribution converges toward the fallback distribution as the OOD signal grows. The fallback distribution $p\_0(y\|x)$ can reflect domain knowledge, if available, or rely on a domain-agnostic stationarity assumption. TrustMix does not require retraining of the base model and can be applied to both regression and classification problems. We evaluated TrustMix on several synthetic and real-world distribution shifts, including tabular, image, and natural language processing tasks. We tested TrustMix on datasets such as CIFAR-10/100 and ImageNet and on numerous architectures including Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and Transformers. TrustMix remains competitive in-distribution while improving predictive uncertainty under distribution shift, with gains in predictive likelihood for regression and calibration for classification. Finally, we showed that TrustMix can provide useful predictive distributions for decision-making applications by utilizing it as a surrogate for Bayesian Optimisation.

</details>

<a id="paper-36"></a>

### 36. [Canonical Correlation Neural Networks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07212v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07212

**作者：** Andrea Cavallo、Sundeep Prabhakar Chepuri、Charilaos Kanatsoulis、Elvin Isufi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07212v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07212v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 CANE，把单视图内的协方差与视图间的交叉协方差作为两种关系归纳偏置，在谱域构造参数高效的多视图网络，并给出有限样本协方差扰动下的输出误差界，在脑成像与运动传感数据上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其用交叉协方差与谱滤波对齐两个视图并抵抗估计噪声的做法，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据与 latent 状态的对齐、融合及表示稳定性提供可核查的架构线索，但需确认迁移是否超出线性二阶统计的适用条件。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Canonical Correlation Neural Networks

**abstract/A1：** Relational inductive biases such as grids or graphs allow neural networks with fewer trainable parameters and improved generalization, but require a known structure beforehand. When no structure is available, the covariance matrix provides a principled substitute, and coVariance Neural Networks (VNNs) have shown that using it yields models that are theoretically grounded in Principal Component Analysis, stable to finite-sample estimation errors and parameter-efficient. This framework, however, is limited to single-view data, whereas many applications observe the same phenomenon through multiple views, each with its own in-view covariance and a cross-view covariance linking them. To incorporate these matrices as inductive biases in a principled way, we introduce CAnonical correlation NEural networks (CANEs), architectures that use in-view and cross-view covariances as distinct relational biases to solve downstream tasks. Via spectral analysis, CANEs draw analogies with Canonical Correlation Analysis (CCA), a classical technique to project two-view data onto a correlated subspace. However, while CCA's objective is to compute shared view representations, CANEs are trained to solve a downstream task directly. We bound their output error under finite-sample covariance perturbations and show that it depends on controllable Lipschitz constants of the learned filters in place of the singular-value gap that governs the stability of CCA, thus guaranteeing better robustness. Experiments on seven datasets from brain imaging and motion sensing show that CANEs match or outperform single-view, two-view and CCA-based baselines, often with fewer parameters, and retain their performance when covariances are re-estimated from fewer samples.

</details>

<a id="paper-37"></a>

### 37. [Loopback Transformer: Towards a Unified View of Residual Looped Transformers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07226v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07226

**作者：** Xuan Ouyang、Zefan Cai、Chenlong Zhang、Cheng Luo、Ke Wan、Junjie Hu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07226v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07226v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Loopback Transformer，从残差连接的「深度-记忆」视角分析每层存储何种表示、后续读取如何加权，并把共享堆栈的输入与逐层更新分成两条路径，用几何衰减设定总更新权重并提供近因先验、由注意力学习更新分配，在 191M–3B 规模上与标准 Transformer 及七种循环残差设计比较验证损失和下游任务表现。

**相关性简评：** 其把循环计算中的历史状态与新增更新显式分离、并分别控制输入强度与更新权重分配的做法，可能为 LVR 中循环推理状态如何累积、读取与衰减提供可核查的结构线索，但论文为纯文本语言建模、未涉及视觉证据或推理轨迹。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Loopback Transformer: Towards a Unified View of Residual Looped Transformers

**abstract/A1：** Looped Transformers reduce parameter count by repeatedly applying shared blocks, but parameter sharing alone does not determine how the results of repeated computation are reused. We take the depth-memory view of residual connections and analyze which representation is stored after each block application and how later reads weight it. A full block output contains both its input and its new update, so weighting that output also reweights the history already carried by the input. Reading individual updates instead, with the shared stack's input kept on a separate path, gives separate controls over input strength, total update weight, and allocation across updates. The Loopback Transformer implements these controls: geometric decay sets the total update weight and supplies a recency prior, and attention learns how to allocate that weight. On ClimbMix, Loopback reaches lower validation loss than standard Transformer baselines with 40-75% of their Transformer parameters (excluding token embeddings and the output head) at reference scales from 191M to 3B, with training tokens and executed depth matched. It also has higher mean accuracy over thirteen downstream tasks at 1.4B and 3B. At 191M, 374M, and 778M, it has the lowest validation loss and the highest thirteen-task mean among the standard Transformer and seven looped residual designs.

**comment/C1：** 31 pages, 9 figures. Code: https://github.com/yancyou/Loopback ; Project page: https://yancyou.github.io/Loopback\_page/

</details>

<a id="paper-38"></a>

### 38. [$D^2$-OPD: Separating Direction and Distance in Extrapolative On-Policy Distillation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07241v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07241

**作者：** Bowen Fang、Dingmin Wang、Hengzhi Pei

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07241v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07241v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 D²-OPD，把 on-policy 蒸馏中的外推方向与外推距离解耦：用 per-prefix 的 hard KL 约束限制目标偏离教师的幅度，方向则由教师训练后偏移与初始师生差距的自适应组合决定，并以 forward KL 优化学生。

**相关性简评：** 属纯文本蒸馏的目标构造，不涉及视觉或 latent 状态；但「约束目标-教师距离、仅外推方向」的思路可能可供核查于 latent 轨迹蒸馏，用于控制状态漂移、避免学生过度偏离教师表示。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** $D^2$-OPD: Separating Direction and Distance in Extrapolative On-Policy Distillation

**abstract/A1：** On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student model to match a teacher's next-token distributions on student-generated prefixes. Extrapolative OPD further extends the teacher's log-probability shift relative to a reference policy to construct stronger learning targets. However, existing methods couple extrapolation direction and distance, allowing the magnitude of the teacher's shift to dictate how far the target moves. We propose $D^2$-OPD, decoupling these two factors: a per-prefix hard KL constraint bounds the target's distance from the teacher, while an adaptive combination of the teacher's post-training shift and the initial student--teacher gap determines the extrapolation direction. We optimize the student toward this target using forward KL, providing learning signals even for low-probability tokens. We evaluate three settings: where standard OPD performs well, where it struggles with base-model initialization, and where no teacher reference is available. On mathematical reasoning benchmarks, $D^2$-OPD attains the highest score in all three settings, outperforming the strongest baseline by up to 9.45 points. Ablations validate the contributions of each component.

</details>

<a id="paper-39"></a>

### 39. [MAC-v2: Long-horizon model-based value expansion from pixels](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07245v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07245

**作者：** Kwanyoung Park、Sergey Levine

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07245v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07245v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究像素级离线强化学习中的长时程模型价值展开，提出 MAC-v2：用无重建的 latent 世界模型配合动作分块，从预测与控制目标学习表示，以减少递归想象误差并降低价值展开所需的多步预测次数。

**相关性简评：** 其「动作分块减少递归 latent 预测次数」与「无重建、面向控制的 latent 表示」可供核查多步 latent 推理中的误差累积与信息坍缩问题，但属机器人 RL 场景，与 VLM 推理的迁移仍需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MAC-v2: Long-horizon model-based value expansion from pixels

**abstract/A1：** We study whether \\emph{long-horizon model-based value expansion} can serve as an effective framework in pixel-based offline reinforcement learning (RL). Long-horizon model-based value expansion from pixels is substantially harder than state-based environments, as the agent must learn both the representation and its dynamics, and errors from either can accumulate recursively over imagined trajectories. We introduce MAC-v2, which enables long-horizon model-based expansion from pixels by training a reconstruction-free latent world model with action chunking. By learning representations from prediction and control objectives, MAC-v2 does not need to encode control-irrelevant visual details that can hinder the dynamics prediction. Action chunking further mitigates the prediction error by reducing the number of recursive model predictions required for value expansion. Across diverse visual benchmarks including visual OGBench, MMBench2, Robomimic, and LIBERO-10, MAC-v2 achieves strong performance against model-free and model-based offline RL baselines. Our analyses show that long-horizon model-based value expansion is critical for the performance of MAC-v2, and that both action chunking and latent learning objectives are essential for reliable long-horizon latent imagination.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages (12 pages of appendix &amp; references), submitted to ICLR 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-40"></a>

### 40. [TriAttention: Unified Sparse Mixed-Precision Attention for Long-Context LLM Inference](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07256v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07256

**作者：** Hanzhi Zhang、Qiao Zhang、Qinglei Cao、Heng Fan、Yan Huang、Kewei Sha、Yunhe Feng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07256v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07256v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 TriAttention，按 query-key tile 的敏感度将其分配到跳过、4-bit 或 8-bit 计算，用两个对角距离边界紧凑表示每个 head 的计算区域，并通过离线校准与全局预算分配选择边界，配合融合 kernel 与压缩 KV cache 加速长上下文推理。

**相关性简评：** 论文观察到「某些交互可容忍低精度但不容忍被移除」，这种按敏感度区分的注意力交互保留与精度分配，可供核查其是否有助于刻画 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与 KV 缓存对 latent 内容保真度的影响。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TriAttention: Unified Sparse Mixed-Precision Attention for Long-Context LLM Inference

**abstract/A1：** Sparsity and quantization reduce the cost of attention by skipping interactions and lowering the precision of those retained. However, useful interactions differ in their precision requirements: some tolerate low-precision scores while remaining sensitive to removal. We therefore introduce TriAttention, which assigns query-key (QK) tiles to skipping, 4-bit, or 8-bit computation, using 4-bit to retain quantization-tolerant interactions at lower cost. To represent compactly, we encode each head's computation regions with two diagonal-distance boundaries, keeping policy storage independent of context length. Offline calibration then evaluates candidate boundary pairs using normalized projected-output errors and complete-tile execution costs. Using these measurements, a model-wide allocator selects each head's boundaries to minimize calibration error under a shared computation budget. During prefill, the selected boundaries generate precision-grouped traversals for a fused kernel that skips omitted tiles and combines contributions from both QK precisions into one normalized attention output. The quantized keys and values prepared during prefill are then reused in a compressed key-value (KV) cache for decoding, reducing repeated reads of prompt data. Experiments show that TriAttention achieves end-to-end generation speedups of up to 7.88x over the Torch attention and 2.65x over the BF16 baseline. Meanwhile, TriAttention maintains quality comparable to the original models on general-purpose and long-context tasks. These results show how a compact retention-and-precision policy translates local sensitivities into attention computation and repeated KV-cache access. The implementation is https://github.com/HanzhiZhang-Ulrica/TriAttention.

</details>

<a id="paper-41"></a>

### 41. [How Deep is the Signal? Time-Context up to 30 Years for Colorectal Cancer Prediction in a Primary-Care EHR Model](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07265v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07265

**作者：** Achille Raviez、Lan Wang、Brendan Delaney

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、q-bio.QM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07265v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07265v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出以日历年回溯窗口定义的「time-context」设计轴，在340万基层医疗患者、14亿编码事件上预训练34.6M参数解码器transformer，并在固定队列（1008例/9582对照，注册满30年）上用2至30年的15个嵌套窗口评估结直肠癌预测，发现AUROC由2年0.551升至18年0.590后趋平。

**相关性简评：** 其「回溯窗口加长后收益递减」与「RoPE在默认基频下约20年后出现时间混叠、需重调θ」的观察，可能与LVR中视觉证据需回溯多远、位置编码与长度泛化的预测工作区问题相关，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** How Deep is the Signal? Time-Context up to 30 Years for Colorectal Cancer Prediction in a Primary-Care EHR Model

**abstract/A1：** EHR foundation models read medical events within a fixed context window, and long context has so far been measured only in tokens. How far back in time a model must look to capture the earliest predictive signal is unknown. We introduce time-context, the calendar lookback window before the prediction point, and provide, to our knowledge, the first systematic evaluation of decade-scale time-context for an EHR foundation model. We pretrained a decoder-only transformer (34.6M parameters) on 1.4B coded events from 3.6M primary-care patients in North West London and fine-tuned it to predict incident colorectal cancer under a 12-month pre-diagnostic buffer. A frozen cohort of 1,008 cases and 9,582 matched controls, all registered for at least 30 years, was evaluated at 15 nested lookback windows from 2 to 30 years, so that every truncation is compared within the same patients. Discrimination rose from an AUROC of 0.551 at 2 years to 0.590 at 18 years, then plateaued, with about 80% of the gain reached within 10 years; an XGBoost baseline converged to the same level (peak AUROC 0.589). A simulation further suggests that rotary position embeddings (RoPE) over elapsed time alias beyond about 20 years at the default base frequency, motivating a retuned $θ$. Time-context is a measurable design axis for clinical prediction models, distinct from token-context, and should be reported.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at Machine Learning for Health (ML4H) 2026, Findings Track. 10 pages, 6 figures, 3 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-42"></a>

### 42. [Graph Neural Networks for Resource Allocation in Integrated Sensing and Communication](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07277v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07277

**作者：** Junyu Liao、Lili Chen、Tian Han、Jamie Evans

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.IT

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07277v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07277v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对车载感知通信一体化网络中的功率分配，论文提出 GNN-ISAC：用两类边分别刻画跨服务商干扰与服务商内部功率预算共享，并以带感知惩罚的和速率损失进行无标签训练，同时改造 WMMSE 作为对照；仿真显示其和速率接近 WMMSE、计算开销更低，且无需重训即可泛化到更大规模网络。

**相关性简评：** 其消息传递式的迭代状态更新、区分「干扰」与「共享」的类型化边，以及免标签任务损失训练和规模外推，可供核查是否能借鉴为 LVR 中关系型 latent 状态的更新与长度泛化设计，但领域相距较远，联系需阅读原文确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Graph Neural Networks for Resource Allocation in Integrated Sensing and Communication

**abstract/A1：** In vehicular integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) networks, each service-provider vehicle uses a limited power budget to serve multiple sensing targets and communication receivers. Maximizing the communication sum-rate subject to sensing signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) constraints is nonconvex, and iterative optimization is costly to rerun for every network layout. This paper proposes a graph neural network (GNN)-based power allocation algorithm, termed GNN-ISAC. Concretely, GNN-ISAC addresses the aforementioned problem by considering two distinct edge types in the graph representation, one capturing the inter-provider interference, the other representing the intra-provider power-budget sharing. GNN-ISAC is trained without labels using a sensing-penalized sum-rate loss. We also adapt the weighted mean-square error minimization (WMMSE) algorithm to this problem by introducing a sensing-power reservation step. Simulation results show that GNN-ISAC achieves communication sum-rates close to WMMSE with low sensing-link violation rates and much less computation. It also outperforms an existing heterogeneous GNN and a channel-blind baseline, and generalizes to larger networks without retraining.

**comment/C1：** Submitted to IEEE ICC 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-43"></a>

### 43. [ArtiVidRM: Verifiable Artifact-as-Rubric Reward Modeling for Explicit Quality Articulation in Video Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07301v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07301

**作者：** Caorui Li、Yangguang Ji、Shuxia Lin、Yu Chen、YiCheng Xiao、Yongliang Wu、Zhenxiang Jiang、Licheng Tang、Deng Li、Zhuxuanzi Wang、Jiahao Sun、Chonghan Liu、Lirian Su、Jay Wu、Xu Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07301v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07301v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 ArtiVidRM，一个「先观察后打分」的视频奖励模型：先生成结构化的可见生成伪影列表，再以该列表作为显式评分标准进行质量评估，并用多奖励强化学习联合优化伪影预测与成对偏好排序。

**相关性简评：** 虽属视频生成奖励建模，不在 LVR 主线，但其「显式中间表示＋对中间证据的可验证奖励＋与下游判断联合优化」可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据筛选如何被显式监督、中间状态是否被答案真正使用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ArtiVidRM: Verifiable Artifact-as-Rubric Reward Modeling for Explicit Quality Articulation in Video Generation

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement learning has become an effective approach for improving video generation, but its success depends heavily on reliable reward models. Existing methods either map videos directly to opaque scalar scores or generate free-form rationales that are not explicitly optimized as the basis of the final judgment. We propose ArtiVidRM, a verifiable, artifact-as-rubrics video reward model that follows an observe-then-score paradigm. It first identifies visible generation artifacts and organizes them into a structured artifact list, then uses these artifacts as explicit rubrics for quality assessment. This intermediate representation grounds each score in concrete video-level evidence, making the reward more interpretable and informative for optimization. We further introduce a multi-reward reinforcement learning strategy to jointly optimize artifact prediction and quality assessment. The reward decomposes into complementary objectives that evaluate artifact prediction quality, including semantic coverage and prediction efficiency, together with a pairwise preference objective that supervises quality ranking through score differences. By jointly optimizing the intermediate artifact representation and the downstream preference prediction, the model learns to produce informative artifact lists while improving the accuracy of video quality assessment. Experiments show that ArtiVidRM achieves state-of-the-art performance on video preference benchmarks, provides effective signals for video diffusion optimization, and enables artifact-guided refinement of closed-source video generators.

</details>

<a id="paper-44"></a>

### 44. [Rethinking Data Selection: The Importance of Coverage over Difficulty in LLM Fine-Tuning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07323v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07323

**作者：** Lalchand Pandia、Kanishka Misra、Allyson Ettinger

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07323v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07323v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文发现难度导向的数据选择在 LLM 微调中常不如随机采样，据此提出基于聚类的、强调覆盖输入分布的选样方法，并在单领域任务与 Tülu 3 多领域混合数据上验证其效果。

**相关性简评：** 其“覆盖优于难度”的选样准则可供核查 LVR 训练数据（如推理轨迹、视觉证据样本）的筛选与多源混合配比，但论文本身不涉及视觉或多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Rethinking Data Selection: The Importance of Coverage over Difficulty in LLM Fine-Tuning

**abstract/A1：** Selecting high-quality training data can reduce computation cost for LLM fine-tuning. Prior work on data selection developed a variety of difficulty-based scores, motivated by the intuition that harder examples are more informative. While effective for smaller classification models, we find that such methods often fail to outperform random selection for LLM fine-tuning. Our analysis reveals that random selection performs better because it is more representative of the underlying input space---i.e., has better coverage. To validate the importance of coverage, we implement a simple clustering-based selection method which selects data that is more representative of the underlying input distribution. On a set of diverse single-domain tasks, this method yields performance at or above that of random selection. On the diverse, multi-domain Tülu 3 mixture, where random sampling no longer guarantees a balanced subset, our method outperforms all methods. Overall, our results indicate that prioritizing coverage over difficulty leads to more effective data selection for LLM fine-tuning.

</details>

<a id="paper-45"></a>

### 45. [EnvOpt: Automated Optimization for Agent Environments](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07372v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07372

**作者：** Mingxuan Li、Huanzhi Mao、Qiuyang Mang、Hanchen Li、Joseph E. Gonzalez、Alex Dimakis

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07372v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07372v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 EnvOpt，对 agent 所处的「静态环境」（如共享工作区、数据源、文档与工单集合）进行端到端自动优化，无需 grader 或参考答案，在多个基准上降低 agent 成本并提升或保持通过率，且优化后的环境可迁移到不同模型与 harness。

**相关性简评：** 其核心观察「信息如何呈现给 agent 会影响成本与成功率」以及无参考答案的自动优化信号，可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选、可供 agent 获取的外部信息组织与无监督优化目标提供可核查线索，但该工作本身不涉及多模态 latent 推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EnvOpt: Automated Optimization for Agent Environments

**abstract/A1：** An LLM agent's cost and success depend on its model, its harness, and its environment. In addition to the widely studied directions of parameter tuning and harness optimization, we propose the third direction: optimization over stationary environments. Agents are often deployed in rich environments shared by many tasks, such as a company's Slack workspace or a complex collection of data sources, documents or tickets. We call these environments stationary environments. We introduce EnvOpt, a method for automated end-to-end optimization of stationary environments for any given model and harness, using real or synthetic tasks without needing any graders or reference answers. On AutomationBench, EnvOpt reduces the cost of Claude Haiku 4.5 by 72.7% and raises its pass rate by 14.34%. The same environment transfers to Opus 5, cutting its cost by 68.1% with a 1.34% gain. Added on top of Meta-Harness's optimized harness for Haiku 4.5, it lowers cost by a further 83.4% and adds 11.17% accuracy over Meta-Harness alone. Across 5 benchmarks, EnvOpt significantly reduces the cost of agents while improving or maintaining pass rate, and is transferable to different models and different harnesses. We also discuss common successful and rejected environment optimization strategies during automated optimization. More broadly, our results show that how information is represented to an agent shapes both its cost and its success, making the environment worth optimizing in addition to the parameters and the harness.

</details>

<a id="paper-46"></a>

### 46. [When Scores Conflict with Preferences: Pre-Alignment Diagnosis and Adaptive Correction of Constraint Drift in DPO](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07375v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07375

**作者：** Ping Liu、Yan Yan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07375v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07375v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文刻画 DPO 中偏好对与点式指标（长度、正确性、安全分）冲突所导致的「约束漂移」，给出可由数据集统计计算的闭式诊断量 ρ\*eq，并据此设计冲突对降权强度加自适应 margin 偏移的 CoDPO。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理无直接关联，但其「用数据统计推导冲突样本的降权强度而非人工调参」的监督加权思路，可能可供核查于 LVR 轨迹训练中正确性与偏好信号相冲突的样本处理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Scores Conflict with Preferences: Pre-Alignment Diagnosis and Adaptive Correction of Constraint Drift in DPO

**abstract/A1：** Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) is increasingly used in settings where pairwise preferences coexist with pointwise criteria such as response length, answer correctness, or safety scores. When the two disagree, standard DPO treats pairs whose preferred output scores worse on the pointwise criterion (conflict pairs) in the same way as the remaining (matching) pairs. We characterize a systematic failure mode, constraint drift: for sufficiently small updates, conflict pairs tend to increase constraint cost while matching pairs tend to repair it, and the net change depends on their balance. This balance yields a closed-form pre-alignment diagnostic, $ρ^{\*}\_{\\mathrm{eq}}$, computable from dataset statistics, that indicates whether a dataset is self-correcting or requires intervention. Building on this diagnostic, we propose Conflict-aware DPO (CoDPO), which down-weights conflict pairs with a strength set from $ρ^{\*}\_{\\mathrm{eq}}$ rather than tuned, and adds an adaptive margin shift that addresses residual drift during training. Empirically, drift grows monotonically with conflict exposure on the conflict-dominated domains, and the intervention decision given by $ρ^{\*}\_{\\mathrm{eq}}$ matches the observed drift on every domain, including one that it identifies as self-correcting. On all four domains of the main comparison, CoDPO reduces drift while largely preserving the preference learning of vanilla DPO. Compared with hard filtering and recent constrained and multi-objective baselines, CoDPO also achieves a more favorable drift-preference trade-off.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to NeurIPS 2026. Corresponding author: Yan Yan

</details>

<a id="paper-47"></a>

### 47. [TRiST: Topology-Reinforced Swin Transformer for Medical Image Analysis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07431v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07431

**作者：** Pengfei Gu、Huimin Li、Guangyu Meng、Hao Zheng、Haoteng Tang、Bin Fu、Danny Z. Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07431v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07431v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 TRiST，在 Swin 的四个原生阶段分别计算 2D 持续同调（H0/H1），经按持续性分带的门控残差模块细化后转成注意力偏置，用于医学图像分类与分割。

**相关性简评：** 该文不涉及 VLM 推理或 latent 状态生成，但把拓扑描述子直接写入 shifted-window attention logits、并在多尺度上独立对齐 token 网格的做法，可供核查能否作为视觉结构先验保留在内部表示中、支持证据筛选与细粒度几何使用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TRiST: Topology-Reinforced Swin Transformer for Medical Image Analysis

**abstract/A1：** Medical images contain topological patterns, such as connected components and loops, at scales ranging from small lesions to organ boundaries. Although hierarchical Vision Transformers have advanced medical image analysis, they do not explicitly incorporate topology throughout their multiscale representations. We propose Topology-Reinforced Swin Transformer (TRiST), a framework that integrates topological information into feature construction, refinement, and attention. First, a Hierarchical Multi-Scale Topology Representation algorithm computes 2D persistent homology (H0 and H1) independently at four native Swin stages, producing descriptors aligned with each stage's token grid rather than downsampling fine-scale topology. Second, a Band-Aware H0/H1 Residual Refinement module uses gated residual networks to process short-, medium-, and long-persistence information separately. Third, a Persistence-Band Progressive Attention Bias converts stage-specific descriptors into learned key-saliency scores that directly modify shifted-window attention logits. We instantiate TRiST as Topo-SwinV2-B for classification and Topo-Swin-UNet for segmentation, preserving the underlying Swin-based backbones. Experiments on five medical image datasets show consistent improvements over Swin-based baselines and competitive performance relative to existing topology-augmented methods.

</details>

<a id="paper-48"></a>

### 48. [Echoes of Error: Residual-Directional Local Rollout Consistency for Timeseries Forecasting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07473v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07473

**作者：** Zheng Wang、Kaixuan Zhang、Xiaonan Lu、Wanfang Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07473v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07473v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究时间序列预测中自生成误差回灌后对后续预测的扰动，提出局部 rollout 一致性目标，并用沿实际反馈扰动的 Jacobian-vector product 一阶替代，约束自由运行轨迹保持在预测流形附近的稳定管内。

**相关性简评：** 其 teacher forcing 与自由运行状态的一致性正则，可能为 LVR 中 latent 轨迹训练与自生成误差累积的监督提供线索，但需核查如何映射到视觉推理状态及可变长度 rollout。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Echoes of Error: Residual-Directional Local Rollout Consistency for Timeseries Forecasting

**abstract/A1：** In time-series forecasting, rollout errors are not merely one-step fitting discrepancies: once fed back into the evolving state, they can perturb future predictions. We formalize this effect through local rollout consistency, which compares the next prediction produced under teacher forcing with that produced under free running. In principle, this consistency can be enforced directly by tracking the residual-induced perturbation of the next state. In practice, however, the residual-to-state alignment operator is often difficult to design when the input state and output block have different dimensions, heterogeneous components, or architecture-dependent layouts. We therefore use a first-order local surrogate that penalizes the Jacobian-vector product of the predictor along the actual feedback perturbation, suppressing precisely the directions through which self-generated errors remain visible to future predictions. We further give the same loss a geometric interpretation near a predictive manifold: by controlling residual-induced directions that probe the normal bundle, the objective yields theoretical conditions under which free-running trajectories remain inside a stability tube around the manifold. Experiments on time series forecasting tasks support this view, showing that the proposed regularization improves predictive accuracy. More broadly, our results suggest a training principle for time-series forecasters and sequential predictors: a model should not only make small one-step errors, but also learn not to let its own errors echo into the future. Code is available at https://github.com/Wangzzzzzzzz/Echoes-Of-Error.

**comment/C1：** Accepted for publication at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-49"></a>

### 49. [Adversarial Robustness of Kernel Machines: A Comprehensive Analysis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07474v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07474

**作者：** Rahul Kumar Yadav、Tunir Ghosh、Sudhendu Ahir、Parthe Pandit、Arjun Bhagoji

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07474v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07474v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文对核机器（含随机特征分类器与 Recursive Feature Machines）在二分类与多分类图像、表格任务上的对抗鲁棒性做系统实证评估，涵盖白盒与黑盒逃逸攻击，并提出一种近似对抗训练算法。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线关联很弱，但 Recursive Feature Machines 的迭代式特征更新与近似对抗训练，可能为「循环计算下的内部状态更新」及扰动下表征稳定性评价提供可供核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Adversarial Robustness of Kernel Machines: A Comprehensive Analysis

**abstract/A1：** Kernel machines are an important class of models in machine learning. However, the question of how robust modern kernel machines are to adversarial examples has not been studied comprehensively. In this paper, we provide a thorough empirical investigation of the adversarial robustness of kernel-based models for both binary and multiclass classification tasks, via white-box and black-box evasion attacks. We evaluate the native robustness of kernel machines, random feature classifiers, and the relatively newer Recursive Feature Machines. In addition, we provide an algorithm for approximate adversarial training of kernel machines. Our experiments on classification tasks on image and tabular datasets strongly indicate that kernel machines are natively more robust than neural networks, i.e., their performance degrades more gracefully against adversarial perturbations.

</details>

<a id="paper-50"></a>

### 50. [Compact Motion Embeddings for Feed-forward Unified Human Animation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07481v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07481

**作者：** Seonwook Park、Hyunsoo Cha、Amrita Mazumdar、Shengze Wang、Tianye Li、Hanbyul Joo、Koki Nagano、Shalini De Mello

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07481v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07481v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 FUHA 前馈 transformer 人体动画框架：用 Q-Former 将冻结视觉编码器提取的驱动特征压缩为紧凑 motion embedding，并以对比损失使其与外观解耦，从而无需外部姿态估计即可实时驱动人脸、半身与全身。

**相关性简评：** 其「视觉特征压成定长 latent + 对比损失剔除身份外观」的训练思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态如何保留任务必要视觉信息、抑制无关内容，以及用检索式指标诊断 latent 中信息泄漏；但论文任务是动画生成，与推理状态生成无直接关系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Compact Motion Embeddings for Feed-forward Unified Human Animation

**abstract/A1：** Animating a reference image with a driver's motion requires a motion representation that captures face, hand, and body articulation but excludes the driver's appearance. Most body animation methods take motion from external pose estimators and render it with many denoising steps; compact learned motion representations mostly cover the face. We present FUHA, a real-time-capable feed-forward transformer that learns a compact motion embedding of the whole human and uses it for face, upper-body, and full-body animation. A Q-Former compresses the driver's features from a frozen visual encoder into this embedding. To disentangle motion from appearance, a novel contrastive loss pulls it toward the motion embedding of the driver's rendered keypoint image and away from that of the same person in another pose. At inference time, FUHA needs no external pose estimators, keypoints, or parametric body models. As a retrieval key, the motion embedding matches body pose better than the learned global embeddings of ten vision encoders. Its same-person retrieval rate is 0.1%, against 9.5% for its own DINOv3 backbone. On our upper-body benchmark, FUHA outperforms all baselines on every self-reenactment metric and on cross-reenactment identity similarity. It is competitive on face and full-body and runs at up to 38.9 FPS at 512x512 on a single RTX 4090.

**comment/C1：** 28 pages, 13 figures, 11 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-51"></a>

### 51. [SAVeR$^2$: Reasoning-based Safety Alignment for Large Reasoning Models via Verifiable Rewards](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07482v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07482

**作者：** Yichen Sun、Qirui Liu、Yan Wang、Jianan Lin、Linbo Jiang、Shiyu Wang、Zhibo Wang、Zhixuan Chu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07482v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07482v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对大型推理模型显式 CoT 带来的安全风险，提出 SAVeR²：把安全对齐目标拆成四个细粒度可验证奖励，配合难度感知数据选择，并用保持推理能力的梯度投影解析安全与推理梯度冲突。

**相关性简评：** 其细粒度可验证奖励与梯度投影消解多目标冲突的做法，可能为 LVR 训练中「视觉证据忠实性」与「答案正确性」等目标的联合优化提供可核查线索；但论文为纯文本安全对齐，不涉及 latent 状态或视觉推理，联系较间接。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SAVeR$^2$: Reasoning-based Safety Alignment for Large Reasoning Models via Verifiable Rewards

**abstract/A1：** Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) have achieved impressive performance via explicit Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning, yet this process introduces critical safety risks. We formalize LRM safety objectives as requiring non-malicious reasoning and answers, safety consistency, and explicit intent identification. We argue that existing alignment methods suffer from poor adversarial generalization, a significant \`\`safety tax'' on reasoning, and unfaithful coarse-grained rewards, primarily due to their neglect of the LRM's intrinsic reasoning capabilities for safety. To address these, we propose \\textbf{SAVeR$^2$}, a novel \\textbf{S}afety \\textbf{A}lignment method via \\textbf{R}ewards with \\textbf{R}easoning capability. SAVeR$^2$ decomposes the safety objective into four fine-grained verifiable reward signals and utilizes a difficulty-aware data selection strategy to stabilize training. Crucially, we introduce a reasoning-preserving gradient projection mechanism that analytically resolves conflicts between safety and reasoning gradients, effectively eliminating the safety tax. Extensive experiments on models ranging from 1.5B to 671B demonstrate that SAVeR$^2$ achieves superior safety performance and significantly lower over-refusal rates across multiple benchmarks without compromising general reasoning capabilities.

</details>

<a id="paper-52"></a>

### 52. [Concertina: Embodying Manipulation and Cognition in One Diffusion Vision-Language Model](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07513v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07513

**作者：** Chengcheng Wang、Wei He、Wenhao Li、Hongguang Li、Kai Han、Jianyuan Guo、Chang Xu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07513v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07513v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Concertina，把连续轨迹表示、分块生成和物理坐标监督直接放在共享 diffusion VLM 主干的计算与参数路径上，每个控制步编码为一个隐藏空间中的连续 latent action token，并用与多模态预训练相同的「破坏—重建」算子按时间顺序生成动作块。

**相关性简评：** 这是机器人操作工作，但与 LVR 有可核查的具体线索：连续 latent token 落在共享主干隐藏空间、以破坏—重建方式分块生成时间有序序列，且用 Action Interface Audit 逐层定位跨时间耦合出现在中后层，可供参考用于 latent 状态深度/位置效应的分析与视觉信息是否真正被使用的诊断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Concertina: Embodying Manipulation and Cognition in One Diffusion Vision-Language Model

**abstract/A1：** Most vision-language-action (VLA) policies decouple manipulation from cognition by bolting an external action mechanism onto a vision-language model (VLM)--either quantizing actions into discrete tokens whose errors bear no stable correspondence to physical displacements, or delegating continuous trajectory generation to a separate action expert whose gradients compete with or are blocked from updating the VLM backbone. We argue that manipulation and cognition should instead be embodied in the same model, shifting from a decoupled VLA to an Embodied Vision-Language Model (eVLM). We introduce Concertina, an eVLM that places continuous trajectory representation, blockwise generation, and physical-coordinate supervision directly on the computation and parameter path of a shared diffusion VLM backbone. Each control timestep is encoded as one continuous latent action token in the backbone hidden space, and the shared Transformer reconstructs temporally ordered action blocks via the same corrupt-and-reconstruct operator used for multimodal pretraining. Active action projections account for only 0.21% of parameters in the 4.46B model with zero separate action-generator layers, compared with 16.5%-35.1% dedicated action-expert capacity in representative continuous VLAs. Using our proposed Action Interface Audit, we confirm that cross-time trajectory coupling emerges in the middle and late layers of the shared backbone rather than at the action interfaces. On standard benchmarks, Concertina achieves 98.15% average success on regular LIBERO, 85.9% on the 10,030-task LIBERO-Plus benchmark, and 4.142 average completed chain length on CALVIN ABC-D. Code: https://github.com/lose4578/Concertina Hugging Face: https://huggingface.co/collections/Traly/concertina

</details>

<a id="paper-53"></a>

### 53. [Understanding Data Influence in Transportation Networks: A Physics-Constrained Influence-Function Framework](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07526v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07526

**作者：** Hamidreza Attari、Yueyue Fan、Johannes Royset

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07526v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07526v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出硬物理约束下的影响函数框架，在流量守恒等网络约束定义的可行空间内刻画训练样本对预测的影响，并用正交分解量化扰动经约束传播与投影消除的比例。

**相关性简评：** 「影响只沿物理可行方向传播」及约束投影分解，可能为 LVR 中训练样本或推理轨迹对 latent 状态的受限贡献归因提供可核查线索，但能否迁移到多模态表示约束仍需确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Understanding Data Influence in Transportation Networks: A Physics-Constrained Influence-Function Framework

**abstract/A1：** Understanding how individual training observations influence model predictions is important for developing reliable and interpretable data-driven transportation systems. Existing influence-function methods, however, are primarily formulated for unconstrained learning and do not explicitly account for physical constraints that couple different components of a transportation network. Under such constraints, perturbing a training observation can affect model parameters and predictions only through physically feasible directions, potentially changing how its influence propagates across the network. This paper develops a hard physics-informed influence-function framework for constrained learning, in which training-data influence is characterized within the physically feasible space defined by network constraints. The analytical results reveal how physical constraints alter the magnitude, direction, and spatial distribution of training-sample influence relative to conventional influence measures. Using traffic prediction under flow-conservation constraints as a case study, we show that influential observations identified by the proposed framework differ systematically from those identified by classical influence functions. Through orthogonal decomposition, we quantitatively characterize how much of a perturbation in the training data is propagated through the network via the flow-conservation constraints and how much is removed by the constraint projection. The proposed influence framework provides a physically consistent approach for evaluating training-data influence in constrained transportation learning problems, with potential applications to data diagnostics, sensor evaluation, and targeted data collection.

</details>

<a id="paper-54"></a>

### 54. [MetricTrail: Revisiting the Power of Metric Depth and Local Optimization for Long-Sequence Streaming 3D Reconstruction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07555v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07555

**作者：** Jingnan Gao、Jiahui Huang、Sven Elflein、Jianhao Zheng、Bingxin Ke、Xiaokang Yang、Yichao Yan、Laura Leal-Taixé、Qunjie Zhou、Jiawei Ren、Shengyu Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07555v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07555v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MetricTrail，一个面向长单目视频的流式相机位姿估计与稠密重建系统：在 DPVO 的稀疏局部估计器上引入单图度量深度作为 patch 深度初始化与优化中的软约束，并用冻结 DINOv3 特征经 adapter 增强匹配特征与循环上下文。

**相关性简评：** 其用 adapter 把冻结视觉骨干的细节特征注入循环状态、并以软约束在局部优化窗口中持续修正状态的做法，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据如何稳定写入并更新 latent 推理状态提供可核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MetricTrail: Revisiting the Power of Metric Depth and Local Optimization for Long-Sequence Streaming 3D Reconstruction

**abstract/A1：** We present MetricTrail, a streaming system for accurate camera motion estimation and dense reconstruction from long monocular videos. To keep inference efficient over long sequences, we revisit DPVO's sparse local estimator. We find that single-image metric depth substantially improves its trajectory accuracy, without retraining the correspondence network or enlarging the optimization window. Metric predictions initialize patch depths and remain as soft constraints during optimization. We further improve correspondence estimation through learned adapters that augment matching features and recurrent context with frozen DINOv3 representations. The correspondence estimator and adapters are trained on diverse short clips without additional long-sequence training. This modular design also allows the depth provider to be independently upgraded and supports supplied camera intrinsics and calibrated stereo observations without retraining. Under the reported RGB-only protocols, MetricTrail achieves the lowest average trajectory error among evaluated methods on KITTI, Oxford Spires, and VBR. Pairing the estimated poses with single-image depth predictions also produces strong dense reconstruction on indoor and outdoor benchmarks and coherent reconstructions of in-the-wild videos. With supplied intrinsics, the system processes KITTI-02 at 12.70 FPS with 3.21 GB peak GPU memory.

**comment/C1：** Project Page: https://research.nvidia.com/labs/sil/projects/metrictrail/, Code: https://github.com/nv-tlabs/vipe

</details>

### 55. [MambaTG: Candidate-Aware State Space Modeling for Future Link Prediction on Temporal Graphs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07569v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07569

**作者：** Nguyen Hong Quan、Sitao Luan、Yilun Zheng、Lihui Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07569v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07569v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对时序图未来链接预测中重复边与首次出现边表现不均的问题，提出 MambaTG，将信号分解为历史序列演化与候选—历史兼容性，并用 Mamba 的选择性递归和输入依赖状态转移联合建模二者。

**相关性简评：** 该工作虽属时序图领域且不涉及视觉，但其「候选条件化的状态转移」思路可供核查是否能迁移到 LVR 中依据视觉证据选择性地更新或筛选 latent 状态，与 LOOK 式的证据使用与状态更新问题可能相关。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MambaTG: Candidate-Aware State Space Modeling for Future Link Prediction on Temporal Graphs

**abstract/A1：** Future link prediction on temporal graphs involves both repeated interactions between previously connected node pairs and first-time interactions between pairs with no prior interaction. While repeated-edge prediction can exploit direct pairwise evidence, unseen-edge prediction must generalize beyond historical recurrence. Existing temporal graph models often exploit signals tied to recurring interactions or historical overlap, which are highly informative for repeated edges but become limited or unavailable for unseen edges, contributing to substantial performance degradation on unseen-edge prediction. Based on this observation, we identify two complementary signals for modeling sequential dynamics beyond historical recurrence: (1) historical-sequence evolution, which captures dependencies among the source node's chronologically ordered interactions, and (2) candidate--history compatibility, which captures how well a candidate fits the source node's evolving history. Based on the decomposition, we propose MambaTG, a candidate-aware Mamba-based framework that jointly models these two signals. Mamba provides a natural mechanism for this design: its selective recurrence captures the evolution of ordered interaction histories, while its input-dependent state transitions allow the history state to adapt to different candidate inputs. Extensive experiments on 13 temporal graph datasets against 13 representative baselines show that MambaTG achieves strong and consistent performance across both repeated and unseen edges while remaining computationally efficient.

</details>

<a id="paper-56"></a>

### 56. [Subspace Learning with Outlier Exposure for Out-of-Distribution Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07580v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07580

**作者：** Hongxu Shen、Kun Fang、Jiahui Zheng、Jian Sun、Qinghua Tao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07580v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07580v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 RecOE，在 Outlier Exposure 框架下从 InD 数据与辅助离群样本中学习若干特征子空间，以子空间内重建误差、跨子空间抑制项和正交正则共同塑造判别性表示，推理时用子空间重建误差差作为 OoD 分数。

**相关性简评：** 与 VLM 推理无直接关系，但其「把特征空间分解为任务主变分子空间并以重建误差差值作判据」的做法，可能为 LVR 中区分视觉证据相关与无关的内部子空间、以及用子空间几何刻画 latent 状态提供可核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Subspace Learning with Outlier Exposure for Out-of-Distribution Detection

**abstract/A1：** Out-of-Distribution (OoD) detection is pivotal for reliable deployments of Deep Neural Networks (DNNs), aiming to distinguish anomalous samples from the In-Distribution (InD) data in training. By introducing outliers as surrogate OoD data to finetune DNNs, Outlier Exposure (OE) has greatly advanced OoD detection in recent years. Existing OE-based research remains predominantly logit-centric, either by generating diverse outliers or by regularizing them with objectives operating at the prediction-output level. Nonetheless, these approaches leave much of the rich information in feature spaces untapped. In this work, we propose an OE-based detection framework, named RecOE, that learns appropriate feature subspaces from InD data and auxiliary outliers, such that the InD-OoD discrepancy can be effectively characterized through the learned feature subspaces. Specifically, the feature subspaces are optimized to capture the dominant variations of InD and outliers by minimizing reconstruction errors therein. Besides, a sample-level objective discourages reconstruction in the opposing subspace, while an orthogonality regularizer reduces subspace overlap, further promoting discriminative subspaces. At inference, the difference in reconstruction errors between the learned subspaces serves as the OoD detection score. Extensive experiments show the superiority of RecOE on multiple benchmarks, in which significant performance gains are achieved over recent strong baselines with only slight extra overhead from two small-size matrices of subspace bases. The source code is available at https://github.com/shenhongxu123/RecOE.

</details>

<a id="paper-57"></a>

### 57. [TACT-KV: Tri-Axis Cosine Transform for Compressing Volumetric KV Caches in Medical VLMs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07593v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07593

**作者：** Chongyu Qu、Ritchie Zhao、Yufan He、Dong Yang、Zhengyi Lu、Junchao Zhu、Tianyuan Yao、Juming Xiong、Junlin Guo、Yanfan Zhu、Yuechen Yang、Daguang Xu、Bennett Allan Landman、Yucheng Tang、Yuankai Huo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07593v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07593v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对 3D 医学影像 VLM 的 KV 缓存显存瓶颈，提出 TACT-KV：沿三个解剖轴做可分离 3D 离散余弦变换，按频谱能量分配每层比特并用固定码本量化，在医学与通用 VLM 上降低 1-bit 预测翻转率。

**相关性简评：** 其频域能量集中与按能量分配比特的思路可能可供核查：视觉 KV 被压缩时哪些证据成分被保留、哪些坍塌，可为 LVR 中视觉细节保留与信息坍缩提供度量线索；但论文不涉及 latent 推理状态或答案对 latent 内容的依赖。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TACT-KV: Tri-Axis Cosine Transform for Compressing Volumetric KV Caches in Medical VLMs

**abstract/A1：** Vision-language models (VLMs) applied to 3D medical scans face a structural memory wall. A single CT or MRI volume produces tens of thousands of vision tokens, and the resulting key-value (KV) cache scales linearly with both batch and context. Existing KV compression methods operate on the cache's feature or token statistics, or apply 1D and 2D transforms, leaving the three-axis spatial structure in the cache unexploited. We propose TACT-KV (Tri-Axis Cosine Transform), a calibration-free KV compression method that exploits strong spatial correlations in visual KV states along all three anatomical axes of 3D scans. This correlation concentrates the KV cache energy under a frequency-domain transform. TACT-KV applies a separable 3D discrete cosine transform to the KV cache, allocates a per-layer bit budget across frequency bins via reverse water-filling (more bits to high-energy components), and quantizes each bin with a fixed scalar codebook. We derive a close-form relation: the distortion reduction from adaptive bit allocation is dominated by the spectral concentration of the transformed cache. Across six medical VLMs and two 3D CT benchmarks, TACT-KV achieves the lowest 1-bit prediction-flip rate on eleven of twelve model and dataset pairs in our main comparison. The same advantage reproduces on four general-purpose VLMs evaluated on video tasks, indicating that the benefit of exploiting three-axis structure generalizes beyond medical imaging. At equal high-bandwidth memory (HBM), TACT-KV scales the decoding batch size on a single GPU by up to $8\\times$.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-58"></a>

### 58. [Binding Paraphrased Instructions to Encountered Objects in sLLM Agents under Partial Observability](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07599v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07599

**作者：** Chanyoung Kim、Harim Song、Byoungchan Ko、Keonyoung Lee、Myungwon Lee、Dahuin Jung

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07599v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07599v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建 ALFWorld-Para 基准，考察 RL 微调的小模型具身智能体对指令改写的鲁棒性，发现性能下降主要源于物体同义词；并提出免训练的 BINDIntent，用语义接近度与对未见候选的持续性估计概率化地把指代表达绑定到已观察标签上。

**相关性简评：** 虽为纯文本具身任务，但「保有同义词指称知识却未在行动中使用」的绑定缺口与 LVR 中表示含信息却未被生成利用的问题相似；其持久性估计与部分可观测下的状态维护机制可供核查是否能迁移到视觉证据筛选与信念更新。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Binding Paraphrased Instructions to Encountered Objects in sLLM Agents under Partial Observability

**abstract/A1：** Reliable deployment of LLM agents requires grounding diverse user expressions in their environments. Although RL-finetuned smaller large language model (sLLM) agents rival larger prompted LLMs on embodied tasks, their robustness to instruction paraphrases remains unclear. We introduce ALFWorld-Para to evaluate this robustness under controlled paraphrases, revealing 21-52% declines in success rates (SRs), predominantly driven by object synonyms. Despite retaining knowledge of synonym referents, agents often fail to take the corresponding objects even after observing them, exposing a gap between referential knowledge and object binding. Partial observability further complicates binding, as referents may remain unseen or have already been encountered under different labels. To address this challenge, we propose BINDIntent, a training-free framework that probabilistically binds referring expressions to observed labels using semantic proximity and estimated persistence against unseen candidates. Without additional training or LLM calls, BINDIntent improves synonym-paraphrase success of RL-finetuned sLLMs by 17.5-49.1 percentage points (pp) with only 0.4 ms of CPU overhead per step. It also raises GiGPO-7B's overall SR from 61.8% to 77.4%, surpassing a prompted 24B LLM. Code and benchmark are available at https://github.com/cau-hai-lab/ALFWorld-Para.

**comment/C1：** 44 pages, 26 figures, 18 tables

</details>

### 59. [Embedding-Space Fingerprinting of LoRA Fine-Tunes: Recovering the Training-Data Domain from the Model Artifact Alone](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07610v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07610

**作者：** Shivesh Rane

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07610v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07610v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文用同一基座模型在18个数据集、5个领域上训练36个LoRA微调，并分别提取白盒权重统计、灰盒激活与黑盒生成文本三类特征，在预先固定的留一数据集协议下检验能否仅凭模型产物恢复训练数据所属领域，结果激活（61%）与输出（64%）均高于权重统计（42%）。

**相关性简评：** 其「固定probe集激活＋留一数据集」的探针式诊断可供核查，或可迁移为判断VLM内部状态是否保留视觉证据、以及内部表示与输出信息量是否一致的评测设计；但该工作为纯文本微调取证、非推理任务，线索仍需人工验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Embedding-Space Fingerprinting of LoRA Fine-Tunes: Recovering the Training-Data Domain from the Model Artifact Alone

**abstract/A1：** When a base language model is fine-tuned with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), how much of the training data domain leaks into the resulting artifact? This paper studies whether a simple classifier can recover the domain of a fine-tune given only the model artifact (weight deltas, internal activations on a fixed probe set, or generated text) without ever observing the training data. Using one base model (Qwen3.5-0.8B), 36 LoRA fine-tunes are produced over 18 public datasets in five domains (medical, code, mathematics, non-English text, narrative prose), and three feature families are extracted at decreasing levels of access: white-box weights, gray-box activations, and black-box outputs. A leave-one-dataset-out (LODO) protocol, frozen before any result was seen, tests whether the domain of a fine-tune trained on a previously unseen dataset can be identified. The domain is recoverable well above chance (20%) from gray-box activations (61%) and black-box outputs (64%); white-box weight statistics reach 42%. Contrary to the pre-registered hypothesis that signal decays monotonically from weights to activations to outputs, activations and outputs were more informative than the weight-delta statistics. The black-box result holds unchanged (64%) when only generated text is used, without token probabilities. Domain accuracy is also stable when a third run per dataset is trained on an independently drawn data subsample, and black-box predictions are unaffected by the LoRA rank (8-64). All code and per-run feature vectors are released.

</details>
