### 60. [Towards Real-Time Full-Waveform LiDAR Transformers via Intensity-Guided Token Reduction and Physics-Aware Augmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07613v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07613

**作者：** Kotaro Oishi、Kazuma Ikeda、Ryosei Hara、Ryo Yoshida、Mariko Isogawa、Kentaro Yoshioka

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07613v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07613v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对全波形LiDAR提出FWL-ToPM（按强度分布对背景冗余token做剪枝与合并）与FWLAug（保持飞行时间波形与视锥几何的数据增强），在Ghost-FWL上实现实时Transformer并同时提升精度与OOD泛化。

**相关性简评：** 强度引导的token剪枝/合并与「保留关键波形结构」的压缩思路，可能为VLM/LVR中视觉证据筛选、任务必要细节保留与信息坍缩问题提供可核查的借鉴；但原任务为LiDAR检测而非多模态推理，迁移性需进一步确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Towards Real-Time Full-Waveform LiDAR Transformers via Intensity-Guided Token Reduction and Physics-Aware Augmentation

**abstract/A1：** LiDAR is a critical sensor for autonomous driving and robotics, yet it remains vulnerable to adverse conditions such as fog, rain, transparent objects, and low-reflectance surfaces. Full-waveform LiDAR (FWL) addresses these limitations by capturing the complete return waveform as a histogram, preserving rich reflection characteristics including multi-path echoes and weak signals. While recent Transformer-based approaches have demonstrated promise on FWL data, two fundamental bottlenecks remain: high computational cost from redundant tokens in low-intensity background regions, and limited generalization due to scarce annotated training data. We address both bottlenecks by exploiting the physical structure inherent to FWL data. First, we propose FWL-ToPM, a token pruning and merging mechanism that leverages intensity distributions to selectively reduce tokens while retaining critical waveform content. Second, we introduce FWLAug, a data augmentation framework designed as a regularization method that preserves waveform structure, retaining the Time-of-Flight waveform of each ray and the frustum geometry, to improve generalization to unseen scenes. Evaluated on the Ghost-FWL benchmark, FWL-ToPM achieves over 8 times speedup at real-time throughput alongside a nearly 9-point F1 gain over the prior state of the art, establishing the first real-time FWL Transformer that simultaneously improves accuracy. FWLAug further improves performance on both the standard benchmark and an out-of-distribution test set of unseen scenes. Together, these methods push FWL-based perception toward practical deployment. The code and the OOD dataset are publicly available and can be accessed via the project page: https://keio-csg.github.io/FWL-ToPM

**comment/C1：** Accepted to NeurIPS 2026 (Main)

</details>

<a id="paper-61"></a>

### 61. [Monotone Viability Learning for Offline Safe Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07616v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07616

**作者：** Ziqian Wang、Zhen Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07616v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07616v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 MoViL，通过单调有限时域集合递推从离线转移数据学习 Hamilton-Jacobi 可行集，用二值监督避免折扣导致的尺度衰减，并在部署时用学到的可行性估计认证动作。

**相关性简评：** 其「单调跨时域递推 + 跨时域固定尺度监督」可能为 latent 推理中的长度泛化与停止决策监督提供线索，可供核查；但研究对象是安全 RL 的状态可行性，与视觉多模态推理无直接关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Monotone Viability Learning for Offline Safe Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Offline safe reinforcement learning requires reliable long-horizon safety estimates from fixed data. Learning Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) viability in this setting calls for semantic preservation, convergence, and numerical stability. To address these challenges, we propose Monotone Viability Learning (MoViL), which learns viable sets from offline transitions through monotone finite-horizon set recursion. The recursion preserves finite-horizon safety semantics and converges to the infinite-horizon viability kernel. Binary supervision maintains a fixed target scale across horizons, avoiding discount-induced numerical attenuation. We further establish sufficient conditions for recovering behavior-supported viable subsets. The learned viability estimates are used to certify actions at deployment. Dubins car experiments validate the numerical accuracy of the learned viable sets. Evaluations on safe RL benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art reward-cost performance. MoViL satisfies the average cost constraint on every task and achieves the lowest average cost among the compared methods. These results highlight MoViL's potential to extend long-horizon HJ viability analysis to safety-critical systems where reliable dynamics models are unavailable and online exploration is unsafe.

</details>

<a id="paper-62"></a>

### 62. [Measuring the Marginal Value of Verifier-Based RL Beyond Supervised Fine-Tuning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07639v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07639

**作者：** Amit Sagtani、Saloni Garg

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07639v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07639v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建含 1,877 条合成 GPU/LLM 训练故障诊断任务的确定性基准，由程序化验证器带部分分评分，并在预注册设定下控制对比 SFT 与 GRPO 式 verifier RL 的边际贡献：SFT 承担了大部分提升，RL 仅在分布偏移下有微弱增益。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理无直接关系，但「训练阶段边际贡献」的预注册对照测量范式，以及 SFT 残留误差高度系统化、压缩组内学习信号的观察，可能可供核查 LVR 轨迹监督与 RL 贡献分配、监督信号多样性的设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Measuring the Marginal Value of Verifier-Based RL Beyond Supervised Fine-Tuning

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) is usually evaluated as an alternative to supervised fine-tuning (SFT), or as a stage that follows it, with the implicit assumption that the RL stage contributes gains of its own. We measure that contribution directly, in a controlled setting where both stages can be run to completion and compared exactly. We built gpu-train-debug, a deterministic benchmark of 1,877 synthetic GPU and LLM training failure diagnosis tasks across 30 archetypes, graded by a programmatic verifier with partial credit, and calibrated so that a mid-tier model scores in a 0.60 to 0.75 band. A pilot produced a surprise: 1,200 gold demonstrations nearly solved the benchmark (SFT 0.785 on the hard split), while 15 steps of GRPO from the base model did not move it (0.314 vs 0.311 base). We therefore amended the study, in writing and before the full run, to ask the sharper question: what does verifier-based RL add beyond demonstrations? At the pre-registered scale (60 GRPO steps, learning rate 1e-6), the answer is: no detectable improvement in-distribution (+0.22 points, 95% CI \[-0.06, +0.51\]), and a modest but measurable improvement under distribution shift (+0.33 points on a held-out OOD split, CI \[+0.02, +0.68\]). SFT accounted for most of the available improvement, while verifier-based RL produced only modest additional gains under distribution shift. The residual errors SFT leaves are mostly systematic, identical across samples, which likely reduces the within-group learning signal available to GRPO. A full 60-step cold-start GRPO arm confirms the pilot at the full horizon. The whole study cost $8.69 of compute and is fully reproducible from archived generations, frozen configs, and tagged code.

**comment/C1：** 8 pages, 2 figures. Code and artifacts: https://github.com/saloni-garg/gpu-train-debug

</details>

<a id="paper-63"></a>

### 63. [Neural Image Compression with Internal-External Prior Fusion and Alignment](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07644v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07644

**作者：** Tiancheng Si、Wenlong Xu、Dahua Gao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07644v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07644v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 IE-PFA 上下文模型：以编解码共享的可学习字典作为外部先验，用基于 RWKV 的空间-通道先验融合模块与仅训练期生效的自蒸馏对齐机制整合内外先验，在单一网络内支持 64 档变码率图像压缩。

**相关性简评：** 可学习字典式外部先验与内部上下文融合、以及训练期对齐以避免推理开销的做法，可能可供核查如何向 LVR 的 latent 状态注入共享视觉先验并施加辅助监督；但该工作只面向压缩的码率-失真，不涉及推理决策与证据筛选，借鉴性需进一步判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Neural Image Compression with Internal-External Prior Fusion and Alignment

**abstract/A1：** Existing context models for learned image com- pression rely primarily on internal priors from the current image, overlooking common patterns and typical textures shared across natural images. To address this limitation, we propose an Internal-External Prior Fusion-Alignment (IE-PFA) context model built upon quadtree spatial-channel joint modeling, where a learnable dictionary shared by the encoder and decoder serves as an external prior. Specifically, an RWKV-based Spatial- Channel Prior Fusion (RSCPF) module efficiently fuses inter- nal and external priors along spatial and channel dimensions with linear complexity, while a training-only Self-Distilled Prior Alignment (SDPA) mechanism aligns them without inference overhead. Moreover, our model supports variable-rate coding over 64 quality levels within a single network and is accelerated by custom CUDA operators. Experiments on seven benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art rate-distortion performance, achiev- ing an average BD-Rate saving of 21.65% over VTM-22.0-Intra, with only 29.9M parameters and the fastest coding speed among advanced neural codecs (61 ms encoding and 52 ms decoding). Code is available at https://github.com/stcnjtech/DCVC-X-Intra.

**comment/C1：** IEEE International Conference on Visual Communications and Image Processing 2026 (VCIP 2026)

</details>

<a id="paper-64"></a>

### 64. [Generator-Aligned Reward Modeling with Target-Aware Bounded Optimization for Visual Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07648v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07648

**作者：** Ziqiao Weng、Yuanzhi Liang、Haibing Huang、Weidong Cai、Xuelong Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07648v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07648v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出生成器对齐的奖励建模（GARM）与目标感知的有界优化（TABO），并将 Flow-GRPO 改造为 TA-GRPO，用于视觉生成模型的属性后训练，以缓解训练数据与生成分布错配、内容混淆及奖励过度优化。

**相关性简评：** 其内容匹配对比式奖励与分时间步 credit assignment 可供核查如何为 LVR 多步推理轨迹分配监督、抑制无关视觉内容对目标信号的混淆；但原文面向图像/视频生成质量提升，未涉及推理状态或证据获取，迁移仅为可能线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Generator-Aligned Reward Modeling with Target-Aware Bounded Optimization for Visual Generation

**abstract/A1：** Post-training aims to enhance desired attributes in visual generative models while preserving their general generation capabilities. Reward-guided post-training faces two challenges: real training data may mismatch the generator's output distribution, and attribute supervision may be confounded by unrelated content. Unbounded reward maximization can also cause overoptimization and degrade generation quality. We propose a framework combining Generator-Aligned Reward Modeling (GARM) and Target-Aware Bounded Optimization (TABO). GARM mitigates distribution mismatch through generator-support matching and reduces content confounding using content-matched real contrasts, yielding generator-relevant, attribute-focused reward supervision. TABO maps the learned reward to a smooth, upper-bounded function calibrated from real target-positive examples. This transformation is independent of the optimization algorithm and supports both direct reward-gradient and policy-based optimization. We further introduce Target-Aware GRPO (TA-GRPO), an adaptation of Flow-GRPO that combines fixed stage-wise advantage scaling, a generator-to-target curriculum, and timestep-balanced credit assignment for flow-based video optimization. Experiments show that GARM improves target-attribute expression while maintaining competitive generation quality. TABO better balances attribute enhancement and visual quality than unconstrained reward maximization, while TA-GRPO improves target enhancement over vanilla Flow-GRPO and reduces unintended attribute leakage on neutral prompts. These results highlight the importance of generator-aligned reward supervision and controlled, data-calibrated optimization for targeted visual generation.

**comment/C1：** 24 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-65"></a>

### 65. [An Information Bottleneck for Post-hoc Memorization Mitigation in LLMs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07669v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07669

**作者：** Dina EL Zein、Shashi Kumar、James Henderson

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07669v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07669v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 在冻结的 LLM 主干与语言建模头之间插入非参数变分信息瓶颈，压缩最终隐藏表示以降低训练数据可提取性，并在书籍段落抽取和语言建模基准上评估记忆削减与通用性能的权衡。

**相关性简评：** 虽为纯文本隐私去记忆工作，其对最终隐藏状态的瓶颈压缩与效用折衷，可能为 LVR 中视觉细节保留、信息坍缩和 latent 容量控制提供可核查的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** An Information Bottleneck for Post-hoc Memorization Mitigation in LLMs

**abstract/A1：** Recent studies have shown that large language models (LLMs) can memorize and reproduce copyrighted training data, raising serious privacy and intellectual property concerns. In this work, we investigate whether memorization can be mitigated without modifying or retraining the underlying model through an information bottleneck. We propose a modular plug-and-play framework that inserts a nonparametric variational information bottleneck (NVIB) between a frozen LLM backbone and its language modeling head. By compressing the final hidden representations before token prediction, the proposed bottleneck aims to preserve general linguistic knowledge while filtering out highly specific memorized sequences. We evaluate our approach on memorization extraction tasks involving book passages and on standard language modeling benchmarks to measure the trade-off between memorization reduction and downstream utility. Our experiments show that the NVIB layer substantially reduces extractability while maintaining competitive performance on benchmark tasks. These results suggest that adding an information bottleneck offers a practical and architecture agnostic direction for post-hoc memorization mitigation in pretrained LLMs.

**comment/C1：** 13 pages, 4 figures, including appendix

</details>

<a id="paper-66"></a>

### 66. [Egocentric Action Recognition with Objects and Vision-Language Affordances](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07673v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07673

**作者：** Raúl Boix-Aldeguer、Manuel Benavent-Lledo、David Ortiz-Perez、Jose Garcia-Rodriguez

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07673v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07673v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文面向第一视角动作识别，将冻结的 SlowFast 视频专家与注意力物体专家晚融合，并用视觉语言模型零样本生成物体可供性与交互可供性，以判断检测框中的物体是否正被手部操作。

**相关性简评：** 不涉及 latent 推理状态，但「交互可供性作为门控」可提供视觉证据筛选、抑制非活跃检测的线索，可供核查其是否适用于 LVR 推理前的证据选择；冻结专家晚融合也提供模态组合参考。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Egocentric Action Recognition with Objects and Vision-Language Affordances

**abstract/A1：** Action recognition, and more specifically egocentric action recognition, is a core task in computer vision, with applications such as monitoring the daily life activities of elderly people and task assistance. Recent methods rely on visual modalities such as RGB video or depth. However, in egocentric video, an action is largely defined by its objects, which these methods rarely model. We propose a method that combines a frozen SlowFast video expert with an attention-based object expert by late fusion. Each object is represented with its affordances, that is, what can be done with it, generated zero-shot by a vision-language model. Since datasets such as MECCANO annotate only the objects involved in each action, evaluating with these boxes tells the model which objects are active. We therefore train on annotated boxes and evaluate the method with detections from the official active-object detector, and with annotated boxes as an upper bound. Without affordances, our fusion reaches 68.4% Top-1 with annotated boxes but 49.0% with detections. About half of this drop comes from non-active detections. To remove them, the same vision-language model generates an interaction affordance that decides whether a hand is manipulating each detected object. On MECCANO, objects raise the Top-1 of the video expert from 44.9% to 49.0%, and the two affordances raise it to 49.9%, above SlowFast and object-aware RGB baselines. Code will be released upon acceptance.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages, 6 figures. Under review at IEEE FG 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-67"></a>

### 67. [Non-Parametric Mutual Information Estimation with Vine Copulas](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07693v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07693

**作者：** Sigurd Holmsen、Berit Omli Øksnes、Ingrid Hobæk Haff、Sylvia Richardson、Ali Ramezani-Kebrya

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07693v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07693v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 VINE，一种基于简化 D-vine copula 的非参数互信息估计器：用简化 D-vine 近似联合密度，使密度比化简为成对 copula 的乘积并逐项非参数估计，在低至中维数据上以更低计算成本取得与神经 MI 估计器相当（competitive）的精度。

**相关性简评：** 互信息估计可作为量化 latent 状态保留多少视觉证据、诊断信息坍缩或做贡献分配的工具线索；但该方法仅在低中维数据上验证，能否用于 VLM 高维隐状态与视觉证据之间的 MI 度量可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Non-Parametric Mutual Information Estimation with Vine Copulas

**abstract/A1：** We introduce VINE: a non-parametric mutual information (MI) estimator based on simplified D-vine copulas. Approximating the joint density with a simplified D-vine allows the density ratio to simplify to a product of pair copulas, which we can estimate non-parametrically. This structural simplification yields VINE, a flexible and computationally efficient MI estimator. We compare VINE against SotA neural MI estimators on a range of datasets, both under settings where the simplified D-vine assumption holds and where it is violated, demonstrating competitive accuracy at a lower computational cost for low- to medium-dimensional datasets.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the conference on Neural Information Processing Systems (NeurIPS 2026)

</details>

<a id="paper-68"></a>

### 68. [InfMasking: A Unified Variational Framework for Synergistic Multimodal Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07702v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07702

**作者：** Liangjian Wen、Qun Dai、Jiang Duan、Yong Dai、Jianzhuang Liu、Dongkai Wang、Jun Wang、Fuyuan Cao、Zenglin Xu、Zhao Kang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07702v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07702v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 InfMasking：融合前随机掩蔽各模态特征，要求不同部分观测下的融合表示都与参考目标（无监督时为未掩蔽融合视图，有标签时为类代理）保持一致，并以高斯变分界优化无穷多掩码的期望目标。

**相关性简评：** 其用随机掩蔽抑制主导模态与共享信息捷径的训练目标，可能为 LVR 中防止 latent 状态依赖语言旁路、迫使推理真正使用视觉证据提供可核查的监督线索；但该工作面向多模态融合分类，与推理期状态生成无直接关系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** InfMasking: A Unified Variational Framework for Synergistic Multimodal Learning

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal models are expected to exploit synergistic information, that is, information available only when multiple modalities are observed together. In practice, however, a fusion module can fit the training objective by relying on a dominant modality or on information that modalities share, and the synergy between modalities is often left unused. We propose InfMasking, a unified multimodal learning framework based on Infinite Masking. Before fusion, InfMasking randomly masks the features of each modality; each sampled mask produces a different partial combination of cross-modal evidence and a fused representation of the corresponding modality interactions. All masked fused representations are required to remain consistent with a reference target: the unmasked fused view when training is unsupervised, and the class proxy of the task label when labels are available. Maintaining this consistency across diverse partial combinations discourages reliance on a dominant modality or on shared information alone, and drives the fusion module to learn cross-modal interaction patterns that remain meaningful under partial observations. Since the objective is an expectation over infinitely many masks, we model the masked fused representations as Gaussian and optimize closed-form variational bounds, whose population-moment gaps are controlled by the masking-induced covariance under this model. On the evaluated benchmarks, InfMasking improves predictive performance and yields higher mean PID estimates of task-label synergy in learned modality-specific representations, alongside higher InterSHAP interaction attribution in the trained predictors. Code is available at https://github.com/brightest66/InfMasking.

**comment/C1：** Accepted for publication in IEEE Transactions on Pattern Analysis and Machine Intelligence (TPAMI). 16 pages; supplementary material provided as an ancillary file. Code: https://github.com/brightest66/InfMasking

</details>

<a id="paper-69"></a>

### 69. [Selective Event Evidence and Persistent Temporal Priors for Event-Based Point Tracking](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07713v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07713

**作者：** Xiaokai Song、Zhang Li、Leqi Liu、Yibin Ye、Kun Wang、Xichao Teng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07713v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07713v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出仅用事件数据的长序列任意点跟踪框架 SEPT：任务自适应事件证据调制学习有界可靠度场，在空间、时间与通道上加权事件张量；持久轨迹先验以 Schur 补边缘化局部二次轨迹模型，跨重叠窗口传递几何约束。

**相关性简评：** 其证据可靠度门控与跨窗口状态传递机制，可能为 LVR 的视觉证据筛选与 latent 状态延续提供方法线索，但需核查该几何设定能否迁移到 VLM 推理场景。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Selective Event Evidence and Persistent Temporal Priors for Event-Based Point Tracking

**abstract/A1：** Event cameras enable tracking under fast motion and challenging illumination. However, stable long-term point tracking remains challenging: event activity is sparse and varies with scene content and motion, complicating correspondence, while trajectory estimates can drift over long sequences. Together, these challenges motivate a tracker that selectively uses informative event evidence and retains trajectory constraints across temporal windows. We propose Selective Event Evidence and Persistent Temporal Priors (SEPT), an event-only framework for tracking any point (TAP) over long sequences. SEPT introduces two complementary modules: Task-Adaptive Event Evidence Modulation (TEEM) and Persistent Trajectory Prior (PTP). Using only tracking supervision, TEEM learns a bounded reliability field that weights the event tensor across space, time, and channels before feature extraction. PTP carries geometric information across overlapping windows by marginalizing a fixed local quadratic trajectory model with a Schur complement. Its visibility-aware correction refines overlap coordinates, while a clipped terminal velocity initializes coordinates at newly exposed timestamps. SEPT builds on the ETAP architecture. SEPT is trained exclusively on synthetic EventKubric data and evaluated on five datasets across TAP and feature tracking. Results show that SEPT outperforms the retrained ETAP baseline in average Jaccard for TAP and in feature age and expected feature age for feature tracking; it also surpasses several methods combining event and frame inputs on EC and EDS. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/sssxxxkkkk/SEPT.

</details>

<a id="paper-70"></a>

### 70. [Multimodal Information Interactions Bottleneck for Multimodal Self-Supervised Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07718v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07718

**作者：** Liangjian Wen、Jiangtao Zheng、Jiang Duan、Qun Dai、Yong Dai、Jianzhuang Liu、Jun Wang、Shu-Dong Huang、Xinwang Liu、Zenglin Xu、Zhao Kang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07718v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07718v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 MIIB 框架，借助部分信息分解显式区分多模态交互中的冗余、独有与协同信息，用双路径编码加融合结构并以变分界和基于 score 的熵估计构造可处理目标，学习最小充分表示。

**相关性简评：** 其「抑制冗余、保留独有与协同信息」的表示约束与 LVR 中视觉细节保留、信息坍缩问题相通，可供核查能否用作 latent 推理状态的训练监督目标；但论文面向多模态自监督表示学习，未涉及推理期状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Multimodal Information Interactions Bottleneck for Multimodal Self-Supervised Learning

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal self-supervised learning is challenged by heterogeneity, unequal informativeness, and complex information interactions across multimodal data sources such as images, text, audio, and tabular data. While contrastive methods effectively align shared semantics, they typically rely on strong multi-view redundancy assumptions, overlooking modality-specific and synergistic information, thus leading to suboptimal representations. We propose the Multimodal Information Interactions Bottleneck (MIIB), a principled framework grounded in Partial Information Decomposition that explicitly encodes redundant, unique, and synergistic information within multimodal interactions. MIIB employs a dual-path model architecture for modality-specific encoding and multimodal fusion, optimized using surrogate tractable entropy-based objectives that enforce minimal sufficient representations without requiring explicit mutual information estimation. By leveraging variational bounds and score-based entropy estimation, MIIB efficiently captures complex cross-modal interactions while suppressing superfluous information. Experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art multimodal self-supervised methods. Code is available at https://github.com/wanglingzaishi/MIIB.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages, 3-page supplementary material provided as an ancillary file. Accepted for publication in IEEE Transactions on Pattern Analysis and Machine Intelligence (TPAMI)

</details>

<a id="paper-71"></a>

### 71. [Evaluating Language Model Features for Zero-Shot Time-Series Anomaly Detection Under Isolation Assumption](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07720v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07720

**作者：** Romain Hermary、Nesryne Mejri、Djamila Aouada

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07720v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07720v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文把 LLM 仅当作特征提取器，采用每 token 一个时间步的简单分词以保留局部结构，在零样本设定下用 Isolation Forest 检验多变量时间序列异常的隔离性与特征可分性，并考察性能是否源于语言预训练。

**相关性简评：** 其「异常特征应与正常特征隔离」的可分性诊断与细粒度 token 保真设计，或可供核查 LVR 内部状态是否保留任务必要证据、是否发生信息坍缩，但需从时间序列域迁移并补充视觉侧验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Evaluating Language Model Features for Zero-Shot Time-Series Anomaly Detection Under Isolation Assumption

**abstract/A1：** Time-Series Anomaly Detection (TSAD) is a fundamentally challenging task: it is unsupervised, requires high local precision capabilities, and suffers from limited labelled data. Recent work has explored adapting Large Language Models (LLMs) to time-series via cross-modal transfer. However, existing approaches are poorly suited to Multivariate TSAD (MTSAD), as tokenisation often obscures fine-grained temporal and variables structures, and most methods optimise auxiliary objectives such as forecasting or reconstruction. We posit that these surrogate objectives become unreliable as model capacity increases. Highly expressive models risk reconstructing or forecasting anomalies, undermining the foundations of both strategies. Instead, we propose to use LLMs purely as feature extractors and directly analyse their induced feature spaces. In this work, investigate the ability of LLMs to provide useful representations for MTSADs in a zero-shot setting through classification. To this end, we study the separability of the extracted features, under the assumption that anomalous features should be isolated from normal ones. We analyse their temporal and contextual discriminative ability using simple step-wise tokenisation schemes, with one time-step per token, which preserve fine-grained local information. Their usability for MTSAD is assessed using an unsupervised Isolation Forest, enabling a direct evaluation of the resulting representation quality. We further investigate whether the observed performance can be attributed to the language-related pre-training of the LLMs or arises independently of it. Our results provide empirical insight into the ability of LLMs to represent multivariate time-series for anomaly detection and highlight broader applications of cross-modal foundation models.

</details>

<a id="paper-72"></a>

### 72. [Reinforcing What the Student Has Learned: Teacher-Guided On-Policy Distillation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07722v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07722

**作者：** Bozhen Fang、Linhao Zhong、Xuehao Wang、Huanyi Zheng、Hao Chen、Chunhua Shen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07722v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07722v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文指出 on-policy distillation 的师生 log-prob 反馈存在幅度失衡，风格位置约为数学位置的 3.5 倍，并提出 SD-OPD：以中心化的「学生-预训练基座」差异作为更新系数，仅在符号与师生反馈一致时保留该系数，在 5 组 Qwen3 配置上提升数学平均 pass@1。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本方法，但其按 token 位置与符号一致性筛选监督信号、以基座差异刻画后训练所得偏好的做法，或可为 LVR 训练中的贡献分配、风格与任务必要内容区分提供可核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Reinforcing What the Student Has Learned: Teacher-Guided On-Policy Distillation

**abstract/A1：** On-policy distillation (OPD) improves language-model reasoning through dense teacher feedback on student-generated responses.We first observe an amplitude disparity in this feedback: on math reasoning responses, style-related positions exhibit 3.5 times the mean absolute teacher--student log-probability difference of mathematical positions.Scaling OPD updates by these discrepancies thus directs much of the update strength toward matching the teacher's expression. We instead take the student's own pretrained base as a reference: the student--base difference records the token preferences acquired during post-training and provides supervision that does not depend on the student's distance from the teacher. Building on this reference, we propose Student-Delta On-Policy Distillation (SD-OPD), which uses centered student--base differences as update coefficients, keeps each coefficient only when its sign agrees with centered teacher--student feedback, and recomputes the student signal every training round. Across 5 teacher--student configurations with Qwen3 students of 1.7B to 8B parameters, SD-OPD improves six-task mathematics average pass@1 (avg@16) over OPD by 7.76 to 10.37 percentage points. Code is available at https://github.com/aim-uofa/sd-opd.

</details>

<a id="paper-73"></a>

### 73. [ConvPE: Convolutional Position Embedding Improves Transformers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07749v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07749

**作者：** Zehao Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07749v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07749v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 ConvPE：对隐状态的低维投影施加轻量因果卷积，生成窗口化、内容相关的位置偏置并注入注意力分数，作为插件兼容 RoPE、注意力掩码与 KV 拼接，并在语言建模、长上下文扩展和视觉任务上评测。

**相关性简评：** 其内容相关、局部窗口式的注意力偏置可能可供核查 LVR 中位置、缓存与注意力结构如何影响 latent 状态相似度及长上下文证据使用，但原文不涉及推理状态生成或多步推理，只是方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ConvPE: Convolutional Position Embedding Improves Transformers

**abstract/A1：** ConvPE (Convolutional Position Embedding) improves both the performance and efficiency of Transformer models in language modeling, long-context extension, and vision. It injects a windowed, content-dependent bias into attention scores through a lightweight causal convolution over a low-dimensional projection of the hidden states. As a lightweight plug-in, ConvPE is compatible with RoPE, attention masks, and KV concatenation. It also supports high-dimensional position embeddings via high-dimensional convolutions. In a reproduction, ConvPE outperforms five 1D baselines and eight 2D baselines. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/wzhagi/convpe.

</details>

<a id="paper-74"></a>

### 74. [Foresee to Trade: Forecast-Augmented Policy Optimization for Portfolio Management](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07750v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07750

**作者：** Zeyu Liu、Xuanzhi Feng、Haoran Xu、Lai Sing Kwong、Yang Li、Hualei Zhang、Hao Chen、Jie Zhang、Xiaoyi Pang、Song Guo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07750v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07750v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 FRPO，在金融交易决策中联合学习表征：训练期用 Financial State Head 预测未来相对收益以提供资产层面监督，并以同路径分组相对优化比较账户轨迹，自适应门控按预测方差与梯度对齐调节辅助监督，该头仅在训练期使用。

**相关性简评：** 其「辅助预测头仅训练期存在、推理只保留编码器与策略」以及按预测方差和梯度对齐门控辅助损失的做法，可供核查 LVR 中如何在不增加推理成本的前提下为 latent 状态施加监督并调节多目标贡献。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Foresee to Trade: Forecast-Augmented Policy Optimization for Portfolio Management

**abstract/A1：** Online investment services must translate asset opportunities into investment outcomes for users' accounts, yet the same market opportunity can warrant different account actions. We study how supervision from future asset opportunities can strengthen account-level trading decisions and propose Forecast-Augmented Relative Policy Optimization (FRPO). FRPO separately supervises asset opportunities and account decisions through a jointly learned representation: a Financial State Head (FSH) learns future relative-return targets to provide asset-level opportunity supervision, while Same-Path Group-Relative Optimization compares trading trajectories under the same market path and initial account to provide account-level relative-outcome supervision. An Adaptive Gate coordinates auxiliary supervision using predictive variance and gradient alignment. FSH is used only during training; inference uses the encoder and policy for trading. Across DJIA30, TOPIX Core30, and three investment-style portfolios, ten-seed evaluations show that FRPO achieves the highest mean cumulative and annualized returns among the compared methods in all five settings. These results support strengthening investment agents' trading decisions through joint supervision from asset opportunities and trading outcomes.

</details>

<a id="paper-75"></a>

### 75. [BiGrounder: Bidirectional Training Unlocks the Potential of GUI Grounding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07765v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07765

**作者：** Dengyao Shi、Tianyu Fu、Fei Hu、Anyang Su、Hanning Wang、Shuo Wang、Minghui Wu、Chenxu Zhao、Zhen Lei

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07765v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07765v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究 GUI grounding，提出双向训练范式：SFT 阶段让模型由目标坐标反向生成操作描述，RL 阶段对同一目标在线采样语义一致的指令变体并重新定位，并用循环一致性门控将语义质量与定位成功绑定，以自我生成可刷新的强化轨迹。

**相关性简评：** 其「生成—回定位」循环一致性门控可能为 LVR 中自生成推理轨迹的筛选与奖励设计提供核查线索，但该工作只做坐标级 grounding，不涉及 latent 推理状态或视觉证据选择。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BiGrounder: Bidirectional Training Unlocks the Potential of GUI Grounding

**abstract/A1：** Graphical User Interface (GUI) grounding aims to map natural language instructions to precise operational coordinates, serving as a critical task for autonomous agent interaction. Current approaches formalize GUI grounding as predicting action-coordinate pairs from instructions, with end-to-end training supervised solely by final coordinates. Training datasets typically provide only a small number of descriptions per target coordinate, whereas real-world scenarios involve a much larger set of varied descriptions for the same target, which limits robustness to linguistic variation. While recent GUI grounding paradigms have begun employing reinforcement learning to enhance localization accuracy, models with limited exploratory capability before reinforcement yield constrained improvements even with identical training data during the reinforcement phase. To overcome these limitations, we propose a bidirectional reward paradigm that enables model self-reinforcement. During SFT, bidirectional training equips the model to generate action descriptions from target coordinates. During RL, the model samples semantically consistent instruction variations on-policy and grounds each variation back to its target. A cycle-consistency gate couples semantic quality to grounding success, turning the model's own instruction policy into a source of continually refreshed reinforcement trajectories. Our 4B-parameter model achieves competitive accuracy across five grounding benchmarks in our evaluation, indicating the effectiveness of the proposed training paradigm. https://github.com/Spell-shi/BiGrounder

**comment/C1：** Preprint, 22 pages, 10 figures, including appendix

</details>

<a id="paper-76"></a>

### 76. [Optimal Learning in Games with Arbitrary Delays: Best-of-Both-Worlds for Self-Play and Adversarial Opponents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07773v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07773

**作者：** Emmanuel Esposito、Andrew Jacobsen、Hao Qiu、Alexander Ryabchenko、Mengxiao Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07773v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07773v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究零和博弈中带任意未知延迟反馈的学习，提出 MAO-OMD 这一算法，在平滑博弈自对弈中取得快速率，同时保持对任意对手的 minimax 最优个体遗憾界。

**相关性简评：** 其用乐观镜像下降与多锚点处理「任意延迟、未完成梯度」的思路，可能为 LVR 中延迟验证信号下的轨迹训练与贡献分配提供算法线索，与序列 latent 状态更新的可迁移性有待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Optimal Learning in Games with Arbitrary Delays: Best-of-Both-Worlds for Self-Play and Adversarial Opponents

**abstract/A1：** We study learning in zero-sum games with delayed feedback. We introduce Multi-Anchor Optimistic Online Mirror Descent (MAO-OMD), a single algorithm which obtains optimal fast rates in self-play on smooth games, while simultaneously maintaining minimax optimal guarantees against arbitrary opponents. In particular, in smooth zero-sum games with arbitrary and unknown feedback delays, MAO-OMD obtains $O(σ\_{\\max}+1)$ social and individual regrets when used by both players, where $σ\_{\\max}$ denotes the maximum number of outstanding gradients, while also guaranteeing minimax optimal $O(\\sqrt{T+d\_{\\mathrm{tot}}})$ individual regret against arbitrary opponents, where $d\_{\\mathrm{tot}}$ is the total delay. Moreover, we show that these optimal rates cannot be obtained simultaneously using the standard black-box reduction to the undelayed setting. We also extend our results to multi-player games, and to games with player-dependent delay sequences.

</details>

<a id="paper-77"></a>

### 77. [PBPO: Prefix-Block Policy Optimization for Diffusion Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07783v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07783

**作者：** Katherine Tieu、Dongqi Fu、Si Zhang、Hanqing Zeng、Tianxin Wei、Chenglin Wei、Yinglong Xia、Hong Li、Jingrui He

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07783v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07783v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对 diffusion LLM 的块式解码，构造前缀-块状态并用单次模型评估给出因子化的一步块似然代理，提出 PBPO：在块级条件下对每个 token 分别做重要性加权与裁剪，并重采样加噪的 prompt 与前缀视图，同时分析了块乘积加权的梯度方差。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本 dLLM 的 RL 工作，但其「块级状态 + 一步代理似然 + 位置级信用分配与裁剪」以及加噪前缀重采样，可能为并行/扩散式推理状态更新的贡献分配与轨迹监督提供可核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PBPO: Prefix-Block Policy Optimization for Diffusion Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) commonly decode responses by proceeding autoregressively across blocks while iteratively denoising multiple tokens within each block. This structure makes block-level policy optimization a natural adaptation of autoregressive policy optimization. However, the exact likelihood of a completed block is intractable because it marginalizes over the intermediate states and reveal orders of the denoising process. To realize the block-level formulation, we construct a prefix-block state and use a single model evaluation to define a factorized one-step surrogate for the block likelihood. The resulting block surrogate ratio is a product of its token surrogate ratios, allowing variation at individual positions to propagate across the entire block before clipping. We therefore introduce Prefix-Block Policy Optimization (PBPO), which preserves block-level conditioning but applies surrogate importance weighting and clipping separately to each token. PBPO further diversifies repeated updates by resampling corrupted views of the prompt and revealed prefix, a design motivated by masked-dLLM pre-training. Under our analysis assumptions, block-product weighting amplifies the variance of gradient contributions relative to token-local weighting. Across two LLaDA-family initializations, six benchmarks, and three generation lengths, PBPO achieves competitive performance against existing dLLM RL methods.

</details>

<a id="paper-78"></a>

### 78. [CMDiff: Left Atrial Scar Segmentation from 3D LGE MRI Using Conditional Mask Diffusion Model: A Feasibility Study](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07814v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07814

**作者：** Jing Zhang、Bastien Bergere、Jonas Leite、Emilie Bollache、Mikaël Laredo、Alban Redheuil、Nadjia Kachenoura

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07814v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07814v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 CMDiff，用动态编码器条件的 3D 正弦 UNet 做条件掩码扩散，从 3D LGE MRI 分割左心房瘢痕，并在 60 例公开数据五折交叉验证中与确定性基线对比，结果技术可行但性能未提升。

**相关性简评：** 其「干净掩膜预测明显优于噪声预测」的负结果，可供核查扩散式迭代状态生成的参数化选择及其相对确定性单步预测的收益与成本，可能对扩散类 latent 推理状态生成有借鉴意义。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CMDiff: Left Atrial Scar Segmentation from 3D LGE MRI Using Conditional Mask Diffusion Model: A Feasibility Study

**abstract/A1：** Late gadolinium enhancement (LGE) cardiac MRI enables non-invasive assessment of left atrial (LA) scar, an imaging marker associated with atrial fibrillation ablation outcome. However, automatic scar segmentation is hindered by low contrast, the thin atrial wall, scarce labels, and extreme class imbalance. Conditional diffusion offers iterative mask generation, but its suitability for sparse 3D LA scar segmentation remains unclear. To address this gap, we propose Conditional Mask Diffusion (CMDiff), which predicts clean masks using a dynamic-encoder-conditioned 3D sinusoidal UNet. We compared CMDiff with a deterministic baseline on identical cases in five-fold cross-validation of 60 scar-labelled public LASCARQS subjects, with 48 training and 12 held-out cases per fold. CMDiff achieved a scar DSC of 0.47 $\\pm$ 0.08, HD95 of 12.89 $\\pm$ 6.39 mm, and ASD of 1.91 $\\pm$ 0.96 mm, compared with deterministic baseline values of 0.51 $\\pm$ 0.08, 10.86 $\\pm$ 5.39 mm, and 1.58 $\\pm$ 1.03 mm, respectively. Under the same implementation, clean-mask prediction performed substantially better than the noise-prediction configuration evaluated in this study. Thus, CMDiff was technically feasible but did not improve segmentation performance: DSC was lower, and both HD95 and ASD were higher. Direct mask diffusion therefore provides an analytical alternative, but not a competitive performance advance for this task. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/jizhang02/CMDiff.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages, 3 figures, 4 tables

</details>

### 79. [Generate-then-Rank: Learning Symbolic Goal Interpretation for Embodied Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07825v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07825

**作者：** Jisen Lei、Huizhi Liang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07825v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07825v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究具身智能体的目标解释，即把自然语言指令转成规划器可执行的符号目标，提出先用监督微调适配输出规范、再用自生成候选上的偏好排序（CORE）选出完整输出，并在 Embodied Agent Interface 家庭场景上评测多种开源 LLM。

**相关性简评：** 方法线索是「生成多候选 + 按自身错误学习偏好排序」，可能为 latent 推理中的候选筛选、验证与「何时停止生成」提供可核查借鉴，但工作本身不含视觉状态或 latent 表示。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Generate-then-Rank: Learning Symbolic Goal Interpretation for Embodied Agents

**abstract/A1：** Goal interpretation is to translate a natural language instruction into a formal symbolic goal that a planner can execute. It is critical for embodied agents because any error here propagates to every downstream planning and action step. Doing it well requires both generating suitable candidates and reliably selecting among them. We present a two-stage generate-then-rank approach to Goal Interpretation in the Embodied Agent Interface (EAI). First, supervised fine-tuning adapts a Large Language Model (LLM) to EAI's output convention. Second, Candidate-Output Ranking from Errors (CORE) learns preferences over complete outputs from the model's own generated alternatives. We conducted experiments on the household scenes of the Embodied Agent Interface Benchmark. Multiple open source LLMs including DeepSeek-V3 chat, DeepSeek-Reasoner, and Qwen2.5-7B and Qwen2.5-72B were evaluated. The proposed method outperformed the baseline models including LLMs with simple prompting, chain-of-thought, and knowledge graph based approaches.

**comment/C1：** 14 pages, 2 figures. Accepted at the 10th Euro-China Conference on Intelligent Data Analysis and Applications (ECC 2026)

</details>

<a id="paper-80"></a>

### 80. [Computation, not weight access: large language models as auditors of clinical prediction networks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07847v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07847

**作者：** Zhiyu Liu、Xinyu Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07847v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07847v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把「读权重」与「运行计算」拆成两种条件，让 GPT 系列模型审计模拟临床预测网络，检验它们在无工具时能否从数值权重中恢复植入关系、发现亚组标签偏差，并与仅暴露预测的黑箱访问及脚本基线对比。

**相关性简评：** 其「权重可见性并非关键、计算与提示诱导才决定成败」的结论，可供核查 LVR 中模型是否真正使用内部 latent 内容而非依赖外部旁路计算，以及黑箱/白箱访问差异对证据获取与评价设计的影响。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Computation, not weight access: large language models as auditors of clinical prediction networks

**abstract/A1：** Frontier large language models (LLMs) have been reported to read, edit and write the weights of small neural networks, raising the prospect of auditing clinical prediction models from their files alone. Public demonstrations, however, mixed reading raw weights with running code. We separated these conditions in internally pre-specified simulation experiments. Tanh networks of 105 to 38,401 weights were trained on simulated cohorts of patients with subarachnoid haemorrhage, each with a planted relationship or none, and one network was trained on labels that under-recorded deaths in a subgroup. GPT-6 Astra and four other GPT models answered single prompts under a default or an explicit system instruction, either with the weights in the prompt and no tools, or with a calculation-only tool that exposed the weights (white box) or only the network's predictions (black box). Without tools, Astra recovered planted relationships in 353-weight networks but became unreliable at 3,457 weights, and under the default instruction it declined 11 of 12 audit and repair tasks as unidentifiable; the explicit instruction removed these refusals. With the tool, it recovered the planted relationship in all 29 runs on networks of up to 38,401 weights, raised no false alarm in 15 runs on null networks, and found the biased subgroup in 6 of 6 runs. Black-box access performed as well as white-box access, and other GPT-6 models read as accurately. A well-designed script matched the comparator models on the audit and the best LLM repair. For auditing these networks, what mattered was computation and elicitation, not access to the weights.

**comment/C1：** 13 pages, 2 figures, 6 tables. Code, simulated networks, prompts and replies: https://github.com/Chth1z/llm-clinical-network-audit

</details>

<a id="paper-81"></a>

### 81. [OcTreeMamba: Topology-Aware Mamba for 3D Medical Segmentation via Octree Sparsification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07865v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07865

**作者：** Kaixiang Mai、Jiaqi Yu、Yuzhu Ji、Yiqun Zhang、Zhijie Wang、Haijun Zhang、Yiu-ming Cheung

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07865v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07865v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 OcTreeMamba：用分层门控与 Gumbel-Softmax 做解剖自适应的结构化稀疏区域选择，再由动态 Octree 组织多层次拓扑，并用双向 Tree-SSM 沿叶—父—根轨迹传播上下文，用于 3D 医学分割。

**相关性简评：** 其可微分层区域选择与按拓扑结构传播的状态更新，可能为 LVR 的视觉证据筛选和 latent 状态结构化组织提供借鉴线索；但原文属分割任务，未涉及推理、答案使用或停止决策，迁移潜力有待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** OcTreeMamba: Topology-Aware Mamba for 3D Medical Segmentation via Octree Sparsification

**abstract/A1：** Accurate segmentation of 3D anatomical structures is crucial to medical image analysis. Recently, Mamba-based approaches have achieved promising segmentation performance in capturing long-range dependencies with linear-time computational complexity. However, heuristic selective scanning strategies inherently disrupt the intrinsic spatial hierarchy and increase computational redundancy, ignoring the inherent sparsity of 3D anatomical structures. To this end, we propose OcTreeMamba, a hierarchical framework that incorporates Octree structures into SSM, achieving 3D topology-aware long-range context feature propagation and integration. Specifically, a Structure Learner performs anatomy-adaptive structured sparsification through hierarchical gating and Gumbel-Softmax sampling, enabling differentiable selection of anatomical regions. The selected regions are subsequently organized by a Dynamic Octree Builder into a multi-level topology that preserves their hierarchical connectivity. Based on the constructed Octree, a bidirectional Tree-SSM propagates and integrates contextual features along connectivity-aware leaf--parent--root and root--parent--leaf trajectories. Extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets show that our proposed OcTreeMamba achieves competitive performance \\wrt topological continuity and computational efficiency, particularly for recovering the complex structure on vascular datasets. The code is available at https://github.com/MikeMak123/OcTreeMamba.

**comment/C1：** 8 pages, 5 figures, accepted by IEEE BIBM 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-82"></a>

### 82. [FARR: Forecast-Aware Residual Refinement with Matched Endpoint Evidence for Long-Horizon Time-Series Forecasting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07870v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07870

**作者：** Yiyang Zhang、Yuli Zheng、Bin Li、Meimei Zhang、Qiang Sun

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07870v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07870v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 FARR：在标准条件下得到有效预测、在预设 null 条件下得到对照预测，以两者差值作为「端点证据」，再由共享残差精修器和逐步门控校正长时程时间序列预测误差。

**相关性简评：** 用有效条件与 null 条件的输出差来刻画辅助条件对预测的实际影响，并按预测步门控残差贡献，可供核查是否能迁移到 VLM 中视觉证据贡献分配与按推理步调节 latent 更新。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FARR: Forecast-Aware Residual Refinement with Matched Endpoint Evidence for Long-Horizon Time-Series Forecasting

**abstract/A1：** Large language models use auxiliary conditioning to incorporate additional information into time-series forecasts. However, auxiliary conditioning does not consistently improve accuracy and can increase forecasting error when its influence is poorly matched to the observed temporal context. We propose Forecast-Aware Residual Refinement (FARR), which learns to correct forecasts using endpoint evidence. FARR obtains a valid forecast under the standard condition and a null forecast under a predefined null condition. Their difference reveals how conditioning changes the prediction. A shared residual refiner combines this endpoint evidence with historical features to estimate the remaining error of the valid forecast. Horizon-wise gating then controls the contribution of the predicted residual to the final output. Experiments on four long-horizon forecasting benchmarks show that FARR achieves the best dataset-average MSE and MAE among the compared methods. Code will be released at https://github.com/blossomrichzyy-star/farr-forecast-aware-residual-refinement.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 3 figures, 4 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-83"></a>

### 83. [InnerLogic-Surg: An Open Surgical Foundation Model via Continual Pretraining](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07883v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07883

**作者：** Kun Yuan、Hao Ding、Yiqing Shen、Gasper Podobnik、Benjamin Killeen、Tito Porras、Axel Krieger、Mathias Unberath

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07883v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07883v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文构建 InnerLogic-Surg 手术视觉编码器族，在约 2900 小时网络手术视频上沿 DINOv3 流程做持续预训练，并加入可学习的特征上采样模块以低 token 代价支持高分辨率稠密预测，在 20 项手术任务线性探针上验证迁移性。

**相关性简评：** 其「低 token 成本下恢复高分辨率稠密特征」的上采样模块，可能为 LVR 中在有限视觉 token 预算下保留细粒度视觉证据的内部表示设计提供可供核查的线索，但领域为手术图像、不涉及推理状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** InnerLogic-Surg: An Open Surgical Foundation Model via Continual Pretraining

**abstract/A1：** Vision foundation models are the backbone of modern surgical image analysis, yet most surgical systems still rely on encoders pretrained on natural images or on specialist models trained from scratch on comparatively small datasets. Pretraining encoders on large amounts of surgery-specific data has the potential to generally enhance the performance of surgical image analysis methods simply by providing stronger backbones. Here we present InnerLogic-Surg, an open family of surgical vision encoders (21M--300M parameters) obtained by continually pretraining a transformer-based image encoder on millions of surgical images curated from 11,965 internet-sourced surgical videos spanning ~2,900 hours and more than fifteen procedure types. Our training recipe inherits DINOv3's pretraining stage to preserve representation ability after surgical adaptation, and additionally applies a learned feature-upsampling module for high-resolution dense prediction at low token cost. Across a benchmark covering workflow, instrument and anatomy recognition, segmentation, detection, and monocular depth, under the same architecture, the largest InnerLogic-Surg model achieves the best linear-probe performance in all 20 surgical tasks. We demonstrate that surgical continual pretraining yields transferable representations that generalize across procedures, anatomical regions, and task types, showing our surgical image encoders as a promising and scalable alternative to the procedure-specific representation learning approaches that have traditionally dominated surgical computer vision.

</details>

<a id="paper-84"></a>

### 84. [When Bigger Becomes Brittle: Why Compression Hurts More as Language Models Scale](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07891v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07891

**作者：** Gautam Gupta、Dikshant Kukreja、Unique Kumar、Avni Mittal、Deepanshu Dabas、Kshitij Sah、Erik Cambria、Rajiv Ratn Shah、Zhengkui Wang、Avinash Anand

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07891v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07891v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究量化与剪枝为何在模型变大时更伤性能：以 in-context retrieval 所依赖的一小群 attention heads 为例，把消融造成的期望损失精确分解为被网络其余部分恢复的部分（并归因到具体组件）与最终传导到输出的部分，再跨模型规模比较二者。

**相关性简评：** 其「损伤—补偿」精确分解与组件归因为分析 LVR 中答案是否真正使用 latent/视觉证据提供了可核查的线索：或可把被内部补偿掩盖的损伤与真正影响输出的损失分开，用于诊断视觉旁路与贡献分配，但迁移到多模态推理的有效性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Bigger Becomes Brittle: Why Compression Hurts More as Language Models Scale

**abstract/A1：** Quantization and pruning reduce the cost of deploying language models, but their effects are usually reported as benchmark scores. Such scores record only the loss that remains at the output. Inside the model, compression damages some components more than others, and the rest of the network can partly compensate. The observed loss therefore reflects both the damage and this compensation, which benchmark scores cannot separate. How the two combine, and whether their balance changes with model size, remains unclear. We study this compensation for in-context retrieval, a capability carried by a small, identifiable set of attention heads. To separate the two, the expected loss from ablating these heads is decomposed exactly into the part restored by the rest of the network, attributed to the components responsible, and the part that reaches the output. This decomposition is then compared across model sizes. For damage of comparable size, smaller models restore most of the loss, whereas larger models retain most of it, because the compensating responses offset less of it as models grow. Compressing the heads produces the same pattern. Compression that is nearly harmless in small models can therefore be costly in large ones.

</details>

<a id="paper-85"></a>

### 85. [DISCERN: Distributional Synthetic Replay for Retain-Free Incremental Machine Unlearning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07892v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07892

**作者：** Nicolás Segura-Kunsagi、Juan Luis Suárez-Díaz、Iván Sevillano-García、Francisco Herrera

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07892v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07892v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 DISCERN，在无保留集条件下用每类参数分布（Gaussian、Laplace、Student's t 及 von Mises–Fisher、Fisher–Bingham）采样的合成嵌入替代真实数据，实现增量类别遗忘，并在多个骨干上验证其稳定性。

**相关性简评：** 其用统计检验为各类嵌入选择欧氏或方向性分布、并强调 transformer 嵌入近似位于超球面的做法，可能可供核查 LVR 内部状态的表示几何与 latent 相似度结构；论文本身不研究视觉推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DISCERN: Distributional Synthetic Replay for Retain-Free Incremental Machine Unlearning

**abstract/A1：** Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of specific training data from a trained model without retraining from scratch. In real deployments, however, deletion requests arrive over time, calling for incremental class unlearning (ICU): classes are removed sequentially, previously forgotten knowledge must remain forgotten, and the remaining classes must be preserved. We present DIstributional SynthetiC rEplay for Retain-free iNcremental unlearning (DISCERN), an intervention-based class unlearning method that replaces access to the retain set with synthetic embeddings sampled from per-class parametric distributions. Unlike prototype-based replay, which assumes an isotropic Gaussian around each class centroid, DISCERN fits Euclidean (Gaussian, Laplace, Student's $t$) and directional (von Mises--Fisher, Fisher--Bingham) families to each class and selects among them through statistical tests and, when none of them accepts, cross-validated log-likelihood. This design is motivated by the architecture-dependent latent geometry: transformer embeddings lie approximately on a hypersphere and concentrate around class directions, unlike those of convolutional networks. We evaluate DISCERN against seven retain-free baselines on MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100 with two convolutional and two transformer backbones, under single-class, multi-class, and incremental unlearning. DISCERN is competitive when classes are removed at once and is the most stable method in ICU on transformers: on ViT-B/16, after 20 sequential deletions it retains a harmonic mean of $0.797$, whereas the strongest method in the simultaneous setting drops from $0.918$ to $0.048$. A paired ablation shows that per-class distribution selection substantially reduces the resurfacing of previously forgotten classes, acting as a stabilizer of accumulated forgetting rather than a uniform improvement.

**comment/C1：** 32 pages, 10 figures

</details>

### 86. [Persistent Retrieval for Conversational Agents: A Study of Retrieval Efficiency](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07899v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07899

**作者：** Karthik Ravula、Sathwik Kowtha、Rishabh Verma、Kala Mehak Jain、Gunjit Mittal

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.IR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07899v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07899v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文比较三种对话检索架构——主 agent 内置技能检索、非持久检索子 agent、以及跨轮维护检索历史的持久检索子 agent——在企业多轮问答上衡量 token 消耗、估计 API 成本与响应延迟，其中持久设计把子 agent 从「合成答案」转为「维护检索状态并回传证据」。

**相关性简评：** 其「跨轮维护检索状态、直接回传可供主 agent 评估的原始证据」及配套成本核算，可能为 LVR 中证据筛选与重复取证的缓存/状态管理提供借鉴线索，但纯文本检索与 latent 视觉状态的对应关系尚需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Persistent Retrieval for Conversational Agents: A Study of Retrieval Efficiency

**abstract/A1：** Conversational agents can retrieve information directly or delegate retrieval to a subagent. This design choice affects how retrieval history is maintained, what context each model call receives, and what information must be re-retrieved in follow-up questions. We compare three designs: skill-based retrieval in the main agent, a non-persistent retrieval subagent that generates a findings response, and our persistent retrieval subagent, which maintains retrieval history across turns and returns retrieved evidence directly to the main agent. Our approach also allows the main agent to request corrections from the same subagent. We evaluate the three designs on enterprise questions posed independently, within shared conversations, and as dependent follow-ups. Across five multi-turn settings, our approach uses 43.3% fewer recorded agent-model tokens and incurs 45.0% lower estimated API cost than skill-based retrieval, while reducing average response time by 9.1%. Compared with non-persistent retrieval, it reduces response time by 15.6 seconds per question, while using 65.1% more tokens and incurring 29.1% higher estimated API cost. Our approach shifts the retrieval subagent from producing synthesized responses to maintaining retrieval state and supplying evidence that the main agent can directly evaluate and use for answer generation.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages, 4 figures, 6 tables

</details>

### 87. [GuidedWAM: Training-Free Energy Guidance for World Action Models in Autonomous Driving](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07912v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07912

**作者：** Xiyan Su、Finn Rasmus Schäfer、Hojun Lim、Frank Diermeyer、Johannes Betz、Markus Lienkamp

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07912v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07912v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 GuidedWAM：在冻结的自动驾驶 world action model 采样阶段，对采样器的中间干净动作估计施加可微能量约束，配合引导调度、逐能量归一化与速度有界修正，无需重训即可注入新驾驶目标。

**相关性简评：** 其「仅在采样期通过能量约束干预中间状态、只做前向访问」的机制，可能为 LVR 中免训练地引导 latent 推理状态或注入推理目标提供方法线索，但跨领域迁移需核查，故列 P2。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GuidedWAM: Training-Free Energy Guidance for World Action Models in Autonomous Driving

**abstract/A1：** World action models (WAMs) for autonomous driving couple the driving policy and the visual world model in a single generative network whose objectives are fixed post-training. Conditioning on a new driving objective requires retraining. We study energy guidance as a general mechanism and present GuidedWAM, a model-agnostic framework for injecting driving objectives into frozen WAMs at sampling time. Our framework constructs each objective as differentiable energies on the sampler's intermediate clean-action estimate, which needs only forward access to the frozen model, and is stabilized by sampling-time guidance scheduling, per-energy normalization, and velocity-bounded correction. On the NAVSIM v2 hard-split, guidance improves all three frozen, publicly released WAMs by 1.8 to 3.6 EPDMS, averaged over three sampler seeds, and adds less than 10% to the sampling time per step. These results position energy guidance as a practical post-training control channel for WAMs.

</details>

### 88. [Adaptive Semantic Branching: Disagreement-Localized Cross-Trajectory Regeneration for Reasoning Candidate Expansion](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07918v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07918

**作者：** Jeong-Yoon Kim、Tae-Yoon Lee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07918v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07918v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Adaptive Semantic Branching：按最终答案对独立采样的文本推理轨迹分组，用单调动态规划对齐步骤，以「持续分歧」目标定位路径分叉点，再结合竞争轨迹的推理片段重新生成候选，并按答案簇结构分配至多三次额外生成；在 MATH 上以 oracle 覆盖率提升验证。该方法免训练。

**相关性简评：** 轨迹对齐与分歧点定位、分支式候选扩展、按簇多样性分配生成预算，可为 latent 推理轨迹的分支搜索、贡献分配与自适应计算/停止决策提供可核查的方法线索；但材料仅涉及纯文本 MATH，无视觉证据或内部状态，与 LVR 的联系需读原文判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Adaptive Semantic Branching: Disagreement-Localized Cross-Trajectory Regeneration for Reasoning Candidate Expansion

**abstract/A1：** Reasoning trajectories generated for the same problem often arrive at conflicting answers. These disagreements reveal alternative intermediate decisions that can be reused to explore new solutions. We introduce Adaptive Semantic Branching (ASB), a training-free inference procedure that turns cross-trajectory disagreement into targeted candidate generation. ASB first groups independently sampled trajectories by extracted final answer, selects a representative from each group, and aligns their reasoning steps with a monotonic dynamic program. A persistent-divergence objective identifies the point at which initially similar reasoning paths develop different continuations. For each selected directed pair, ASB retains the source trajectory's textual prefix, extracts a short reasoning window from a competing donor trajectory, and generates a fresh solution conditioned on both. A diversity-aware policy allocates up to three additional generations according to the answer-cluster structure. In a 500-problem MATH evaluation with Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, ASB increases oracle candidate coverage from 445/500 (89.0%) with seven initial samples to 455/500 (91.0%), recovering correct candidates for 10 of the 55 initially uncovered problems. The method uses 576 additional generation calls across the evaluation set, averaging 8.152 calls per problem. We provide a precise specification of the branching operator, alignment objective, generation policy, and experimental configuration.

</details>
