### 89. [StitchEdit: Stitching Depth Priors into Image Editors via Per-Layer Gradient Probing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07922v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07922

**作者：** Qin Guo、Dan Xu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07922v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07922v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对文本引导图像编辑中的空间与几何编辑，通过逐层梯度探测发现编辑目标与深度目标在浅层兼容、在深层冲突，据此提出 StitchEdit：先用轻量深度去噪 adapter 激活骨干的几何能力，再以探测引导的逐层学习率调度微调，并构建包含 150 个几何依赖编辑的 StitchEditBench。

**相关性简评：** 逐层梯度探测定位目标兼容与冲突层、并据此分配逐层学习率的做法，可能可供核查 LVR 训练中「保留视觉细节」与「推理微调」之间的贡献分配与干扰问题，但论文本身面向图像编辑与深度先验，不涉及 latent 推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** StitchEdit: Stitching Depth Priors into Image Editors via Per-Layer Gradient Probing

**abstract/A1：** Text-guided image editing with diffusion models struggles with spatial edits (e.g. manipulating objects, changing viewpoints, manipulating occlusions) that depend on the 3D structure understanding of a scene. Marigold and follow-up work demonstrate that a pretrained diffusion backbone already harbors strong geometric priors that can be activated for depth estimation, yet how to redirect these priors toward editing remains unresolved. Through per-layer gradient probing, we discover that the editing and depth objectives are compatible in early transformer layers but actively conflict in late layers, explaining why naive depth-conditioned editing either ignores geometry or degrades visual quality. We present StitchEdit, which first activates the backbone's geometric capability via a lightweight depth-denoising adapter, then selectively fine-tunes this adapter for editing using a probe-guided per-layer learning rate schedule that preserves depth knowledge where it helps and prunes it where it conflicts. On the proposed StitchEditBench, a benchmark of 150 geometry-dependent edits spanning spatial transformation, occlusion manipulation, and viewpoint change, StitchEdit consistently surpasses both instruction-based and depth-conditioned baselines on perceptual quality and geometric fidelity.

</details>

<a id="paper-90"></a>

### 90. [AttnSR: Attention Step Reward for Long-Horizon Agentic RL](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07932v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07932

**作者：** Mengsong Wu、Tianchen Zhou、Zhaochen Su、Zhenhua Liu、Zhe Cui、Aimin Zhou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07932v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07932v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 AttnSR：在长程 LLM agent 的 RL 训练中，训练回放时回溯策略模型的 attention 图来估计各中间步对最终回答的相关性，并以此做步级奖励分配，无需额外奖励模型或 rollout；在 ALFWorld、ScienceWorld 上优于 PPO 基线。

**相关性简评：** 其用 attention 图回溯估计步级贡献的思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中哪些视觉或 latent 推理步真正影响最终答案这一贡献分配与监督问题，但该工作面向纯文本 agent 任务，能否迁移到多模态内部状态尚不确定。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AttnSR: Attention Step Reward for Long-Horizon Agentic RL

**abstract/A1：** Long-horizon LLM agents make multi-step decisions, yet outcome-reward-only reinforcement learning provides little direct supervision for individual steps. We introduce AttnSR, a step-level reward augmentation method that derives reward allocations from policy attention extracted during training replay, without a separately trained reward model or additional rollouts. AttnSR traces backward through the policy model attention graph to estimate the relevance of earlier steps to the final response. The terminal step, measured relative to a running baseline, determines whether these rewards reinforce or penalize the selected steps. In a controlled Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct study, AttnSR consistently outperforms the PPO baseline across all evaluated checkpoints on ALFWorld, raising final held-out success from 91.2% to 97.1%. The ablation experiment shows that the benefit depends on how intermediate rewards are assigned, rather than simple accumulation of step rewards over time. AttnSR also improves ScienceWorld at every checkpoint while cutting environment interactions by 29.9%. These results support policy attention as a practical prior for step-level reward allocation in long-horizon agentic RL. Code is available at https://github.com/fairyshine/AttnSR .

**comment/C1：** 20 pages, 4 figures, including appendices. Code: https://github.com/fairyshine/AttnSR

</details>

<a id="paper-91"></a>

### 91. [The Blindfolded Controller: Decision Model-Guided Training for Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07934v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07934

**作者：** Muhammad Ibrahim Khan、Aniket Dixit、Faizan Ahmed、James Brusey

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07934v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07934v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 DMGT：用决策模型（DM）依据环境与任务描述产生的行为来初始化强化学习策略，使智能体从最初几个回合即可获得奖励，再由 RL 继续改进并超越 DM，在七个稀疏奖励任务上验证并给出任务描述指南。

**相关性简评：** 其「先用任务描述条件化的外部模型提供初始策略、再用奖励信号继续校正」的结构，可能可供核查 LVR 中以教师轨迹初始化 latent 推理策略、再以结果奖励改进的训练安排；但该工作无视觉输入与推理期 latent 状态，迁移线索需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Blindfolded Controller: Decision Model-Guided Training for Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement learning (RL) provides a framework for continuous improvement through interaction with an environment. However, RL agents struggle against tasks that offer exploration challenges and sparse rewards, because learning efficient policies from scratch is slow and sample-inefficient. In contrast, decision models (DMs), given a description of the environment and task, can perform well in sparse-reward tasks, but, as they do not learn from reward, they are not able to improve towards the optimal solution. In this paper, we present Decision Model-Guided Training (DMGT), a technique that uses a DM with a description of the environment and task to initialise an RL policy, combining the DM's ability to act without training with RL's ability to improve from reward. Instead of exploring from scratch, the agent reaches the goal and receives reward from its first episodes, and RL then improves its policy beyond what the DM achieves. We demonstrate through experiments on seven tasks that our approach outperforms RL in all seven and DM in five. In addition, we provide comprehensive guidelines, derived from ablation studies, for describing an environment and task to a DM.

**comment/C1：** 19 pages, 6 figures, 6 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-92"></a>

### 92. [WAM-Sim0.1: World Anchored Memory Meets Online RL for Consistent and Realistic Driving Simulation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07939v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07939

**作者：** Jiahao Cui、Jiaye Li、Wenhao Zhang、Yaoyi Li、Shan Luan、Quanhui Tang、Jianhua Han、Lin Cheng、Hang Xu、Siyu Zhu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07939v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07939v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 WAM-Sim 自动驾驶世界模型，以分层持久记忆（姿态对齐结构 token、只读场景 token、一次性写入的实例 token）锚定自回归 rollout，并用在线 flow-matching RL 结合层级视觉奖励与 VLM 物理评判做后训练，以缓解长时程的结构漂移、身份漂移与质量衰减。

**相关性简评：** 其「持久分层记忆 + 在线 RL 奖励」可能为 LVR 的长程 latent 状态维持、语义坍缩抑制与轨迹监督设计提供核查线索，但属视频生成域，是否可迁移至 VLM 推理状态尚需阅读正文确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** WAM-Sim0.1: World Anchored Memory Meets Online RL for Consistent and Realistic Driving Simulation

**abstract/A1：** Autoregressive driving world models systematically suffer from structural drift, identity drift, and quality decay over long rollout horizons due to transient conditioning signals that lack persistent state. We present WAM-Sim, a driving world model anchored by a persistent hierarchical memory and optimized via online flow-matching RL. WAM-Sim structures persistent context across three temporal and semantic scales: pose-aligned structural tokens encoding persistent road topology, read-only scene tokens supplying geolocated background appearance, and write-once instance tokens preserving individual traffic participant identities across occlusions and chunk boundaries. To restore visual sharpness and physical plausibility degraded by multi-scale conditioning, we introduce an online post-training framework that optimizes self-generated rollouts via flow-matching RL guided by hierarchy-aligned visual rewards and a vision-language model physical judge. The invariant memory empirically confines policy optimization within the anchored context, helping suppress semantic collapse and reward hacking, while online RL systematically enforces physical commonsense and fine-grained visual fidelity. On the nuScenes benchmark, WAM-Sim achieves a superior generation fidelity (FID=3.47, FVD=25.72) and strong downstream perception performance (24.16 mAP, 71.23 road mIoU). On minute-scale rollouts of self-collected data, WAM-Sim substantially mitigates structural and identity drift, preserving high scene similarity and subject identity coherence across extended horizons. Project page: https://fudan-generative-vision.github.io/WAM-Sim.

</details>

<a id="paper-93"></a>

### 93. [Circe: Training-Free LLM Self-Evaluation via Component Interaction Consistency](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07948v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07948

**作者：** Shuyi Miao、Zheng Li、Xu Xie、Linrui Xu、Fei Shen、Xiaoyu Du、Tat-Seng Chua

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07948v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07948v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出免训练的自评方法 Circe，用「组件交互一致性」（早层注意力更新与后层注意力引起的 FFN 输出变化的方向对齐）、隐状态轨迹的表示几何特征和受限似然置信度融合成正确性评分，在五个 LLM、六个基准上优于现有无参考基线。

**相关性简评：** 其按层刻画隐状态轨迹几何与注意力—FFN 交互方向一致性，可能为判断 latent 状态是否真正承载任务相关信息、以及基于内部信号的停止决策提供可核查的度量线索，但研究对象是文本自评，需核查迁移到多模态视觉证据时的有效性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Circe: Training-Free LLM Self-Evaluation via Component Interaction Consistency

**abstract/A1：** As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in real-world applications, evaluating the correctness of their responses is crucial for reliable use. However, such evaluation is challenging because reference answers are rarely available in practice. Existing reference-free methods mainly rely on output signals, which may assign high scores to incorrect responses and thus do not necessarily reflect correctness. This motivates exploring the model's internal computation for complementary correctness signals. Recent studies exploit internal states, but they mainly analyze hidden-state trajectories across layers, which record only the combined updates of attention and feed-forward networks (FFNs) and overlook how these components interact. We therefore propose Circe, a training-free self-evaluation method that assesses a response using both internal and external signals, without reference answers or repeated sampling. For internal signals, we first introduce component interaction consistency (CIC), which measures the directional alignment between attention updates in earlier layers and attention-induced FFN output changes in later layers. We further incorporate representation geometry (RG) to characterize normalized displacement and angular changes along layerwise hidden-state trajectories. For the external signal, we convert the mean negative log-likelihood of generated tokens into a bounded likelihood confidence (LC) score. Finally, we fuse CIC, RG, and LC into a unified correctness score. Experiments on five LLMs and six benchmarks across four domains show that our method distinguishes correct from incorrect responses more effectively than existing baselines, and generalizes to multilingual, multimodal, and open-world settings.

</details>

<a id="paper-94"></a>

### 94. [Invariance before structure: two modes of emergent compression in representation learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07952v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07952

**作者：** Hardik Rajpal、Dan Goodman

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.NE、q-bio.NC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07952v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07952v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 EARL 框架，区分表征学习中两种涌现压缩：状态空间压缩（表征对自身未来状态的预测力超出单个单元）与参数空间压缩（不同参数的网络收敛到同一表征），并用 Fisher 敏感度和集成层面的信息论指标度量；在一个带训练瓶颈的循环 reservoir 中发现前者比后者晚约 30 个 epoch 出现，且仅在任务时序结构被学到时上升。

**相关性简评：** 其区分「跨种子不变」与「状态可预测性」的度量，可能为核查 LVR 中 latent 状态是否真正承载任务必要的时序/结构信息、而非仅由训练动力学决定的稳定偏置提供工具；但实验规模小且非视觉任务，迁移性需人工核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Invariance before structure: two modes of emergent compression in representation learning

**abstract/A1：** Learned representations show striking invariance across biological and artificial neural networks, with different substrates and variations driven by drift, heterogeneity and initialisation. To understand this invariance, we propose Emergent Adaptive Representation Learning (EARL), a framework that distinguishes two modes of emergence in learned representations: state-space compression, where a learned representation gains predictive power over its own future beyond what the network's units can predict individually, and parameter-space compression, where networks with different micro-level parameters converge to the same representation. We formalise both as Fisher sensitivities and introduce ensemble-level information-theoretic measures for each. In a recurrent reservoir with a trained bottleneck, parameter-space compression rises within the first few epochs of training and partly independently of the task, whereas state-space compression rises about 30 epochs later and only when the network begins to learn the temporal structure of the task. These results suggest that invariance to the substrate and task-specific emergent structure are acquired in distinct phases of learning.

**comment/C1：** Extended abstract, UniReps workshop at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-95"></a>

### 95. [CoRD: Coupled Rollouts Fill the Blind Spot of On-Policy Distillation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07959v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07959

**作者：** Guhan Chen、Bohan Li、Jun Rao、Hejin Wang、Songtao Tian、Yexin Xie、Xiaojun Meng、Peng Yuan、Jiansheng Wei、Xuebo Liu、Zixiong Yu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07959v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07959v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CoRD：保留 on-policy 蒸馏损失，仅将训练状态的分布通过 student 与 teacher 的几何插值及 speculative decoding 的极大耦合推向教师，使干预尽量稀疏并随学生变好而减少，在五个数学基准上提升 pass@1 与 pass@8。

**相关性简评：** 其「学生自采样状态覆盖不到教师可达状态」的诊断与按需替换 token 的轨迹构造，或可供核查 LVR 中 latent 轨迹训练的状态分布覆盖与监督分配问题；但该工作为纯文本蒸馏，与视觉证据或 latent 推理状态无直接联系，迁移性需进一步判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CoRD: Coupled Rollouts Fill the Blind Spot of On-Policy Distillation

**abstract/A1：** On-policy distillation trains a student on its own rollouts, so states the teacher would reach but the student does not are seldom supervised. We introduce CoRD (coupled-rollout distillation), which keeps the on-policy loss and moves only the distribution of training states toward the teacher, drawing rollouts from a geometric interpolation of student and teacher through the maximal coupling of speculative decoding. The coupling keeps the student's token wherever the two distributions agree and otherwise substitutes a token from the part of the target the student underweights, so interventions are as rare as any exact sampler allows and recede as the student learns. Across six base students from two model families (Qwen3 and Qwen3.5, 0.6B to 4B), CoRD raises average pass@1 over on-policy distillation by 0.9 to 2.0 points and pass@8 by 1.2 to 3.4 points on five public math benchmarks at equal steps and data. On frozen teacher-reached states, a 4B student trained with CoRD reaches in 16 steps a gap closure that on-policy distillation does not reach in 80, and substitution rules intervening at least as often in other directions fall short of CoRD.

</details>

<a id="paper-96"></a>

### 96. [Learning Dynamics of On-Policy Distillation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08016v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08016

**作者：** Guan Zhe Hong、Sumeet Ramesh Motwani、Liang Chen、Yangchen Pan、Junchi Yu、Jialin Yu、Philip Torr、Yi Ren

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08016v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08016v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文对语言模型 on-policy distillation 的学习动力学进行机制分析，将其拆为蒸馏信号转化为参数更新、以及更新经广播改变各前缀预测并重塑 rollout 分布两个阶段，并考察离线蒸馏的预备作用。

**相关性简评：** 其关于教师监督、学生几何与 rollout 分布反馈的分析，可能为 LVR 轨迹训练中的监督分配和 on-policy 状态分布漂移提供核查线索，但不涉及视觉信息或推理期 latent 状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning Dynamics of On-Policy Distillation

**abstract/A1：** On-policy distillation (OPD) is emerging as an important paradigm for language-model post-training, combining dense teacher supervision with training on student-generated trajectories. This creates a feedback loop where each update changes the student and thereby the training distribution that generates subsequent updates. Yet, how this feedback shapes learning and generalization remains poorly understood. We conduct a mechanistic analysis of OPD's learning dynamics, distinguishing the two feedback loop stages: conversion of the distillation signal into parameter updates, and broadcast through which these updates change predictions across prefixes and reshape the rollout distribution. This framework helps us understand both limited student responsiveness despite substantial teacher-student disagreement, and degeneration when updates that improve local teacher agreement also increase the probability of unsuccessful continuations, exposing the student to unreliable supervision in subsequent rollouts. Finally, we show that offline distillation can prepare a student for OPD without significantly improving immediate held-out accuracy. While OPD alone may degrade generation, it can yield substantial accuracy gains after sufficient offline preparation. Together, our results connect teacher supervision, student geometry, and the evolving rollout distribution in determining the learning dynamics of OPD and its effects on downstream performance.

</details>

<a id="paper-97"></a>

### 97. [Can an LLM Actually Reason and Program about Neural Network's Weights or is it just memorization on steroids? Exploring "Weight-Space Programming"](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08070v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08070

**作者：** Nazmus Sakib Ahmed

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08070v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08070v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文检验 Claude Opus 5.5 能否直接读取小型 CNN 的原始权重，发现其表现更像局部算术加数据集先验，而非追踪权重流，并提出相应审计协议。

**相关性简评：** 其标签置换、分层追踪与局部追踪核验的审计思路，可供核查 LVR 中答案是否真正使用 latent 视觉内容而非数据集先验；这属于方法类比，迁移有效性仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Can an LLM Actually Reason and Program about Neural Network's Weights or is it just memorization on steroids? Exploring "Weight-Space Programming"

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) have recently been reported to read and write the raw weights of neural networks directly, a capability termed weight-space programming. We ask whether the reading side of this capability reflects reasoning about weight flows or a combination of local arithmetic at the network's edges and memorized priors over public datasets. We test Claude Opus 5.5 on small convolutional networks of about 125,000 parameters trained on five image datasets, providing every weight as plain text with no tools, no forward passes, and no dataset or class names. The model performs accurate local calculations: batch-normalization statistics lead it to infer about three optimizer steps on networks stopped after three updates, and output-layer sign patterns recover the vehicle/animal split of CIFAR-10. The sessions report reading the first and last layers closely and sampling the middle, and several claims built on partial tracing fail verification, although we did not run a direct forward-pass test. Semantic judgments lean on familiar datasets: the model names CIFAR-10 for CIFAR-10 networks but also for all three fast-food networks, relabels KMNIST as MNIST, reads a 99.57%-accurate SAT-6 network as CIFAR-10 animal photos, and calls "frog" the output favored by a feature that fires most on trees. Permuting CIFAR-10's labels preserves the output split at the new indices, but every class pair the model interprets is then wrong, indicating reliance on remembered label order. These are exploratory results from mostly single runs. We conclude that zero-shot weight reading by this model is better described as local arithmetic plus dataset priors than as weight-flow tracing, and we provide a protocol for auditing future weight-space claims.

</details>

<a id="paper-98"></a>

### 98. [Optimal Competitive Ratios for Chasing Convex Functions with Quadratic Growth](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08078v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08078

**作者：** Linzhe He、Sifan Yang、Wei Jiang、Lijun Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08078v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08078v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究凸函数追逐在线博弈，提出 Clipped Proximal Descent（近端平衡步加朝当前最优解的有界修正），并对 Regularized Online Balanced Descent 给出紧致竞争比分析，在欧氏与平方欧氏移动代价下分别得到 O(κ^{1/3}) 与维度无关的最优竞争比及匹配下界。

**相关性简评：** 其「保持当前状态与移向最优解之间的正则化折中」可作为 latent 状态更新规则的方法线索，竞争比则可能为 THINK/LOOK/STOP 这类动态计算策略提供最坏情况成本评价思路；与视觉推理的映射仍需核查，属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Optimal Competitive Ratios for Chasing Convex Functions with Quadratic Growth

**abstract/A1：** Chasing convex function has attracted much attention in recent years, which can be formulated as a game between a learner and an adversary. In each round, the adversary reveals a convex function, and the learner selects a decision and incurs a hitting cost and a movement cost for changing its decision. The performance of the learner is measured by the \\emph{competitive ratio}, which compares its cumulative cost with that of an optimal offline decision sequence. For Euclidean movement, existing upper and lower bounds for $κ$-well-conditioned functions exhibit a polynomial gap. For squared Euclidean movement, dimension-free competitive guarantees have been established under quadratic growth, but the optimal competitive ratio remains unclear. In this paper, we establish optimal dimension-free competitive ratios for convex function chasing under two types of movement costs. For Euclidean movement, we propose Clipped Proximal Descent (CPD), which combines a balanced proximal step with a clipped correction toward the current minimizer. CPD achieves an $O(κ^{1/3})$ competitive ratio for smooth convex functions satisfying quadratic growth, improving the previous $O(\\sqrtκ)$ upper bound and matching the existing $Ω(κ^{1/3})$ lower bound. For squared Euclidean movement, we establish the exact optimal dimension-free competitive ratio for convex functions satisfying quadratic growth. Our result is based on a new tight competitive analysis of Regularized Online Balanced Descent (R-OBD) and a matching lower bound against randomized algorithms.

</details>

<a id="paper-99"></a>

### 99. [Spatiotemporal Dynamics-Aware Industrial Anomaly Detection and Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08080v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08080

**作者：** S-Wei Chen、Siangyu Yang、Yi-Chen Jan、Chun-Yi Lee、Shao-Yuan Lo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08080v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08080v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文面向工业异常检测中依赖运动而非静态外观的缺陷，提出 MUSTARD 框架，通过光流引导的关键帧选择与增强模态为 MLLM 补充细粒度时空动态信息，并以少量正常参考样本做对比式推理，无需模型更新。

**相关性简评：** 其「按运动显著性挑帧并送入 MLLM」的做法可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选与有限 token 下细节保留提供可供核查的线索，但论文不涉及 latent 推理状态或答案使用内部表示的机制，联系主要在输入侧证据构造。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Spatiotemporal Dynamics-Aware Industrial Anomaly Detection and Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Industrial Anomaly Detection (IAD) is a long-established problem that aims to detect defects in industrial products during manufacturing for quality control. Most existing studies focus on static visual patterns as the basis for detection. However, in real manufacturing environments, many anomalies are dynamics-based, such as irregular movements that cannot be perceived from static cues alone. We observe that even recent Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM)-based approaches fall short in identifying such dynamic anomalies due to limited temporal modeling capability. To address this, we introduce MUSTARD: MUltimodal SpatioTemporal Anomaly Reasoning and Detection, a framework that equips MLLMs with fine-grained spatiotemporal dynamics awareness for detecting and reasoning about dynamics-based defects. MUSTARD introduces optical-flow-guided keyframe selection and augmented modalities to capture critical motion irregularities through local motion patterns and global dynamic evolution. Its normal pattern-based reasoning pipeline requires only a few normal reference samples as the detection basis, avoiding any model updates. This enables efficient adaptation across various products and provides high decision interpretability. MUSTARD is the first MLLM-based framework for dynamics-based IAD. Experiments demonstrate its superior performance in detecting spatiotemporal anomalies and providing plausible explanations. The code will be made publicly available.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at IEEE/CVF Winter Conference on Applications of Computer Vision (WACV) 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-100"></a>

### 100. [Value or Attention: Investigating Layer-wise Relevance Propagation for Graph Attention Networks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08088v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08088

**作者：** Ken Schreiber、René P. Klausen、Sebastian Lapuschkin、Wojciech Samek、Jonas Naujoks

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08088v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08088v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文针对 Graph Attention Network 的层级相关性传播（LRP），将节点获得的相关性拆成 value 路径的内容贡献与 attention 路径的路由贡献，用一个带自由权重的乘积规则统一现有做法，并在有真值的分类任务上展示同一节点在三种设置下可获得负、正或近零相关性。

**相关性简评：** 把「内容贡献」与「注意力/路由贡献」分开归因并解释符号方向，可能为 LVR 中 latent 内容与实际被使用的视觉证据之间的贡献分配和筛选诊断提供可核查的迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Value or Attention: Investigating Layer-wise Relevance Propagation for Graph Attention Networks

**abstract/A1：** Graph Attention Networks (GATs) are increasingly used in domains where scientists need to read a model's reasoning, from medicine to materials science, yet the routing they learn remains opaque to most explainers. Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (LRP) follows the model's computation, but in an attention layer the relevance reaching a node is the sum of two mechanically different quantities: its content relevance through the value path and its routing relevance through the attention path. Existing rules either drop one or add them into a single number. We write a single product rule with free weights on the two factors that recovers current approaches as special cases. The value path measures what content a node supplied against a zero baseline; the attention path measures how the node shaped what was passed relative to its neighborhood, and its sign records whether the node's features moved the gating the way the prediction demanded. On a ground-truth classification task, we show that the same node receives negative, positive and near-zero relevance under three settings of the rule. The separated relevance maps yield a hypothesis about the model: it gains by not reading that node. We give reading rules for both paths and argue that they should be interpreted separately.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the XAI4Science Workshop @ NeurIPS 2026 (non-archival). 17 pages, 9 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-101"></a>

### 101. [SAE Interprets MoE: Sparse AutoEncoders Reveal That MoE Experts Exhibit Human-Interpretable Fine-Grained Specialization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08089v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08089

**作者：** Anyuan Zhuo、Shaohua Zhang、Yuan Lin、Pinyan Lu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08089v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08089v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文在 MoE 层输出上训练 SAE，利用编码器的仿射性将每个 latent 的激活精确分解到各专家，从而以「贡献」而非路由频次刻画专家专门化，并在 Qwen3-30B-A3B 上用自动解释方法观察细粒度分工。

**相关性简评：** 其按贡献而非被选中频次做特征归因的分解思路，或可供核查用于 LVR 中 latent 状态的贡献分配与视觉证据筛选诊断；但研究对象为纯文本 MoE 可解释性，与多模态推理无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SAE Interprets MoE: Sparse AutoEncoders Reveal That MoE Experts Exhibit Human-Interpretable Fine-Grained Specialization

**abstract/A1：** Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models route each token to a few of many experts, yet what an individual expert is responsible for remains unsettled. Existing analyses count how often an expert is selected on domain-labeled corpora, an instrument that fixes the taxonomy in advance, stays coarse, and records selection rather than contribution. We instead study specialization in feature space by training a sparse autoencoder (SAE) on the output of an MoE layer. Since this output is a router-weighted sum of expert outputs and the SAE encoder is affine, the pre-activation of every latent decomposes exactly over the experts. Aggregated over a corpus, this gives each expert a dominant set of latents whose activation it principally drives. On layers 4, 30 and 46 of Qwen3-30B-A3B, contribution is far more concentrated than gating, most latents have only one or two dominators, and the overlap between dominant sets concentrates on a few expert pairs. Auto-interpreting these sets reveals a division of labor below coarse domains, with distinct experts for Java and front-end web code, or for geometry and for arithmetic and polynomials, while the coarse domains themselves take very unequal numbers of experts. Our SAEs and code are available at https://github.com/Bytedance-Seed/SAE-Interprets-MoE.

</details>

<a id="paper-102"></a>

### 102. [Gabor Gabor: E-Gaborformer, a Strictly Causal Speech Recognizer with Gabor-Dictionary Convolutions and Delta-Rule Memory](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08092v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08092

**作者：** Nir Krakowski、Tamir Cohen、Ohad Zohar、Chen Zarfati

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL、cs.SD

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08092v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08092v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 E-Gaborformer 严格因果语音识别编码器，将 Gated DeltaNet-2 循环层与滑窗注意力按 3:1 组合，并用固定因果 Gabor 原子字典构造时间卷积，使流式状态保持常数级，并在 LibriSpeech 与生产级希伯来语／英语流式转写上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其 delta-rule 固定大小记忆与滑窗注意力混合是替代随长度增长 KV cache 的状态更新方案，可能为 LVR 中长度泛化的 latent 工作区与缓存结构影响提供可核查线索，但需判断能否迁移到多模态推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Gabor Gabor: E-Gaborformer, a Strictly Causal Speech Recognizer with Gabor-Dictionary Convolutions and Delta-Rule Memory

**abstract/A1：** Real-time voice agents need a speech recognizer that never waits for future audio and whose memory per call does not grow with the length of the call. We present E-Gaborformer, a strictly causal encoder that combines Gated DeltaNet-2 recurrent layers with sliding-window attention in a 3:1 ratio, and builds every temporal convolution from a fixed dictionary of causal Gabor atoms. Its streaming state is constant, about 19 MB per call at production scale including the prediction network, where a causal-attention encoder of the same size accumulates over 1 GB after ten minutes. In a controlled causal study on LibriSpeech, E-Gaborformer leaves the initial plateau earlier, reaches a given accuracy up to 18% sooner, and matches or beats an E-Branchformer trained with the identical front end and recipe. At production scale, a 0.6B-parameter Hebrew and English streaming transducer built on E-Gaborformer has 28 to 31% lower word error rate (WER) than NVIDIA's Nemotron 3.5 ASR Streaming, a model of the same size, on English voice-agent traffic, and up to 20% lower Hebrew WER than our previous production system. The same weights serve strictly causal and 480 ms-lookahead decoding with no loss in accuracy, and a two-class formulation of the RNN-T lattice trains the transducer 2.6x faster with exact loss and gradients.

**comment/C1：** 19 pages, 6 figures, 9 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-103"></a>

### 103. [Towards Practical 20 kbps Generative Video Compression via Adaptive Tokenization and Entropy Modeling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08099v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08099

**作者：** Jiancheng Zhao、Jixiang Luo、Haibin Huang、Chi Zhang、Xuelong Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08099v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08099v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文面向超低码率生成式视频压缩，把预训练因果视频 VAE 改造为离散 tokenizer，并配小型自回归熵模型，用可微码率代理联合优化 tokenizer 与熵模型以降低编码代价。

**相关性简评：** 其离散视觉 token 化、自回归 token 依赖建模与率代理联合训练，可能为 LVR 中视觉 latent token 的表示设计与监督信号提供可核查线索，但目标是压缩而非推理，需确认表示是否保留任务必要细节。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Towards Practical 20 kbps Generative Video Compression via Adaptive Tokenization and Entropy Modeling

**abstract/A1：** Video compression at ultra-low bitrates remains challenging due to the loss of perceptually important details under limited bit budgets. Recent generative video compression methods leverage pretrained video priors to improve perceptual quality and temporal coherence, but many follow a compress-then-generate paradigm, where generative priors are primarily exploited during reconstruction. Moreover, representations learned for generation and reconstruction are not inherently optimized for entropy coding. In this work, we propose \\method, which adapts pretrained video representations into compression-oriented representations while preserving their reconstruction capability. Specifically, we adapt the pretrained causal video VAE of MiniMax-H3 into a discrete video tokenizer and develop a small autoregressive entropy model that captures local and global token dependencies, with grouped prediction for parallel decoding. To further improve coding efficiency, we jointly optimize the tokenizer and entropy model using a differentiable rate surrogate, allowing rate gradients to adapt token assignments toward lower coding costs while preserving reconstruction quality. Experiments on UVG, MCL-JCV, and HEVC Class B demonstrate superior perceptual rate--distortion performance, achieving BD-LPIPS savings of 82.2\\%--87.5\\% and BD-DISTS savings of 78.3\\%--85.0\\% relative to GLC-Video.

</details>

<a id="paper-104"></a>

### 104. [ProtoGestalt: Interpretable Prototypes for Facial Phenotyping and Diagnosis of Rare Genetic Disorders](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08125v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08125

**作者：** Ranga Baminiwatte、Alexander Hustinx、Fabio Hellmann、Aaron J Masino

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08125v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08125v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ProtoGestalt，在原型学习中加入约束使视觉特征选择特定人脸区域，从而以局部、可加的证据支持罕见遗传病的表型分析与筛查识别。

**相关性简评：** 把原型绑定到具体解剖区域并给出局部加性归因的做法，可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选与答案归因提供方法参照；但该工作属医学图像分类，与推理期 latent 状态生成无直接联系，可迁移性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ProtoGestalt: Interpretable Prototypes for Facial Phenotyping and Diagnosis of Rare Genetic Disorders

**abstract/A1：** Rare genetic disorders are difficult to diagnose due to their diversity and overlapping clinical features. Facial morphology can provide diagnostic clues, yet most diagnostic models offer limited interpretability and are not designed to support broad screening for undiagnosed disease. We present ProtoGestalt, an interpretable prototype-based framework for facial phenotyping, rare-disease screening, and syndrome identification. The method extends prototype learning by constraining learned visual features to select facial regions, allowing predictions to be explained through localized and additive evidence. We assess ProtoGestalt's ability to distinguish syndromic from unaffected faces on multiple control datasets and class prevalence values. We further evaluate it's diagnostic performance on syndromes observed during training. The learned prototype-based representations provide significant separation between syndromic and case-matched control images and retain useful performance as the proportion of controls increases. The framework also demonstrates the ability to differentiate among syndromes. Although reasonably accurate specialized models for facial classification of rare disorders already exist, ProtoGestalt offers an interpretable alternative that links predictions to anatomically meaningful facial regions, potentially supporting clinical decision-making and the discovery of phenotype-gene associations.

</details>

<a id="paper-105"></a>

### 105. [FreeOcc: Decoupling Ego-Motion for Efficient 4D Occupancy Forecasting via Continuous Flow Matching](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08128v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08128

**作者：** Zeping Zhang、Zhuoya Zhao、Samy Metari、Robert Laganière

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08128v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08128v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** FreeOcc 面向自动驾驶 4D 占据预测，用 SE(2) 几何解耦显式剥离自车运动，在条件流匹配框架下只对残余智能体动力学做 ODE 演化，并以不确定性感知的混合路由把高置信体素交给单次解码。

**相关性简评：** 不确定性路由「易判区域单次解码、歧义区域才求解 ODE」可能可供核查 LVR 中按需分配计算与停止决策的设计，但其占据世界模型背景与 VLM 推理状态生成距离较远，需较多判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FreeOcc: Decoupling Ego-Motion for Efficient 4D Occupancy Forecasting via Continuous Flow Matching

**abstract/A1：** Current 4D occupancy world models use heavy cross-attention or deep recurrence to disentangle ego-motion from scene dynamics. This requires significant computational capacity. However, this effort is largely redundant since the ego-vehicle's intended future motion is provided by the downstream motion planner. We present FreeOcc, a lightweight continuous-time world model that introduces explicit $SE(2)$ geometric decoupling into a conditional flow-matching framework. By mathematically factoring out self-motion, we create a purely dynamic feature space. This space supports attention-free temporal fusion and allows physics-constrained ODEs to operate strictly on residual agent kinematics. To maximize deployment efficiency, FreeOcc introduces an uncertainty-aware blending mechanism. This routes well-observed voxels to a single-pass decoder, reserving the ODE solver exclusively for ambiguous, high-uncertainty regions. Requiring only 28.3M parameters-roughly 40% of comparable models-our optimal ensemble mode (FreeOcc-E) runs at 27 FPS. On nuScenes, FreeOcc achieves state-of-the-art forecasting performance with a 0.47 m Near Field Chamfer Distance (NFCD) and 32.1% mIoU. Furthermore, the model transfers zero-shot to SemanticKITTI. It also proves highly robust, retaining 92.8% of its ideal mIoU under significant sensor packet loss and timestamp jitter.

</details>

<a id="paper-106"></a>

### 106. [How Far Can Untrained Neural Networks Go in Anomaly Detection? From Tables to Pixels](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08136v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08136

**作者：** Hyojun Yi、Geunyoung Kim、Hogeon Seo、Seunghyoung Ryu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08136v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08136v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 PRISM，用固定随机非线性特征结合基于正常参考的 Mahalanobis 打分，在表格检测与视觉像素级定位上考察未经训练的神经网络能达到何种异常检测水平，并在 ADBench 的 47 个表格数据集及 MVTec AD、VisA 上与现有方法对比。

**相关性简评：** 其「先于学习」的随机特征基线可供核查 LVR 中任务必要的视觉细节是否已存在于固定内部表示、学习式表示又带来多少增益，但异常检测与多模态推理状态的关联尚需判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** How Far Can Untrained Neural Networks Go in Anomaly Detection? From Tables to Pixels

**abstract/A1：** Anomaly detection (AD) is often framed as a representation-learning problem, where strong performance is expected to require learned features or AD-specific optimization. However, this view can overlook the intrinsic separability of anomalies from normal references, even in the absence of learned representations. We therefore investigate how far untrained neural networks can go in anomaly detection. To this end, we propose PRISM, a framework combining fixed random nonlinear features with normal-reference Mahalanobis scoring for tabular detection and dense visual localization. Across 47 tabular datasets in ADBench, PRISM-T achieves the highest mean AUROC (86.4%) and the second-highest mean AUPRC (65.9%) among the compared methods. On MVTec AD and VisA, covering 27 visual categories, PRISM-V achieves mean image-level and pixel-level AUROCs of 90.4% and 96.4%, respectively. Notably, both models achieve these results without neural weight optimization or pretrained representations. A theoretical analysis further characterizes how nonlinear transformations affect the mean and covariance contributions to the Mahalanobis score gap, while empirical analyses examine the resulting score separability. These findings establish PRISM as both a competitive anomaly detector and a prior-to-learning baseline for assessing the added value of learned representations in anomaly detection.

**comment/C1：** 16 pages, 13 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-107"></a>

### 107. [Fault-Guided Cross-Layer Prediction for Unsupervised Anomaly Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08140v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08140

**作者：** Tao Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08140v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08140v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文面向单类无监督工业异常检测，把冻结 ViT 的深度视为一系列离散转移，训练逐 token 的块到块隐状态预测器，并用相邻层余弦相似度划分出「断层」段以固定源/目标层，再配合仅用训练图像校准的前景先验检测异常。

**相关性简评：** 其跨层隐状态预测与逐层相似度定位「断层」的做法，可供核查 LVR 中深层语义保留而局部细节丢失、以及 latent 相似度受深度与位置影响的问题，但属异常检测应用，迁移仅为可能线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Fault-Guided Cross-Layer Prediction for Unsupervised Anomaly Detection

**abstract/A1：** Feature reconstruction is widely used for unsupervised industrial anomaly detection in the single-class setting, where only normal images from one product category are available for training. We propose a single-class detector that asks what information one block can pass to another. We treat the depth of a frozen vision transformer as a sequence of discrete transitions. A directed predictor learns a block-to-block hidden-state transition pointwise over tokens. Adjacent-layer cosine similarity exposes four sharp fault lines in DINOv2 ViT-L, separating stable depth segments. The deepest blocks retain semantic context but lose local detail; a prediction from deep or shallow sources to sensitive targets therefore exposes the information that cannot be transported. The fault structure fixes the source and target roles, and the same rule transfers to ViT-B and RADIO-B. We use a bounded foreground prior calibrated solely on training images: it attenuates background evidence without also erasing defects outside the estimated foreground. Using one predictor architecture and a common training-only calibration protocol, image AUROC is 99.75 on MVTec-AD, 99.42 on VisA and 94.27 on Real-IAD. On Real-IAD the object-level AUROC is 96.26. At 256x256 pixel resolution, P-AUROC/P-AUPRO are 98.46/95.97 on MVTec-AD, 99.21/96.12 on VisA and 99.26/96.59 on Real-IAD.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages including appendix; 4 figures and 21 tables. Preprint

</details>

<a id="paper-108"></a>

### 108. [A Generative Foundation Model for Multimodal Tokamak Tasks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08153v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08153

**作者：** Yingshuai Ji、Haodong Feng、Guohong Deng、Gelin Bian、Yue Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08153v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08153v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文扩展 TokaMark 基准到 20 个 MAST 托卡马克任务，并提出 GenToka：用共享条件扩散 Transformer 配合各信号专用编解码器，按 schema 联合生成波形、剖面、场与标量等异质目标，实现跨任务预训练与适配。

**相关性简评：** 其「schema 条件选择＋模态专用 codec＋共享扩散生成」的结构，可能为 LVR 中异质多模态证据的条件生成、状态解码与跨任务复用提供可核查线索，但论文面向聚变诊断，未涉及视觉推理或 latent 状态分析。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Generative Foundation Model for Multimodal Tokamak Tasks

**abstract/A1：** Tokamak experiments record diverse measurements, including waveforms, profiles, fields, and scalars, which are used to reconstruct the plasma, forecast its evolution, recover associated control signals, and estimate disruption risk. Most models are built separately for one task and a fixed set of inputs and outputs, and each relies on a prescribed subset of diagnostics rather than structuring the full multimodal observation set to extract global, cross-signal information about the discharge. These limitations motivate a reusable foundation model that adapts to each task's input-output schema and models heterogeneous target signals jointly, rather than retraining a separate model for every configuration. In this work, we extend the TokaMark benchmark with two groups of tasks for actuator action inversion and disruption prediction, yielding 20 MAST tasks in six groups with explicit schemas and evaluation protocols. Then, we introduce GenToka, a generative tokamak foundation model that jointly generates schema-selected targets through a shared conditional diffusion Transformer, with signal-specific codecs preserving native time series, profiles, fields, and scalars; foundation pretraining and task-specific adaptation transfer cross-signal structure across all six groups. Evaluation on MAST measurements shows that GenToka reduces macro-average normalized root mean squared error relative to TokaMind by 4.7% on Groups 1-4 and by 17.7% on Group 5, and improves macro-average window-level F1 on Group 6 by 8.4% at a common validation-selected threshold. GenToka provides a reusable, generative, and multimodal pathway for experimental tokamaks, linking plasma-state reconstruction, actuator inference, and disruption warning from shared measurements to support more integrated, data-driven fusion plasma prediction and control research.

</details>

<a id="paper-109"></a>

### 109. [Improving Context-Shift Robustness of Convolutional Networks via Context-Regularized Cross-Entropy](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08157v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08157

**作者：** Jinen Setpal、Chaoyue Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08157v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08157v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对图像分类中的上下文漂移问题，提出更忠实的空间归因方法 ContrastiveCAMs，并据此设计上下文正则化交叉熵 CR-CE，抑制模型对背景区域的依赖，在 Waterbirds、Spawrious、ImageNet-BG 等数据集上提升鲁棒性。

**相关性简评：** 其「归因忠实性 + 抑制非因果区域」的思路，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与 latent 状态里任务必要信息的保留提供训练期线索，但归因图与推理状态间的对应关系尚需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Improving Context-Shift Robustness of Convolutional Networks via Context-Regularized Cross-Entropy

**abstract/A1：** Image classification models often encounter objects in diverse and unpredictable contexts at test time, which may differ substantially from those seen during training. This phenomenon, termed context shift, exposes a key fragility in neural networks: when models rely on non-causal contextual cues for prediction, shifts in context can lead to significant performance degradation. In this paper, we propose a novel training framework to improve the robustness of convolutional neural networks under context shift. Our approach leverages spatial feature attribution to guide models toward making predictions that rely less on contextual regions and more on object-relevant features. As a first step, recognizing a theoretical limitation of existing feature attribution methods we introduce a new variant, ContrastiveCAMs, which produces more faithful attribution maps of model predictions. Building on ContrastiveCAMs, we further propose Context-Regularized Cross-Entropy (CR-CE), a modification of the standard cross-entropy loss that regularizes the model's attention by suppressing the influence of contextual regions, thereby improving robustness to context shift. We evaluate the effectiveness of our approach on several medium-to-large scale datasets (Waterbirds, Spawrious, ImageNet/ImageNet-BG, Hard-ImageNet), and report consistent improvements in context-shift robustness.

**comment/C1：** accepted to NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-110"></a>

### 110. [BUMP: Brief Unexpected Mid-Interpolation Peak](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08182v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08182

**作者：** Melinda Flóra Kiss、Márton Muntag、Adrián Csiszárik、Dániel Varga

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08182v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08182v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文报告了在置换对齐网络间线性插值时，测试精度在接近端点处短暂回升、随后降至中点的现象（BUMP），并在多数据集、架构与训练设置下验证其稳健性；同时据此提出结合权重混合与预测空间混合的集成方案，在精度与校准权衡上优于标准 logit 集成。

**相关性简评：** 其「权重空间混合＋预测空间混合」的组合思路可供核查是否能类比用于合并多条 latent 推理轨迹或多个 latent 假设，并影响输出校准；但论文本身不涉及视觉推理或多模态内部状态，联系较弱。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BUMP: Brief Unexpected Mid-Interpolation Peak

**abstract/A1：** We identify an intriguing phenomenon when linearly interpolating between permutation-aligned neural networks: test accuracy briefly improves near the interpolation endpoints before dropping toward the midpoint, which we term BUMP (Brief Unexpected Mid-interpolation Peak). We observe that BUMP appears consistently across datasets, architectures, training regimes, and remains robust under substantial perturbations such as early checkpoints and extreme pruning. Moreover, controlled ablations suggest that it cannot be attributed to trivial regularization effects. While similar near-endpoint gains are visible in prior interpolation plots, they have not been explicitly documented or systematically and thoroughly analyzed. Leveraging this effect, we propose a simple, novel ensembling scheme: weight-prediction mixed ensembling that combines weight mixing with prediction-space mixing. Across our experiments, the resulting ensembling method consistently outperforms standard logit ensembling in test accuracy and yields improved calibration trade-off, highlighting complementary benefits of weight- and prediction-space mixing.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-111"></a>

### 111. [Multimodal Hierarchical Mixture-of-Experts for 3D Medical Vision-Language Modeling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08185v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08185

**作者：** Gizem Mert、Szymon Płotka、Pedro R. A. S. Bassi、Wenxuan Li、Zongwei Zhou、Jarosław B. Ćwikła、Arkadiusz Sitek、Ewa Szczurek

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08185v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08185v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MedHiVE，用按解剖尺度组织的两级 Soft Mixture-of-Experts 同时路由 3D 影像与报告文本 token，并以解剖级和全局对比学习对齐，在分割、分类、检测任务上验证其优于 11 个基线方法。

**相关性简评：** 其可学习的层级 token 路由与跨尺度融合或可为 LVR 中按需筛选视觉证据、把细粒度解剖细节保留到内部状态提供机制线索，但该工作属预训练表示学习，未涉及推理期 latent 状态生成或计算停止。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Multimodal Hierarchical Mixture-of-Experts for 3D Medical Vision-Language Modeling

**abstract/A1：** Medical 3D scans and reports are inherently hierarchical, with diagnostic cues concentrated in specific anatomical regions across multiple levels of organization. Yet, most medical vision-language models ignore this structure and fail to focus on clinically relevant anatomy. We introduce MedHiVE, which combines hierarchical vision-language Soft Mixture-of-Experts (SMoE) with contrastive learning to jointly model fine-grained anatomy and global scan context. To our knowledge, MedHiVE is the first framework to route both vision and text tokens through a two-level SMoE hierarchy organized by anatomical scale, coupling this with report-derived anatomy-level contrastive alignment for 3D medical imaging. This replaces the direct region-text alignment of statically pooled features and vision-only hierarchical routing of prior work with dynamic, slot-based cross-modal information flow across anatomical scales. MedHiVE operates via a two-level, learnable token-routing mechanism, where local image and text features are first routed through a local SMoE and then fused by a global SMoE into unified multimodal representations. Contrastive learning aligns both anatomy-level and global vision-language representations in a unified space. Pre-trained on 25,000+ CT scans with anatomical supervision, MedHiVE consistently surpasses 11 state-of-the-art methods across 6 datasets on 3 tasks: segmentation, classification, and detection, demonstrating the importance of hierarchical modeling. The code for MedHiVE is at github.com/szczurek-lab/MedHiVE.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at WACV 2027. Copyright 2027 IEEE. Personal use of this material is permitted. Permission from IEEE must be obtained for all other uses

</details>

### 112. [SMART: State-based Multi Agent Reasoning Traces for Verification-oriented Problem-Solving](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08196v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08196

**作者：** Sai Vallurupalli、Francis Ferraro

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08196v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08196v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出多智能体框架 SMART，用角色化提示与多种 LLM 抽取问题语境中参与实体的隐式状态，整理为多样、互补且与解法相关的前提，用于科学声明验证，并在三个数据集上报告验证性能提升。

**相关性简评：** 其把实体状态显式化为可筛选前提的做法，可供核查能否作为 LVR 中 latent 状态应保留哪些任务必要信息的内容设计或监督信号参照；但该工作是纯文本、多智能体流程，不涉及视觉输入或 latent 推理状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SMART: State-based Multi Agent Reasoning Traces for Verification-oriented Problem-Solving

**abstract/A1：** Complex problems with limited context, such as claims, require a means to obtain additional knowledge necessary for solution reasoning. We propose a multi-agent framework, SMART, to obtain knowledge through capturing the implicit state of participating entities in a problem context. SMART curates the state knowledge as diverse, complementary and solution-salient premises using a variety of LLMs and a structured role-based prompt strategy. Using knowledge from these real-world verified curated premises, SMART's knowledge guided solution reasoning improves validation performance up to 20% on complex scientific claims spanning 3 datasets. We show that grounding premises in real-world knowledge is necessary and improves performance up to 5%.

</details>

<a id="paper-113"></a>

### 113. [ProQuant: Progressive Quantization-aware Training for Edge MLLMs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08216v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08216

**作者：** Yufei Xue、Yushi Huang、Jiawei Shao、Pingcheng Dong、Yonghao Tan、Shiyao Li、Kwang-Ting Cheng、Jun Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08216v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08216v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 ProQuant，一个面向边缘低比特（如 W4A8）MLLM 的权重-激活量化感知训练框架，包含自重新标注的数据重采样与渐进块激活机制，并引入视觉敏感因子来定位多模态关键层。

**相关性简评：** 其逐层「视觉敏感因子」及对多模态理解与语言建模能力的解耦，可能可供核查为判断视觉信息在哪一层被保留或损失的度量线索；但论文面向部署压缩，未涉及推理期 latent 状态生成与使用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ProQuant: Progressive Quantization-aware Training for Edge MLLMs

**abstract/A1：** Multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) with impressive perception and understanding capabilities will enable a range of downstream applications. However, their substantial computational and memory requirements hinder the real-world implementation, particularly for resource-constrained edge devices. Quantization has emerged as an effective solution for deploying large models. While post-training quantization (PTQ) successfully retains the performance at moderate bit-width (e.g., W8A8), it suffers from severe performance degradation under low-bit quantization (e.g., W4A8), especially for small-scale models that are more suitable for edge deployment. In this paper, we propose ProQuant, a novel weight-activation quantization-aware training (QAT) framework tailored for low-bit MLLMs. First, to align the limited finetuning dataset distribution with the pretrained dataset distribution, we propose a self-relabeling (SRL) scheme to resample the responses of the open-source multimodal dataset. Second, we are the first to take a decoupled view of multi-modal (MM) understanding and language modeling abilities. We propose a progressive block activation (PBA) mechanism to prioritize the MM understanding recovery. We also introduce a vision-sensitive factor $\\vecτ$ that effectively identifies the MM-critical layers. Extensive experiments across various benchmarks, covering models with 2B-8B parameters, show that ProQuant significantly outperforms existing methods. For example, our W4A8 Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct improves average accuracy by impressively 6.6% and achieves performance comparable to full precision counterparts with only approximately 1% performance drop. Code is available at https://github.com/yufeixue-ai/ProQuant/.

</details>

<a id="paper-114"></a>

### 114. [Beyond Quadratic Attention: Separable Gaussian Operators for ODE Vision Transformers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08236v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08236

**作者：** Carlos Boned Riera、David Romero Sanchez、Oriol Ramos Terrades

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08236v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08236v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SSoG 可分离高斯求和空间算子替代稠密自注意力，把每层代价从 O(N²) 降到次二次，并在离散、权重共享循环与连续深度（Neural ODE）ViT 上报告精度、吞吐和显存收益。

**相关性简评：** 其轴分解高斯混合与 ODE/循环权重的连续深度计算，可能为长视觉 token 下循环 latent 状态更新的低成本算子与稳定性（全局 Lipschitz、解唯一）提供可核查线索，但论文本身未涉及 latent 推理或证据使用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Quadratic Attention: Separable Gaussian Operators for ODE Vision Transformers

**abstract/A1：** Self-attention carries a quadratic cost in token count, which limits how far Vision Transformers can scale with input resolution. We introduce SSoG, a separable sum-of-Gaussians spatial operator that replaces dense self-attention with a learned mixture of axis-factorized Gaussian kernels, reducing the per-layer cost from $\\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ to subquadratic in $N$. Unlike dot-product self-attention, whose Lipschitz constant is unbounded outside a compact input domain, SSoG with fixed kernels is globally Lipschitz continuous, which, via the Picard--Lindelöf theorem, guarantees that the continuous-depth variant has a unique solution on the whole integration interval, a global guarantee that dense-attention counterparts lack in general. On CIFAR-100 at $224\\times224$, in a matched discrete-Transformer setting and in the continuous setting, SSoG improves Top-1 accuracy by 2.2--4.1 points across three model widths while using 8.8--16.6\\% fewer parameters, with depth and training recipe held fixed. In a looped-Transformer setting, SSoG improves Top-1 accuracy by 5.4--8.1 points. We further probe inference-time scaling by evaluating on inputs up to $1024\\times1024$ at matched batch size: in the continuous-depth (Neural ODE) architecture, SSoG reaches up to 154\\% higher throughput and over 60\\% lower peak memory than dense attention, with the gap widening as resolution grows, consistent with the difference in asymptotic cost. In the discrete and looped settings, SSoG also achieves over 30\\% higher throughput than dense attention at this resolution. Together, these results establish SSoG as a practical, parameter-efficient, and theoretically grounded alternative to dense attention across discrete, weight-shared, and continuous-depth Transformer variants.

</details>

<a id="paper-115"></a>

### 115. [FewStepRL: Fine-tuning Few-step Diffusion Models with Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08240v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08240

**作者：** Haitao Lin、Minsi Ren、Yang Li、Xingtong Ge、Zheyuan Hu、Peiyan Hu、Tailin Wu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08240v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08240v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该技术报告系统地把扩散模型的 RL 目标迁移到 few-step 模型：先在 x 空间统一各类目标，再把梯度分解为「指向参考的自回归项 + 奖励增强项」，并按 few-step 模型的传输终点或切线计算锚点，从而在一个模块化代码库中给出 FlowGRPO、AWM、DiffusionNFT 等的少步版本并比较效果。

**相关性简评：** 其将奖励目标拆为参考锚定项与奖励项的梯度视角，以及「锚点取在轨迹何处」的选择，可能可供核查 latent 推理轨迹训练的监督锚定与贡献分配；但研究对象是图像生成的少步采样，未涉及视觉推理状态，需人工判断可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FewStepRL: Fine-tuning Few-step Diffusion Models with Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a standard post-training method for diffusion models, but most RL objectives roll out the current policy at every iteration, and with a many-step sampler these rollouts typically dominate the cost of training. Few-step models cut this cost, yet they model an average velocity over an interval, whereas existing RL objectives are built for an instantaneous velocity with a many-step sampler. We adapt RL objectives to few-step models systematically in three phases, namely a transformation, a decomposition and an adaptation. The transformation to $x$-space expresses each objective we consider through a common policy prediction, at the clean end by default and at an explicitly marked level for transition or flow-map objectives, so that objectives stated over densities, velocities or rewards become comparable. The decomposition writes the gradient of each of these objectives as the sum of a self-regression toward a reference and a reward augmentation, so that a method reduces, at the gradient level, to a choice of anchors and weights. The adaptation computes each anchor from a few-step model at the end of its transport or along its tangent, with the instantaneous velocity recovered from the network itself or from an auxiliary network. This yields few-step versions of FlowGRPO, AWM, DiffusionNFT, DiffusionOPSD and ReFL in one modular codebase. In our experiments, reward-gradient-based methods are the most effective when the reward is differentiable and its gradient is affordable; otherwise DiffusionNFT-style methods generally reach high rewards in relatively few iterations despite a sometimes higher per-iteration cost. Extra inference steps show no systematic aggregate benefit in our experiments, although selected outputs visibly improve with two to four times as many sampling steps as in training.

**comment/C1：** 86pages, 12 figures, technical report

</details>
