### 116. [Nowcasting of Tropical Cyclone Wind Fields and Intensity from Geostationary Imagery](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08255v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08255

**作者：** Simon Donike、Pritthijit Nath、Cristina Radin、Arthur Avenas、Ruben Cartuyvels、Emiliano Diaz、William K. Jones、Anna Jungbluth

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08255v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08255v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作用简单神经网络从静止卫星云图同时预测热带气旋二维风场与风暴尺度强度指标，并通过消融实验考察共享 latent 空间联合预测的效果。

**相关性简评：** 原文从同一 latent 空间联合预测稠密场与标量指标、并以消融检验哪类预测改善标量检索，可供核查多任务监督如何影响 latent 状态中的信息保留与贡献分配，但领域相距较远，需人工判断可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Nowcasting of Tropical Cyclone Wind Fields and Intensity from Geostationary Imagery

**abstract/A1：** High-resolution tropical cyclone surface wind speeds derived from synthetic aperture radar (SAR) observations can support storm structure and intensity modelling, but remain spatially and temporally sparse and unavailable near-real-time. Geostationary satellites capture cloud tops continuously in space and time, but do not directly resolve surface winds. Here, we present simple neural networks to predict 2D wind fields and storm-scale intensity metrics from geostationary imagery. Through a series of ablation studies, we find that joint prediction of 2D fields and scalar metrics from the model latent space improves the retrieval of the (radius of) maximum wind, especially for rapid intensification events.

**comment/C1：** Accepted as a workshop paper at Tackling Climate Change with Machine Learning, NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-117"></a>

### 117. [Scaling Neural Motor Decoding via Decoupled Behavioral Pretraining](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08263v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08263

**作者：** Divyansha Lachi、Vinam Arora、Shivashriganesh P. Mahato、Alexandre Andre、Eva L. Dyer

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08263v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08263v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作提出 BEEMO 多会话框架，把行为表示与神经表示的学习解耦：行为解码器仅从行为轨迹学习运动动态，神经信号通过 cross-attention 在可用时注入，从而能混合使用配对神经-行为数据与大量未配对行为数据做预训练。

**相关性简评：** 其「廉价单模态数据独立预训练 + 另一模态经 cross-attention 接入」的做法，可供核查是否能类比为不经视觉输入先训练推理状态、再以视觉为旁路注入，以及配对监督稀疏时的贡献分配问题；与视觉推理主线本身无关。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Scaling Neural Motor Decoding via Decoupled Behavioral Pretraining

**abstract/A1：** Recent neural decoding approaches achieve strong performance by training on large collections of paired neural-behavioral recordings, but such datasets are expensive, invasive, and difficult to scale. In contrast, behavioral data is abundant and easy to collect, raising the question of whether neural decoding can benefit from decoupling behavioral and neural representation learning. We introduce BEEMO, a flexible multi-session framework that enables training with arbitrary mixtures of paired neural-behavioral recordings and unpaired behavioral data. A behavior decoder learns movement dynamics directly from behavioral trajectories, while neural activity is incorporated through cross-attention layers when available, allowing behavioral and neural supervision to scale independently. Across multiple intracortical motor datasets and tasks in nonhuman primates, we show that incorporating unpaired behavioral data improves decoding performance, particularly when transferring to new tasks, and that adding even a small amount of behavioral data from the target task further improves transfer. By decoupling neural and behavioral data, we unlock a new dimension for scaling: unpaired behavioral data, which is substantially cheaper and easier to collect than paired neural recordings, can be used directly to improve decoding performance. We further show that behavior-only pretraining, without any neural pretraining, achieves performance comparable to models pretrained on substantially larger neural datasets. Together, our results suggest that scaling behavioral data offers a practical and cost-effective path toward neural decoding models that generalize across subjects and tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/nerdslab/beemo.

</details>

<a id="paper-118"></a>

### 118. [LeanContext: Disentangling Mathematical Resources from Local Formal Context in Lean Whole-Proof Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08277v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08277

**作者：** Hang Zhang、Qihao Ye、Litong Liu、Yunbei Zhang、Yugyung Lee、Yue Zhou、Bowen Liu、Emily First、Cheng Han、Qiao Zhuang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08277v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08277v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 LeanContext——129,826 道 Lean 整证明生成问题的基准，将目标选中的数学声明（MC）与剩余局部环境命令（EC）分组，用四个对齐输入视角做训练/推理交叉评测，衡量目标相关声明相对可比替代项的增益，并审计被目标引用却遗漏的声明。

**相关性简评：** 其「固定其他条件、交叉训练/推理视角」以归属某类输入贡献的方法，可能可供核查 LVR 中必要视觉证据与干扰上下文的分离及贡献分配；但领域为形式化证明，无视觉或多模态状态生成，联系尚属类比。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LeanContext: Disentangling Mathematical Resources from Local Formal Context in Lean Whole-Proof Generation

**abstract/A1：** Neural theorem proving increasingly uses retrieved premises and project context to support proof generation. Evaluating these resources requires measuring the advantage of target-selected declarations over comparable alternatives and how this advantage depends on training. We introduce LeanContext, a benchmark of 129,826 Lean whole-proof generation problems, to investigate these questions. We group selected complete preceding mathematical declarations as Mathematical Context (MC) and the remaining selected local source commands as Environment Context (EC). Four aligned input views support crossed training and inference evaluations with shared targets and original Lean verification environments. For both diffusion- and autoregressive-based models, selected declarations outperform comparable alternatives by a larger margin when training includes MC. Final-input audits identify omitted declarations referenced by the target. Prioritizing these dependencies improves Pass@32 by 1.40% and 1.70% for LLaDA and Llama, respectively, on 2,000 targets. The selection experiment operates without access to target reference proofs and holds checkpoints, EC, and inference budgets constant. The code and dataset are available at https://github.com/haaaardcvZ/LeanContext.

</details>

<a id="paper-119"></a>

### 119. [ManimCode: Learning Visual Self-Correction for Educational Animation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08294v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08294

**作者：** Zhengyang Liang、Lizi Liao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08294v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08294v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建 ManimData 数据集与 ManimCode 训练框架，用「生成—执行渲染—视觉评审—修订」轨迹做角色条件监督微调，再用渲染结果与无参考质量反馈对 Reviser 做强化学习，使 4B 模型学会写作、审查和修订教育动画程序。

**相关性简评：** 渲染结果与视觉评审作为外部证据驱动多轮修订，其轨迹监督与在自生成状态上训练 Reviser 的做法，可能为 LVR 的视觉证据筛选、修订收益分配与何时停止迭代提供可核查线索，但本身不涉及模型内部 latent 推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ManimCode: Learning Visual Self-Correction for Educational Animation

**abstract/A1：** Manim supports precise, programmatic video generation, but a successfully rendered educational animation can still have visual defects, missing explanations, or incorrect content. Rendering does not directly identify the code changes needed to correct these visual failures, so feedback-driven revision is needed. Existing agentic frameworks use iterative rendering and revision but typically rely on large models, often proprietary. Existing Manim datasets primarily contain final programs and often lack quality verification and the trajectories of feedback and revisions needed to learn this refinement process. We introduce ManimData and ManimCode, a data and training framework for feedback-driven educational animation generation. We construct ManimData through a render-guided pipeline that iteratively generates, executes, reviews, and revises Manim programs with retrieved API documentation. Its 24,034 render-verified, quality-filtered records preserve intermediate programs, execution errors, visual critiques, and corrective revisions. We use role-conditioned supervised fine-tuning on these trajectories to train ManimCode, a single 4B model, to write, review, and revise animation programs. To improve correction of errors that differ from those in the collected trajectories, we then train the Reviser with reinforcement learning on self-generated states, using rendering outcomes and reference-free quality feedback. ManimCode achieves 96.6% and 97.0% render success on ManimData-test and ManimBench, compared with 52.6% and 82.0% for the untrained 4B model under the same inference setup, while also improving video quality scores from 2.48 to 2.94 and from 3.00 to 3.44. Integrating ManimCode into two long-form educational video generation systems increases completed videos from 19/117 to 107/117 and from 53/239 to 239/239, alongside improvements in educational effectiveness and visual quality.

</details>

<a id="paper-120"></a>

### 120. [First-Token Attraction in Mamba Dynamics](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08303v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08303

**作者：** Trinh T. Nguyen、Minh-Khoi Nguyen-Nhat、Duy-Tung Pham、Hoang-Son Do、Tan Minh Nguyen、Thieu N. Vo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08303v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08303v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文分析深层 Mamba 的 token 动态，提出基于高维不变盒与退出时间的分析框架，证明首 token 的极限方向会吸引后续 token，导致 token 聚类与 attention-sink，并在 Falcon-Mamba-7B 上验证其随深度增强。

**相关性简评：** 其关于矩阵值交互下收敛及首 token 支配的分析，可供核查 LVR 中视觉 token 信息坍缩、视觉旁路与注意力汇现象；但研究对象并非多模态推理，迁移适用性待验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** First-Token Attraction in Mamba Dynamics

**abstract/A1：** We study token dynamics in deep Mamba models, with a particular focus on the attracting role of the first token. Unlike attention mechanisms, where token interactions are governed by positive scalar coefficients, Mamba dynamics involve full matrix-valued interaction coefficients with no positivity or definiteness guarantees. This structural difference makes Mamba dynamics substantially harder to analyze and prevents the direct application of existing techniques for attention-based models. To overcome this challenge, we introduce a new analytical framework based on a high-dimensional invariant box and a sharp exit-time argument, which together recover an effective form of sign-definiteness. Using this framework, we characterize the possible limit points of token trajectories and prove explicit exponential convergence rates. Our analysis reveals that the first token has privileged dynamics: its limiting direction attracts subsequent tokens, inducing token clustering and attention-sink phenomena in deep Mamba models. We further show that this attraction mechanism extends to broader sequence dynamics, including state-space models, causal attention, and classical time-series models. Experiments on Falcon-Mamba-7B validate our predictions, showing that concentration on the first token increases with depth and dominates in later layers.

</details>

<a id="paper-121"></a>

### 121. [Benchmarking Large Language Models on Heterogeneous Cognitive and Biomedical Knowledge Graphs: The Role of Graph Grounding in Multi-Hop Reasoning and Hallucination Mitigation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08315v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08315

**作者：** Ali Sarabadani、Kheirolah Rahsepar Fard、Tahereh Jeirani

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08315v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08315v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文在五个生物医学与认知神经科学知识图谱上对五类 LLM 做基准评测，用分层多跳查询数据集比较「图谱接地」与「无接地」两种条件下的推理深度、事实准确率与幻觉率。

**相关性简评：** 其「接地/无接地」对照加分层复杂度查询的评测设计，可能可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据通道是否被真正使用、以及推理深度上升时收益如何衰减，但该工作纯文本、无 latent 状态，迁移需较多自行设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Benchmarking Large Language Models on Heterogeneous Cognitive and Biomedical Knowledge Graphs: The Role of Graph Grounding in Multi-Hop Reasoning and Hallucination Mitigation

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in biomedical and cognitive neuroscience, but their ability to reason reliably over heterogeneous knowledge graphs (KGs) remains unclear. We benchmark five LLMs (GPT-5.1, Gemini 2.0 Pro, Llama 3, Qwen, and BioGPT) across five KGs: DrugBank, NeuroSynth, Cognitive Atlas, Brain Map, and GDPKG. We introduce a multitier query dataset to evaluate reasoning depth and factual accuracy under KG-aware (graph-grounded) and KG-agnostic conditions. Inter-annotator agreement was substantial, with Cohen's kappa of 0.781 (range: 0.753-0.807) and Fleiss' kappa of 0.77. Results show that general-purpose models, particularly GPT-5.1 and Gemini 2.0 Pro, generally outperform domain-specific models on multi-hop and high-level reasoning tasks, although performance declines as query complexity increases. KG grounding reduces hallucination rates across all evaluated models. Contrary to our initial hypothesis, domain-specific models do not consistently outperform general-purpose models on specialized tasks without graph grounding. These findings highlight the value of combining broad language-model capabilities with structured domain knowledge. We provide practical guidance for selecting LLM-KG integration strategies based on task complexity and computational constraints, and discuss hybrid neurosymbolic architectures for personalized medicine, cognitive disorder diagnosis, and explainable AI.

</details>

<a id="paper-122"></a>

### 122. [OraRefer: Segmentation-Guided Multi-Photograph Fusion for Oral Cancer Screening with Guaranteed Patient-Level Sensitivity](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08360v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08360

**作者：** Rupam Mukherjee、Mehakdeep Singh、Shirin Dasgupta、Subhamoy Mandal

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、eess.IV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08360v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08360v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 OraRefer，一个患者级口腔癌筛查模型：用分割引导的基础模型让分类决策以预测病灶区域为条件，再融合同一患者的多张照片与风险因素，并通过在本地校准样本上标定阈值获得有限样本的敏感度保证。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线无直接关系，但「以预测病灶区域为条件的视觉证据路由」与「多视图证据聚合＋带统计保证的决策阈值标定」可供核查，可能启发 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与停止/转诊式决策点的设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** OraRefer: Segmentation-Guided Multi-Photograph Fusion for Oral Cancer Screening with Guaranteed Patient-Level Sensitivity

**abstract/A1：** Oral cancer and the oral potentially malignant disorders (OPMD) that can precede it are visible on the oral mucosa, which makes smartphone photographs a promising tool for screening. Deep learning models for this task classify individual photographs. However, screening refers patients, not photographs: a patient is usually photographed several times, and the referral decision must miss no more than a set share of patients with disease. We propose OraRefer, a patient-level screening model that reads each photograph with a segmentation-guided foundation model that conditions its class decision on the predicted lesion, combines all photographs of a patient with risk factors, and refers through a threshold with a finite-sample sensitivity guarantee. The model localizes the mouth itself, so inference needs no annotation. On 3000 photographs of 714 patients, patient-level fusion reaches an AUROC of 0.919 and outperforms attention-based multiple-instance learning. At a 90% sensitivity target, OraRefer detects 95% of patients with OPMD or cancer and misses 3 of 61 patients with cancer, at a specificity of 0.610. Because the threshold is calibrated without retraining, each clinic can set its own guaranteed operating point on a local calibration sample.

</details>

<a id="paper-123"></a>

### 123. [StrataFlux: Memory-Hierarchy-Native Low-Bit LLM Inference](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08363v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08363

**作者：** Jake Hyun、Ziyu Zhao、Jae-sun Seo、Mohamed S. Abdelfattah

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.DC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08363v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08363v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 StrataFlux 框架，联合设计低比特权重与 KV cache 量化的精度补偿表示、选择与传输，从主机内存和 SSD 按需流式补充精度并与计算重叠，在保持量化内存与吞吐优势的同时恢复推理准确率。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线无直接关联，但其分层精度流式与 KV/权重位宽可作为核查「内部状态数值保真度如何限制长上下文推理」的工具线索，例如视觉证据保留是否先于语言能力被量化损伤，需人工判断可用性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** StrataFlux: Memory-Hierarchy-Native Low-Bit LLM Inference

**abstract/A1：** Quantization reduces the memory and bandwidth demands of large language model (LLM) inference but can degrade accuracy; offloading higher-precision data can instead stall generation. We introduce StrataFlux, a framework that challenges this tradeoff by recovering accuracy lost to low-bit weight and key-value (KV) cache quantization while retaining quantization's memory and speed advantages. StrataFlux co-designs refinement representation, selection, and delivery to make host RAM and solid-state drives useful sources of additional precision. Its selective precision streaming reduces transfer volume, overlaps delivery with computation, and reuses fetched precision across decoding steps so generation need not wait for every transfer. We evaluate 2-4-bit weights and 2-3-bit KV-cache on dedicated GPUs and unified-memory platforms. For 2-bit Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct weights, StrataFlux reduces WikiText-2 perplexity from 9.46 to 8.40 while maintaining 95.4% of the quantized base's throughput on RTX 5090. With 2-bit weights and KV, StrataFlux raises Qwen3-8B's AIME 2024/2025 accuracy from 11.0% to 47.1% at unchanged GPU memory use. At 128K context, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct with 3-bit weights and 2-bit KV achieves comparable perplexity and speed to vLLM's 4-bit AWQ weights and 8-bit KV on RTX 5090, using approximately half the GPU memory. These results show that GPU-resident precision need not set the accuracy ceiling, nor offloading-tier latency dictate decoding speed.

**comment/C1：** 34 pages, 18 figures, 44 tables. Includes appendix

</details>

<a id="paper-124"></a>

### 124. [StepBack: Step-Level Error-Localized Resampling for Efficient Test-Time Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08403v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08403

**作者：** Yang Ouyang、Jung-Eun Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08403v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08403v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 StepBack，先用轻量信号在少量草稿轨迹中定位首个可能的错误步，再重采样该步及其后续内容并做多数投票，以在数学推理和多跳问答上接近 self-consistency 精度同时降低 token 消耗。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本 CoT 场景，但其「步级错误定位＋从该步重采样」的思路可供核查：或可迁移为 latent 推理中的中间状态回退重算、步级贡献分配与动态计算/停止决策线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** StepBack: Step-Level Error-Localized Resampling for Efficient Test-Time Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Self-consistency (SC) is a widely used decoding strategy for improving chain-of-thought reasoning, but it requires sampling many complete reasoning trajectories with a preset budget. Its efficient variants reduce the cost mainly by stopping full-trajectory sampling early or distributing the number of samples across questions. In this paper, we propose StepBack, a step-level error-localized resampling method for efficient test-time reasoning. Given a small set of draft trajectories, StepBack localizes likely first error positions using lightweight signals and then resamples the detected error step and its suffix, followed by majority answer voting. Empirically, StepBack matches the accuracy of SC on average across seven mathematical reasoning and multi-hop QA benchmarks, while reducing token usage by up to 41.26%. In addition, by nature, StepBack can be combined with adaptive inference methods such as Adaptive Consistency, further improving their inference-time efficiency.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-125"></a>

### 125. [Unpacking the Compute Efficiency of Strong-to-Weak OPD against GRPO](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08453v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08453

**作者：** Zeyu Leo Liu、Yang Li、Bo Pang、Emre Can Acikgoz、Xuan-Phi Nguyen、Anurag Koul、Srijan Bansal、Erik Nijkamp、Shafiq Joty、Semih Yavuz

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08453v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08453v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 系统比较 reverse-KL on-policy distillation 与 GRPO 在达到目标准确率时所需训练 FLOPs，并考察教师来源、模型规模、领域及多教师合并的影响。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本后训练研究，但「稠密逐 token 教师监督 vs RL 奖励」的精度—FLOPs 权衡及响应长度膨胀，可供核查 LVR 轨迹监督、贡献分配与训练成本核算；不涉及视觉或 latent 状态，迁移线索需谨慎评估。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Unpacking the Compute Efficiency of Strong-to-Weak OPD against GRPO

**abstract/A1：** On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own rollouts under a frozen teacher's per-token supervision. Does this dense signal reduce the training FLOPs needed to reach a target accuracy compared with reinforcement learning? We systematically compare vanilla reverse-KL OPD with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) across task difficulty, student and teacher size, domain, and optimization choices, measuring held-out accuracy against training FLOPs. With an external post-trained teacher, OPD generally loses to GRPO in accuracy per training FLOP at matched settings, even when the teacher is further trained with GRPO, and often suffers response-length inflation. Yet a different teacher changes the picture: when the teacher is the student's own GRPO checkpoint, OPD recovers that checkpoint's accuracy on math with $50\\times$ fewer distillation FLOPs than GRPO needs to reach the same accuracy. Following the same teacher setting, we consistently observe this high efficiency with a larger student, on code, and in a second model family. On a single domain, however, the GRPO checkpoint is already the desired model, so distilling it adds to total compute. Across domains, separate GRPO checkpoints hold different capabilities, and no single checkpoint serves every domain. Multi-teacher OPD (DeepSeek-AI, 2026) consolidates per-domain GRPO experts into a single student that matches each expert's accuracy, with the distillation stage costing only 5.2% (on 2 domains) and 8.0% (on 4 domains) of the specialist training FLOPs. Counting specialist training and distillation together, this pipeline reaches joint GRPO's performance on the 2-domain mix with 42.0% fewer total FLOPs.

</details>

<a id="paper-126"></a>

### 126. [Towards interpreting any residual stream direction by scaling activation inversion](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08465v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08465

**作者：** Celeste De Schamphelaere、Juan J. Vazquez、Ari Spiesberger、Tomáš Gavenčiak、Gavin Leech、Euan Ong、Jiahai Feng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08465v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08465v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MAEM：用无标签的 RL 目标训练语言模型，把 subject model 的残差流方向反演成能强激活该方向的文本示例，使 activation inversion 可扩展到任意方向；在 Qwen 上优于语料检索与 NLA，能激活多数 held-out SAE 特征、识别 steering vector 概念、恢复 LoRA 后门载荷，且示例可用 subject model 前向传播验证。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本的「残差流方向→可验证文本」反演工具，可能可供核查 VLM 内部 latent 状态是否真的承载视觉证据与实体关系线索；其用前向传播验证解释、以激活相似度作为 grounded 目标的思路，或可借鉴到 LVR 中 latent 内容贡献分配与停止决策的评估，跨模态迁移效果待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Towards interpreting any residual stream direction by scaling activation inversion

**abstract/A1：** To interpret a direction in a language model's residual stream, we can look for text that strongly activates it. But when such text is found by scanning a corpus, it is limited to what that corpus contains. We develop Maximally-Activating Example Metamodels (MAEMs), language models which map residual stream directions from a subject model to text exemplars that strongly activate those directions. Our objective is to maximise the cosine similarity of a supplied residual stream direction with the activations of the text generated by the MAEM. This label-free RL objective helps to scale MAEMs on diverse sources of residual stream directions. Empirically, we find the resultant metamodel produces informative exemplars: a Qwen3.6-27B MAEM inverts held-out activations better than corpus search or a natural-language autoencoder (NLA), beats corpus search (at 10M tokens) on a majority of held-out languages/domains, and activates 89% of held-out SAE features. It also identifies concepts behind steering vectors about as well as NLAs, recovers the payloads of LoRA backdoors more often than corpus search, and can even verbalise some model workspace inner monologue. Unlike explanations produced by other metamodels (e.g. NLAs), MAEM exemplars can be verified with a forward pass on the subject model, making the MAEM objective a grounded alternative for scaling the training of metamodels.

**comment/C1：** Euan Ong and Jiahai Feng advised equally

</details>

<a id="paper-127"></a>

### 127. [User Embeddings are Superpositions of Interpretable Behavioral Modes](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08487v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08487

**作者：** Jonathan Kozaczuk、Zheng Dong、Noyan Evirgen、Keivan Majidi、Eddie K. H. Ng、Chris Porter、Qi Zhao、Jia Geng、Changmao Li、Brandon R. Feng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08487v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08487v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文论证用户嵌入可看作可解释行为模式的稀疏叠加，用稀疏自编码器恢复这些模式并自动赋予自然语言标签，在合成数据和两个真实数据集上验证，并支持对未训练文本的嵌入做零样本受众检索与刻画。

**相关性简评：** 其基于 SAE 的稀疏字典分解与「叠加」视角，可供核查 LVR 内部 latent 状态是否由可解释的视觉细节或几何模式叠加而成、存在多少纠缠与坍缩，但对象是用户行为嵌入，与多模态推理无直接联系，迁移价值需阅读原文确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** User Embeddings are Superpositions of Interpretable Behavioral Modes

**abstract/A1：** User embeddings are central components of modern recommendation, personalization, and behavioral modeling systems. Though these embeddings can compactly encode rich behavioral information, they are inherently dense and opaque, limiting interpretability. To address this gap, we argue that user embeddings can be understood as sparse superpositions of interpretable behavioral modes. This structure is directly predicted by generative models of user behavior for linear embeddings and extends approximately to deep-learning representations by the superposition hypothesis. Superimposed modes can naturally be recovered by sparse dictionary learning methods, such as sparse autoencoders (SAEs), and recovered modes can be automatically named and queried in natural language. We validate mode recovery against known ground truth on synthetic data, where SAEs recover modes better than classical sparse baselines, including on transformer-encoded embeddings. We then apply the method to classical and transformer-based user embeddings from two real-world datasets. We also show how the corresponding natural-language labels enable zero-shot audience retrieval and characterization over embeddings never trained on text. The framework is post-hoc and model-agnostic, requiring no modification to the upstream embedding system.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-128"></a>

### 128. [Formalizing inverse problems in the age of AI: possibilities, pitfalls, and an invitation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06962v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06962

**作者：** Christian Clason、Tapio Helin、Medet Nursultanov、Lauri Oksanen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06962v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06962v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文介绍 AutoIP：一个由 AI agent 自动形式化的 Lean 库，覆盖经典线性正则化理论及其所依赖的泛函分析；作者主张把人的精力放在核对形式陈述是否符合自然语言原意而非检查证明，并给出陈述级自动测试可暴露已有文献中假设过强问题的例子。

**相关性简评：** 其「证明已被机器检查、风险转移到陈述是否忠实于意图」的结构，可能与 LVR 中「答案正确但内部 latent 是否真正承载视觉证据」的忠实性核查相通；陈述层面的低成本自动测试思路可供核查是否用于 latent 内容与答案贡献分配的评价设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Formalizing inverse problems in the age of AI: possibilities, pitfalls, and an invitation

**abstract/A1：** Proof assistants such as Lean check mathematical proofs down to the axioms, and AI agents have recently made writing such proofs cheap. We describe AutoIP, a Lean library in which classical linear regularization theory, together with the functional analysis it rests on, has been autoformalized by AI agents. A machine-checked proof certifies only the formal statement it proves, so the remaining human task is to confirm that each formal statement says what was intended. We organize the library around this task: a reviewer compares the formal statements with their natural-language counterparts and never needs to read a proof. Our experience is that the risks lie in the statements rather than in the proofs, and that simple automated tests on the statements can expose problems even in the established literature. We illustrate this with a textbook theorem whose hypothesis is more restrictive than asserted. We invite the inverse problems community to extend and build on the library.

</details>

<a id="paper-129"></a>

### 129. [Uncertainty calibration in Bayesian M/EEG source imaging](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06979v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06979

**作者：** Ismail Huseynov、Mohammad Orabe、Srikantan Nagarajan、Stefan Haufe

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06979v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06979v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文构建仿真框架，评估 M/EEG 源成像中经验贝叶斯后验可信区间的经验校准，比较 BMN+sLORETA 与 SBL+S-FLEX，并尝试等渗后校准。

**相关性简评：** 其可信区间覆盖率与后校准思路，可能为评估 latent 推理状态的置信度是否校准提供参考，但领域相距较远，能否迁移到视觉推理尚待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Uncertainty calibration in Bayesian M/EEG source imaging

**abstract/A1：** M/EEG source imaging requires accurate localization and reliable uncertainty estimates, yet posterior credible regions are rarely assessed for empirical calibration. We present a simulation-based framework for uncertainty quantification and calibration of empirical Bayesian inverse methods with closed-form conditional Gaussian posterior summaries. We compare Bayesian minimum norm (BMN) with sLORETA depth weighting and sparse Bayesian learning (SBL) with sparse basis field expansions (S-FLEX). S-FLEX allows retained coefficients to contribute nonzero marginal posterior variance at locations where voxel-wise SBL yields degenerate variances. For BMN, we derive joint source- and noise-variance updates through evidence maximization. We test whether adaptive noise learning yields near-calibrated uncertainties. Both methods retain residual miscalibration, motivating post-hoc isotonic recalibration of credible-region levels using empirical coverage. Simulations assess fixed- and free-orientation models across signal-to-noise ratios (SNR), source sparsity levels, and subject-specific head models, using oracle, baseline-estimated, and adaptively learned noise. Before recalibration, SBL+S-FLEX yields lower Wasserstein localization error, smaller posterior uncertainty, and coverage closer to nominal credibility than BMN+sLORETA. Isotonic recalibration improves both methods, with larger gains for conservative BMN+sLORETA posteriors; transfer across mismatched SNR, sparsity, and head models remains condition dependent. Noise specifications yield broadly consistent reconstruction and uncertainty trends within each estimator. In real auditory MEG data, SBL+S-FLEX provides more focal localization and tighter uncertainties than BMN+sLORETA, suggesting greater statistical power for identifying bilateral auditory sources.

</details>

<a id="paper-130"></a>

### 130. [Robust Stream Classification using Time Neutralising Decision Trees](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06991v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06991

**作者：** Brandon Gower-Winter、Georg Krempl

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06991v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06991v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Time Neutralising Trees（TNT），一种在概念漂移数据流中训练时过滤掉过时样本、并在其重新相关时再纳入的决策树架构，并在 24 个真实与半真实数据流上与其他流学习算法比较。

**相关性简评：** 其按当前上下文判定样本「过时/重新相关」的保留与淘汰机制，可能为 LVR 中推理状态与视觉证据缓存的留存、丢弃与再取提供类比线索，漂移判定与重训练触发是否可对应动态计算与停止决策可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Robust Stream Classification using Time Neutralising Decision Trees

**abstract/A1：** Data Streams are characterised as a potentially infinite source of data subject to non-stationarity. This non-stationarity, also called Concept Drift, often results in deployed predictive models having to be perpetually retrained as the underlying data generating process of the stream changes over time. Drift Detectors are a class of methods used to identify these changes, triggering these retraining procedures. Historically, Stream Learning has operated under the assumption that instances are only seen once, and cannot be stored for later use. Contemporary work relaxes this assumption, yet drift detection and simple resetting procedures are still ubiquitous. Building on related work which has shown that traditional Batch Learning algorithms often outperform Stream Learning algorithms when instances can be stored, we introduce Time Neutralising Trees (TNT), a Decision Tree architecture that enables robust stream classification in settings with Concept Drift. During training, TNT will filter out ("neutralise") old training samples that are no longer relevant given the current context of the stream. If those instances become relevant again, TNT will then re-include them. We evaluate TNT, and related algorithms on 24 real and semi-real classification data streams from the USP DS repository. Results show strong evidence that TNT achieves best-in-class performance on a wide-range of data streams with varying concept drift types.

**comment/C1：** 33 pages, 9 Tables, 10 Figures. Accepted at Neurips 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-131"></a>

### 131. [Autonomous AI Agents Systematically Diverge From Human Preferences in Proof Formalization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06995v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06995

**作者：** Bálint Gyevnár、Simon DeDeo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.HC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06995v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06995v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出「simulated collaboration」方法，在九个 Lean 形式化协作项目（逾 6K 次提交）中对比 AI agent 与人类的证明构建行为，发现 agent 更被显式标记的缺口吸引、相对回避稠密连接的核心与复杂目标，并对「惊人」证明体趋向正则化。

**相关性简评：** 其关于表层语法与深层语义注意力、以及下一步行动偏好偏差的刻画，可能为诊断 latent 推理状态是否保留任务必要语义、评估 LOOK/STOP 类证据筛选与停止决策的系统性偏差提供可核查的类比框架，但领域与方法相距较远，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Autonomous AI Agents Systematically Diverge From Human Preferences in Proof Formalization

**abstract/A1：** Artificial intelligence (AI) systems have ushered in a new age of proof abundance, but helping humans understand these proofs requires new attention to the differences in how AI agents and humans pursue a proof-making task. We present "simulated collaboration", a methodology that allows us to examine two key questions: (1) the relative attention AI agents pay to surface syntax or, conversely, deeper underlying semantics, and (2) how and where AI agent preferences for what to do next diverge from human preferences. On a set of nine major human collaborations in Lean with 4.7M definitions across more than 6K commits, we find that, over the course of proof construction, AI agents (1) are far more strongly drawn than humans to explicitly marked gaps; (2) show a relative aversion to densely connected proof centers and to complex or surprising proof goals; and (3) are relatively attracted to surprising proof bodies, which they tend to regularize toward baseline. Our results highlight important blind spots and potential interventions for future human-AI collaboration in mathematics.

</details>

<a id="paper-132"></a>

### 132. [TraCE: Trajectory-Aware Concepts for Explainable Video Understanding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07009v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07009

**作者：** Wooil Lee、Junpyo Hong、Jongseo Lee、Tuomas Oikarinen、Tsui-Wei Weng、Jinwoo Choi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07009v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07009v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 TraCE，通过聚类训练视频中的光流轨迹发现来源灵活（人、物、交互）的运动概念，并与物体、场景概念构成类型化接口，用于可解释的动作识别与视频问答。

**相关性简评：** 其运动扰动与概念移除测试可能为 LVR 中「视觉证据是否被因果使用、如何分配贡献」提供评价思路，但该框架面向视频 XAI，与 latent 推理状态生成的联系仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TraCE: Trajectory-Aware Concepts for Explainable Video Understanding

**abstract/A1：** Video understanding models often rely on motion, yet their explanations can obscure which motion sources support a prediction. We identify motion-source under-specification as a limitation of existing video XAI: region-centric explanations can be source-ambiguous, while motion vocabularies restricted to single-person pose sequences are source-incomplete. We propose TraCE (Trajectory-Aware Concepts for Explainable Video Understanding), a concept-based framework that discovers source-flexible motion concepts by clustering optical-flow trajectories from training videos. These concepts represent dynamic evidence from people, objects, and interactions, while object and scene concepts account for static context. The resulting typed trajectory/object/scene interface supports class prediction for action recognition and question-conditioned concept matching for video question answering (VQA). We evaluate TraCE across five random seeds on six benchmarks: NTU RGB+D Mutual, Chiral SSv2, UCF-101, KTH, TOMATO, and STAR. Relative to non-interpretable baselines, TraCE maintains competitive task performance, with an average accuracy drop of 1.0 points for action recognition and a nominal gain of 0.4 points for VQA. Motion perturbation and concept-removal tests provide complementary evidence of directional semantic alignment and decision faithfulness, while sample-level interventions illustrate targeted model debugging. Our project page is available at https://leewooil.github.io/TraCE\_project\_page/.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to NeurIPS 2026. Project page: https://leewooil.github.io/TraCE\_project\_page/

</details>

### 133. [Adapting the Process of Adaptation: Darwinian, Lamarckian, and Agent-Directed Evolution in AI](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07048v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07048

**作者：** Andrew W. Lo、Chaoyi Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07048v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07048v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建理论框架，比较环境变化后达尔文式、拉马克式与智能体自主导向的演化机制，分析学习有效性、变体生成数量与搜索投入如何影响适应速度，并讨论评估目标错设与递归自我改进的安全风险。

**相关性简评：** 其「搜索投入取决于学习能力、问题难度与实验成本」及目标错设下更多搜索会加速错误收敛的论点，可能为 LVR 的停止决策、动态计算分配与评价监督提供概念类比，但该抽象模型能否映射到多模态推理仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Adapting the Process of Adaptation: Darwinian, Lamarckian, and Agent-Directed Evolution in AI

**abstract/A1：** Evolution has shaped intelligence for billions of years. Artificial intelligence (AI) now raises a striking possibility: intelligence may begin to direct its own evolution. As AI agents become capable of modifying and evaluating their own behavior, how quickly could they improve, and how can that improvement remain safe? We develop a theoretical framework comparing Darwinian, Lamarckian, and agent-directed evolution following environmental change. Darwinian evolution relies on natural selection, Lamarckian evolution adds inherited learning, and agent-directed evolution allows agents to generate and evaluate behavioral variants and choose how extensively to search. When learning is sufficiently effective, these mechanisms exhibit progressively faster adaptation. Generating more variants accelerates adaptation, while optimal search effort depends on learning ability, problem difficulty, and experimentation costs. However, an agent's ability to direct its improvement does not ensure that improvements are evaluated against the intended objective. When the evaluation target is misspecified, additional search accelerates convergence toward the wrong behavior, even as measured performance improves. In recursive self-improvement, such errors may persist across generations, posing challenges for AI safety. Independent evaluation and oversight must keep pace with autonomous AI development. The central challenge is not simply to accelerate the evolution of intelligence, but to preserve humanity's ability to guide its direction, limit its speed, and intervene when necessary.

</details>

<a id="paper-134"></a>

### 134. [Closed-Loop Agent Evaluation: Layered Measurement and Self-Calibration](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07051v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07051

**作者：** Ruifeng Ren、Gun Ahn

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07051v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07051v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出面向已部署 agent 的「闭环评估」：将评估建模为有偏评分者与截尾结果下的性能估计，按 span、trace、对话、参与度四层分析，沿 Execution、Substance、Trajectory、Results 主线度量，并用来源追溯与裁决组件认证证据，辅以内外两环审计与回归检查，在业务分析 agent 和公开对话数据上验证证据—规则式判断优于整体打分。

**相关性简评：** 论文不涉及视觉或多模态 latent 状态，但其「证据可追溯 + 规则化裁决 + 闭环自校准」的评价框架，可能为核查 LVR 中视觉证据是否被实际使用、以及评估器可靠性提供方法线索，需人工核查其层级划分能否迁移到推理轨迹与证据筛选的评测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Closed-Loop Agent Evaluation: Layered Measurement and Self-Calibration

**abstract/A1：** Once an agent is live, teams want to know whether it is doing its job, not just whether it throws errors. Yet most evaluation is one-off: a benchmark run, a look at traces, a single score. Its conclusions neither check themselves nor reach the agent. We propose closed-loop evaluation, an evaluation method for deployed agents. It models evaluation as performance estimation under biased raters and censored outcomes, and it requires every level's conclusion to trace back to atomic data and to be computed by versioned rules. The method covers four levels of analysis (span, trace, conversation and engagement), measures along a spine of Execution, Substance, Trajectory and Results, treats Conduct as a constraint throughout, and uses Provenance and Adjudication components to certify the evidence and the evaluators. Evaluator agents generated by an Agent Factory carry these components, each with a domain knowledge pack and an adapter harness. Two loops close the evaluation: an inner loop audits the evidence, rules and metrics themselves, and an outer loop uses per-component regression checks to drive agent improvement. We also give a pipeline that runs this process automatically. Using the business-analysis agent SharpInsight as a case and porting the method to public conversation data, we show how problems differ across levels and how evidence-and-rule judgment is more reliable than holistic scoring (ICC 0.86 vs. 0.81; kappa 0.81 vs. 0.63).

**comment/C1：** 7 pages

</details>

### 135. [Stochastic Dynamics of Generative AI: From Autoregressive Models to Interacting LLM Networks and Neural SDEs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07065v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07065

**作者：** Vikram Krishnamurthy、Yiming Zhang、Luke Snow

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、eess.SP、eess.SY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07065v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07065v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文用随机动力系统视角统一刻画三类生成模型：把 LLM 表述为高维非线性自回归模型，用平均场 ODE 描述多个 LLM 交互网络的集体行为与结构性偏置，并以 neural SDE 建模不规则时间间隔的连续轨迹生成。

**相关性简评：** 其连续时间状态演化与多模型交互的平均场刻画，可能为 LVR 中 latent 推理状态的动态更新、循环计算与停止时机提供概念性表述框架，但无视觉或推理实验，需核查可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Stochastic Dynamics of Generative AI: From Autoregressive Models to Interacting LLM Networks and Neural SDEs

**abstract/A1：** This paper develops a stochastic dynamical-systems perspective on three classes of generative AI models. First, we formulate large language models (LLMs) as high-dimensional nonlinear autoregressive models with attention-based dependencies. This formulation connects pretraining by conditional maximum likelihood to sequential token generation and provides a probabilistic interpretation of LLM operation. Second, we consider networks of interacting LLMs and characterize their collective behavior through mean-field ordinary differential equations. These dynamics provide a framework for understanding emergent preferential attachment and persistent structural biases, including type-dependent glass-ceiling effects. Third, we consider neural stochastic differential equations (SDEs) as continuous-time generative models for stochastic trajectories, including those observed at irregular time intervals. We discuss their training with particular emphasis on Wiener-space cubature methods based on rough-path analysis as an efficient alternative to Monte Carlo estimation. Together, the three settings address probabilistic prediction, information exchange and emergent behavior in interacting networks, and path-space generative modeling.

</details>

<a id="paper-136"></a>

### 136. [Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning via Epigraph-Form Guided Exploration](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07066v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07066

**作者：** Sungil Son、Hoseong Jung、Dahyun Oh、H. Jin Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.MA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07066v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07066v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 EFXPLORER，一种用于多智能体强化学习的约束式探索框架：在保持既有任务性能的约束下最大化探索目标，用 epigraph 重表述引入自适应探索预算，并加入基于 successor distance 的内在奖励以刻画长程依赖，在 SMAX、VMAS、MPE 上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「探索目标与任务目标分离、按任务可行性自适应分配探索预算」的表述，可能为 LVR 中 THINK/latent 计算预算的启停与收益—成本权衡提供概念参照，successor distance 的长程信用分配也与 latent 轨迹贡献分配有潜在类比，均需人工核查其可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning via Epigraph-Form Guided Exploration

**abstract/A1：** Discovering cooperative behavior in multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) is challenging due to the combinatorial complexity of joint state-action spaces, which hinders the emergence of coordinated behaviors from trial-and-error alone. Intrinsic rewards are often used to aid discovery, but naively combining them with team objectives can distort the learning signal, compromising task performance. In this paper, we propose EFXPLORER, a constrained exploration framework that maximizes exploration objectives subject to a constraint that preserves established task performance. We solve the resulting problem using an epigraph reformulation that introduces adaptive exploration budgets. This approach separates intrinsic rewards from task objectives and regulates exploration through task feasibility. To further encourage diverse and temporally extended exploration, we incorporate a successor distance-based intrinsic reward that captures long-horizon dependencies. Empirically, our method outperforms strong baselines and induces novel cooperative strategies across SMAX, VMAS, and MPE benchmark suites.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-137"></a>

### 137. [Finite-Time Convergence and Regret Guarantees for Neural Reinforcement Learning in POMDPs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07069v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07069

**作者：** Jingjuan Huang、Zhiyao Zhang、Alvaro Velasquez、Nathaniel D. Bastian、Jia Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07069v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07069v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究部分可观测 MDP 中带非线性神经价值函数逼近的强化学习，基于有限记忆窗口的 superstate MDP，给出有限时间 critic 误差界与对最优 POMDP 值的次线性 regret 保证，并用数值实验验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「用有限历史窗口构造超状态、价值差距随窗口长度衰减」的框架可能与 LVR 中 latent 状态需保留多少历史与视觉证据、推理长度与状态充分性如何权衡的问题相通，可供核查，但不涉及多模态与生成机制。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Finite-Time Convergence and Regret Guarantees for Neural Reinforcement Learning in POMDPs

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement learning (RL) in Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (POMDPs) is computationally challenging due to the curse of long-range dependence on history. Fortunately, recent studies have shown that a range of POMDPs that satisfy the filter stability assumption can be well approximated by a superstate Markov Decision Process (MDP) based on a truncated history in a finite-memory window, with a value gap that decays with the window length. However, existing finite-time convergence analyses on such superstate MDP surrogates are either limited to over-simplified linear function approximation or rely on an i.i.d. sampling oracle that is violated by POMDP trajectories, both of which severely limit the applicability of these theoretical results in practice. This motivates us to bridge the gap by investigating RL in POMDPs with nonlinear neural value function approximation. Our main contributions are threefold: i) We first establish a new finite-state MDP point-wise approximation error bound, which lays the foundation for our RL algorithm design and analysis in POMDPs; ii) By applying Temporal-Difference (TD) learning to POMDP-generated superstate transitions and targeting the Bellman fixed point of the superstate MDP, we prove a finite-time critic error bound with nonlinear neural value function approximation; and iii) By combining the neural critic function approximation error bound with the policy mirror descent framework, we obtain a finite-time sublinear regret against the optimal POMDP value. To the best of our knowledge, such a finite-time sublinear regret based on neural function approximation is the first in the literature for finite-memory POMDPs. Our numerical experiments also verify our theoretical results.

**comment/C1：** 33 pages, 5 figures

</details>

### 138. [Wide View Synthesis from Overlapping Perspective Cameras in Autonomous Driving](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07081v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07081

**作者：** Kaixuan Zhang、Zhipeng Xiong、Minxian Li、Mingwu Ren、Xiatian Zhu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07081v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07081v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作定义「宽视角合成」任务，用前馈 Gaussian Splatting 从多个分离的环视相机合成统一宽视角图像，并提出分片透视渲染与跨相机光度建模以缓解边缘拉伸和亮度不一致，同时构建 WideDrive 合成基准。

**相关性简评：** 纯合成渲染任务、不涉及推理状态或停止决策，但其前馈式多视角几何融合与可见性/遮挡建模，可能为 LVR 中跨视角视觉证据的几何一致性聚合与筛选提供可供核查的表征线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Wide View Synthesis from Overlapping Perspective Cameras in Autonomous Driving

**abstract/A1：** Multi-perspective-camera systems are widely adopted since they provide broad visual coverage for autonomous driving, motivating the synthesis of a unified wide view from spatially separated observations. In this work, we formulate wide view synthesis (WVS), which aims to synthesize a geometrically consistent wide view at a specified virtual viewpoint from multiple perspective cameras. Unlike conventional image stitching, WVS requires reasoning about 3D geometry and visibility to handle depth-dependent parallax and occlusions arising from spatially separated camera centers. While vanilla 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables high-fidelity view synthesis, its reliance on per-scene optimization limits its applicability to driving scenes. Furthermore, WVS introduces additional challenges, \*e.g.\*, peripheral stretching under perspective projection and photometric inconsistencies across cameras. To address these challenges, we propose \*\*Wide-View Gaussian Splatting (WV-GS)\*\*, a feed-forward Gaussian-splatting framework that synthesizes wide views without per-scene optimization. Specifically, we introduce Piecewise Perspective Rendering (PPR) to mitigate peripheral stretching through local perspective projections sharing a common camera center, and Spatial Affine Photometric Modeling (SAPM) to account for camera-dependent photometric variations during training. We further introduce \*\*WideDrive\*\*, a synthetic benchmark providing dense ground-truth wide views and adapt a LiDAR-projected sparse-reference protocol to real-world datasets. Extensive experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of WV-GS for WVS. Dataset and code will be publicly released.

</details>

<a id="paper-139"></a>

### 139. [Server-Side Sparse Assignment for Federated Mixture-of-Experts](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07093v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07093

**作者：** Xue Zheng、Aylin Yener

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.IT

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07093v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07093v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 S^3-MoE 服务端稀疏专家分配框架，从部分观测估计各客户端与专家的相关性，并用 exploit–explore 机制修正分配，同时给出相关性估计误差与精确 Top-K 恢复的理论保证。

**相关性简评：** 联邦 MoE 的资源分配与 LVR 的视觉证据筛选相距较远，但其在部分观测下估计相关性并给出 Top-K 恢复保证的思路，可能为「证据只能部分获取时如何筛选与验证」的可靠性评估提供类比线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Server-Side Sparse Assignment for Federated Mixture-of-Experts

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly recognized for their remarkable capabilities; federated learning (FL) offers a promising framework for collaboratively fine-tuning LLMs on edge devices while achieving data privacy. However, edge devices are inherently heterogeneous: clients may exhibit diverse data distributions and resource constraints, including limited memory and computation budgets. Recent federated Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) approaches address these challenges by using sparse expert activation or partial expert loading. However, existing partial-expert methods rely on heuristic full-pool observations or prioritize system-wide load balance, without guarantees for recovering client-specific relevant experts from partial observations. We propose $S^3$-MoE, a server-side sparse expert assignment framework that estimates client-specific expert relevance from partial observations and refines assignments through an exploit--explore mechanism. We also establish relevance-estimation error and exact Top-$K$ recovery guarantees. Experiments using a CNN-based MoE on CIFAR-100 show that $S^3$-MoE recovers task-dependent client--expert preferences and reduces conflicts caused by correlated expert selection across clients. Compared with Full-20 FedAvg, which gives each client access to all 20 experts for local Top-5 routing, $S^3$-MoE achieves 1.31 percentage points higher mean final accuracy while loading only $5$ experts per client.

**comment/C1：** Accepted for publication in the proceedings of the 2026 IEEE Information Theory Workshop (ITW 2026)

</details>

<a id="paper-140"></a>

### 140. [PromptOps: Iterative Prompt Optimization for LLM-as-a-Judge Systems](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07123v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07123

**作者：** Sven Kleinkop、Tamás Janusko、Ricardo Bochnia、Gunnar Hempel、Steffen Tomschke、Jürgen Anke、Maik Thiele

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07123v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07123v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把部署中 LLM-as-a-Judge 与预期人类评价标准逐渐失配的问题称为「judge drift」，提出闭环框架 PromptOps，将「何时优化、用哪种优化流程、用哪些反馈记录、如何更新判分提示」拆为四类决策，并以 GEPA、SIMBA、评分规则合成与样例选择等五种方法在受控分数分布漂移下检验重校准效果。

**相关性简评：** 属评价运维而非 latent 推理本身，但其「何时触发优化」与反馈记录筛选机制，可能为 latent 推理轨迹的在线评判漂移监测及重评/停止决策提供类比线索，可供核查触发策略是否可迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PromptOps: Iterative Prompt Optimization for LLM-as-a-Judge Systems

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as automated judges for evaluating the behavior of chatbots and other AI systems. In deployment, however, their agreement with the intended human evaluation policy may degrade as user interactions and score distributions change. Existing automatic prompt optimization methods are primarily studied as one-shot procedures on static development sets and therefore provide limited guidance for maintaining LLM judges over time. We frame this problem as judge drift and introduce PromptOps, a closedloop framework for monitoring and iteratively recalibrating LLM-as-a-Judge (LLMaaJ) systems. PromptOps separates four operational decisions: when to optimize, which optimization procedure to apply, which feedback records to use, and how to update the judge prompt. We instantiate the framework with five prompt optimization methods: GEPA, SIMBA, Rubric Synthesis, and exemplar selection using either simulated annealing or a Tree-structured Parzen Estimator. Our experiments demonstrate that iterative prompt optimization can recalibrate LLM judges and recover performance after controlled shifts in the referencescore distribution. However, effective maintenance is inherently contextdependent: neither optimization algorithm choice nor update timing generalizes universally across settings. These findings establish prompt optimization for deployed LLM judges as an inherently iterative maintenance problem and demonstrate that PromptOps provides a practical abstraction for studying and implementing this process.

**comment/C1：** CONVERSATIONS 2026, International Symposium on Chatbots and Human-Centred AI, Preprint

</details>

### 141. [Scalable Bayesian Gaussian Process Classification for Cancer Histopathology: Calibrated Uncertainty Under Distribution Shift](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07127v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07127

**作者：** Yunru Zheng、Wun Ting Chan、Sampras Dsouza、Zhenyu Hong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07127v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07127v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在冻结的病理基础模型嵌入上搭建可扩展的贝叶斯高斯过程分类框架，结合低秩核分解的 RPCholesky、非中心参数化的 HMC 后验采样与 pivots 诱导点预测，在 PCam 和 CAMELYON17 上评估校准与跨医院分布漂移下的诊断表现。

**相关性简评：** 其在低秩潜表示上做贝叶斯后验采样并检验分布漂移下的校准，可能为 LVR 中潜状态不确定性度量与基于置信的停止决策提供线索，但方法本身不涉及视觉推理轨迹，可迁移性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Scalable Bayesian Gaussian Process Classification for Cancer Histopathology: Calibrated Uncertainty Under Distribution Shift

**abstract/A1：** Reliable uncertainty quantification is essential for clinical deployment of cancer diagnosis models, yet neural networks and variational Gaussian process methods can produce overconfident predictions, particularly under the distribution shifts that arise when models trained at one hospital are applied at another. We present a scalable Bayesian Gaussian Process Classification (GPC) framework that operates on frozen pathology foundation model embeddings (Phikon) and combines accelerated Randomly Pivoted Cholesky (RPCholesky) for low-rank kernel factorization, Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (HMC) with non-centered parameterization for posterior sampling, and a pivots-as-inducing-points scheme for prediction. This makes HMC posterior sampling in the low-rank latent representation tractable at N approximately 300,000 training points, enabling HC-based low-rank GPC on substantially larger datasets than prior MCMC-based demonstrations. We evaluate on two histopathology benchmarks for breast cancer metastasis detection: PCam and CAMELYON17-WILDS, where the latter tests calibration under hospital-level distribution shift. Compared to stochastic variational GP and neural network baselines, our method improves calibration and predictive performance - reducing expected calibration error by up to 88% and false negative rate by up to 90% - with the largest gains appearing under distribution shift, suggesting that HMC-based posterior sampling provides more trustworthy uncertainty estimates for cross-site pathology classification.

</details>

<a id="paper-142"></a>

### 142. [Perceptual Distortion for Overfitted Video Coding: Sliced Matching in Space, Error Alignment in Time](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07148v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07148

**作者：** Tianyi Yang、Ge Gao、Fan Zhang、David Bull

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07148v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07148v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文针对过拟合神经视频编码中的感知失真建模，提出空间上的局部 sliced Wasserstein 失真以放松特征匹配的空间对应，并用沿运动轨迹约束误差的流残差保持时间一致性。

**相关性简评：** 其「放松可放松的对应、保留必须保留的对应」的一维最优传输分布匹配思路，可能为 LVR 中 latent 相似度量如何权衡位置对应与内容/分布一致性提供可核查的度量线索，但与推理机制本身无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Perceptual Distortion for Overfitted Video Coding: Sliced Matching in Space, Error Alignment in Time

**abstract/A1：** Recent works in neural video coding have increasingly been trading off perceptual quality against reconstruction fidelity at a fixed bitrate. Wasserstein Distortion (WD) offers a principled approach to balancing these objectives by relaxing spatial correspondences in feature matching, enabling lightweight overfitted image codecs to compete with computationally intensive generative decoders. Extending vanilla WD to video, however, presents two challenges: its independent Gaussian approximation limits the expressiveness of feature distribution matching, while unconstrained texture resampling across frames introduces temporal inconsistencies. We address these challenges by distinguishing between the correspondences that can be relaxed and those that must be preserved. Spatially, we introduce a local sliced Wasserstein distortion (SWD) that matches empirical feature distributions through one-dimensional optimal transport, capturing cross-channel dependencies without enforcing exact spatial alignment. Temporally, we employ a novel flow residual that constrains coding errors along motion trajectories, preserving temporal consistency without imposing additional reconstruction fidelity constraints. Evaluated on UVG and HEVC Class B with the overfitted codec COOL-CHIC, our framework achieves BD-rate reductions of 9.2% in DISTS and 3.0% in LPIPS relative to frame-wise WD, averaged over test sequences, while lowering tOF by 10.2% at matched rate.

**comment/C1：** 5 pages, 4 figures; submitted to IEEE ISCAS

</details>

<a id="paper-143"></a>

### 143. [Depth or Description? When a One-Step Latent Score Replaces Lookahead in Proactive Information Acquisition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07155v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07155

**作者：** Biswa Sengupta

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07155v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07155v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在主动提问式信息获取任务中比较精确 lookahead 与一步 latent score，主张隐因的描述方式决定一步评分能否捕获答案间的交互、从而替代大部分搜索深度。

**相关性简评：** 其「latent 是否编码决策信息决定所需计算深度」的论点，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态设计及浅层计算与停止决策何时足够的讨论提供概念线索，但设定较为简化，可供核查其迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Depth or Description? When a One-Step Latent Score Replaces Lookahead in Proactive Information Acquisition

**abstract/A1：** A proactive agent must decide what to ask before it acts. Exact lookahead values combinations of questions but is expensive and leans on a fitted model; a one-step score is cheap but can miss them. Our main finding: the need for depth often lies in how the hidden cause is described. A one-step score on the label can be blind to an interaction among answers. A score on a suitable latent cause is not, and where that latent encodes the decision it replaces most of depth at a fraction of the search. Described differently, the same problem can defeat the score while exact values do not move, so keep the latent small and check it against exact values. Probabilities learned from one language model's answers do not carry to another unless planning allows for both; those read from tokens are worth only a few labels.

</details>

<a id="paper-144"></a>

### 144. [A Survey of Nonlinear Filtering Methods for Data Assimilation and Recent Advances in Generative AI-Enabled Score Filters](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07160v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07160

**作者：** Cath Pham、Kadesha Reynolds

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.PR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07160v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07160v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该综述梳理数据同化中的非线性滤波方法，比较 Kalman、粒子与核方法的三类失效模式，并以 Ensemble Score Filter 为主线介绍其数学基础及 14 项扩展。

**相关性简评：** 滤波问题即由观测序列推断隐状态后验，与 LVR 中信念状态更新、证据整合及停止决策的概念相近；其免训练得分估计与高维非高斯稳定性可供核查是否能用作 latent 状态转移或视觉证据筛选的替代更新机制，但领域相距较远、尚无多模态线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Survey of Nonlinear Filtering Methods for Data Assimilation and Recent Advances in Generative AI-Enabled Score Filters

**abstract/A1：** Many real-world applications, such as numerical weather prediction or wildfire tracking, involve modeling highly nonlinear dynamical systems that are chaotic and influenced by various sources of uncertainty. To improve model prediction accuracy and reduce such uncertainties, data assimilation (DA) combines the dynamical model with partial, noisy observations to estimate the evolution of the system's state. Doing so depends on solving the optimal filtering problem: computing the conditional distribution of a hidden state given a sequence of observations. Classical approaches fail along three distinct axes: Kalman-based methods assume Gaussian posteriors and produce biased estimates under nonlinear observation operators; particle-based methods represent distributions with weighted samples and collapse in high dimensions due to the curse of dimensionality; and kernel-based methods move beyond discrete particle representations but cannot maintain their density approximation affordably as dimension grows. The Ensemble Score Filter (EnSF) avoids all three constraints by encoding the filtering density in a score function and approximating that score with a training-free Monte Carlo estimator, enabling stable filtering in high-dimensional, chaotic systems. This survey traces EnSF's mathematical foundations, establishes it as the state of the art for non-Gaussian, high-dimensional filtering, and examines fourteen extensions that expand its operational reach. Twelve of the fourteen extensions preserve this training-free Monte Carlo estimator at the core of the update, with only two departing from it to achieve planetary-scale deployment.

</details>

<a id="paper-145"></a>

### 145. [Meta-Frieren: Beyond Single Level Online Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07169v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07169

**作者：** Vahan Arsenyan、Arnak Dalalyan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07169v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07169v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究两阶段在线优化：meta-learner 每轮先选一个 agent-environment 组合，再由该 agent 与环境交互并获得奖励；作者提出 Meta-Frieren 框架，给出 UCB 式元层选择准则，并在 Bayesian 与 frequentist 假设下用 GP-UCB 实例化，还给出 Feel-Good Thompson Sampling 的高概率 regret 界，实验用于多模型类的超参优化。

**相关性简评：** 其「上层用 bandit 准则选择下层执行者并据反馈更新」的结构，可能为 LVR 中动态选择推理算子、计算预算或停止决策的元层控制提供问题表述与评价线索，但与原论文的视觉推理任务相距较远，需核查可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Meta-Frieren: Beyond Single Level Online Learning

**abstract/A1：** We study a two-stage online optimization problem: at each round, a meta-learner first selects an agent-environment pair, then the chosen agent interacts with the environment, and receives a reward. We introduce Meta-Frieren, a general framework for maximizing cumulative reward in this setting. Our analysis yields a simple UCB-style criterion for meta-level selection and recovers prior guarantees as special cases while extending them to broader settings. We instantiate Meta-Frieren with GP-UCB under Bayesian and frequentist assumptions. We also derive, to our knowledge, the first high-probability regret bound for Feel-Good Thompson Sampling, specialize it to finite and parametric function classes, and integrate it into Meta-Frieren. Experiments support our theoretical results and demonstrate that Meta-Frieren can be successfully applied to hyperparameter optimization across multiple model classes.

</details>

<a id="paper-146"></a>

### 146. [Structured Machine Theory of Mind from Agent Trajectories](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07208v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07208

**作者：** Nigel F. Doering、Rahath Malladi、Arshia Sangwan、M Tanjid Hasan Tonmoy、Jacob Doan、David Danks、Tauhidur Rahman

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07208v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07208v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 SMToM 框架，在 BDI 因果结构下从智能体轨迹中独立监督地归因显式的信念与欲望表示，核心是一个只消费心智状态通道的 goal head，并在可控行人导航域检验归因准确率、通道干预与轨迹级反事实诊断。

**相关性简评：** 其「结构化瓶颈 latent + 独立监督 + 对 latent 直接施加干预」的做法，可能为 LVR 中推理状态是否被使用、如何做因果作用检验提供参照，但该工作不含视觉语言推理，迁移价值需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Structured Machine Theory of Mind from Agent Trajectories

**abstract/A1：** Predictive models of human behavior trained on large-scale trajectory data optimize for statistical accuracy without representing the mental states that causally generate behavior. Such models support prediction but not principled intervention: they cannot answer how an agent's behavior would change if its beliefs or preferences were different. We introduce Structured Machine Theory of Mind (SMToM), a framework that attributes explicit, independently supervised belief and desire representations from observed trajectories within a Belief-Desire-Intention (BDI) causal structure. The central architectural element is a goal head that consumes only the predicted mental-state channels and a current-trajectory embedding; counterfactual intervention on beliefs and desires is a direct operation. We instantiate SMToM on a controlled pedestrian navigation domain where ground-truth mental states are known by construction, enabling evaluation of attribution accuracy, local channel interventions, and trajectory-level counterfactual diagnostics. The resulting model, BDIBottleneck, improves over trajectory-only and unsupervised-context neural baselines. It is also competitive with a matched auxiliary-supervised context baseline while providing an editable mental-state interface that the unbottlenecked baseline lacks. Counterfactual experiments on desires confirm that substituting an agent's inferred preferences with a different activity type coherently shifts predicted destinations toward relevant locations. Counterfactual experiments on beliefs confirm that marking a location as unavailable in the agent's belief state reduces its predicted probability as a destination, though in a complex way. These results demonstrate, in a controlled navigation setting, that explicit BDI-structured supervision is a viable foundation for interpretable and counterfactual analysis of longitudinal trajectory data.

**comment/C1：** Published in Transactions on Machine Learning Research (TMLR), 10/2026. 37 pages. OpenReview: https://openreview.net/forum?id=PfSNLg0zcK. Code: https://github.com/NigelDoering/structured-machine-tom

</details>

<a id="paper-147"></a>

### 147. [Fractional physics-informed neural networks to model social media virality](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07215v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07215

**作者：** Alex Koong、George Em Karniadakis

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07215v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07215v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 用分数阶物理信息神经网络联合学习社媒参与度的时间记忆与非线性增长律，以符号回归导出可解释方程；针对观测噪声下点式物理残差导致的分数阶下界伪影提出白化残差，并对 348 条推文做 24 小时窗口的分段建模。

**相关性简评：** 分数阶记忆算子与噪声白化残差可能为 latent 状态更新中的长程记忆先验和含噪监督提供线索，分段 teacher-forcing 也可供核查多阶段推理状态的分段训练，但原文不涉及视觉或多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Fractional physics-informed neural networks to model social media virality

**abstract/A1：** Existing models of social media virality either prescribe the functional form of engagement growth or assume a fixed mechanism by which past activity influences future engagement. We present a fractional physics-informed neural network that learns both the temporal memory and nonlinear growth dynamics of social media engagement, using symbolic regression to translate the learned dynamics into interpretable equations. Synthetic experiments at the deployment resolution of 24 hourly observations demonstrate that the fractional order and reaction law can be jointly recovered from clean data. Under observation noise, however, the standard pointwise physics residual drives the fractional order toward its lower bound through a scale artifact. We address this failure using a fully whitened residual that propagates observation noise through the fractional operator. Applying the framework to 348 posts from X (formerly Twitter), we find that a single-window model estimates strongly memory-dependent dynamics but fails to accurately reconstruct held-out trajectories. Partitioning the 24-hour window into four teacher-forced segments substantially improves performance, yielding segment-specific fractional orders that increase from 0.23 to 0.99 and interpretable growth laws for each phase. The segmented model reconstructs 64% of held-out trajectories within a mean squared error of 0.01, compared with 44% for the strongest of nine classical baselines. Together, these results demonstrate the potential of fractional physics-informed equation discovery to uncover interpretable engagement dynamics while highlighting the importance of noise-aware estimation and temporal segmentation.

</details>

<a id="paper-148"></a>

### 148. [Neural Compression over Equivalence Classes: Exact Gauges and Functional Rate-Distortion](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07233v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07233

**作者：** Toshiaki Koike-Akino

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07233v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07233v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把神经网络同一函数存在多种参数化这一现象当作压缩资源，在精确保持函数的 gauge 变换族与受控函数扰动下搜索更低码率的等价表示，交由原压缩器决定取舍；在 SmolLM2 与 Qwen3 上使 Zstandard 压缩后的 checkpoint 体积最多减少约 23.4%，gzip、Brotli、ZipNN 下相对降幅相近。

**相关性简评：** 「函数等价类内的表示自由度」与「零失真／允许小失真的率失真分层」可能为判断 latent 状态能否在保持任务功能的前提下被压缩或抽象提供概念参照，但对象是权重存储而非推理期状态，需核查其等价性度量能否迁移到状态层面。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Neural Compression over Equivalence Classes: Exact Gauges and Functional Rate-Distortion

**abstract/A1：** Neural networks admit many parameterizations of the same or nearly the same function, but deployment typically stores whichever parameter vector training happens to produce. We show that this representational freedom can itself be used for compression. Rather than redesigning the codec, we search over exact function-preserving transformations and controlled functional perturbations, using the unchanged physical compressor itself to select cheaper representations. Exact gauge families provide a zero-distortion reference under the strict audit; allowing small functional changes exposes much larger rate-distortion gains. Across SmolLM2 and Qwen3, our method reduces Zstandard-compressed checkpoint size by up to 23.4\\%, with similar relative reductions under gzip, Brotli, and ZipNN, establishing trained-network representational freedom as a practical storage resource.

**comment/C1：** 38 pages, 7 figures

</details>
