### 149. [KG-Neighbor: Knowledge-Guided Neighborhood Construction for Embedding Visualization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07246v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07246

**作者：** Chang Won Kim、Jaewook Lee、Haeun Min、JunNyeong Jeong、Minwoo Sim、Barom Kang、Kyohoon Jin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07246v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07246v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 KGN，用实例知识图谱三元组共享度作为成对语义证据调整嵌入距离、重建 top-k 近邻图以改进降维可视化，并引入 IDP 区分投影放置问题、标签问题与未决案例。

**相关性简评：** 其「嵌入近邻未必反映内容关系」的诊断与用外部关系知识重标定相似度、分离投影失败与标注问题的思路，可能为 LVR 中 latent 相似度是否承载内容及失配归因提供参照，但需核查是否触及多模态推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** KG-Neighbor: Knowledge-Guided Neighborhood Construction for Embedding Visualization

**abstract/A1：** In embedding visualizations, nearby instances are interpreted as semantically related, although neighborhoods derived from input embeddings may not reflect content relationships. Neighborhood-based dimensionality reduction (DR) methods can propagate this limitation by optimizing layouts from embedding-based neighborhood graphs. We propose Knowledge-Guided Neighborhood Graph Construction (KGN), which represents relational knowledge derived from each instance as a set of knowledge graph (KG) triples. It uses the degree of triple sharing as pairwise semantic evidence to adjust embedding distances and reconstruct the top-k neighborhood graph; neither KG extraction nor neighborhood construction uses class labels. We also introduce IDProfile (IDP), which interprets residual mismatches by independently assessing whether the source content supports the assigned class label and the neighborhood-majority class. Across eight text and image dataset-encoder conditions, we characterize KGN over the full guidance-strength sweep and four KG similarity measures. Across this sweep, the maximum NMI and LT\_2D each exceed the corresponding kNN baseline in all eight conditions, with the largest gains reaching 0.68 and 1.35, respectively. The gains and the extent of neighborhood reconfiguration vary across conditions and are larger under encoder conditions with lower kNN baseline scores. The IDP analysis shows that the largest reductions are concentrated in Placement Issue candidates, whereas Label Issue and Unresolved Case instances account for a larger share of the remaining review set. These results indicate that KGN can narrow the evaluated gap between embedding-based neighborhoods and content-supported semantic relations, while IDP shows that a residual mismatch should not automatically be interpreted as a projection failure.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at WSDN 2027 findings

</details>

<a id="paper-150"></a>

### 150. [Goal Revelation as a Bridge Between Information-Theoretic Empowerment and Capability-Based Human Power](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07247v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07247

**作者：** Jaime Ruiz-Serra、Tomáš Gavenčiak、Jobst Heitzig

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.IT、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07247v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07247v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在 Markov 博弈框架下比较两种权力度量，即信息论 empowerment 与基于目标达成概率的个体能力，并引入「目标揭示」互信息，构造「达到下界—揭示—empowerment 上界」的链条，指出各量可同时很大却互不等价，并以交互日志统计给出可监控的满足度下界。

**相关性简评：** 用互信息衡量 latent 目标与实现轨迹之间的可辨识度，可能为判断 LVR 内部状态是否真正携带任务必要信息、以及「信息量高但目标未达成」这类失效模式提供度量视角，其向视觉推理状态评价的可迁移性有待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Goal Revelation as a Bridge Between Information-Theoretic Empowerment and Capability-Based Human Power

**abstract/A1：** Power matters for AI safety. Optimal agents tend to acquire power across a wide range of objectives (instrumental convergence). As we embed AI agents into socio-technical systems, humans risk gradual disempowerment. Both concerns call for a formal, quantitative measure of power. Two such measures in the context of Markov games are information-theoretic empowerment, the capacity of the channel from an agent's actions to future states, and capability-based individual power, a measure of goal attainment built from the probabilities that a human would attain each goal in a family of candidates were they pursuing it. We show where the two meet. From individual power, Fano's inequality yields a floor $B(s)$. This floor is a lower bound on what we term goal revelation, the mutual information $\\mathbb{I}(G;ξ\\mid s)$ between the human's latent goal and the realised trajectory. Empowerment caps revelation from above, so that $B(s) \\le \\mathbb{I}(G;ξ\\mid s) \\le \\mathbb{I}(\\mathbf{A}\_h \\to ξ\\mid s) \\le \\mathfrak{E}\_h(s)$. Each quantity can be large while the one below it stays at zero, highlighting the following failure modes. An assistant maximising empowerment can prefer an interface offering broad influence over goal-irrelevant degrees of freedom to one that lets the human attain every goal they might hold. An assistant maximising revelation cannot tell a working interface from one rewired so that every goal yields the wrong outcome, since relabelling goals leaves mutual information unchanged while zeroing every attainment probability. Positive guarantees therefore come only from the attainment end of the chain. A satisfaction floor, computable from two statistics of interaction logs, offers a way to monitor the power users retain in a deployed system, and simulations in random environments show the three quantities are correlated but far from interchangeable.

**comment/C1：** 19 pages, 3 tables. Code: https://github.com/RuizSerra/revealing-goals

</details>

<a id="paper-151"></a>

### 151. [Using Transformers for Teaching and Learning via Symbolic Distillation of Neural A-life Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07254v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07254

**作者：** Shuwei Wang、Vadim Bulitko、Matthew R. G. Brown、Michael Youngblood、Ramon Lawrence、Roscoe Smith、Shinichi Nakagawa、William Yeoh

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.SC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07254v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07254v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究把 A-life 智能体中不透明的神经策略蒸馏为紧凑符号策略：先用随机感知-动作数据预训练 transformer，再由神经架构搜索确定结构，以替代遗传算法式的符号空间搜索，从而加快蒸馏，并考察符号空间对种群自我反思与教-学动态的影响。

**相关性简评：** 用预训练模型摊销符号策略搜索、把不透明神经策略压缩为可读符号策略的思路，可能为 LVR 内部 latent 状态的可解释替代物与监督信号构造提供线索，但该工作与视觉多模态推理尚无直接联系，需核查其蒸馏保真度评价方式是否可迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Using Transformers for Teaching and Learning via Symbolic Distillation of Neural A-life Agents

**abstract/A1：** Neural networks controlling behavior of A-life agents can evolve over multiple generations. While effective in making the agents survive, such networks are generally opaque to a human observer. Furthermore, the neural weights cannot be transferred between agents with different neural architectures. Distilling such neural networks into compact symbolic policies can help humans understand agent behavior in A-life. Additionally, it allows the agents to share their knowledge in a compact fashion, improving population-level learning. Distilling a neural policy into a symbolic policy is typically performed by searching the space of symbolic policies to find one that approximates the neural policy. However, even with a domain-specific language the space of policies can be large, necessitating incomplete stochastic search such as genetic algorithms. Yet the distillation remains computationally expensive. We propose to instead use a transformer pre-trained on readily available random percept-action data. Doing so speeds up distillation substantially. We derive the transformer architecture via a neural architecture search. Empirical results compare different distillation methods and the influence of the symbolic space on A-life population dynamics through self-reflection and teaching and learning.

</details>

<a id="paper-152"></a>

### 152. [Evolutionary Adaptive Neighborhood Learning: A Genetic Algorithm for Instance-Dependent k Optimization in Nearest Neighbor Classification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07257v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07257

**作者：** Isaac Touza、Kaladzavi Guidedi、Kolyang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07257v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07257v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 EANK，用低维策略把局部邻域描述子（密度、标签熵、投票边际、集中度）映射为实例相关的邻居数 k(x)，并以实数编码精英遗传算法在留一准确率加邻域平滑惩罚下优化该策略，附带理论性质与合成数据及十二个公开数据集的实验。

**相关性简评：** 其「按局部证据特征逐实例分配计算量」的表述与 LVR 中动态 THINK/LOOK/STOP 预算策略可能相通，用进化搜索优化不可微的实例级控制策略、以平滑惩罚约束策略规律性也可供核查，但该文属经典 k-NN 领域，与视觉 latent 表示无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Evolutionary Adaptive Neighborhood Learning: A Genetic Algorithm for Instance-Dependent k Optimization in Nearest Neighbor Classification

**abstract/A1：** The number of neighbours k is the central hyperparameter of the k-nearest neighbour (k-NN) classifier and is usually selected globally through cross-validation or evolutionary search. However, the bias-variance trade-off is inherently local: dense and noise-free regions may favour small neighbourhoods, whereas sparse or noisy regions may require larger ones. Since global-k optimization is one-dimensional, replacing exhaustive search with a genetic algorithm offers little computational advantage. We therefore optimize an instance-dependent neighbourhood policy instead. We propose EANK (Evolutionary Adaptive Neighborhood K), which uses a low-dimensional policy to map local neighbourhood descriptors, including density, label entropy, vote margin, and concentration, to an adaptive number of neighbours k(x). A real-coded elitist genetic algorithm optimizes this policy using leave-one-out accuracy with a neighbourhood-smoothness penalty. Precomputed neighbour votes reduce fitness evaluation costs. We investigate theoretical properties of the method, including dominance over global-k solutions on the training criterion, evolutionary convergence, policy regularity, and generalization guarantees. Experiments on synthetic data with spatially heterogeneous structure and twelve public datasets evaluate predictive performance, robustness, and computational cost. Under nested cross-validation, EANK achieves competitive performance relative to tuned global-k baselines and improves upon the default k=5. We also examine the gap between training and test performance to assess the practical value of adaptive neighbourhood selection.

</details>

<a id="paper-153"></a>

### 153. [Reweighting is not Harmless: Understanding the Role of Dataset Characteristics](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07258v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07258

**作者：** Amalia Ionescu、Jose G. Hernandez、Emily F. Wong、Anil K. Saini、Jason H. Moore、Tiffani J. Bright

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07258v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07258v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 通过 3000 组合成数据模拟，考察按群体重加权训练如何在缩小群体间公平性指标差距的同时，把漏检与错误转移给另一群体，而总体准确率几乎不变。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理无直接关联，但其「聚合指标掩盖误差在子群体间重分配」的诊断思路，可能可供核查 latent 推理训练中监督加权与贡献分配是否存在同类掩盖效应。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Reweighting is not Harmless: Understanding the Role of Dataset Characteristics

**abstract/A1：** Reweighting is a widely used pre-processing method for promoting discrimination-free model behavior by assigning different weights to data points during model training. While it is often credited with improving fairness, particularly by mitigating systematic underestimation for underrepresented groups, it can also introduce unintended harms under certain data conditions. Using controlled simulations that vary group size and baseline event prevalence, we show how reweighting can yield misleading improvements in fairness by reducing underestimation for one group while introducing it for another. Across 3,000 simulated datasets with event prevalence of 7-14%, reweighting improved parity in positive classification and false negative rates (FNR) between groups, but did so partly by decreasing positive classifications and increasing FNR for the group with higher baseline prevalence. These effects were most pronounced when that group was a minority: its FNR rose by 6 percentage points, approximately 82 additional missed cases per 10,000 group members, and roughly three-quarters of the improvement in FNR parity came from this increase rather than from gains for the other group. The pattern held across six classifiers, from logistic regression to neural networks, while overall accuracy was essentially unchanged, leaving the shift invisible in aggregate performance metrics. These findings mirror patterns reported in real-world applications and underscore the critical role of data characteristics in shaping the impact of this fairness intervention. While reweighting may create desirable outcomes in some applications, practitioners should critically evaluate it relative to their problem area and goals. Specifically, when the goal is to reduce underestimation in one group without introducing underestimation in another, reweighting may not be the appropriate approach.

</details>

<a id="paper-154"></a>

### 154. [Satisficing as competitive Bayesian weighting: a minimal model of goal-directed choice under an unattained target](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07270v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07270

**作者：** Moto Shinriki、Yu Kono、Tatsuji Takahashi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07270v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07270v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把风险敏感满意化（RS）赌博机规则改写为类贝叶斯估计器 ACB：以抱负水平作为各动作先验均值、以有限试次预算决定证据覆盖强度，并分析其「伪遗忘」与目标不可达时探索不收敛的现象。

**相关性简评：** 其「未达成目标作为先验约束、有限注意力预算限制证据、目标不可达则探索不止」的表述，可能为 LVR 中停止决策与计算预算分配提供概念类比，是否可用于解释「继续思考 vs 停止」的动态尚待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Satisficing as competitive Bayesian weighting: a minimal model of goal-directed choice under an unattained target

**abstract/A1：** Animals and humans often do not maximize; they act relative to goals (aspiration levels) that are frequently not yet met. How can a goal that is not (yet) realized organize choice? Deacon (2012) calls such absence-referenced organization \\emph{ententional}, and García-Valdecasas and Deacon (2024) argue that ends are best understood as constraints rather than as particular states. We examine this idea in a minimal formal setting: Risk-sensitive Satisficing (RS), a bandit learning rule that values each action by its share of past trials multiplied by its surplus or shortfall relative to an aspiration level. We show that RS can be rewritten as a Bayesian-like estimator, Attention Competitive Bayes (ACB), in which the aspiration level is the prior mean of every action and the finite budget of trials sets how strongly evidence can override it. Two consequences follow. First, actions that are no longer sampled are partially overridden by the goal rather than by past observations: their estimates relax toward the aspiration level without any data being discarded. This \`\`pseudo-forgetting'' distinguishes ACB from models with an explicit forgetting rate. Second, when the aspiration is unattainable, exploration does not settle; in Bernoulli bandit simulations, adjusting the aspiration level from evidence reduced regret (expected loss). ACB offers an analyzable model in which an unattained goal and a limited attentional resource jointly constrain behavior, and it yields testable predictions about how organisms revalue options they have abandoned.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages, 4 figures, 2 tables, 1 algorithm

</details>

<a id="paper-155"></a>

### 155. [How do Transformers Perform Low-Level Control In-Context?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07291v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07291

**作者：** Ebonye Smith、Aidan Andrews、Phoenix Wilson、Alexis Frias、Ayush Pandey、Gireeja Ranade

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、eess.SY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07291v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07291v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究 GPT-2 风格多头 Transformer 能否仅凭「系统族归属」与一段无控演化轨迹作为上下文，对参数未知的非线性系统（cartpole、acrobot）执行低层控制，并分析关键训练技巧、随上下文长度变化的性能以及分布外镇定能力。

**相关性简评：** 上下文轨迹实质是隐式系统辨识的证据，其随上下文增长而改善、并能外推到 OOD 的现象，可能为 LVR 中证据筛选、状态抽象与长度泛化的预测提供类比线索；但该工作无视觉或多模态成分，迁移价值尚待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** How do Transformers Perform Low-Level Control In-Context?

**abstract/A1：** One of the remarkable abilities of Transformer-based neural networks is in-context learning. This motivates us to think about "in-context" control. Can we control a system knowing only (1) that it belongs to a particular family of systems and (2) some data on system evolution (provided as the context trajectory) but no specific parameter information? Specifically, we investigate the ability of Transformer-models to perform "in-context" control of unstable, nonlinear dynamical systems, where context consists only of the unforced system dynamics. We leverage a multi-head, GPT-2-style architecture, and we evaluate performance for two classical nonlinear systems -- the cartpole system and the two-link acrobot system, where the system parameters are unknown to the Transformer. We consider a setting where trajectories from an expert controller are available at training time but not at inference time. Here, we identify training techniques that are essential to train Transformers to perform low-level control. Through an analysis over checkpoints, we show that in-context control is possible, and the learned policy performance generally improves as context increases. Furthermore, the Transformer models can generalize to stabilize systems that are out-of-distribution.

**comment/C1：** 6 pages, 5 figures, Accepted to the 65th IEEE Conference on Decision and Control (CDC 2026)

</details>

<a id="paper-156"></a>

### 156. [Provably Efficient Representation Learning for Low-Rank CMDPs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07295v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07295

**作者：** Kaixuan Liu、Guojun Xiong、Shengpu Tang、Wanyun Si、Jian Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07295v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07295v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究低秩约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）中的表示学习，提出无模型算法 MFRLC，将 LSVI-UCB 与原始-对偶框架结合，直接学习表示到值函数的映射并给出次线性奖励 regret 与约束违反保证。

**相关性简评：** 低秩表示上的值函数直接映射可视为一种状态抽象与表示几何问题，原始-对偶平衡奖励与约束的思路或可供核查 THINK/LOOK/STOP 中收益-成本权衡的表述方式；但该文纯理论、无视觉证据，与 LVR 主线距离较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Provably Efficient Representation Learning for Low-Rank CMDPs

**abstract/A1：** We study representation learning in low-rank Constrained Markov Decision Processes (CMDPs), where both value and reward functions are approximated by a set of unknown representation vectors, and the transition dynamics admit a low-rank factorization. The objective is to maximize expected cumulative rewards while complying with constraints on the expected cumulative utility. To achieve this, we propose \\texttt{MFRLC} (Model-Free Representation Learning for Low-Rank CMDP), a model-free algorithm that uses a primal-dual scheme to effectively balance reward regret and constraint violations. To the best of our knowledge, \\texttt{MFRLC} is the first representation-learning method for low-rank CMDPs that combines the Least-Squares Value Iteration with Upper Confidence Bound (LSVI-UCB) and primal-dual techniques. \\texttt{MFRLC} further enhances value function estimation with bonuses for function approximation and learns the mapping from representations to value functions directly, avoiding explicit estimation of the transition kernel. We prove that, with high probability, \\texttt{MFRLC} achieves $\\widetilde O(K^{3/4})$ reward regret and cumulative constraint violation over $K$ deployment rounds, independent of the size of the state space. To our knowledge, these are the first sublinear regret and violation guarantees for CMDPs with unknown features.

</details>

<a id="paper-157"></a>

### 157. [WeightLoop: Weight-Space Programming with Experimental Feedback](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07319v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07319

**作者：** Mengran Li、Bo Li、Weijing Zhao、Chengyang Zhang、Boyu Zhang、Jinfeng Xu、Zelin Zang、Victor C. M. Leung、Stan Z. Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07319v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07319v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** WeightLoop 让 LLM 为任务模型编写有界、可复用的参数编辑程序，并利用新实验测量定位预测偏差、以观测结果验证候选权重修改后写回原模型，形成无需梯度重训的持续更新闭环。

**相关性简评：** 其「用外部观测验证候选修改并决定是否写回」的机制，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选、latent 状态更新的验证与信用分配提供类比；但论文不涉及视觉或 latent 推理，迁移可行性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** WeightLoop: Weight-Space Programming with Experimental Feedback

**abstract/A1：** Weight-space programming (WSP) shows that language models can reason about and rewrite neural network weights; recursive self-improvement (RSI) motivates feedback-driven improvement. Models guiding experiments receive concrete feedback: completed assays reveal prediction errors and inform what to test next. We introduce WeightLoop, a framework that turns experimental feedback into persistent updates to deployed predictors. GPT-6 Astra inspects a task specification and the model implementation, then writes a bounded, reusable parameter-editing program. As new measurements arrive, the program identifies prediction discrepancies, validates candidate edits using observed outcomes, and writes the selected changes back to the original model. The editor remains fixed while the predictor evolves, enabling repeated updates without further LLM calls or gradient retraining while preserving the original inference interface. In offline replay, targeted edits improve mean ranking utility in seven of eight endpoint-budget settings (all eight versus random edits) and mean final discovery across three sequential tasks. WeightLoop connects WSP with an RSI-inspired improvement loop, making experimental evidence a direct driver of model change and subsequent decisions.

**comment/C1：** 5 pages, 2 figures, 3 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-158"></a>

### 158. [Permanent and Transient Representations for Continual Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07327v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07327

**作者：** Nishanth Anand、Doina Precup

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07327v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07327v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究持续强化学习中的稳定性—可塑性权衡，采用参数化永久价值函数与非参数瞬态价值函数互补的双系统，并为复杂任务提出非参数瞬态估计方法，在图像任务和 Craftax 上验证在线学习效果。

**相关性简评：** 其参数化长期表征与非参数即时适配的分工，可能启发 LVR 中长期知识与当前视觉证据的缓存、状态更新设计；但领域较远，具体可迁移性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Permanent and Transient Representations for Continual Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Continual Reinforcement Learning (CRL) agents struggle to adapt to new situations while retaining prior knowledge, leading to a stability-plasticity trade-off. One approach to solving this problem is by using complementary learning systems: one for slow, long-term learning and another for quick, transient adaptation. Anand &amp; Precup (2023) instantiated this idea using permanent and transient value functions, but used the same features in both systems. Intuitively, long-term learning should rely more on parametric representations for broad generalization, while transient learning would be best served by non-parametric representations for situation-specific adaptation. In this paper, we explore this idea both theoretically and empirically. By introducing a novel non-parametric approximation for estimating the transient value function in complex tasks, we demonstrate that our method enables online learning and outperforms competitive baselines on image-based tasks and Craftax-Classic, underscoring the effectiveness of our system-level decomposition.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the 40th Conference on Neural Information Processing Systems (NeurIPS 2026)

</details>

<a id="paper-159"></a>

### 159. [AtlasULP: Domain-aware Universal Link Prediction via Relation Atlas](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07347v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07347

**作者：** Yujing Liu、Yixin Liu、Yu Zheng、Lianhua Chi、Alan Wee-Chung Liew、Heng Tao Shen、Shirui Pan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07347v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07347v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 AtlasULP，用「关系图谱」（relation atlas）从全局与局部两个视角刻画节点对之间的结构关联，并据此生成领域感知的提示 token，实现免训练、可在未见图上推理的通用链路预测。

**相关性简评：** 其将高阶、长程关系压缩为多尺度紧凑表示并用作条件提示，可能与 LVR 中关系证据在 latent 工作区中的组织与域条件化读取有概念上的相通，但跨模态适用性有待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AtlasULP: Domain-aware Universal Link Prediction via Relation Atlas

**abstract/A1：** Link prediction (LP) is a widely applied task in graph learning. Conventional LP approaches typically follow a dataset-specific paradigm, requiring independent training for each graph and incurring high computational and maintenance costs in large-scale applications. Motivated by these limitations, recent work explores Universal Link Prediction (ULP), aiming to enable training-free inference on arbitrary unseen graphs. However, existing methods primarily rely on subgraph sampling strategies to construct universal representations, which are unable to capture higher-order information due to the exponential growth of higher-order neighborhoods. As a result, they often under-perform on graphs where long-range signals are essential, such as biological networks. Moreover, their prediction models are typically domain-agnostic and lack the ability to adapt to different graph domains, leading to limited generalization. To address these challenges, we propose an Atlas-based Universal Link Prediction framework (AtlasULP), which introduces the relation atlas to capture structural associations between node pairs from both global and local perspectives, enabling a comprehensive characterization of connectivity signals across diverse graph domains. Building upon this representation, we develop a domain-guided prompting ULP model, which generates domain-aware prompt tokens from contextual structures and performs prompt-guided in-context prediction for adaptive link inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AtlasULP consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods across diverse graph domains.

</details>

<a id="paper-160"></a>

### 160. [Adversarial Multiplicity: Cherry-Picked Models and the Limits of Disclosure](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07378v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07378

**作者：** Cassandra C. Chou、Benjamin Q. Huynh、Allison Koenecke、Elizabeth T. Chin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.AP

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07378v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07378v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究「预测多重性」下的对抗操纵：区分软操纵（挑选超参、特征等无害模型选择）与硬操纵（直接扰动模型参数），考察当事方能否在几乎不损精度且通过黑箱/白箱审计的前提下翻转个体预测，并提出预注册、敏感性分析等缓解建议。

**相关性简评：** 其「同等精度的模型在个体预测上任意分歧、行为审计难以识破」的结论，可能提示 LVR 中以行为或聚合指标判断 latent 是否真正使用视觉证据同样可被模型选择与微调操纵，可供核查评测可信度与因果归因设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Adversarial Multiplicity: Cherry-Picked Models and the Limits of Disclosure

**abstract/A1：** Predictive multiplicity -- where similarly accurate models disagree on individual predictions -- can be leveraged to optimize secondary metrics (e.g., fairness) without loss in predictive performance. However, exploiting multiplicity opens the opportunity for motivated parties (or coding agents gaming their objectives) to steer predictions toward their own ends, such as in litigation or consumer disputes with automated decision-making tools. In these adversarial multiplicity settings, we distinguish soft manipulation, where parties optimize over innocuous model choices (e.g., hyperparameter tuning, feature engineering), from hard manipulation, where parties directly perturb model parameters. We find a sufficiently flexible model can be manipulated to reverse any individual prediction with minimal change in predictive performance while passing black-box audits, making hard manipulation viable in most regulatory settings. Soft manipulation has smaller effects but can pass white-box audits requiring full access to code and data. Even aggregate estimates can be manipulated: a model's estimated effect of climate change on wildfires can be fully reversed by hard edits and soft search. We find the Reconcile algorithm typically resolves most disagreements between models, but more often in favor of the manipulator when the opposing model is chosen adversarially compared to at random. We map which manipulations can plausibly pass existing regulations and disclosure policies, and recommend mitigations such as pre-registration, proof of model training, sensitivity analyses, multi-metric evaluations, and committing to versioned public models.

</details>

<a id="paper-161"></a>

### 161. [Spectral symmetry fingerprints in equivariant representations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07410v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07410

**作者：** Jiacheng Cen、Anyi Li、Ning Lin、Zihe Wang、Ji-Rong Wen、Wenbing Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cond-mat.mtrl-sci、math-ph、physics.chem-ph

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07410v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07410v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 SymFP，用等变表示的奇异值谱作为结构对称性的统一指纹：通过秩模板匹配识别离散点群，并以超出目标秩约束的谱能量量化连续对称破缺，在合成结构、分子、配合物与大型生物组装上验证其识别与追踪局部、按度数分辨的对称变化的能力。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线无直接关联，但其「用内部表示的奇异值谱刻画被保留与被约束的信息」这一诊断思路，可能启发对 latent 视觉状态中几何与任务必要信息保留程度、以及表示秩约束如何限制推理容量的核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Spectral symmetry fingerprints in equivariant representations

**abstract/A1：** Symmetry governs the organization of physical structures, while symmetry breaking reveals their transformations. In geometric deep learning, symmetry imposes rank constraints on equivariant representations, limiting the expressive capacity of equivariant graph neural networks (GNNs). Here we show that these constraints can instead be exploited: the singular-value spectra of equivariant representations provide a unified fingerprint of structural symmetry. We introduce spectral symmetry fingerprints (SymFP), which enable discrete point-group identification through rank-template matching and continuous symmetry analysis by quantifying spectral energy beyond target rank constraints. SymFP is orientation invariant and architecture agnostic, enabling a parameter-free GNN implementation. Its spectra can be reused across multiple target groups, and the resulting leakage scores satisfy subgroup monotonicity. Across synthetic structures, molecules, coordination complexes and large biological assemblies, SymFP accurately identifies point groups, tracks controlled symmetry breaking and reveals local and degree-resolved symmetry changes that can be obscured by global geometric measures. These results establish the spectral structure of equivariant representations as a general framework for characterizing symmetry across physical systems.

</details>

### 162. [IMUDINO: Self-distilled Foundation Model for Inertial Sensing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07437v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07437

**作者：** Zhoutong Ye、Chengwen Zhang、Zixuan Wang、Xufeng Jian、Zhe He、Qingyang Wan、Xutong Wang、Jiaqi Liu、Mingze Sun、Yu Mei、Chun Yu、Yuanchun Shi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07437v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07437v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 IMUDINO，将 DINO 自监督范式适配到惯性传感数据，用保持语义的运动变换进行自蒸馏，并采用带频率感知位置编码的普通 Transformer 支持变长输入与不同采样率，在活动识别、健康、动作捕捉和里程计等任务上验证跨用户、跨传感器配置的通用性。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线关联较弱，但其频率感知位置编码处理变长输入与采样率变化、以及语义保持变换下自蒸馏的不变性，可能为 latent 表示的长度泛化与任务必要信息保留提供可供核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** IMUDINO: Self-distilled Foundation Model for Inertial Sensing

**abstract/A1：** We present IMUDINO, the first foundation model (FM) for inertial sensing to achieve competitive performance across diverse sensing tasks including human activity recognition (HAR), health, motion capture, and odometry. Prior inertial FMs often focus on one subfield of inertial sensing and sometimes underperform state-of-the-art supervised methods (e.g., in HAR), making their scope considerably narrower than FMs for vision and natural language. Moreover, they often require fixed input length and sampling rate, and generalize poorly to unseen sensor locations. To build a general-purpose foundation model for inertial sensing, we adapt the DINO self-supervised learning (SSL) paradigm to inertial data through a set of semantics-preserving motion transformations that simulate variations in motion pattern and sensor setup. For model architecture, we use a plain transformer encoder with frequency-aware positional encoding that natively supports varying input length and sampling rate. This simple setup proved remarkably effective for learning global and dense features generalizable across users, sensor setups, tasks, and, in the case of robot odometry, embodiments. We hope IMUDINO can serve as a backbone for inertial sensing and kickstart the development of general-purpose inertial FMs.

**comment/C1：** Official repo: https://github.com/Cambrian-yzt/IMUDINO-Release

</details>

### 163. [VIPA: View-Invariant Projector-Based Adversarial Attack](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07443v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07443

**作者：** Jiyu Han、Qingyue Deng、Bingyao Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07443v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07443v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 VIPA，用视角不变的投影-相机仿真建模图案与真实表面的物理交互，并对多视角攻击损失联合优化，得到在物理世界中跨视角、跨分类器都有效的对抗投影。

**相关性简评：** 其跨视角扰动与视角不变表示可提供一个压力测试线索：投影造成的物理视觉扰动可能用于核查 LVR 内部视觉状态是否保留视角不变的细节证据，但论文本身针对普通图像分类器，与推理状态机制无直接关联，需进一步核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** VIPA: View-Invariant Projector-Based Adversarial Attack

**abstract/A1：** Projector-based adversarial attack aims to physically manipulate real-world scenes by projecting adversarial patterns, thereby causing deep image classifiers to produce incorrect predictions. However, existing stealthy projector-based adversarial attack methods model the project-and-capture process in a single and static view, limiting their view-invariant capability and practical applicability in dynamic environments. In this paper, we introduce View-Invariant Projector-Based Adversarial Attack (VIPA), a novel method designed to overcome these limitations by achieving both view-invariant and classifier-agnostic adversarial attacks in the physical world. We first leverage a view-invariant projector-camera system simulation method to model the physical interactions between projected patterns and real-world surfaces. To ensure robustness and stealthiness of the attack across different views, VIPA optimizes adversarial projections by aggregating simulated attack losses from multiple views. This joint optimization across diverse views helps maintain robustness regardless of the viewpoints. Finally, the optimized patterns are physically projected into real-world scenes, where they successfully fool various classifiers from different viewpoints, thereby enabling robust and practical view-invariant adversarial attacks. Our experiments in both targeted and untargeted attacks demonstrate that VIPA consistently achieves higher attack success rates than existing methods, while also enhancing stealthiness and ensuring minimal perceptual degradation across all tested views.

**comment/C1：** 16 pages, 12 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-164"></a>

### 164. [Two-stage residual learning for calibrated uncertainty in solar irradiance forecasts](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07444v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07444

**作者：** RR Flanagan、E Kasha、PW Talbot、AS Epiny、MR Deinert

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、eess.SY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07444v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07444v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出太阳辐照度预测的两阶段残差学习：第一个 LSTM 预测期望辐照度，第二个网络单独建模预测误差分布，再与均值相加，从而同时给出点预测与不确定性，并用概率积分变换评估校准程度。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 无直接关联，但其「均值＋显式误差分布」的分解与校准思路，可能为 latent 推理中内容与不确定性/残差的分离建模、以及基于置信度的停止决策提供概念类比，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Two-stage residual learning for calibrated uncertainty in solar irradiance forecasts

**abstract/A1：** Accurate forecasting of renewable generation requires not only point predictions but also reliable estimates of the uncertainty attached to them, particularly at extended horizons. Machine learning models are increasingly used to predict solar output, but they typically report a single value and capture the associated uncertainty poorly. The standard remedy is to train a model to predict the parameters of an assumed probability distribution directly. Here we develop an alternative two-stage method, motivated by synthetic solar signal generation, in which the forecast error is modeled separately from the expected irradiance. A first long short-term memory (LSTM) network predicts the expected global horizontal irradiance, and a second network models the distribution of the forecast error, which is added back to the mean to give both the forecast and its uncertainty. Using historical weather data from the National Solar Radiation Database and a probability integral transform evaluation, we show that explicitly modeling the error brings the predicted standard deviation markedly closer to the historical value, reducing the deviation between the two by 34.6 percent for a standalone LSTM and by 56.5 percent when the LSTM is coupled to Gaussian process regression, the most reliable of the configurations tested. Because the method models the total forecast error directly, it captures the full predictive uncertainty that matters for decisions, rather than only the model contribution that dropout-based approaches estimate.

</details>

<a id="paper-165"></a>

### 165. [FINCOSMOS: An Open-World Evaluation Paradigm for Agents in Finance](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07464v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07464

**作者：** Yupeng Cao、Xueqing Peng、Lingfei Qian、Haohang Li、Yangyang Yu、Anke Xu、Wenbo Cao、Yan Wang、Yuechen Jiang、Yuyan Wang、Zhuohan Xie、Yankai Chen、Zehan Li、Jimin Huang、Xue Liu、Jian-Yun Nie、Sophia Ananiadou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、q-fin.CP

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07464v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07464v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 FINCOSMOS：一个面向金融任务 agent 的开放世界评测范式，用户隐藏策略、agent 须自行从网络取数并在时间切点下自建可验证回测，由程序做时点正确性核查与指标重算，无参考答案评分。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉主线无直接关系，但其“何时向用户追问、自行取证据、程序化判定何时可交付”的设置，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据获取与停止决策的评价设计提供概念参照，值得核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FINCOSMOS: An Open-World Evaluation Paradigm for Agents in Finance

**abstract/A1：** Language agents increasingly work for users on the open Web, and finance is where trusting them is hardest. The data must come from the Web, the strategy lives in the user's head, and correctness depends on time, so an agent can fail silently: a strategy the user never asked for, unverified data, or a backtest that uses the future yields a report as convincing as a correct one. Existing benchmarks close the world somewhere, fixing the task, the data, or the timeline, and thereby remove these failures instead of measuring them. We introduce FINCOSMOS, an open-world evaluation paradigm for agents doing financial work. A simulated user holds a hidden strategy sampled from a compositional grammar and reveals it only when asked. The agent receives no data, so it must acquire data from the Web, build its own backtest under a data cutoff, and deliver verifiable results. Grading needs no reference answer. Programs verify point-in-time correctness and recompute every reported metric, the delivered strategy is checked against the hidden specification, and a run is accepted only when the user is satisfied and none of 15 veto checks fails. Across four coding agents and a model without tools in 150 sessions, no session is accepted: every session that the simulated user signed off fails a program check, and agents raise only 54--64\\% of the details they were not told. Human experts attribute 88.33\\% of failures to the agents. With FINCOSMOS feedback, an agent with persistent memory learned all ten tasks.

</details>

<a id="paper-166"></a>

### 166. [Degree-Equalized View-Graph Selection under Tight Pair Budgets](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07498v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07498

**作者：** Minhyeok Im、Jongseong Brad Choi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07498v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07498v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究 Structure-from-Motion 在严格图像对预算下如何一次性挑选进入完整匹配的视图图边，提出无需训练的贪心规则 DEAL，对端点已被充分覆盖的候选对做指数降权，在九个 1DSfM 集合与标准基准上提升配准图像比例。

**相关性简评：** 其「按已分配预算均衡覆盖、避免证据集中于少数项」的分配准则，可能为 LVR 中在有限计算下筛选待验证视觉证据、评估覆盖失衡导致的失败提供可核查的思路，但与原论文的推理状态机制无直接关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Degree-Equalized View-Graph Selection under Tight Pair Budgets

**abstract/A1：** Fully matching all image pairs in Structure-from-Motion is quadratic in the number of images, so practical pipelines pass a budgeted subset to the final matching and reconstruction backend. One-shot selectors score candidates with image retrieval, cheap geometric probes, or a learned prior and finalize B pairs before full matching. Their priorities do not account for the degree already allocated to each image, which can concentrate edges on a few images. Across nine large 1DSfM internet photo collections at B=2N, the coverage top-B selector leaves 54% of covered images with realized degree at most two. Reconstruction can then fail even when the selected graph is nearly connected and almost fully verified. Our training-free greedy rule, Degree-Equalized Allocation (DEAL), exponentially downweights pairs whose endpoints are already well covered. On these collections, DEAL improves the one-shot selector built on the same prior (from 0.776 to 0.929) and matches the mean registered-image ratio of an SfM-supervised edge prior under the shared backend (0.929 vs 0.930), without training. On the largely saturated 38-scene standard benchmark suite, DEAL gives the highest mean among the evaluated training-free selectors (0.875 vs 0.858 one-shot).

</details>

<a id="paper-167"></a>

### 167. [Coverage Hides Churn: Auditing and Preserving Conformal Decisions as VLM Label Registries Grow](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07502v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07502

**作者：** Jai Kumar Sharma、Peeyush Tapadiya

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07502v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07502v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文指出 VLM 标签注册表扩张会在模型与旧校准数据不变时改变基于 softmax 的 conformal 分数，从而使旧类别的判定悄然变化，并提出生命周期审计指标与 PrefixCP（为每类保留加入时的注册表前缀）来保持旧判定不变。

**相关性简评：** 其「覆盖率掩盖判定漂移」的审计思路可能可供核查：LVR 中聚合指标（准确率、整体覆盖率）是否同样掩盖了标签集或上下文扩展后逐样本推理/停止判定的变化，以及历史状态前缀是否能稳定旧决策。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Coverage Hides Churn: Auditing and Preserving Conformal Decisions as VLM Label Registries Grow

**abstract/A1：** Vision-language classifiers can learn new labels from text alone, so the label lists they serve keep growing. We show that this growth silently changes conformal decisions about old labels. Common softmax-based conformal scores compare each label with an average over all labels; adding labels changes that average, and with it the score of every old label, even when the model, the image, and the old calibration data stay the same. Recalibrating after the update keeps the coverage guarantee, yet across eleven settings it changes at least one old decision on 53-90% of new images. Coverage cannot reveal this, because it checks only the true label: in five controlled settings that keep the old calibration, so that only the normalizer changes, old-class coverage moves by at most 0.01 while 60-67% of images change. We propose a lifecycle audit that measures these changes alongside coverage and set size, and a simple fix, PrefixCP: each class keeps the label registry that existed when it was added. Its old decisions then never change, each class keeps exact class-conditional coverage under exchangeability, and one prefix scan computes all historical normalizers at once. PrefixCP recovers 40.9-58.5% of the set-size gap between never updating the reference and recalibrating, which gives smaller sets but changes old decisions.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at WACV 2027. Includes supplementary material

</details>

<a id="paper-168"></a>

### 168. [RLGPA: A Reinforcement Learning-Guided Granularity Planning Attack for Decision-Based Sparse Adversarial Examples](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07512v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07512

**作者：** Chao Zhou、Shenghua He、Jiaxin Mo、Qiangguo Yu、Fengchuang Xing

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07512v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07512v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 RLGPA：用 masked Dueling Double DQN 在硬查询预算下在线调度稀疏对抗扰动的支撑集缩减执行器及其粒度，并以可行性保持执行器反馈支撑集增益与查询成本。

**相关性简评：** 其「硬预算下按状态调度不同粒度算子并回传收益/成本」的机制，可能为 LVR 中 THINK/LOOK 的动态计算分配与成本控制提供问题表述线索，但任务本身与视觉推理无关，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RLGPA: A Reinforcement Learning-Guided Granularity Planning Attack for Decision-Based Sparse Adversarial Examples

**abstract/A1：** Decision-based sparse attacks must reduce perturbation support using only the victim's predicted label. The best restoration strategy varies as support contracts: coarse removal works early, while tail states need finer, history-aware tests. We propose RLGPA, an online reinforcement learning method that schedules support-reduction executors and their granularity under a hard query budget. A masked Dueling Double DQN selects among seven stable policy--scale actions; each action produces support-relative restoration groups that are rebuilt after every macro decision. A feasibility-preserving executor commits only adversarial restorations and feeds support gain and query cost back to the planner. Under the matched top-1-label oracle, RLGPA reaches 61\\% targeted ASR at sparsity 0.01 on CIFAR-10 (vs. 25\\% SparseEvo, 0\\% Pointwise), and 75.5\\%/73.0\\% on ResNet50/ViT at sparsity 0.005 (vs. 29.0\\%/15.5\\%). A fixed-probe test reduces median \\(L\_0\\) from 165 to 23 after 40 images without any update. State-blind and state-shuffled controls degrade significantly, confirming that the learned policy uses attack state; the main gains come from state-conditioned executor scheduling and dynamic repartition. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Acui001/RLGPA.git.

</details>

<a id="paper-169"></a>

### 169. [Graph learning-assisted logic-based Benders decomposition for distributionally robust in-station train rescheduling under disruptions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07520v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07520

**作者：** Xiaobin Li、Yanbin Gao、Jianing Long、Wuming Lei

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07520v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07520v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究扰动下车站列车重调度：构建滚动时域分布鲁棒模型，随扰动解除的观测更新剩余关闭时长预测，并用「GNN-LBBD」以逻辑型 Benders 分解协调路径与资源调度。

**相关性简评：** 其滚动更新隐藏量（剩余关闭时长）并用观测修正决策、以及比较「有/无观测」收益的做法，可能可供核查 LVR 中证据使用价值与停止决策的评价设计，但领域相距较远，联系仅为概念类比。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Graph learning-assisted logic-based Benders decomposition for distributionally robust in-station train rescheduling under disruptions

**abstract/A1：** Uncertainty in resource reopening times complicates train holding and rerouting decisions when resource closures coincide with arrival delays and processing-time extensions. We develop a rolling-horizon distributionally robust model that updates predictions of the remaining closure duration as observations of disruption resolution become available. The model jointly optimizes train routes, sequences, and timings. It minimizes the worst-case expected total completion delay over an ambiguity set of scenario probabilities. The solution algorithm, GNN-LBBD, uses logic-based Benders decomposition to coordinate routing and resource scheduling. Conditional cuts are constructed from resource-use patterns, and a graph neural network guides the order in which resource relaxation subproblems are solved. Across 28 test instances for a five-track station, GNN-LBBD achieved a mean total completion delay of 61.13 train-min. This was 16.63\\% lower than tabu search and 20.06\\% lower than variable neighborhood search, with a mean total computation time close to that of tabu search. Information comparisons on six representative instances showed that using observations of disruption resolution reduced mean total completion delay by approximately 21.7\\%. Scenario-based and conditional-mean predictions produced similar overall results under the same information conditions. The benefits of information were more pronounced in some instances with alternative routes. This suggests a relationship between the use of observations, the station resource structure, and the scope for schedule adjustments.

</details>

<a id="paper-170"></a>

### 170. [Goal-Branching LaCAM: Temporary Goals as Search Branches in Dense Multi-Agent Pathfinding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07528v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07528

**作者：** Stephen Chang、Daniel Harabor

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07528v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07528v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 Goal-Branching：为造成死锁的阻塞智能体临时设定目标，并把这些目标作为 LaCAM 联合配置搜索的额外分支，在密集 MAPF 实例上以更高解代价换取显著提升的求解率与速度。

**相关性简评：** 「临时子目标作为搜索分支」与 LVR 中多步推理的子目标分解、备选分支枚举与回溯搜索在概念上可能有联系，可供核查能否把这种分支机制和完备性论证类推到 latent 推理状态搜索；但论文处于 MAPF 规划语境，与视觉表示和 VLM 无直接关系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Goal-Branching LaCAM: Temporary Goals as Search Branches in Dense Multi-Agent Pathfinding

**abstract/A1：** LaCAM is a search-based solver for multi-agent pathfinding (MAPF) that runs a depth-first search over joint configurations, using PIBT to generate successors and lazily adding single-agent constraints to explore alternatives. Although LaCAM is complete, it can struggle on dense, corridor-heavy instances because extracting blocking agents from deadlocks requires coordinated moves of several agents, often away from their goals, which PIBT's greedy one-step successors rarely propose and which LaCAM can only reach by enumerating constraints one agent at a time. This paper proposes Goal-Branching, where blocking agents are given temporary goals, each tried as an additional branch of LaCAM's search. We examine random and corridor-aware rules for choosing these temporary goals and show that completeness is preserved for any such rule. On dense instances (60-90% of free cells occupied) of three maze and room maps, Goal-Branching solves 89% of instances against 44% for LaCAM\*, and, on instances both solve, it finds a first solution 10-14x faster on maze-32-32-2 and room-32-32-4, and nearly 200x faster in median time at 75% density on room-32-32-4. Solution cost increases by 15-22% on instances both solve, while on open maps and the full MovingAI benchmark its solutions cost no more than those of LaCAM\* and it is at most 3% slower.

</details>

<a id="paper-171"></a>

### 171. [Integrating Learned Priors through Prior-Space Reparameterization for Physically Based Inverse Rendering](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07573v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07573

**作者：** Hun Hwangbo、Minjun Park、Yongsung Kim、Wooseok Song、Jungmin Ko、Junghyun Hwang、Sungroh Yoon

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07573v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07573v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究基于物理的逆向渲染中如何注入学习到的材质先验：将多视角材质预测融合到网格上，在预测特征空间构建 permutohedral lattice，使预测相似的表面点共享材质变量，并在保持纯光度目标不变的前提下与几何、光照联合优化。

**相关性简评：** 其「用先验特征空间重参数化限制优化空间，并借变量共享汇聚多视角噪声梯度」的思路，可能为 LVR 中先验约束 latent 状态空间、相似状态合并及训练信号分配提供可供核查的类比，但领域相距较远，迁移需较多改造。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Integrating Learned Priors through Prior-Space Reparameterization for Physically Based Inverse Rendering

**abstract/A1：** Physically based inverse rendering decomposes appearance into materials and illumination, an ambiguous problem for which learned material predictions provide useful prior information. Most existing methods incorporate these cues through auxiliary regularization losses, which alter the optimization objective and require balancing their influence against photometric fitting. We propose a prior-guided material reparameterization that uses the predicted feature relationships to restrict the optimization space while leaving the objective unchanged. We fuse multi-view material predictions onto a mesh and construct a permutohedral lattice in the resulting prior feature space. Surface points with similar predictions share material variables, which are optimized jointly with geometry and illumination using a purely photometric objective. Experiments with two learned predictors on three synthetic benchmarks and Stanford-ORB demonstrate improved material decomposition and relighting. Our method improves relighting PSNR by 1.5-2.5 dB over the strongest evaluated mesh-based baseline on Synthetic4Relight and TensoIR-Synthetic, and outperforms a regularization loss using the same prior in controlled comparisons. We further show that our reparameterization pools noisy photometric gradients, producing updates that are more consistent across sampled views. This consistency enables a shortened joint optimization schedule that takes less than a minute while retaining relighting quality above all evaluated baselines on TensoIR-Synthetic.

</details>

<a id="paper-172"></a>

### 172. [Georeferenced Cross-View 3D Reconstruction from Ground and Satellite Images](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07575v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07575

**作者：** David G. Shatwell、Mubarak Shah

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07575v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07575v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 X-VGGT，一种前馈跨视角几何 Transformer，联合处理地面图像与带地理参考的卫星图像，同时预测地面相机位姿、稠密点图及含重力、尺度、偏航和平移的场景级变换，并给出该联合任务的评测协议。

**相关性简评：** 与 VLM 推理主线较远，但其用显式几何变换参数把局部重建对齐到外部参考系，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据的几何一致性状态与跨视角选择提供概念线索，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Georeferenced Cross-View 3D Reconstruction from Ground and Satellite Images

**abstract/A1：** Georeferenced 3D reconstruction from ground images requires recovering not only the local scene geometry, but also localizing every camera center and 3D point in a geographic coordinate frame. Existing cross-view geo-localization methods are able to estimate the GPS coordinate of a single ground camera, but do not reconstruct dense 3D geometry, while recent feed-forward multi-view 3D reconstruction models predict point maps and camera poses only in a local coordinate frame. We introduce X-VGGT, a feed-forward cross-view geometry transformer that unifies these tasks by jointly processing a set of ground-level images and a georeferenced satellite image to predict ground-view camera poses, dense 3D point maps, and a scene-level transform parameterized by gravity, scale, yaw and translation. This transform projects the local reconstruction into the satellite plane, resulting in the geo-registration of all ground images with the georeferenced satellite image. We further propose an evaluation protocol for this joint task and show that, across multiple cross-view datasets, X-VGGT produces accurate georeferenced reconstructions and outperforms prior cross-view geo-localization and multi-view 3D reconstruction models.

**comment/C1：** Accepted in NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-173"></a>

### 173. [Align Before Aggregation: Basis-Consistent Federated LoRA under Heterogeneous Ranks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07598v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07598

**作者：** Pengpeng Qiao、Yang Cao、Lingling Zhang、Guo Cheng、Junwei Chen、Manjiang Yu、Wei Yang Bryan Lim、Masatoshi Yoshikawa

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07598v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07598v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 FedAbA，用于异构秩下的联邦 LoRA：先抽取各客户端的本地基并对齐到全局参考基的相应前缀，做基一致的重建后按数据量与一致性分数加权聚合，再在系数空间做精确 SVD 重分解与 gauge fixing 后分发秩前缀。

**相关性简评：** 其「先对齐子空间基与 gauge、再聚合」的思路或可为跨客户端、跨层 latent 状态的基底对齐与相似度比较提供参照，但属远域方法线索，需核查其对 LVR 状态表示比较与合并是否有实际可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Align Before Aggregation: Basis-Consistent Federated LoRA under Heterogeneous Ranks

**abstract/A1：** Federated fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs) under heterogeneous client capacities is increasingly important, and low-rank adaptation (LoRA) with heterogeneous ranks makes it practical. Existing server-side aggregators compose local matrix updates $ΔW\_i$ from client-specific LoRA factors $(A\_i,B\_i)$ before aggregating them. Although these matrix updates lie in the same weight space $\\mathbb{R}^{m\\times n}$ and can be averaged directly, the LoRA factors that generate them may expressed client-specific local basis. In the typical compose--aggregate--factorize--dispatch loop, this basis inconsistency can make the rank-$r\_i$ reference prefix dispatched to lower-rank clients unstable after server-side refactorization. In this work, we propose \\textbf{FedAbA}, a basis-consistent federated LoRA aggregation framework for heterogeneous ranks. FedAbA first extracts each client's local basis, aligns it to the corresponding prefix of the global reference basis, and constructs a basis-consistent reconstruction of each local matrix update before aggregation. It then aggregates these reconstructions with weights that combine client data size and consistency score, followed by exact coefficient-space SVD refactorization and gauge fixing before rank-prefix dispatch. Our theoretical analysis clarifies the role of basis consistency and supports the proposed consistency-aware aggregation mechanism. Extensive experiments show that FedAbA outperforms representative federated LoRA baselines, with the largest gains under the strongest rank heterogeneity.

**comment/C1：** Accepted by NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-174"></a>

### 174. [FisherDistill: Diffusion Distribution Distillation via Closed-Form Fisher Critics](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07604v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07604

**作者：** Mahesh Bhosale、Yuanhao Zhai、Siwei Lyu、Junsong Yuan、David Doermann、Defang Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07604v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07604v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文面向扩散模型少步蒸馏，提出用冻结教师中间表示估计真实与生成样本的一、二阶统计量，导出闭式 Fisher 判别方向作为轻量精炼信号，替代对抗判别器。

**相关性简评：** 其用教师中间层特征的类内/类间统计构造闭式判别方向，可能为 LVR 中比较不同 latent 表示的内容与深度差异、设计无判别器的表示级训练信号提供可核查的统计工具，但原文并不研究多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FisherDistill: Diffusion Distribution Distillation via Closed-Form Fisher Critics

**abstract/A1：** Recent advances in diffusion distillation have enabled high-quality image generation in only one or a few network function evaluations (NFEs). Despite their efficiency, aggressively distilled generators often retain a residual distribution gap from their multi-step teachers, motivating recent methods to introduce adversarial refinement with a learned discriminator. In this work, we ask whether such refinement necessarily requires a learned adversarial discriminator. We introduce \\textbf{FisherDistill}, a simple alternative based on Fisher linear discriminant analysis. Using intermediate representations from the frozen teacher, we estimate the first- and second-order statistics of real and generated samples and derive a closed-form Fisher direction that maximally separates their feature distributions relative to within-class variation. This direction provides a lightweight refinement signal that complements the original distillation objective without requiring an additional adversarial discriminator or its optimization loop. Across multiple distillation frameworks, our approach consistently improves generation quality over their respective distilled baselines and substantially closes the gap to adversarially refined counterparts. Code: https://bhosalems.github.io/FisherDistill/

</details>

<a id="paper-175"></a>

### 175. [Beneath the Surface: What We Overlook When We Talk About Agent Evolution](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07624v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07624

**作者：** Xiaobo Wang、Zimu Wang、Siyu Ye、Jiaqi Li、Min Tang、Jun Bai、Yuling Shi、Ruobing Wang、Jingjing Zhang、Chaofan Wang、Kai Wu、Mengnan Qi、Kai Cai、Xiaodong Gu、Qi Liu、Zilong Zheng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07624v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07624v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文考察不改动模型权重、仅演化 agent harness 的递归自我改进是否带来可靠、高效且可迁移的提升，通过重复评测、受控扩展、组件干预与准入回放，发现新任务增益往往以丢失已覆盖任务为代价、可靠性提升常伴随部署成本上升，并据此提出 EvoAudit 改进选择—分析—变异—门控流程。

**相关性简评：** 其「覆盖丢失、收益与成本绑定、迁移适用条件、保留感知评测」的诊断框架，可能为 LVR 中 THINK/LOOK/STOP 的收益-成本权衡与更长推理链是否牺牲已解样本的评价设计提供参照，但论文本身不涉及视觉表示或 latent 状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beneath the Surface: What We Overlook When We Talk About Agent Evolution

**abstract/A1：** Agent harness evolution enables recursive self-improvement (RSI) without updating model weights. We examine whether higher benchmark scores yield reliable, efficient, and transferable improvement. We evaluate four methods across harnesses, general and coding tasks. Repeated evaluations, controlled agent expansion, component interventions, and admission replays reveal recurring patterns. All sixteen evolved agents gain previously uncovered tasks while losing previously covered ones. In one general-task case, reliability rises by 5.2 points despite losing ten previously covered tasks. Most reliability gains also increase deployment cost. Specific changes usually outperform generic reminders, but their benefits can change when combined. The highest-scoring agent does not yield the largest mean improvement in any of our four pools, and stricter admission can discard useful alternatives. Transfer selectively preserves gains: coverage usually falls on the general-task target across both harness types and paradigms, but rises on the coding target with the outer-layer-only harness. These findings motivate preservation-aware evaluation, tested decision rules, and explicit applicability conditions. As a constructive follow-up, EvoAudit turns these diagnostics into revisions of select, analyze, mutate, and gate, achieving the best performance.

</details>

<a id="paper-176"></a>

### 176. [Online Learning with Ordered Experts (via Two-point Feedback)](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07629v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07629

**作者：** Shiliang Zuo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07629v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07629v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究全序动作空间下的对抗在线学习：专家建议可任意变化与重排，每轮只查询两个当前建议并付其平均损失；针对分离单调损失与有界变差（BV）损失分别给出 regret 界，核心技术是保边际的端点流估计器与可逆配对采样。

**相关性简评：** 其「仅靠两次局部查询、借助伸缩恒等式为全部候选构造无偏损失估计」及保边际采样，可能为 LVR 中有限次证据获取、用局部观测估计内部状态与计算价值提供概念线索，但距视觉多模态推理较远，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Online Learning with Ordered Experts (via Two-point Feedback)

**abstract/A1：** We study adversarial online learning with expert recommendations in a totally ordered action space, where the experts' recommendations may change and be reordered arbitrarily. Our focus is two-point feedback: on each round the learner plays two current recommendations, observes both, and pays their average. We study two problems. In the separated monotone problem, the loss is the sum of a hidden nondecreasing component and a hidden nonincreasing component, and a query reveals the two components separately. In the BV problem, the losses are bounded and have bounded variation (BV) along the order, and a query reveals only the loss. In the separated monotone problem, two paid observations achieve \\\[ O\\bigl(\\log(\|\\cF\|T)\\sqrt{T\\log\|\\cF\|}\\bigr) \\\] regret against the best of $\|\\cF\|$ experts, within a logarithmic factor of the classical full-information bound. For the BV problem, we obtain $\\wtO(\\sqrt T+\|\\cF\|)$ regret. We give an application in dynamic pricing with expert advice, showing that two-option menus matches the full information rate up to $\\log$ factors. The main technical ingredient is a marginal-preserving endpoint flow estimator. Two observations reveal a loss increment along the order; random paths supply telescoping identities that turn this local information into unbiased loss estimates for all current recommendations. A reversible pair sampler preserves the learner's intended marginal at both played actions. The estimator's second-moment and stability bounds support online updates for both feedback models.

</details>

<a id="paper-177"></a>

### 177. [WaSP: Wasserstein Data Synthesis Pipeline for Solver-Free Operator Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07646v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07646

**作者：** Ryan Liu、Chuwei Wang、Ricardo Baptista、Andrew Stuart、Anima Anandkumar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07646v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07646v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 WaSP 数据合成流程：不依赖数值求解器，而在逐步逼近目标输入分布的中间分布路径上分阶段训练神经算子，每阶段用上一代理生成解、由精确 PDE 算子反推输入，并给出分布偏移的逐步误差传播界。

**相关性简评：** 其「沿中间分布路径分阶段自举、抑制合成数据与目标分布偏移并界定误差累积」的思路，可能为 LVR 中模型自生成推理轨迹的训练监督与分布漂移问题提供类比线索，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** WaSP: Wasserstein Data Synthesis Pipeline for Solver-Free Operator Learning

**abstract/A1：** Neural operators offer fast surrogates for solving PDEs, but training them typically requires expensive input-solution pairs. Two solver-free alternatives include physics-informed learning and training on manufactured data. Physics-informed learning approximates solutions by minimizing PDE residuals. While the residual norm must be strong enough to control solution error, stronger norms increase mini-batch variance and hinder optimization. Manufactured data constructs input-solution pairs for learning by applying the PDE operator to artificial solutions. However, the resulting training distribution can be mutually singular with the target distribution, yielding poor accuracy on the target distribution. We introduce the Wasserstein Data Synthesis Pipeline (WaSP), which trains on manufactured input-solution pairs while gradually mitigating the distribution shift. WaSP synthesizes new pairs by applying the learned operator to a path of intermediate distributions converging to the target input distribution. Training proceeds in stages along this path. At each stage, the previous surrogate generates solutions, and the exact PDE operator derives corresponding inputs, yielding training pairs for the next surrogate. Theoretically, we prove a stepwise error-propagation bound for the distribution shift introduced during synthesis. Empirically, WaSP reduces solution error by $3.9\\times$ to $9.5\\times$ compared to existing solver-free methods on PDEs such as Allen-Cahn, Kuramoto-Sivashinsky, and Ginzburg-Landau, and better reproduces the two chaotic systems' statistical observables.

</details>

<a id="paper-178"></a>

### 178. [PharmAgents2: Towards Autonomous End-to-End Drug Discovery](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07653v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07653

**作者：** Haichuan Tan、Bowen Gao、Tao Huang、Jiaxin Tan、Xin Hong、Baoguo Yao、Yifei Wang、Zhichao Deng、Hanji Du、Dapeng Jiang、Yiheng Li、Weiwei Ma、Xin Ma、Yuqi Shi、Zixiang Wang、Kelin Wu、Tianxiang Wu、Wenxuan Xie、Jie Zang、Huiyu Zhang、Mojia Zhang、Yuying Zhang、Ya-Qin Zhang、Wei-Ying Ma、Yanyan Lan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、q-bio.QM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07653v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07653v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出端到端自主药物发现系统 PharmAgents2：用 Paper2Skill 从公开来源抽取可迁移的科学决策知识，配合专家技能支持研究决策；Self-Scaling Discovery 以继承研究上下文的并行智能体跨靶点、假设与策略扩展探索；并构建含 121 题、九个决策场景的 PharmAgents-Bench v0.1 及过程评分。

**相关性简评：** 属药物发现领域应用，但长程证据获取与筛选、并行智能体间研究上下文/决策状态的继承，以及以过程评分评价探索是否充分，可能为 LVR 的证据筛选、状态传播与停止决策提供类比线索，需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PharmAgents2: Towards Autonomous End-to-End Drug Discovery

**abstract/A1：** Drug discovery requires continuous scientific decision-making across a vast research space. We introduce PharmAgents2, an autonomous system for end-to-end drug discovery that combines knowledge-enhanced decision-making with organized large-scale exploration. Paper2Skill extracts transferable scientific decision knowledge from public sources and, together with expert-developed skills, supports research decisions. Self-Scaling Discovery expands exploration through parallel agents inheriting research context and decision-making capabilities across targets, scientific hypotheses, and discovery strategies. We introduce PharmAgents-Bench v0.1, comprising 121 questions across nine decision scenarios and three end-to-end tasks assessed with expert-defined process rubrics. PharmAgents2 outperforms evaluated baselines in most decision categories and achieves the highest process scores on all three end-to-end tasks. In three real-world studies, autonomous investigations generated findings subsequently supported by human-executed experiments. For LpxH, PharmAgents2 hypothesized a previously unreported allosteric site and screened compounds against it, identifying a novel inhibitor scaffold with experimentally confirmed inhibitory activity and binding. For JAK1, it prioritized a covalent inhibition strategy and nominated compounds with experimentally demonstrated activity. Starting from a known bioactive molecule, Self-Scaling Discovery explored candidate targets across the proteome and identified a new binding target, with the interaction subsequently confirmed experimentally. PharmAgents2 represents a prototype of a virtual pharmaceutical company, marking a step toward autonomous end-to-end drug discovery.

</details>

<a id="paper-179"></a>

### 179. [Simple Meta-Learning for HPO and CASH Using Performance Representations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07659v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07659

**作者：** Emiel Witting、Cheng Yan、Tom Julian Viering

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07659v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07659v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究以「配置在历史任务上的表现向量」替代显式超参数编码作为代理模型输入，用于 HPO/CASH 中的迭代候选选择，并尝试将推荐系统的 MMR 多样性重排作为无后验的采集规则。

**相关性简评：** 用历史表现构成的状态表示、以及按多样性重排挑选下一候选的思路，可能与 LVR 中候选 latent 假设的表示与视觉证据筛选存在形式类比，但任务领域相距较远，是否可迁移需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Simple Meta-Learning for HPO and CASH Using Performance Representations

**abstract/A1：** Bayesian optimization for Hyperparameter Optimization (HPO) and Combined Algorithm Selection and Hyperparameter Optimization (CASH) often relies on explicit hyperparameter encodings as surrogate inputs. In high-dimensional search spaces, however, such representations can make it difficult for standard surrogates to learn useful performance models. We instead study an alternative meta-learning representation that describes each configuration directly as its historical performance across prior tasks. This representation can be paired directly with standard surrogate models. Our scope is the iterative selection component of AutoML, deciding which configuration to evaluate next from a fixed candidate set, rather than a complete framework with features such as runtime estimation, cost-aware budgeting and ensembling. We further apply this representation to maximal marginal relevance (MMR), a diversity-based re-ranking criterion from recommender systems, as a posterior-free acquisition rule. When selecting and optimising across multiple models on the TabArena benchmark, the performance-based representation paired with a linear Bayesian ridge regression model outperforms dedicated meta-learning baselines, achieving the same tuned performance in roughly half the number of trials. For single-model HPO, it remains a competitive second. Across both benchmarks this method closely matches or improves on regret at a fraction of the complexity and cost, requiring no pre-training or tuning and scaling independently of search space dimensionality. Code for reproducing our results is available at https://github.com/EWitting/simple-metalearning.

**comment/C1：** 24 pages, 9 figures, 7 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-180"></a>

### 180. [Which Objects Stay? Learning Hierarchical Persistence Beliefs for Lifelong Open-Vocabulary Object Maps](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07662v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07662

**作者：** Thanh Nguyen Canh、Haolan Zhang、Nak Young Chong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07662v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07662v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 HiPer，用视觉与几何相似实例的观测历史估计物体存续先验，初始化 Beta 信念，并依据检测与漏检进行闭式更新，用于终身开放词表物体地图。

**相关性简评：** 其显式存续信念与漏检更新机制可能为 LVR 中的证据累积及「是否继续看/停止」决策提供概念参照，但任务相距较远，可迁移性仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Which Objects Stay? Learning Hierarchical Persistence Beliefs for Lifelong Open-Vocabulary Object Maps

**abstract/A1：** Lifelong object maps must estimate whether an object remains at its previously observed location after it leaves view. Existing persistence filters rely on class-based survival priors, making them difficult to apply reliably in open-vocabulary settings. We introduce HiPer, a hierarchical persistence filter that derives persistence beliefs directly from data rather than semantic categories. For a new object, HiPer retrieves visually and geometrically similar training instances and estimates an initial survival rate from their observed histories, without requiring class labels. This estimate initializes a conjugate Beta belief over the object's survival probability, which is updated in closed form using detections and missed observations under Rosen's persistence-filter framework. When an object is re-identified at a new location, its persistence belief is transferred with the object, enabling the map to learn object-specific mobility over time. Experiments on 120 held-out simulated sequences show that HiPer consistently achieves the best performance among competing methods. Retrieval-based priors outperform CLIP-derived label priors, and object-specific online learning provides further gains. Results on 47 held-out 3RScan environments demonstrate that the proposed retrieval-based prior also outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in real-world settings.

</details>

<a id="paper-181"></a>

### 181. [CoGenRec: A Unified Generative Framework for Complementary Vision Reconstruction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07665v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07665

**作者：** Yapeng Meng、Yuguo Chen、Xiangru Chen、Shaojun Xu、Xuezhen Wang、Lijian Wang、Zheyu Yang、Yihan Lin、Luping Shi、Rong Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07665v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07665v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 CoGenRec，一个面向互补视觉传感器（长曝光 RGB 通路与短曝光时空差分通路）的统一生成式重建框架：用曝光感知外观建模与双向时序传播编码多尺度时空特征，条件化视频扩散模型并注入 VAE 解码器，统一处理去模糊、高帧率插值与 HDR 融合。

**相关性简评：** 原文指出 latent 空间压缩会丢失细结构，并借助物理测量特征向 VAE 解码器侧注入来补偿；这一「压缩导致视觉细节流失、需外部证据回填」的现象，可能为 LVR 中视觉细节是否保留在内部状态及信息坍缩评估提供可核查线索，但论文不涉及推理生成与停止决策。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CoGenRec: A Unified Generative Framework for Complementary Vision Reconstruction

**abstract/A1：** Capturing scenes with extreme motion and high dynamic range is fundamentally constrained by trade-offs among temporal resolution, spatial detail, signal-to-noise ratio, and dynamic range under practical bandwidth, power, and quantum-efficiency limits. Conventional frame-based cameras therefore suffer from temporal undersampling, exposure-induced motion blur, and sensor saturation. The Complementary Vision Sensor (CVS) addresses these limitations by decomposing visual information across two complementary pathways, a spatially dense, temporally sparse, long-exposure RGB pathway and a temporally dense, spatially sparse, short-exposure patiotemporal difference pathway. Their complementary measurements provide appearance, structural, and motion cues across a wide dynamic range that enable reconstruction beyond conventional imaging. We present CoGenRec, a complementarity-driven generative reconstruction framework that unifies video deblurring, high-temporal-resolution frame interpolation, and HDR fusion. CoGenRec encodes complementary measurements into multi-scale spatiotemporal features using exposure-aware appearance modeling and bidirectional temporal propagation. These features constrain a video diffusion model and are injected into the VAE decoder to recover fine structures lost during latent-space compression, combining the scene fidelity of physical measurements with the appearance recovery capabilities of learned video priors. On SportsSloMo-CVS, CoGenRec improves PSNR over the strongest general-purpose baseline by 4.40 dB for deblurring, 3.89 dB for frame interpolation, and 5.04 dB for HDR fusion, respectively. It also achieves strong perceptual quality on 129 unseen scenes captured with real CVS. These results establish a unified approach to advance high-quality reconstruction under challenging motion and illumination conditions. Project page: https://cogenrec.github.io/.

</details>

<a id="paper-182"></a>

### 182. [Who Should Know What? Empirical Information Design for Multi-Agent LLM Economies](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07667v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07667

**作者：** Mete Erdogan、Mirac Suzgun、Aneesh Pappu、Guowei Xu、Batu El、Andrew A. Schoen、Furong Huang、John P. Dickerson、Mert Pilanci、James Zou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07667v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07667v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出「经验性信息设计」框架，在固定交互规则与模型的前提下，比较 LLM 代理在交易、双边议价和双向拍卖中接收不同粒度信息披露（如精确估值与序数偏好、完全披露与部分披露）时的集体结果，并对十个前沿模型做对照实验。

**相关性简评：** 该工作考察披露信息粒度如何改变代理的决策与集体效率，可为 LVR 中「视觉证据该保留多少细节」「信息坍缩的代价是否随模型而异」的假设提供类比性核查线索，但其观察对象是外部披露策略而非内部 latent 状态，迁移需谨慎。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Who Should Know What? Empirical Information Design for Multi-Agent LLM Economies

**abstract/A1：** The performance of multi-agent LLM systems depends not only on the capabilities of the underlying models but also on information-sharing policies, which govern what information each agent observes and at what level of detail. But does providing AI agents with more detailed information always improve collective outcomes, or can transparency paradoxically degrade efficiency? To investigate this, we introduce empirical information design, an experimental framework that systematically compares disclosure policies across economic markets populated by LLM agents, while holding interaction rules and models fixed. We evaluate ten frontier LLMs in multi-resource trade, bilateral bargaining, and double auctions, using ground-truth utilities to decouple identified opportunities from realized outcomes. We find that finer information disclosure yields highly model-dependent results.
