In trade, revealing exact valuations rather than ordinal preference rankings reduces realized gains for GPT-6 Sol (from 98% to 91% of maximum) yet increases them for Claude Sonnet 5 (from 72% to 86%). In bargaining, exact information leads Grok 4.7 to produce less balanced agreements, whereas Claude Opus 5.5 divides gains symmetrically. In double auctions, full disclosure inflates price demands, triggering market breakdown and lowering welfare for Gemini 3.1 Pro (97% to 78%), Claude Opus 5.5 (94% to 64%), and Grok 4.7 (97% to 62%). Overall, these results suggest that model selection and information design must be co-optimized, as LLMs vary in how their strategic responses to information impact system objectives.

</details>

<a id="paper-183"></a>

### 183. [Calibrated Target Noise Recovers Curvature from the Gradients](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07671v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07671

**作者：** Arash Jamshidi、Katsiaryna Haitsiukevich、Aristides Gionis、Kai Puolamäki

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07671v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07671v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出一种目标扰动方案：向训练目标注入方差与局部输出曲率校准的零均值噪声，仅依靠聚合 mini-batch 梯度的协方差即可恢复 GGN 矩阵，并在 ReLU 前馈网络上恢复总体 Hessian 的对角块，用于预条件优化与早停。

**相关性简评：** 论文用梯度协方差廉价估计曲率并据此早停，可能为 latent 推理中的动态计算分配与 STOP 决策提供不依赖内部状态的信号，可供核查；但其早停针对训练优化而非推理期计算，联系需较多检验。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Calibrated Target Noise Recovers Curvature from the Gradients

**abstract/A1：** Estimating curvature information from gradients alone is a fundamental problem in machine learning. The challenge becomes particularly acute in settings where only aggregate mini-batch gradients are available, yet curvature information is still required for applications such as preconditioning in optimisation, sampling, and early stopping. Perhaps surprisingly, we show that a simple target-perturbation scheme suffices to recover such curvature information from gradients alone. Our method injects zero-mean noise into the targets, with variance calibrated to the local output curvature. The covariance of the resulting noisy batch gradients then recovers practical curvature matrices, without requiring per-sample gradients, second-order derivatives, or other internal network quantities. For a broad class of non-linear models, our scheme recovers the Generalised Gauss--Newton (GGN) matrix; for feed-forward ReLU networks, it additionally recovers the diagonal blocks of the population Hessian. We demonstrate that the resulting estimator is simple, lightweight, provably optimal, and easy to integrate into existing training pipelines. Experiments demonstrate accurate curvature recovery, faster optimisation when the estimator is used for preconditioning, and the utility of the estimated GGN for early stopping.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026. 39 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-184"></a>

### 184. [Instrumental Variables in Continuous-Time Stochastic Processes](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07674v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07674

**作者：** Lars Pennig、Søren Wengel Mogensen、Niki Kilbertus

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07674v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07674v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文为平稳连续时间随机过程（由 SDE 参数化）建立工具变量框架，在漂移项的无穷小层面编码结构稀疏性与因果效应，用路径值工具导出矩条件，并以 signature kernel 扩展到非线性因果效应。

**相关性简评：** 其路径值工具与签名核可能为核查 latent 推理轨迹中状态或视觉证据的因果贡献、以及连续时间状态抽象提供形式化线索，但领域距离较远，需人工评估可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Instrumental Variables in Continuous-Time Stochastic Processes

**abstract/A1：** Causal questions often arise in systems that evolve in continuous time. While instrumental variable (IV) methods provide a principled approach to identify causal effects in static or discrete-time settings, even in the presence of unobserved confounding, they do not yet extend to continuous-time dynamics. In this work, we develop an IV framework for stationary continuous-time stochastic processes parameterized by stochastic differential equations (SDE), where the instrument itself takes the form of a stochastic process. We encode the structural sparsity and causal effects directly at the infinitesimal level of the drift, and contrast these assumptions with the IV assumptions on the discretized version of the same process. Based on these structural assumptions, we derive moment restrictions using path-valued instruments. We show how these restrictions simplify for Ornstein-Uhlenbeck processes and present an identification condition together with a corresponding consistent estimation method. Finally, we extend our estimation framework to nonlinear causal effects by expressing the path-based moment restrictions leveraging the signature kernel. Empirically, our method recovers causal effects in confounded continuous-time systems where maximum-likelihood-based estimation and discrete-time IV methods are systematically biased.

</details>

<a id="paper-185"></a>

### 185. [$O(1/n)$ Convergence Rate for RVI $Q$-Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07676v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07676

**作者：** Avik Kar、Siddharth Chandak、Rahul Singh、Shalabh Bhatnagar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07676v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07676v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文为有限弱通信 MDP 上的同步相对值迭代（RVI）Q-learning 建立有限时间收敛率：先对非扩张、线性正则映射驱动的随机 Krasnosel'skiĭ–Mann 迭代给出末迭代均方残差界，再推出 O(1/n) 的 Bellman span 残差与贪心策略期望次优性界，并给出 O(ε^{-1}) 迭代复杂度。

**相关性简评：** 其关于非扩张算子迭代的残差型收敛界与迭代预算结论，可能为 latent 循环/迭代式状态更新的停止判据与「计算量—误差」权衡提供一个可核查的分析模板，但需确认高维表示状态是否满足线性正则与噪声条件。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** $O(1/n)$ Convergence Rate for RVI $Q$-Learning

**abstract/A1：** We establish finite-time convergence guarantee for synchronous relative value iteration (RVI) $Q$-learning for finite weakly communicating Markov decision processes (MDPs). For stochastic Krasnosel'skiĭ--Mann iterations driven by a non-expansive, linearly regular map on a finite-dimensional normed space, we establish last-iterate mean-squared residual bounds under martingale-difference noise whose conditional second moment grows at most linearly with the squared residual. Suitable polynomial stepsizes of order $n^{-a}$ yield an $O(n^{-a})$ rate for $0&lt;a\\leq1$. We verify linear regularity and the noise condition for the centered Bellman operator for finite weakly communicating MDPs. Applying the general result gives an $O(n^{-1})$ bound on the mean-squared Bellman span residual of RVI $Q$-learning iterates when a suitable stepsize sequence is used. The positive gap between optimal and suboptimal stationary deterministic policies then yields an $O(n^{-1})$ bound on the expected suboptimality of the greedy policy. Consequently, $O(ε^{-1})$ synchronous iterations suffice for an $ε$-optimal policy in expectation. These guarantees are instance-dependent, with constants that depend on the hitting-time parameters and the suboptimality gap of the second-best policy.

**comment/C1：** 24 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-186"></a>

### 186. [GEKey: Learning Geodesic Eccentric 3D Keypoints via Self-Supervision](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07677v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07677

**作者：** Pierre Onghena、Santiago Velasco-Forero、Beatriz Marcotegui

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07677v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07677v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 GEKey，一个自监督 3D 点云关键点检测框架：以 SE(3) 不变的大地测地偏心度变换刻画实例内的显著点，并将其作为伪标签训练检测模型，在多个点云数据集上做检测与对齐评测。

**相关性简评：** 该文用内在几何测度衡量「哪些点最具信息量」并据此稀疏筛选，可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选与显著性先验提供可核查的概念线索，但其本身不涉及多模态推理或 latent 状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GEKey: Learning Geodesic Eccentric 3D Keypoints via Self-Supervision

**abstract/A1：** Point clouds represent 3D object geometry as sets of spatial coordinates. A fundamental task in their analysis is keypoint detection, i.e., identifying a sparse subset of points that captures the most salient features. While supervised methods rely on manual annotations, unsupervised approaches leverage intrinsic geometric properties to localize keypoints without human input. We introduce GEKey, a self-supervised framework that uses the geodesic eccentricity transform, an SE(3)-invariant geometric measure, to identify instance-specific salient points. These points serve as pseudo-labels to train a model that aligns its predictions with the object's intrinsic structure. GEKey is simple, efficient, and robust, preserving intra-class variation by balancing instance-specific and category-level structure. Extensive experiments on ShapeNetCoreV2, KeypointNet, CAPE, ITOP, and DeformingThings4D show state-of-the-art performance in both keypoint detection and alignment, with strong resilience to noise, downsampling, deformations, and real-world conditions. The code and pretrained models are available at https://github.com/Pierreoo/GEKey.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to BMVC 2026 (Oral)

</details>

<a id="paper-187"></a>

### 187. [MCPHallu: Benchmarking Reasoning, Execution, and Memory Hallucinations in MCP Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07689v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07689

**作者：** Yizhen Jiang、Haowei Guo、Tianyi Bai、Zengjie Hu、Lu Ma、Zhengyang Zhao、Haoze Sun、Peng Pei、Wentao Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07689v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07689v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文构建 MCP 智能体的幻觉诊断基准，沿推理、执行、记忆三条轴用结构化触发器诱发「分支坍缩」「目标不可达」「工具误用」「上下文遗忘」四类失败，在 36 个真实 MCP 服务上评测 14 个前沿模型。

**相关性简评：** 其「目标不可达」下模型仍持续发起工具调用或宣称成功、以及上下文遗忘等失败模式，可能为 LVR 中停止决策与推理状态维护的评价设计提供参照，但该基准为纯文本智能体设定、不含视觉或 latent 状态，与主线距离较远，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MCPHallu: Benchmarking Reasoning, Execution, and Memory Hallucinations in MCP Agents

**abstract/A1：** Large language model agents are increasingly deployed through the Model Context Protocol (MCP), which allows them to discover and invoke tools across multiple servers at runtime. Existing MCP benchmarks primarily measure task completion; where they diagnose failures, they do so after execution rather than building specific reasoning, execution, and memory failure risks into task construction. We introduce MCPHallu, a benchmark for diagnosing hallucination-prone agent failures in MCP environments along three axes: reasoning, execution, and memory. MCPHallu contains 358 tasks across five capability domains and runs on 36 production MCP servers in isolated containers. Each task is designed with a structural trigger that makes one of four subtypes the dominant failure risk: Branch Collapse, Unreachable Goal, Tool Misuse, or Context Forgetting. This design enables subtype-level analysis rather than aggregate task success alone. We evaluate fourteen frontier LLM agents from eight vendors on 5{,}012 trajectories. Current MCP agents remain vulnerable to hallucination-inducing tool environments, achieving an average score of 0.642 on a $\[0,1\]$ scale (higher is better). Failures concentrate on Unreachable Goal tasks, where the average score drops to 0.458 and 23.2\\% of runs pass, indicating that agents often continue issuing tool calls or claim successful completion rather than recognizing infeasible goals. Subtype rankings diverge sharply: a model's rank shifts by 6.9 of 13 positions on average across subtypes, and Branch Collapse is uncorrelated with Unreachable Goal across models ($r = -0.12$). MCPHallu is released at https://github.com/J1zz/mcp\_hallu.

</details>

<a id="paper-188"></a>

### 188. [Equivariant Deep Learning in Direct-Product Space for Electronic-Structure Hamiltonian Prediction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07695v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07695

**作者：** Shi Yin、Zujian Dai、Xinyang Pan、Lixin He

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cond-mat.mtrl-sci、cs.AI、physics.comp-ph

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07695v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07695v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 DP-Learner，把等变消息传递直接建立在直积空间中的类 Hamiltonian 算符块上，用李生成元方向嵌入与不变加权左右积保持 SO(3)/SU(2) 等变性，无需显式 Clebsch–Gordan 分解，并构建了十万级 Hamiltonian 数据集。

**相关性简评：** 其「表示空间选择应与目标结构对齐」的论点与不变标量门控路由，可能为 LVR 中如何组织保留几何与关系结构的 latent 状态提供关于表示几何的线索，但属远域迁移，需核查其与推理状态的关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Equivariant Deep Learning in Direct-Product Space for Electronic-Structure Hamiltonian Prediction

**abstract/A1：** Most existing equivariant architectures for Hamiltonian prediction propagate features in direct-sum spaces of irreducible spherical tensors, whereas the target Hamiltonian is naturally an operator block in a direct-product representation over local orbital and spin bases. This representation mismatch fragments contiguous Hamiltonian blocks, obscures direct manipulation of orbital couplings, and organizes features into irregular angular-momentum components that require numerous transformations and are poorly aligned with efficient hardware execution and memory management. To address both the representation mismatch and computational bottleneck, we propose DP-Learner, an equivariant learning framework that performs message passing directly on Hamiltonian-like operator blocks in the direct-product space. Theoretically, we propose Lie-generator-based directional embedding and invariantly weighted left--right products and contractions that preserve equivariance to SO(3), SU(2), and spatial inversions, without explicit Clebsch--Gordan decomposition or inverse reconstruction. Methodologically, we instantiate this principle as a Transformer architecture, in which Hamiltonian-like feature matrices or tensors evolve through equivariant operators in the direct-product space, while non-linear invariant scalar routing controls structured gating over matrix units, directional propagation, attention, and environment-embedded context interactions. We further construct MP-HAM-110K, a large-scale Hamiltonian dataset comprising more than 110,000 material structures covering 68 elements with both Hamiltonian and wavefunction data. Experiments on MP-HAM-110K and Materials-HAM-SOC demonstrate that DP-Learner dramatically improves Hamiltonian prediction accuracy and inference efficiency over the state-of-the-art method.

</details>

<a id="paper-189"></a>

### 189. [FlyingDrones: A Dataset and Benchmark for Optical Flow Estimation from UAV motion](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07705v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07705

**作者：** Laurenz Ruzicka、Roman Pflugfelder、Daniel Cremers

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07705v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07705v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 FlyingDrones，一个基于物理渲染生成、带稠密光流真值与分割掩码的无人机运动估计基准，定义按图像尺寸归一化的 EPE 指标，并在三种分辨率下比较 16 类光流模型，另做合成到真实画面的排序相关性分析与小样本微调实验。

**相关性简评：** 该文不涉及 VLM 或 latent 推理状态；但「外观信息弱时由运动提供线索」及小目标在低分辨率下像素极少的问题，可能与动态背景中视觉证据筛选、分辨率对内部状态信息保留的影响存在概念联系，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FlyingDrones: A Dataset and Benchmark for Optical Flow Estimation from UAV motion

**abstract/A1：** Optical flow is an important cue for drone analysis in aerial surveillance, airspace monitoring, and long-range target tracking, where motion remains informative when appearance is weak. Estimating the motion of small Unmanned Aerial Vehicles, however, is difficult because distant targets occupy few pixels, have low contrast, and move against complex dynamic backgrounds. Standard optical-flow benchmarks emphasize large, object-centric motions and therefore do not characterize distant aerial targets well. We introduce FlyingDrones, a generated benchmark with dense ground-truth optical flow and segmentation masks derived from physically based rendering, and define resolution-normalized metrics (EPE Norm) that normalize flow by image dimensions. We evaluate 16 state-of-the-art model families on a motion- and size-stratified subset of 999 flow pairs at three resolutions. Every family recovers drone motion better than the zero-flow reference, but the gap between families widens with resolution: at 1920x1080, global-matching, recurrent, transformer, and short-window temporal models separate clearly from pyramid, classical, self-supervised, lightweight, and diffusion models. Within-family checkpoint spread is comparable to or larger than between-family spread, so the training curriculum is as important as the architecture. Two complementary studies test the benchmark beyond synthetic accuracy: a ground-truth-free motion-alignment evaluation on 21 cameras of real UAV footage shows that the synthetic ranking carries a moderate, significant signal about real-world performance (Spearman \\r{ho} = 0.63 over 41 checkpoints) while the exact top ranks differ, and a fine-tuning study shows that 800 FlyingDrones pairs reduce held-out drone-region error by up to 37% and improve drone localization on the real footage for all four architectures tested.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026, https://neurips.cc/virtual/2026/loc/sydney/poster/138996

</details>

<a id="paper-190"></a>

### 190. [Towards Generalist Graph-Level Anomaly Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07706v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07706

**作者：** Junjun Pan、Yixin Liu、Yu Zheng、Fuyi Li、Alan Wee-Chung Liew、Shirui Pan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07706v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07706v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 GenGLAD，研究跨域通用的图级异常检测：用基于 GNN 的判别性子图提取学习可迁移的异常知识，并在测试时通过子图与多级上下文的差异及轻量评分模块实现少监督适配。

**相关性简评：** 其「以判别性子结构充当证据、用多视角差异刻画异常、再以信息量评分抑制误导模式」的思路，可能与 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选与信息价值判断有概念联系，可供核查；但该文不涉及视觉或多模态推理，迁移线索较弱。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Towards Generalist Graph-Level Anomaly Detection

**abstract/A1：** Graph-level anomaly detection (GLAD) aims to identify graphs that deviate significantly from the majority. While recent advances have achieved competitive performance, existing approaches mainly follow a dataset-specific paradigm, requiring substantial in-domain data and training cost when applied to new application scenarios. This limits their scalability and practicality in real-world applications where labeled data is scarce and diverse domains are continuously emerging. In this paper, we investigate the problem of generalist GLAD, which seeks to learn a single unified model capable of detecting anomalous graphs across multiple domains with minimal supervision on the target data. This problem introduces key challenges, including learning transferable patterns from heterogeneous graph datasets and effective adaptation under limited supervision. To address these challenges, we propose GenGLAD, a generalist GLAD approach that leverages discriminative subgraph extraction as the core mechanism to learn transferable anomaly knowledge and enable efficient adaptation to new domains. Specifically, GenGLAD employs a graph neural network-based discriminative subgraph extraction model to learn the anomaly-discriminative substructures that distinguish anomalous and normal graphs across diverse domains. During the test-time adaptation phase, multi-dimensional discrepancies between the extracted subgraph and multi-level contextual information are calculated to characterize anomalies from multiple perspectives. For efficient adaptation, a lightweight scoring module is introduced to automatically decide the most informative signals, improving robustness against misleading patterns. Extensive experiments on diverse benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves strong performance, superior generalization ability, and improved data efficiency compared to existing methods.

**comment/C1：** Accepted by NIPS26

</details>

<a id="paper-191"></a>

### 191. [Fast and Accurate Hand Estimation in Pixels, Interaction and World Space](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07711v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07711

**作者：** Jinkun Cao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07711v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07711v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 FAH 系列单目手部网格估计模型：通过微调与特征蒸馏复用 SAM 3D Body 的手部分支，同时预测 2D/3D 关节点与表面点并拟合 MANO/MHR-Hand，可选估计部位与接触，并用因果细化与时序相机估计提升精度与世界坐标跟踪。

**相关性简评：** 论文直接预测 3D 关节点而非由网格投影、再以预测点反向拟合网格，以及时序因果细化状态的做法，可能为 LVR 中几何细节是否保留在内部表示、以及 latent 状态如何迭代更新的监督设计提供可供核查的旁证，但与其推理主线无直接关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Fast and Accurate Hand Estimation in Pixels, Interaction and World Space

**abstract/A1：** Monocular hand mesh estimators commonly predict rig parameters and project the mesh to obtain 2D keypoints. We find that directly predicted 3D joints agree better with 2D predictions, and fitting a mesh to predicted points reduces mesh error. We present FAH (Fast and Accurate Hand Estimation), a family of models built on SAM 3D Body's hand branch through fine-tuning and feature distillation. The models predict corresponding 2D and 3D joints and surface points, fit MANO or MHR-Hand, and optionally estimate anatomical parts and contact without object geometry at inference. Causal refinement reduces acceleration error, and camera estimation places the hands in world space. Experiments show improvements in image-space alignment, hand geometry, contact estimation and world-space tracking over the evaluated baselines. The default ViT-L model runs at 65 fps with mesh fitting on an RTX 4070 Ti SUPER, or 49 fps with parts and contact enabled. The smallest ViT-S model reaches 125 fps without mesh fitting, parts or contact. Code: https://github.com/noahcao/FAH.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages of main text plus 15 pages of supplementary material; 5 figures; 12 tables; code available at https://github.com/noahcao/FAH

</details>

<a id="paper-192"></a>

### 192. [Static Recovery Is Not Dynamic Stability: Dynamics-Aware Benchmarking of Protein Motif Scaffolding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07723v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07723

**作者：** Emile de Bruyn、Moritz Schäffler、Jannik Schneider、Sebastian R. Schmidt、Chanwoong Hwang、Aleena Siji、Utkarsh Upadhyay、Stefan Bauer、Alexander Schug、Stefan Kesselheim、Artur Yakimovich、Alina Bazarova

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、q-bio.BM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07723v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07723v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建 BILBY-PRO 蛋白 motif scaffolding 基准（220 例，源自 PROSITE 关联结构）并比较六个生成模型，同时提出基于参考全原子分子动力学的 BILBY-MD 评估框架，发现多数静态 refolding 成功的解并不能通过动态保真检查。

**相关性简评：** 其「静态通过率不等于动力学保真」的评价思路可能为 LVR 评价提供线索：是否仅检验最终答案、还是需检验推理轨迹与潜在状态的动态保真及证据保持，可供核查；但研究对象是蛋白设计，与视觉推理无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Static Recovery Is Not Dynamic Stability: Dynamics-Aware Benchmarking of Protein Motif Scaffolding

**abstract/A1：** Protein motif scaffolding benchmarks assess whether generative models can build scaffolds around functionally important residues. Their conclusions depend on which motifs are tested and which criteria define success. Existing benchmarks typically use small hand-curated motif sets and static refolding criteria, but protein function often depends on conformational ensembles and transitions. Here, we address both benchmark scope and success criteria. We introduce BILBY-PRO, a systematically derived benchmark of structurally conserved functional motifs from PROSITE-linked experimental structures, comprising 220 cases from 174 PROSITE motif-pattern entries. Evaluating six recent scaffold-generation models reveals distinct patterns in target coverage and unique-solution yield: RFDiffusion and Proteina solve more targets, whereas ESM3 produces more unique successful scaffolds on the targets it solves. We further introduce BILBY-MD, a reference-based all-atom molecular dynamics evaluation framework. Applied to statically solved designs from the original MotifBench benchmark, it compares motif drift, scaffold drift, motif-contact preservation, and motif-dihedral free-energy landscapes against reference simulations. Of 55 statically solved model-problem pairs, 31 satisfy our MD-supported criterion and only 8 pass all checks. Compact, self-contained motifs are often MD-supported, whereas interface-like or context-dependent motifs fail more frequently. Static refolding success therefore provides useful evidence of local motif recovery but does not establish dynamical fidelity of the full scaffold. BILBY highlights three complementary axes of motif-scaffolding evaluation: target coverage, unique-solution yield, and MD-supported dynamical fidelity.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026 (Evaluations &amp; Datasets Track). 23 pages, 10 figures, including supplementary material. The first three authors contributed equally. BILBY-PRO dataset: https://huggingface.co/datasets/HFMI-PROFOUND/BILBY-PRO ; BILBY-MD code: https://github.com/PROFOUND-HFMI/BILBY-MD

</details>

<a id="paper-193"></a>

### 193. [Comparing Classification and Feature Learning by Analyzing Their Loss Functions in Normalized Spaces with Different Scale Factors](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07741v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07741

**作者：** Qiufu Li、Huibin Xiao、Linlin Shen、Weijia Fan、Xi Jia、Yongdong Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07741v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07741v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文区分分类精度所反映的样本级局部性质与特征学习追求的类内紧凑、类间可分等数据集级全局性质，据此重新设计 BCE 损失，并在归一化特征空间中分析 BCE/CE 的极小值均导致 neural collapse，进而研究尺度因子 γ 如何使分类精度与特征几何收敛表现出现分化。

**相关性简评：** 其关于归一化特征空间中表示几何与 neural collapse 的刻画，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态是否保留任务必要信息、以及视觉特征坍缩的度量方式提供参照，但这种跨领域迁移尚需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Comparing Classification and Feature Learning by Analyzing Their Loss Functions in Normalized Spaces with Different Scale Factors

**abstract/A1：** Classification and feature learning are two closely interrelated fundamental tasks. Feature learning in supervised settings is typically achieved by training classification models, under the common assumption that higher classification accuracy implies better feature quality. In this paper, we first point out that classification accuracy reflects the sample-wise local properties of a model on a dataset, whereas feature learning aims to learn features with good dataset-level global properties, such as intra-class compactness and inter-class separability. From the first principle of feature learning, we redesign the binary cross-entropy (BCE) loss, a counterpart of the cross-entropy (CE) loss used in classification. Then, in normalized feature space, we analyze the minima of the BCE and CE losses and show that both can lead to neural collapse, where sample features from different classes become maximally compact and maximally separated. More importantly, by investigating the convergence behaviors of the two losses as the scale factor $γ$ varies in the normalized space, we reveal how classification accuracy and feature quality change differently across spaces of different sizes. Under a fixed training strategy, a very large $γ$ achieves perfect classification accuracy but slows convergence toward the ideal feature geometry, whereas a very small $γ$ makes classification highly sensitive to small perturbations. A moderate $γ$ is therefore necessary for simultaneously achieving reliable classification and well-structured features. Extensive experiments validate these findings and demonstrate that BCE loss exhibits advantages in long-tail and open-set recognitions, as it better aligns with the global requirements of feature learning.

</details>

<a id="paper-194"></a>

### 194. [Neural Collapse on Imbalanced Datasets and Open-Set Scenario](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07753v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07753

**作者：** Qiufu Li、Huibing Xiao、Linlin Shen、Yongdong Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07753v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07753v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在类别不平衡与开集设定下重新推导 Neural Collapse（NC），将分析对象从全局平均损失改为类别平均损失，证明 CE 与 BCE 达到最小时仍出现标准 NC；开集情形下未知类别的分类向量互相重合且与已知类正交；并分析按逆样本频率重平衡的对比损失。

**相关性简评：** NC 是对特征与分类向量几何结构的精确刻画，可能为核查 LVR 中 latent 状态是否按任务要素（如视觉细节、实体、证据类别）形成稳定几何、以及不均衡监督下表示是否坍缩提供分析视角，但原文不涉及多模态推理，迁移性需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Neural Collapse on Imbalanced Datasets and Open-Set Scenario

**abstract/A1：** Neural collapse (NC) describes the elegant geometric structure that sample features and classifier vectors exhibit when a classification model's loss reaches its minimum. Class imbalance, however, generally distorts standard NC. In this paper, we theoretically reformulate NC on imbalanced datasets and in open-set settings. Our analysis is based on an optimization problem over class-wise averaged losses, rather than the global averaged losses used in prior NC studies. We prove that when class-wise averaged losses attain their minima, standard NC is achieved on balanced or imbalanced datasets, for both cross-entropy (CE) and binary cross-entropy (BCE) losses. We further extend the analysis to an open-set-inspired scenario, where some classes contain no training samples yet are still assigned classifier vectors in the losses. We find that in this scenario, when class-wise averaged losses reach their minima, standard NC still emerges on the known classes with training samples, while the classifier vectors of unknown classes without training samples overlap with each other and become orthogonal to those of the known classes. Finally, we analyze Softmax- and Sigmoid-based contrastive losses re-balanced by inverse sample frequency, proving that at their minima on balanced or imbalanced datasets, sample features of different classes likewise form standard NC.

</details>

<a id="paper-195"></a>

### 195. [Scaling Laws for SAE Training Data](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07756v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07756

**作者：** Nikita Koriagin、Nikita Balagansky、Daniil Gavrilov

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07756v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07756v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究固定训练预算下 SAE 需要多少独特激活：质量随缓冲区增大快速提升后进入平台，重放旧激活几乎等同使用新激活，据此给出多样性-重复的有限数据 scaling law，并在 Llama-3.1、Qwen3 多层、多种 SAE 变体上用 EV、MSE、特征吸收、自动解释性与稀疏探测验证，报告存储可降低 8–64 倍。

**相关性简评：** 其「独特数据量与重复的权衡及质量平台」可能为 latent 推理中需要多少不同推理状态、状态缓存与重放的预算分配提供可核查的类比与指标，但与视觉推理主线尚无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Scaling Laws for SAE Training Data

**abstract/A1：** Activation storage is often a bottleneck in sparse autoencoder (SAE) training. We study how many unique activations an SAE needs when its total training budget is fixed. Quality improves rapidly as the buffer grows, then reaches a plateau where replaying the buffer is nearly as effective as using fresh activations throughout training. Beyond this point, additional unique activations bring little benefit to reconstruction or interpretability, so the buffer need not grow with the training budget. We describe this tradeoff between diversity and repetition with a scaling law for limited data. We validate it across dictionary sizes and token budgets, early, middle, and late layers of Llama-3.1, Qwen3 model sizes, and BatchTopK, TopK, and JumpReLU SAEs. Sweeps using mathematical text and Python code test the effect of changing the activation source. Evaluation covers explained variance (EV), reconstruction MSE, feature absorption, automated interpretability, and sparse probing. These results give a practical training rule: choose the smallest buffer that reaches the quality plateau and replay it for the remaining budget. In our experiments, this reduces activation storage by $8\\text{--}64\\times$ with negligible quality loss.

</details>

<a id="paper-196"></a>

### 196. [RIVER: Evidence-guided multi-agent retrieval for enzyme--reaction discovery](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07757v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07757

**作者：** Yifan Chang、Yunze Li、Feifei Jiang、Juntao Wu、Xin Chen、Han Hu、Yucheng Wu、Wei Tang、Mingqian Ma、Wanli Ouyang、Kaipeng Zhang、Lei Bai、Hongting Tang、Feiran Li、Yuchen Ren

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07757v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07757v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** RIVER 提出多智能体框架，协调 LLM、生化计算工具与外部知识资源，在反应化学、蛋白序列与结构域、催化机制、数据库注释等多层证据上完成双向酶—反应检索，并用验证模块检查证据缺口与冲突后重排候选。

**相关性简评：** 其「多源证据汇总—缺口与冲突检查—可追溯重排」流程可能为 VLM 推理中的视觉证据筛选、证据不足判断与停止决策提供问题表述线索，但领域相隔较远，迁移可行性需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RIVER: Evidence-guided multi-agent retrieval for enzyme--reaction discovery

**abstract/A1：** Enzyme function annotation and the identification of candidate enzymes for a given reaction are essential steps in connecting genomic information to biochemical reaction networks. However, existing methods rely on sequence or structural similarity or on training with known enzyme--reaction pairs, making it difficult to integrate biochemical evidence across large candidate spaces; they also struggle with retrieval tasks involving enzyme families whose members are highly similar. Here, we introduce RIVER (Reaction--enzyme Inference via Evidence-guided Retrieval), a multi-agent framework for bidirectional enzyme--reaction retrieval that identifies potentially catalyzed reactions for a given enzyme and ranks candidate enzymes for a given reaction. RIVER coordinates large language models, specialized biochemical computational tools, and external knowledge resources to assemble evidence at multiple levels, including reaction chemistry, protein sequences and domains, catalytic mechanisms, and database annotations. Dedicated validation modules further examine evidence gaps and potential conflicts and rerank candidates using traceable evidence records. On public benchmarks, RIVER achieves the highest success rates in both enzyme retrieval and enzyme annotation. In family-specific enzyme retrieval, RIVER outperforms the evaluated baselines on the selected datasets and metrics. In Escherichia coli enzyme retrieval tasks constructed from recent literature, RIVER achieves the best retrieval results with closed-source and open-source model configurations, substantially outperforming closed-source general-purpose and biology-specialized multi-agent systems, including Claude Code, Codex, and Phylo Max. These results suggest that evidence-guided multi-agent retrieval can prioritize biochemically supported enzyme--reaction relationships, providing a basis for subsequent experimental validation.

</details>

<a id="paper-197"></a>

### 197. [Adapters as Data: The Latent Space of LoRAs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07775v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07775

**作者：** Moritz Grävinghoff、Konstantin Wehmeyer、Aron Asefaw、Boris Knyazev、Damian Borth

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07775v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07775v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文把 LoRA adapter 视为一种数据模态，在同一批身份 adapter 上训练 transformer 自编码器，将其映射到非线性隐空间，并与直接在原始权重上做采样、属性编辑等操作进行比较，进一步扩展以容纳混合 rank 的 adapter。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线无直接关系，但其中的「参数群体在非线性隐空间中的组织、位移鲁棒性及异构结构共享隐空间」可供核查是否能类比用于承载异构视觉 adapter 或条件化 latent 状态，联系较远、需人工判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Adapters as Data: The Latent Space of LoRAs

**abstract/A1：** LoRA adapters are usually treated as finished artifacts rather than as a data modality. Prior work has shown that LoRA adapters populate a low-dimensional manifold that a linear PCA basis already organizes well enough to support different operations such as sampling, editing, and inversion. We ask what a learned, nonlinear latent adds beyond this. We train a transformer autoencoder on the same population of LoRA identity adapters, mapping each into a latent space and compare every operation against the identical operation applied directly to the raw adapter weights. The latent preserves the capabilities already available in weight space, sampling diverse identities and editing individual attributes. Beyond preservation, we show that the latent is far more robust to displacement than weight space, degrading into distorted but still valid faces rather than texture. Fixed-dimensional weight-space representations cannot natively host adapters of more than one rank, so we extend our sequential autoencoder to hold mixed-rank adapters within one shared latent. This gives a path toward treating adapter weights of varying architecture as a single, general modality rather than one tied to a fixed rank, and points to a learned, nonlinear latent as the mechanism that could carry this generalization further, toward heterogeneous populations of adapters.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Neural Network Artifacts as a New Data Modality

</details>

<a id="paper-198"></a>

### 198. [PlayGen2: Learning Real-Time Playable Worlds with Explicit 3D State](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07867v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07867

**作者：** Jihai Zhang、Yuhan Zhou、Junyou Li、Deheng Ye、Qi Dou、Yu Cheng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07867v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07867v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出实时交互世界模型 PlayGen2，将学习式 3D 状态演化（占据栅格静态几何加身份化实体状态）与神经渲染解耦，在 Doom 上联合训练动作条件实体动力学、几何补全与实体发现。

**相关性简评：** 其持久显式状态不接收生成图像回馈的设计，可能与 LVR 中视觉旁路、信息坍缩及状态抽象方式形成对照线索，但属游戏世界模型，可迁移性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PlayGen2: Learning Real-Time Playable Worlds with Explicit 3D State

**abstract/A1：** Building a playable world requires more than generating plausible video: it demands responsive control, persistent spatial structure, and coherent interactions with dynamic entities. Using generated frames as the sole recurrent world representation ties these requirements to view-dependent appearance synthesis, making it difficult to maintain long-term spatial and temporal consistency in generation and interaction. We present PlayGen2, a real-time interactive world model that separates learned 3D state evolution from neural rendering. PlayGen2 maintains a persistent world memory comprising static geometry represented by a volumetric occupancy grid and identity-indexed 3D states for the player and other entities. Within a fixed-size local region, its world-state model jointly learns action-conditioned entity dynamics, geometry completion, and entity discovery, committing updates to a persistent world memory. A separate neural renderer synthesizes first-person observations from depth and semantic projections of the world state, without feeding generated images back into state prediction. Using Doom as a testbed, we demonstrate that the model successfully learns world dynamics including geometry-aware player motion and player--NPC interactions. The framework is computationally efficient, enabling the complete simulation and rendering pipeline to achieve approximately 160ms backend latency on a MacBook Pro with an M4 Pro chip. Beyond single-player interaction, we demonstrate zero-shot deployment on unseen Doom maps, multiplayer interaction through the composition of single-player models, and programmable gameplay through rule-based state interventions. These capabilities demonstrate the transferability and extensibility of the learned game-world model. Code and pretrained model weights are publicly available.

**comment/C1：** Code: https://github.com/MajorDavidZhang/PlayGen-2

</details>

<a id="paper-199"></a>

### 199. [PVDepth: Panoramic Video Depth Estimation via Geometry-Aware Spatiotemporal Adaptation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07873v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07873

**作者：** Chuanxin Song、Peixi Peng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07873v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07873v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文面向 ERP 全景视频深度估计，构建大规模合成 RGB-D 全景视频数据集 PanoCARLA，并提出 PVDepth 框架：以渐进球面噪声初始化引导模型适应非均匀信息密度，并用立方体贴图时间辅助分支校正非线性时间动态。

**相关性简评：** 其「ERP + 立方体贴图」双表示与噪声分布退火，可能为 LVR 中几何一致视觉证据的保留与时空状态初始化提供类比线索，但属深度感知任务，与推理状态生成和使用的关联需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PVDepth: Panoramic Video Depth Estimation via Geometry-Aware Spatiotemporal Adaptation

**abstract/A1：** Panoramic video depth estimation is pivotal for applications such as Virtual Reality and World Models. However, advancements in this field are impeded by two primary obstacles: the scarcity of large-scale training data and the unique spatiotemporal challenges of Equirectangular Projection (ERP), which hinder the direct transfer of perspective models. In this paper, we first present PanoCARLA, a large-scale synthetic RGB-D panoramic video dataset, featuring natural motion trajectories and drone-like roaming perspectives. Building on this foundation, we propose PVDepth, an end-to-end framework adapted from perspective video depth models. To tackle ERP-specific geometric distortions and consequent non-linear temporal dynamics, we introduce two core mechanisms: (1) A Progressive Sphere-aware Noise Initialization strategy that anneals the noise distribution from planar to spherical, guiding the model to adapt to non-uniform information density; and (2) A Cube-rectified Temporal Modeling module that incorporates an auxiliary cubemap temporal branch to rectify non-linear temporal dynamics in the ERP domain. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PVDepth achieves superior performance, generating geometrically accurate and temporally consistent depth sequences. Code and data will be released at https:// github.com/ChuanxinSong/PVDepth.

**comment/C1：** ICML 2026, Project Page: https://chuanxinsong.github.io/pvdepth-project-page

</details>

<a id="paper-200"></a>

### 200. [When, Where, How: Active In-Context Learning for Tabular Foundation Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07886v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07886

**作者：** Christian M. M. Frey

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07886v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07886v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究表格基础模型的主动上下文学习，比较多样性、预测不确定性与目标感知信息增益（TANN、EPIC）在冻结模型不断获得标注时的取值变化，并在二十个基准上与经典规则及 GBDT 对照。

**相关性简评：** 其「何时靠多样性、何处做目标感知选取、如何用预期信息增益衡量标签价值」的分解，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与获证价值的评估提供概念类比，但属表格设定，迁移线索需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When, Where, How: Active In-Context Learning for Tabular Foundation Models

**abstract/A1：** Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) adapt to a new task only through the labeled rows in their context. However, labels are expensive to acquire in real-world scenarios due to the need for highly specific domain knowledge to ensure high-quality data. Active in-context learning transfers classical acquisition rules to this setting, in which the model that guides acquisition changes with every label added. We ask how the value of diversity, predictive uncertainty and target-aware information gain changes as a frozen TFM acquires more context. For that, we study Target-Aware Nearest Neighbor (TANN) acquisition and the Expected Predictive Information Gain in Context (EPIC) and evaluate them against classical rules. Our study across twenty benchmarks with two well-established TFMs and classical GBDTs highlights three findings: (i) When: diversity-based acquisition is best during the first acquisition iterations; (ii) Where: labeling pool rows that serve the target inputs via a simple target-aware nearest-neighbor rule matters substantially; (iii) How: EPIC yields the best models at the label budget, retains on average 80--87% of the chance-corrected full-data performance with only 5-10% of the pool labeled on the eight benchmarks with the largest pools, and selects labels that also improve the predictive performance of gradient-boosted decision trees.

</details>

<a id="paper-201"></a>

### 201. [Structure from Collision via Hypothesis-Space Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07900v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07900

**作者：** Takuhiro Kaneko

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.GR、cs.LG、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07900v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07900v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究从碰撞形变的多视角观测中推断物体内部隐藏空腔（Structure from Collision），把这一逆问题重构为「假设空间推理」：先用显式结构假设变量取代隐式密度更新，通过仅前向的 MPM 仿真做无梯度全局搜索，再用可微 MPM 对选中假设做基于粒子的局部精化。

**相关性简评：** 其把不可直接观测的隐变量写成显式假设集、并以「无梯度搜索＋可微精化」两段式替代隐式密度优化，可能为 LVR 中 latent 推理状态的表示与候选假设搜索/验证提供问题表述上的启发，可供核查是否映射到视觉证据筛选与停止决策。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Structure from Collision via Hypothesis-Space Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Knowledge of the hidden interior structure of an object, including the presence and location of cavities, is essential for predictive simulation, robotic manipulation, and nondestructive inspection. However, such cavities leave no direct signature on the static visible surface, making collision-induced deformation one of the few cues used to infer their presence. Structure from Collision (SfC) addresses this problem by recovering the hidden interior structure of an object from multiview observations of a collision event. SfC is challenging for two reasons: hidden cavities are only indirectly observable through surface motion, and voids correspond to the absence of material, making direct optimization using standard density representations difficult. The density-based SfC baseline inherits both difficulties. Surface-matching gradients do not directly indicate the cavity location, while density gradients can be weak or uninformative inside or near void regions. Therefore, we reformulate SfC as hypothesis-space reasoning, representing hidden cavities as explicit structural hypotheses whose variables encode available structural assumptions rather than relying on implicit density updates. A global stage performs gradient-free optimization over this hypothesis space using forward-only material point method (MPM) simulation. A local stage then refines the selected hypothesis as a Lagrangian particle system through gradient-based optimization using differentiable MPM simulation. On the SfC benchmark, our method improves hidden-structure reconstruction and future prediction over the density-based SfC baseline. Interior-topology stress tests and robustness analyses characterize its scope and limitations. The project page is available at https://www.kecl.ntt.co.jp/people/kaneko.takuhiro/projects/sfc-hypothesis/.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to WACV 2027. Project page: https://www.kecl.ntt.co.jp/people/kaneko.takuhiro/projects/sfc-hypothesis/

</details>

<a id="paper-202"></a>

### 202. [Leakage- and Label-Aware Benchmarking of Class-Imbalance Strategies for Multimodal Chest X-Ray and EHR Classification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07950v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07950

**作者：** Dang-Khoa Bui、Quang-Huy Tran、Minh-Khoi Nguyen-Bui、Duc-Tuan Ngo、Thanh-Trong Tran、Tuan-Khoi Nguyen、Hoang-Anh Ngo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07950v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07950v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文审计了多模态胸部 X 光与 EHR 分类中的类别不平衡基准，发现按记录划分存在患者级泄漏、标签由单次住院诊断码关键词复制到全部影像，并在患者分离评估下发现不平衡策略与 EHR 变量均无实际增益，而影像嵌入与影像时可用诊断历史超出全阳性基线。

**相关性简评：** 其泄漏感知评估协议与模态贡献诊断，可能可供核查 LVR 中「推理是否真正依赖视觉证据、而非患者或 EHR 捷径」这一问题，但论文本身不涉及 latent 推理，需较多迁移核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Leakage- and Label-Aware Benchmarking of Class-Imbalance Strategies for Multimodal Chest X-Ray and EHR Classification

**abstract/A1：** Benchmarks of class-imbalance strategies for multimodal chest X-ray (CXR) and electronic health record (EHR) prediction are often evaluated on record-level splits of cohorts in which one patient contributes several radiographs. We audit such a benchmark on 29,083 MIMIC-CXR radiographs of 10,479 patients with eight diagnosis targets. First, the record-split benchmark was misleading: a lookup table that copies the label of a development-set radiograph with the same EHR vector nearly matched the best model, and on unseen patients every model fell to the level of predicting every case positive. Second, tracing the labels back to MIMIC-IV explained why: each patient's labels are keyword matches on the diagnosis codes of a single admission, copied, with one EHR vector per patient, to all of the patient's radiographs, so about a third of the radiographs carry labels that differ from the codes of their own admission. Third, under patient-separated evaluation with admission-aligned and rule-refined labels, the initial conclusions do not hold: in the evaluated configurations no imbalance strategy raised the observed mean macro F2 over no augmentation by more than 0.01, tuned tree ensembles showed no consistent advantage over tuned logistic regression, and the released EHR variables stayed at the level of the all-positive baseline, whereas the diagnostic history available at imaging time and image embeddings exceeded it, including, with rule-refined labels, a CheXpert-trained encoder that never saw these radiographs; RAD-DINO had seen 96.6% of them during self-supervised pretraining. Adding either encoder to the diagnostic history gave no statistically significant F2 gain; exploratory AUROC gains were significant with RAD-DINO but not with DenseNet. We provide reusable diagnostics, a leakage-aware protocol and a checklist.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 1 figure, 6 tables

</details>

### 203. [A Behavioural Framework for Predicting and Understanding Loss of Control in Frontier Artificial Intelligence Systems](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07953v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07953

**作者：** Yao Huang、Yichi Zhang、Yifan Wang、Yitong Sun、Haoyu Dong、Jifan Ma、Jinzhe Tu、Xiaojian Li、Sören Mindermann、Wei Xu、Yinpeng Dong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07953v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07953v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出一个行为学框架，把前沿AI系统的「失控」风险拆为动机错位、致害能力与规避监控三因素，经13个方面、29项评测任务合成单一LoC分数，并在13个模型上验证该分数与受控长时程风险场景失败率相关（r=0.84），同时用以指导针对性微调与监控。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理主线无直接关系，但其「规避监控」维度及以下游失败率检验前瞻效度的做法，可能为latent推理状态难以审计、难以监控时的评价与监督设计提供可供核查的概念线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Behavioural Framework for Predicting and Understanding Loss of Control in Frontier Artificial Intelligence Systems

**abstract/A1：** Frontier artificial intelligence (AI) systems are increasingly operating over longer horizons with diminishing human oversight, raising the risk of loss of control: systems acting beyond effective human control with potentially irreversible consequences. Because such failures may become observable only after containment is difficult, they must be assessed prospectively. We introduce a behavioural framework that decomposes loss-of-control risk into three factors: misaligned motive, harm-enabling capability, and monitoring evasion. We operationalise these factors through 13 measurable aspects and 29 evaluation tasks, whose scores are combined into a single loss-of-control (LoC) score. Across 13 frontier models, the LoC score shows strong prospective validity, correlating with failure rates in contained long-horizon risk scenarios (Pearson r = 0.84). Risk varies systematically across models and cannot be explained by capability alone. We further show that the framework can guide targeted mitigations: fine-tuning and monitoring directed at diagnosed vulnerabilities reduce failure rates while largely preserving general performance. These results provide a practical basis for predicting, understanding, and mitigating loss-of-control risk before it materialises.

**comment/C1：** 39 pages, 5 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-204"></a>

### 204. [Bri3D: Bridging the Object-Scene Gap in Single-Image 3D Generation via Scene-Anchor](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07961v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07961

**作者：** Hongye Hou、Shuai Zhang、Hongjie Li、Zhuoxiao Li、Haiping Wang、Yuan Liu、Wufan Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07961v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07961v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Bri3D，从单张图像生成组合式 3D 场景：先预测带实例颜色的体素场景脚手架，刻画粗粒度物体几何与空间布局，再将每个实例提取为 scene anchor 并细化成保持位姿的体素支撑，供冻结的物体生成器合成细节纹理几何。

**相关性简评：** 该工作把「场景组织」与「物体细节合成」分离，并用实例级体素锚点作为生成的显式空间参照；这可能为 LVR 中按实体绑定的结构化中间表示或粗到细潜在工作区提供概念借鉴，但与视觉推理问题没有直接联系，需核查其锚点表示能否支撑证据选择与状态更新。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Bri3D: Bridging the Object-Scene Gap in Single-Image 3D Generation via Scene-Anchor

**abstract/A1：** Generating compositional 3D scenes from a single image requires recovering detailed object geometry and coherent spatial arrangements. Existing object-to-scene methods, including SAM3D and MIDI, extend object generation through image-context reasoning or inter-instance modeling. Coordinating objects remains challenging with limited scene-level supervision. Directly extending object-centric generators to entire scenes can degrade geometry and fail to preserve instance scale, orientation, and pose. We propose Bri3D, a framework bridging scene-level structural reasoning and object-level generative priors through instance-aware voxel scene anchors. Bri3D predicts a color-coded scene scaffold encoding coarse object geometry and spatial arrangements. Each instance is extracted as a scene anchor and refined into a pose-preserving, prior-compatible voxel support, enabling a frozen object generator to synthesize detailed textured geometry in the anchor-defined spatial reference. This coarse-to-fine design separates scene organization from object-scale detail synthesis, focusing scene-level training on spatial organization and anchor refinement while reusing pretrained object priors. Experiments demonstrate improvements in scene layout, object geometry, visual quality, and generalization. Bri3D predicts scene layouts efficiently; full scene generation is faster than SceneGen and SAM3D.

</details>

<a id="paper-205"></a>

### 205. [TCSExam: Evaluating Frontier LLMs on the Full Range of TCS Research](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07962v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07962

**作者：** Yifan Luo、Zhengying Liu、Yang Yuan、Zhilin Yang、Andrew Chi-Chih Yao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07962v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07962v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** TCSExam 构建了覆盖理论计算机科学研究全范围的统一基准：100 项专家审校任务涵盖定理证明、求解器工程、构造与开放猜想尝试，提交由 Lean 内核或可执行检查器核验；并从中抽取 20 项组成 Lite 版本以降低评测成本，用于比较多个前沿 agent。

**相关性简评：** 该文与视觉、latent 表示无直接联系，但其用形式化检查器为长程推理给出可验证结果，以及用抽样子集保持排名一致性（ρ=0.93）的评测设计，可能可供核查用于推理轨迹的验证式监督与低成本评测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TCSExam: Evaluating Frontier LLMs on the Full Range of TCS Research

**abstract/A1：** Can large language models (LLMs) advance theoretical computer science and resolve its long-standing open conjectures? To study progress toward this goal, we introduce TCSExam, a unified framework and benchmark covering the full range of TCS research. It evaluates theorem proving, solver engineering, and constructions alongside direct attempts to resolve open conjectures, capturing both concrete research accomplishments and progress toward long-standing research goals. TCSExam-Full contains 100 expert-reviewed tasks, with submissions checked by the Lean kernel or an executable task checker. TCSExam-Lite selects 20 tasks, five per track, reducing the number of task attempts by 80% at the same per-attempt budget. We evaluate eight agents on Full and nine on Lite. The TCSExam Index combines all four tracks: GPT-6 Astra leads Full (71.9), and Opus 5.5 leads Lite (72.3). Full and Lite rankings are strongly aligned across eight shared agents (Spearman's $ρ=0.93$), supporting Lite as a compact, discriminative benchmark. The evaluations yield improved solver outputs and mathematical constructions, together with independently verified special cases and bounds from open-conjecture attempts. TCSExam provides an extensible framework with task sources, Docker build recipes, and evaluation tools for reproducible use.

**comment/C1：** 52 pages, 15 figures. Project website: https://moonshotai.github.io/TCSExam/ Code: https://github.com/MoonshotAI/TCSExam

</details>

<a id="paper-206"></a>

### 206. [Towards Multiscale Molecular Representation Learning through Geometry-Based Fragmentation for Polymer Property Prediction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07998v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07998

**作者：** Makoto Takamoto、Chaitanya Sampara、Viktor Zaverkin、Daniel Oñoro-Rubio、Mathias Niepert

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cond-mat.mtrl-sci、physics.chem-ph

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07998v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07998v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文将分子碎片化表述为半径约束的 k-center 式优化，主要利用几何信息在无预设化学规则下划分结构，构建多尺度碎片并设计几何碎片注意力，结合 p-SMILES 与 3D 构象做聚合物性质预测。

**相关性简评：** 其以半径控制粒度自适应生成碎片、并对多尺度碎片做注意力的做法，可能为 LVR 中不经人工规则地划分视觉证据单元、构建多尺度 latent 表示提供线索，碎片划分与注意力融合能否迁移尚待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Towards Multiscale Molecular Representation Learning through Geometry-Based Fragmentation for Polymer Property Prediction

**abstract/A1：** It is well established that molecular substructures can contribute unequally to chemical and physical properties, with specific motifs or side chains often playing dominant roles. Fragment-based representations have therefore emerged as an effective strategy for incorporating chemically meaningful inductive biases, as exemplified by molecular fingerprints and fragment-based attention mechanisms. However, many existing fragmentation-based machine learning methods rely on predefined chemical rules, which may yield suboptimal fragmentation for previously unseen or chemically unfamiliar structures and often overlook the importance of multiscale molecular representations. To address these limitations, we formulate molecular fragmentation as a radius-constrained optimization problem inspired by the k-center formulation, where the number of clusters is not fixed a priori but emerges from the specified radius, and propose a geometry-based fragmentation method that partitions arbitrary molecular or material structures using largely geometric information, without relying on predefined chemical fragmentation rules. The method provides flexible control over fragment size, enabling the construction of multiscale fragment representations for scale-adaptive modeling. Building on this framework, we develop a geometric fragment-based attention mechanism that integrates multiscale structural information and incorporate it into a multimodal machine learning model combining p-SMILES representations with three-dimensional (3D) conformational information. Experiments on a benchmark polymer dataset show that incorporating 3D conformational information improves prediction accuracy for several properties over models based exclusively on p-SMILES representations. Explainable artificial intelligence analyses further provide insights into the physical relevance of the learned representations.

**comment/C1：** 29 pages, 8 figures, accepted for publication in ACS Omega

</details>

<a id="paper-207"></a>

### 207. [Deformable Contact-Aware 3D Object Placement](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08005v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08005

**作者：** Songhe Wang、David Miller、Charles A. Kamhoua

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08005v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08005v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对语言引导的 3D 物体摆放，该文提出 DCAP，把摆放从直接回归刚性 6-DoF 位姿改为「下落至平衡」过程：先由语义意图生成包含落点、尺度与可形变部件的释放状态，再交给刚柔耦合仿真器在重力与摩擦下求解最终位置。

**相关性简评：** 其「只预测低维初始状态、由动力学迭代松弛到平衡」的思路，可能与 LVR 中以 latent 初始化加迭代状态更新替代一次性生成相关，但任务域较远，线索需自行核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Deformable Contact-Aware 3D Object Placement

**abstract/A1：** Current language-guided 3D object placement typically predicts a rigid 6-DoF pose, ignoring the physical reality that indoor placements are dictated by contact. Consequently, predicted poses often float, interpenetrate supports, or fail to deform soft surfaces. We propose Deformable Contact-Aware Placement (DCAP), a framework that reframes placement as a drop-to-equilibrium process. Rather than predicting a static pose, DCAP constructs a semantic release state that specifies the drop location, object scale, and deformable components, allowing a coupled rigid-deformable simulator to settle the object under gravity and friction. This explicitly separates semantic intent from physical realization. Evaluated on a benchmark of 2,063 prompt-asset-scene cases, DCAP significantly outperforms static baselines. On standard placements, it improves VLM Physics &amp; Naturalness by 21.2 points and human preference by 26.5 points over the strongest raw rigid baseline, while reducing mean penetration depth by 72.3%. Compared to simulated rollout baselines, DCAP reduces residual speed by 72.1%. Ultimately, our results prove that physically accurate 3D placement requires modeling semantic intent and physical realization as strictly separate processes.

**comment/C1：** 23 pages, 4 figures; includes technical appendices

</details>

<a id="paper-208"></a>

### 208. [Reproducing Kernel (Quasi-)Banach Spaces with the $\\ell^p$ Coefficient Norms](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08032v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08032

**作者：** Tianfang Wang、Guohui Song、Haizhang Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08032v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08032v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究 ℓ^p 系数范数下的再生核（拟）Banach 空间，重点构造 0&lt;p&lt;1 的 RKQBS，给出插值、正则学习的存在性与稀疏性及表示定理，并比较 p&lt;1、p=1、p&gt;1 的数值表现。

**相关性简评：** 非凸 ℓ^p 稀疏正则与表示定理可能为 latent 表示的稀疏化、训练目标和表示几何提供抽象线索，但未涉及视觉推理或动态状态生成，迁移价值需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Reproducing Kernel (Quasi-)Banach Spaces with the $\\ell^p$ Coefficient Norms

**abstract/A1：** This paper advances the theory of reproducing kernel Banach spaces (RKBSs) by investigating the general $\\ell^p$ regularization framework, with a particular focus on the non-convex regime $0&lt;p&lt;1$. While our preceding work established the foundation for RKBSs with the $\\ell^1$ norm, the pursuit of stronger sparsity---closer to the $\\ell^0$ pseudo-norm---naturally motivates this exploration into $\\ell^p$ quasi-norms. For $0&lt;p&lt;1$, we construct reproducing kernel quasi-Banach spaces (RKQBSs) with $\\ell^p$ quasi-norms and establish existence and sparsity results for interpolation and regularized learning under appropriate assumptions. We characterize the linear and relaxed representer theorems in terms of a generalized Lebesgue constant and derive bounds under kernel decay, sample spacing, and stability assumptions. For $p&gt;1$, we construct RKBSs using a fixed countable dictionary, establish minimizer existence, and identify the optimal relaxed representer factor through an off-sample coefficient operator. These results reveal fundamental differences between the regimes $0&lt;p&lt;1$, $p=1$, and $p&gt;1$. Numerical experiments compare the accuracy and sparsity of solutions for these three cases.

</details>

<a id="paper-209"></a>

### 209. [Should We Pay This Much for Robustness? Efficient Proxy Certificates with Marginal Guarantees](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08035v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08035

**作者：** Soroush H. Zargarbashi、Mohammad Sadegh Akhondzadeh、Aleksandar Bojchevski

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08035v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08035v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出把鲁棒性认证交给廉价代理函数，并借助无标注校准集上的 robust conformal risk control 控制代理与原可靠证书的偏差，使误认证率在边际意义上受限；代理既可单独使用，也可作为回退机制，仅在代理拒绝时才调用昂贵的 randomized smoothing 认证，从而把测试期认证成本降到一次前向。

**相关性简评：** 其「廉价代理 + 统计风险控制 + 仅在拒绝时回退到昂贵验证」的结构，可能为 LVR 中低成本判断推理状态是否可信、以及何时触发额外计算或证据获取（LOOK/STOP 的成本权衡）提供概念与方法线索，但需核查从对抗鲁棒性认证迁移到多模态推理的可行性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Should We Pay This Much for Robustness? Efficient Proxy Certificates with Marginal Guarantees

**abstract/A1：** Modern classifiers remain sensitive to small input perturbations, while strong model-agnostic robustness certificates such as randomized smoothing are often too expensive for deployment, requiring an extensive number (e.g. 2000) of model forward passes per input. We propose a framework that delegates certification to a cheap proxy function while statistically controlling its deviation from the original sound certificate. Given an exchangeable unlabeled calibration set, we tune the proxy via robust conformal risk control to guarantee a bounded marginal (rather than pointwise) false-approval rate: the probability, over future inputs, that the proxy accepts an input whose prediction is flipped due to perturbation. The resulting proxy certificate can be used either as a standalone marginal certificate or as a fallback mechanism that invokes the expensive certificate only when the proxy rejects. We provide both single-sample and sample-heavy calibration recipes. Empirically, as a standalone certificate our method reduces the test-time certification cost to a single model forward while retaining much of the certified performance of randomized smoothing at small radii, and as a fallback it recovers the certified ratio of randomized smoothing at a more modest speedup. This enables cheap yet effective robustness guarantees for larger architectures such as vision transformers and language models.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-210"></a>

### 210. [Performance metrics in machine learning: a scenario-based guide from the confusion matrix to calibration, regression and uncertainty](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08048v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08048

**作者：** Mehmet Baygin、Sengul Dogan、Turker Tuncer

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08048v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08048v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该教程用 15 个单因素受控情景与 22 个混淆矩阵指标，说明类别流行率、决策阈值、聚合规则和软件约定如何改变指标结论，并附指标选择表与报告清单。

**相关性简评：** 其关于区分度与校准分离、阈值与聚合方式、一致性核查的讨论，可能为 LVR 中答案使用率、停止决策等诊断指标的选择与报告提供参考，可借核查其可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Performance metrics in machine learning: a scenario-based guide from the confusion matrix to calibration, regression and uncertainty

**abstract/A1：** Performance metrics can yield different conclusions from the same predictions. This methodological tutorial explains the effects of class prevalence, decision thresholds, aggregation rules and software conventions through controlled scenarios. Twenty-two confusion-matrix metrics are organized in a prevalence-coded map. Fifteen scenarios cover binary and multi-class classification, score discrimination, probability calibration, regression, uncertainty and consistency checks. Inputs are fixed in released files, and one factor is varied in each scenario. All 205 verification checks passed; 202 were independent and 3 were internal. An always-negative rule achieved an accuracy of 0.990 at a prevalence of 0.010, with balanced accuracy of 0.500. At fixed sensitivity and specificity, precision fell from 0.889 to 0.008 as prevalence fell from 0.5 to 0.001. Three probability variants shared a ROC-AUC of 0.825 but had calibration slopes of 2.08, 1.04 and 0.52. With 10 participants, participant-majority accuracy of 0.700 had a 95% interval of 0.348-0.933. Fold-averaged scores were falsely flagged as inconsistent under an inappropriate pooled-test-set model. A goal-based metric table and a reporting checklist are provided. The scenarios illustrate mechanisms and do not estimate their frequency in published studies. A literature audit and a real-data demonstration are specified as future protocols and were not executed.

**comment/C1：** 41 pages, 7 figures, 23 tables, including appendices

</details>

<a id="paper-211"></a>

### 211. [When Imperfect Automated Fact-Checking Backfires: Social Learning in the Presence of Misinformation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08053v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08053

**作者：** Zixuan Liu、Ming Cao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、eess.SY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08053v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08053v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 用序贯社会学习模型刻画：平台提供免费但会出错的自动事实核查、同时保留付费但完全准确的人工核查时，智能体何时放弃精确核查、福利（正确级联概率）如何变化；证明在文章可靠性居中且人工核查成本足够高时，加入该工具反而严格降低福利。

**相关性简评：** 其核心机制是「廉价但有噪的验证信号使本会精确核验的智能体提前停止并被误导」，这与 LVR 中视觉证据筛选和 LOOK/STOP 的收益—成本权衡可能同构，可作为验证成本与停止决策建模的概念参照，具体对应关系可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Imperfect Automated Fact-Checking Backfires: Social Learning in the Presence of Misinformation

**abstract/A1：** Automated fact-checking tools, cheap and fast but imperfect, are increasingly available on social platforms. We study when such tools can backfire in a sequential social learning model of misinformation. Agents observe an article's reliability and their predecessors' actions, which indicate whether they liked or disliked the article. They may also pay to fact-check it. We examine how welfare, defined as the probability of a correct cascade, changes when a free, error-prone automated tool supplements costly but perfectly accurate manual fact-checking. Two opposing effects arise. Broader use of fact-checking helps agents escape wrong cascades. At the same time, errors from the automated tool can mislead agents who would otherwise have verified the article perfectly, reducing the likelihood of correct cascades. We prove that adding the tool strictly lowers welfare for articles of intermediate reliability when truthful articles are not too informative and manual checking is sufficiently costly. Simulations show that the tool can help on either side of the intermediate range, and that it improves on manual-only checking on average only above a threshold accuracy.

**comment/C1：** 8 pages, 5 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-212"></a>

### 212. [NutriVisRAG: Multimodal Exemplar Grounding for Nutrient Estimation with Vision-Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08054v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08054

**作者：** Lubnaa Abdur Rahman、Ioannis Papathanail、Maria Panagiotou、Nefeli Dimitropoulou、Margret Harðardóttir、Elisabeth J. den Brok、Bastiaan E. de Galan、Stavroula Georgia Mougiakakou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08054v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08054v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 NutriVisRAG：用视觉编码器检索带营养标注的相似餐食，作为多模态 in-context 示例在推理时供 VLM 估计营养，无需任务微调；在 4 数据集×4 VLM×3 编码器×2 提示的 96 种配置下评估，收益依配置而异。

**相关性简评：** 「按视觉相似度检索证据并注入上下文」可供核查视觉证据筛选及其保真度如何影响 VLM 判断，但该工作属任务级 RAG 应用，未涉及内部 latent 状态或推理轨迹，借鉴较间接。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** NutriVisRAG: Multimodal Exemplar Grounding for Nutrient Estimation with Vision-Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Diet is a major determinant of health, yet accurate dietary assessment from free-living meal images remains challenging. Existing retrieval-based Vision-Language Model (VLM) approaches to nutrient estimation typically rely on textual food representations, while, to our knowledge, direct grounding on visually similar annotated meals has not been investigated. We present NutriVisRAG, a retrieval-augmented generation framework that uses a vision encoder to retrieve similar meals with known nutritional annotations and provide them as multimodal in-context exemplars for VLM nutrient estimation at inference time, without task-specific fine-tuning. We evaluate NutriVisRAG across four datasets, four VLMs, three retrieval encoders, and two prompting strategies, including free-living meals from adults with insulin-treated diabetes, where carbohydrate estimates are compared against two dietitians' annotations. Across all 96 configurations (4 datasets x 4 VLMs x 3 retrieval encoders x 2 prompting strategies), NutriVisRAG reduced the primary estimation error in 56, with improvements more frequent under whole-meal (35/48) than per-item prompting (21/48). Across the evaluated configurations, the lowest NutriVisRAG error was below the best Vanilla error in 13 of 16 VLM--dataset pairs. For GPT-5.4-mini, under whole-meal prompting, the best-performing retrieval encoder for each dataset reduced the primary error on all four datasets relative to Vanilla, with reductions of 1.7--35.7%. Overall, our results show that grounding in multimodal exemplars comprising images and respective annotations can improve nutrient estimation at inference time without task-specific fine-tuning, although the benefits remain configuration-dependent.

</details>

<a id="paper-213"></a>

### 213. [Gradient Boosting for Efficient Rank Discovery in Tensor Network Kernel Machines](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08093v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08093

**作者：** Albert Saiapin、Kim Batselier

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08093v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08093v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 GB-CPKM，用 gradient boosting 依次叠加固定秩的 CP 张量网络学习器来提升模型容量，并以验证集表现决定集成规模，从而回避显式的张量秩搜索；在回归基准上比交叉验证式秩搜索训练更快、峰值内存更低而预测性能相当。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理无直接关系，但其「按需逐步增加容量、以验证表现决定何时停止」的机制，可能为 LVR 中动态计算分配与 STOP 决策的设计提供可供核查的类比思路。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Gradient Boosting for Efficient Rank Discovery in Tensor Network Kernel Machines

**abstract/A1：** Tensor network kernel machines (TNKMs) combine nonlinear features with low-rank tensor representations to model complex relationships efficiently. However, selecting an appropriate tensor rank remains challenging, as conventional rank-search methods require training multiple candidate models, increasing computational costs. In this work, we focus on a canonical polyadic (CP) tensor network parameterization and propose GB-CPKM, a gradient boosting framework that builds a higher-capacity predictor by sequentially adding fixed-rank learners. This approach provides a new perspective on rank selection by incrementally increasing model capacity, with ensemble size selected based on validation performance rather than through explicit rank search. Experiments on real-world regression benchmarks show that GB-CPKM trains up to 6x faster and uses up to 2x less peak memory than a conventional rank-selection method based on cross-validation, while achieving comparable predictive performance. These results highlight the potential of gradient boosting to improve the computational efficiency and scalability of tensor-network learning.

**comment/C1：** 13 pages, 3 figures, 1 table. Code available at: https://github.com/AlbMLpy/boosting-tnkm

</details>

<a id="paper-214"></a>

### 214. [PersonaAgent: Retrieval-Augmented Transaction Anomaly Scoring with Refreshable Multi-Entity Persona Memory](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08094v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08094

**作者：** Zhengyu Fang、Liang Wang、Yingtong Dou、Yuzhong Chen、Shubham Jain、Junpeng Wang、Xiran Fan、Menghai Pan、Jiarui Sun、Yan Zheng、Yiwei Cai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08094v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08094v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 PersonaAgent，将卡、商户等实体的最新行为信号表示为 persona 并存入可刷新记忆，离线定期批量更新，推理时由轻量适配器检索对应记忆来增强基础交易异常分类器的评分。

**相关性简评：** 其「可刷新实体记忆＋推理期轻量检索注入」与 LVR 中按实体或回合缓存视觉证据、择时注入的状态管理有形式相似，可能为证据缓存与刷新策略提供参照，但任务与视觉推理无关，迁移价值仍需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PersonaAgent: Retrieval-Augmented Transaction Anomaly Scoring with Refreshable Multi-Entity Persona Memory

**abstract/A1：** In large-scale payment networks, entities (card, merchant, etc.) and their interactions play a key role in transaction anomaly detection. However, transaction patterns between different entities usually change more frequently than the production model refresh schedule. Meanwhile, entity embedding tables suffer from high-cardinality and sparse interaction issues. To resolve the above challenges, we present PersonaAgent, an agentic transaction anomaly detection framework which represents the latest entity behavior signals as personas and saves them in a refreshable memory. Offline, a weekly scheduled batch job is used to extract the latest behavior signals and update them in the memory. At inference time, a lightweight adapter retrieves the corresponding entity behavior signals of each transaction from memory and uses them to augment the base anomaly classifier's scoring. Through comprehensive benchmarking with different anomaly detection models at Visa, PersonaAgent is able to score transactions efficiently (a 1,000-transaction CPU batch in 1-2 ms), lifting the PR-AUC by 0.65-6.58% and the production business metric by 8.93-14.24% relatively in deployment.

</details>

<a id="paper-215"></a>

### 215. [Detecting Weather Fronts from Satellite Observations: What Does Reanalysis Add?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08148v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08148

**作者：** Ioannis Dravilas、Miruna Stoicescu、Roope Tervo、Carlos Horn、Oriol Hinojo Comellas、Sina Montazeri、Andrea Meraner、Ana Lucic

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV、physics.ao-ph

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08148v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08148v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文对同一像素级天气锋面分类模型比较两种输入配置——仅用静止卫星红外观测，以及再加入 28 个 ERA5 再分析场——量化再分析数据带来的性能提升，并通过变量可预测性与消融分析定位关键贡献来源。

**相关性简评：** 其「输入源消融 + 逐变量贡献与可预测性」的评价思路可能可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据是否足以承载任务必要信息、以及辅助通道增益如何归因，但任务与 latent 推理状态无直接关联，仅属远域方法启发。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Detecting Weather Fronts from Satellite Observations: What Does Reanalysis Add?

**abstract/A1：** Weather fronts are transition regions between air masses with contrasting thermodynamic properties and are associated with mid-latitude precipitation and severe weather. We investigate machine learning (ML) methods for detecting weather fronts and quantify the contribution of reanalysis data to performance. We compare two configurations of the same ML model for pixel-wise frontal classification over the North Atlantic and Europe: (1) using only geostationary infrared satellite observations, and (2) additionally using 28 ERA5 reanalysis fields. The satellite-only model achieves a neighbourhood critical success index at a 150 km tolerance (CSI@150) of $0.402$, which lies within the range of agreement between analyses from different meteorological services. The satellite$+$ERA5 model achieves $0.575$, an improvement of $0.173$. Our analysis shows that low-level winds are the least predictable from the satellite representation ($R^2 = 0.23$, compared with $0.88$ for temperature), while removing winds or moisture causes the largest decreases in detection performance.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to the 2nd Workshop on Advances in Representation Learning for Earth Observation at NeurIPS 2026

</details>
