### 216. [PCBRouteAgent: How Close Are LLM Agents to Becoming PCB Routing Engineers?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08175v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08175

**作者：** Yue Zhang、Jilei Sun、Liqiang Jing、Yijie Wei、Ruohan Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08175v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08175v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PCBRouteAgent，一个受人类工程师「全局策略+局部执行」协作方式启发的多模态 PCB 布线智能体：全局布线规划器结合板图与结构化电气/几何信息给出连接拓扑、布线顺序、走线通道与层切换，细节执行器调用 KiCad 原生引擎并做有界局部恢复，失败以结构化反馈触发有针对性修订，在 PCBWorld-Bench 四个划分共 247 块板上评测。

**相关性简评：** 其分层「先规划后执行」、失败反馈驱动的有界局部恢复与保留已接受结果，可能为 LVR 中的潜空间规划、视觉证据筛选及停止/回退决策提供类比线索，但任务领域相距较远，可供核查迁移价值有限。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PCBRouteAgent: How Close Are LLM Agents to Becoming PCB Routing Engineers?

**abstract/A1：** Automating printed circuit board (PCB) routing requires coordinating board-level connectivity decisions with geometric execution under strict design rules. Although recent agents can interact with native routing tools, reliable routing on complex boards remains challenging. We present PCBRouteAgent, a multimodal PCB routing agent inspired by how human engineers coordinate global strategy with local execution. A Global Routing Planner combines board images and structured electrical and geometric information to specify connection topology, routing order, corridors, and layer transitions. A Detailed Routing Executor realizes these guides through KiCad's native routing engine and performs bounded local recovery. Unresolved failures are returned as structured feedback, enabling targeted guide revisions while preserving accepted routes. We evaluate PCBRouteAgent on 247 synthetic and real-world boards from four PCBWorld-Bench test splits. It achieves clean-pass rates of 100%, 94.9%, 100%, and 40% on D2, D3-A, D3-B, and D3-C, respectively. On the three real-world subsets, PCBRouteAgent outperforms the best evaluated baseline in clean-pass rate by 13.9, 36, and 10 percentage points, respectively.

</details>

<a id="paper-217"></a>

### 217. [Do sEMG Foundation Models Improve Cross-Subject Generalisation?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08199v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08199

**作者：** William Raftery、Dario Farina

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、q-bio.NC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08199v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08199v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 系统评估三个 sEMG 基础模型在三个手势分类数据集上的会话内与跨被试表现，并用三种微调策略分离预训练本身的贡献；发现预训练仅在同会话划分下带来提升，跨被试时收益基本消失，被试数少的数据集上经典机器学习基线可追平甚至超过基础模型。

**相关性简评：** 其「用受控微调策略隔离预训练贡献＋在分布偏移下检验表示泛化」的评测思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中预训练表示对跨域泛化的真实作用与贡献分配，但模态与任务差距较大，可迁移性需进一步判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Do sEMG Foundation Models Improve Cross-Subject Generalisation?

**abstract/A1：** sEMG Foundation Models aim to learn general purpose representations over diverse subjects and recording conditions that boost performance on a variety of downstream tasks. However, existing evaluations make it difficult to discern whether pretraining alone is sufficient to combat the cross-subject overfitting which makes machine learning on sEMG so challenging. We conduct a systematic evaluation of three sEMG foundation models across three gesture classification datasets under both intra-session and inter-subject splits, using three fine-tuning strategies designed to isolate the contribution of pretraining. We find that pretraining boosts performance when train and test samples are drawn from the same recording session, but this benefit largely disappears under inter-subject evaluation. In low subject count datasets, pretraining provides little to no benefit and classical ML baselines match or exceed foundation model performance.

</details>

### 218. [Signals, Evidence, and Context: User Scrutiny in Real-World LLM Conversations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08232v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08232

**作者：** Ahana Biswas、Yu-Ru Lin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08232v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08232v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文分析 ShareChat 与 WildChat 上约 49 万条助手—用户对话对，考察助手的归因语言、显式 URL、不确定性等线索与用户随后接受、核查、质疑行为的关系，并比较对话层与轮次层的持续性。

**相关性简评：** 其「声称有据」与「可检视证据」之间的落差，以及对核查/质疑触发条件的区分，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与证据呈现方式的评价目标提供参照，但对象是用户行为而非模型内部推理状态，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Signals, Evidence, and Context: User Scrutiny in Real-World LLM Conversations

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) often present synthesized answers without making their supporting evidence easy to inspect. We study how features of these answers relate to users' subsequent accepting, checking, and challenging in 489,283 assistant-user response pairs from ShareChat and WildChat. Broad attribution language appears in 31.9% of qualifying responses, compared with explicit URLs in 1.33%, revealing a gap between claims of grounding and preserved inspectable support. Attribution, uncertainty, and other response cues generally accompany both checking and challenging; only uncertainty shows a small statistically distinguishable pooled association favoring checking. Within Technical Help, uncertainty favors checking relative to challenging; refusals and reasoning traces favor challenging in Seeking Information. Scrutiny is concentrated in some conversations, while its apparent turn-to-turn persistence becomes much smaller when conversation-level differences and response ordering are examined separately. These results suggest that supporting verification requires attention to what evidence can be inspected, which question a user is trying to resolve, and the history of the interaction.

</details>

<a id="paper-219"></a>

### 219. [Value-oriented policy distillation for interpretable wind farm power control](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08245v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08245

**作者：** Max Bruninx、Timothy Verstraeten、Jan Decuyper、Ann Nowé、Jan Helsen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、eess.SY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08245v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08245v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究风电场功率控制中的隐式平衡问题，提出以可解释提升机（EBM）为学生的策略蒸馏框架，并引入价值导向的蒸馏目标和专家知识设计，使控制策略透明且对利益相关方可解释，在比利时电网与海上风电场真实数据上将不平衡成本降低约 82%。

**相关性简评：** 将策略蒸馏到可加性透明模型并采用价值导向目标，可能为 LVR 中 latent 推理状态向可解释形式蒸馏、以及各状态成分的贡献分配提供概念线索，但需核查其与视觉推理的关联性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Value-oriented policy distillation for interpretable wind farm power control

**abstract/A1：** Wind farm power control can help the system maintain balance and avoid blackouts. In Europe, this can be achieved through implicit balancing, where a wind farm adjusts its power setpoint in anticipation of the imbalance price using information published by the Transmission System Operator. Thereby, the wind farm helps restore system balance while also reducing its own imbalance costs resulting from forecast errors. However, implicit balancing is a challenging problem since the settlement price is unknown in advance and can be impacted by the pursued control strategy. Reinforcement learning methods could help to solve this problem, but lack the interpretability needed to be deployed on real wind farms. Therefore, we propose a policy distillation framework using explainable boosting machines (EBMs), a variant of generalized additive models, to obtain a transparent control policy. To increase the value captured by the EBM model, we introduce a value-oriented perspective to policy distillation inspired by research in the field of energy forecasting. Furthermore, we suggest to include expert knowledge during model design, such that the control strategy is also justifiable to stakeholders. Using real data of the Belgian grid and an offshore wind farm, we find that our value-oriented and expert-based controller can reduce imbalance costs by 82%, while outperforming a price threshold controller as well as a policy distillation baseline using decision trees.

</details>

<a id="paper-220"></a>

### 220. [Geometric Relevance Is Not Sufficient: Evaluating Focused Post-Training Supervision in Diffusion Docking](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08249v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08249

**作者：** Michael Brown、Maryam Rahimimovassagh、Mehdi Yazdani-Jahromi、Sanjeda Sara Jennifer、Ivan Garibay

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、q-bio.BM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08249v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08249v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文在 DiffDock 上做固定预算的后训练对比：宽泛继续训练、按 Cartesian 后果或条件目标一致性聚焦扭转监督，以及宽泛与聚焦混合；并从分数保真度、生成位姿质量、best-of-10 采样能力与置信度选择 Top-1 四个层面分别评估。

**相关性简评：** 「聚焦看似更相关的监督反而无稳定收益」与对保真度、采样容量、选择成功率的分离评估，可能为 latent 推理中轨迹监督是否应集中到关键步骤、以及区分内部表示保真与答案成功率提供核查线索，但分子对接领域相距较远，迁移性需人工判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Geometric Relevance Is Not Sufficient: Evaluating Focused Post-Training Supervision in Diffusion Docking

**abstract/A1：** Diffusion-based molecular docking requires learning a corrective score field across a broad corruption distribution, yet individual pose errors can differ substantially in their structural consequences. This creates a post-training conflict between preserving broad score-field coverage and concentrating supervision on corrections that appear more relevant to docking geometry. Using DiffDock, a fixed-budget study compares broad continuation with torsional supervision focused on Cartesian consequence or conditional target consistency, and with a broad/focused mixture. Evaluation separates fixed score fidelity, generated-pose quality, best-of-10 sampling capacity, and confidence-selected Top-1 docking performance. At &lt;5 Angstrom, broad continuation had the highest mean Top-1 success among trained policies, and focused or mixed supervision showed no consistent advantage under the tested budget. The starting model had better fixed score fidelity, whereas the evaluated broad-continuation model had higher mean Top-1 success with the same aggregate best-of-10 recovery. Focusing supervision on structurally consequential or more consistently specified corrections did not consistently improve returned-pose success over broad continuation.

**comment/C1：** 17 pages, 5 figures; accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 AI4DD Workshop

</details>

<a id="paper-221"></a>

### 221. [Accelerating Safe Reinforcement Learning with Massive Parallelism](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08273v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08273

**作者：** Joonyoung Lim、Younghwan Yoo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08273v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08273v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究大规模并行短 rollout 训练与 CMDP 全回合安全约束之间的错配，将错峰环境重置刻画为短轨迹段上的相位混合分布，提出相位聚合的回合级代价估计并把有限样本不确定性与分布失配纳入约束收紧，在 MJX 版 Safety Gym 上以更少实际训练时间达到与非大规模并行实现相当的性能。

**相关性简评：** 其「由短窗口局部片段在显式偏差与不确定性下重构长程全局量」的思路，可能为从分段 latent 状态估计整条推理轨迹的收益与贡献、以及在证据不充分时作收紧或停止决策提供概念类比，聚合方式与偏差分析是否可迁移可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Accelerating Safe Reinforcement Learning with Massive Parallelism

**abstract/A1：** We investigate the problem of scaling safe reinforcement learning (safe RL) to massively parallel training regimes, where a large number of environments are exe- cuted with short rollout horizons. Although this setting improves data throughput and wall-clock efficiency, it creates a mismatch with constrained Markov decision processes (CMDPs), whose safety constraints are defined over full-episode cost returns. We address this mismatch by analyzing staggered environment resets as a phase-mixture distribution over short trajectory segments. This analysis charac- terizes the bias induced by phase coverage and motivates a phase-aggregated cost estimator that reconstructs episode-level cost estimates from short rollout batches. We further incorporate finite-sample uncertainty and distribution mismatch into a constraint-tightening framework, yielding a scalable Safe RL method compatible with on-policy optimization. Using an MJX-based implementation of Safety Gym, our experiments show that the proposed method achieves performance comparable to non-massively parallel implementations while substantially reducing wall-clock training time.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-222"></a>

### 222. [Hierarchical Incremental Beta Splatting for Online Reconstruction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08288v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08288

**作者：** Linus Franke

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.GR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08288v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08288v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向在线 3D 重建，将 beta 核引入增量式 Gaussian Splatting，用可观测性与多视角深度一致性决定新基元的不透明度与形状软硬，并配合误差驱动的重定位和保形合并的 LOD 层级。

**相关性简评：** 其「按证据强度给每个状态单元设定软硬/置信」的做法可能启发 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与 latent 状态可靠性的区分表述，但领域较远，仅概念层面可核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Hierarchical Incremental Beta Splatting for Online Reconstruction

**abstract/A1：** Online 3D Gaussian Splatting reconstructs a scene during capture: each keyframe spawns primitives once and refines them for only a few dozen iterations, leaving little chance to fix poor initialization or missing detail. Gaussians are also inherently soft, needing many overlaps for solid surfaces. We present Hierarchical Incremental Beta Splatting (HIBS), which brings beta kernels to incremental reconstruction. Their shape parameter interpolates between a soft, Gaussian-like profile and a flat-topped, near-opaque surfel, acting as a confidence dial for geometry. Our confidence-aware spawning uses geometric observability and multi-view depth agreement to initialize both opacity and shape, producing solid primitives where geometry is verifiable and soft, correctable primitives where it is not. As online densification, error-driven relocation recycles stale primitives into photometric-error hotspots. Bounded beta kernel support enables exact tile culling and faster rasterization, and a closed-form, shape-preserving merging formulation provides a level-of-detail (LOD) hierarchy for fast optimization and real-time rendering. On Mip-NeRF 360 in the on-the-fly protocol, HIBS improves PSNR by up to 0.8 dB while reducing optimization time by up to 40%. Project page: https://linus-franke.com/hibs/

**comment/C1：** accepted to WACV 2027, project page: https://linus-franke.com/hibs/

</details>

<a id="paper-223"></a>

### 223. [Dynamic Optimistic Constrained OCO with Memory via Delay Equivalence](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08302v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08302

**作者：** Mohammed Abdullah、George Iosifidis、Salah Eddine Elayoubi、Tijani Chahed

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08302v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08302v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究带记忆与时变约束的在线凸优化（COCO-M），提出可接纳不可信预测的乐观算法，并借「记忆到延迟」的归约把状态相关梯度视为延迟反馈，给出同时适应环境非平稳性与预测精度的动态遗憾及累计约束违反界。

**相关性简评：** 其记忆到延迟的等价处理与随预测精度自适应的乐观更新，可能为刻画 latent 推理中循环状态更新、延迟反馈和预测可信度提供概念工具，但与视觉推理的具体接口尚需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Dynamic Optimistic Constrained OCO with Memory via Delay Equivalence

**abstract/A1：** Online Convex Optimization with memory and time-varying constraints (COCO-M) is a recently-introduced framework that captures the dynamics of stateful online learning and non-stochastic control with budget constraints. Despite its expressive power, COCO-M remains largely understudied. In this work, we tackle this problem in its full scale, providing an optimistic algorithm that incorporates untrusted predictions to tame universal dynamic regret and cumulative constraint violation. Our analysis leverages a potent reduction from memory to delay and treats state-dependent gradients as delayed feedback. We establish the first bounds that adapt to both environment non-stationarity (path length $P\_T$) and prediction accuracy, while lifting previous restrictive assumptions such as functional separability. En route to these results, we derive findings of independent interest for the memory-to-delay reduction, and for delayed OCO with time-varying constraints. The proposed framework ensures sublinear worst-case guarantees that improve with prediction accuracy, reaching $\\mathcal O(1)$ regret under perfect predictions, with cumulative constraint violation (CCV) $\\mathcal O(\\sqrt{T})$ for unknown $P\_T$ and $\\mathcal O(\\log T)$ for known or online observable $P\_T$ , while recovering the optimal COCO rates when memory disappears.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the 40th Conference on Neural Information Processing Systems (NeurIPS 2026)

</details>

<a id="paper-224"></a>

### 224. [Interpretable many-body interaction laws for collective motion from few trajectories](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08314v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08314

**作者：** Timon S. Gutleb

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、physics.bio-ph、q-bio.QM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08314v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08314v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 directional atomic cluster expansion（dACE），将原子簇展开迁移到自驱动个体，把个体加速度系统可加、严格等变地分解为 1、2、3 体相互作用与自身历史项，并在模拟集群及斑马鱼、金体美鱥追踪数据上恢复可解释的相互作用律。

**相关性简评：** 其「自项与多体交互分离、按体阶拆分贡献」的思路，以及「下一步预测精度主要反映自驱动与噪声而非交互」的诊断，可能为 LVR 中 latent 内容的贡献分配与视觉证据是否被真正使用的评价提供可供核查的类比。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Interpretable many-body interaction laws for collective motion from few trajectories

**abstract/A1：** Models of collective motion usually reduce social interactions to pairwise forces, despite empirical evidence for many-body effects. We introduce the directional atomic cluster expansion (dACE), which adapts the atomic cluster expansion (ACE) of interatomic potentials to self-propelled agents. dACE provides a systematically improvable, exactly equivariant expansion of an agent's acceleration that splits uniquely into 1-, 2-, 3-body and higher contributions, along with a self term for the agent's own past motion that is fitted so that it does not absorb the interactions. On simulated collectives in two and three dimensions with known rules, dACE recovers the interaction law body order by body order from little data, while the neural model SocialNet, the current closest competitor, needs more data, has much larger errors on many-body or chiral rules and is restricted to 2D. On real data sets of tracked zebrafish and golden shiners, the laws learned by dACE agree better between disjoint halves of the data than SocialNet's. The learned laws are interpretable, and in the shiners the 3-body term opposes the pairwise response as neighbourhoods get dense. Finally, we show that next-step prediction accuracy mostly measures self-propulsion and noise rather than the interactions, and suggest more robust combinations of error measures for interaction learning.

**comment/C1：** 19 pages, 10 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-225"></a>

### 225. [Learning the Task or Learning the Verifier? Rethinking Criteria Exposure in Embodied Self-Improvement](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08318v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08318

**作者：** Wenbo Huang、Jianxin Bi、Shunyu Yang、Ruiyang Si、Rui Ni、Qiang Wang、Shulong Jiang、Yintianrun Zhang、Feixiang Ruan、Kewei Zhang、Ye Huang、Shukai Gong、Daquan Zhou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08318v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08318v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在机器人仿真自改进编码智能体中对比是否向智能体暴露基准成功标准（CE），发现 CE 能提高给定标准下的成功率，但标准轻微改动后优势消失且技能迁移较差，并提出更忠实于指令的评判标准。

**相关性简评：** 其区分「通过验证器」与「完成指令任务」并做标准扰动检验，可能为 LVR/VLM 推理评价中防止验证标准泄漏、核查 latent 内容是否真正服务任务提供借鉴。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning the Task or Learning the Verifier? Rethinking Criteria Exposure in Embodied Self-Improvement

**abstract/A1：** Self-improving embodied agents are increasingly being developed and compared on robot simulation benchmarks. Several recent works expose the benchmark's success criteria to the learning agent, a practice we call Criteria Exposure (CE). These criteria, originally written to verify policies trained by imitation learning, often demand more than the corresponding language instructions, so a score from such a verifier may not faithfully reflect whether the instructed task was completed, especially for robot-control programs written by an agent. As embodied coding agents report ever higher benchmark scores, a natural question arises: when a coding agent is exposed to the benchmark's criteria, does it generate code that better performs the instructed task, or code that simply overfits to the verification criteria? We conduct controlled experiments with a self-improving agent with and without CE, and find that with CE the agent quickly produces code with a higher success rate under the given criteria, but this advantage disappears when the criteria are changed slightly without departing from the original instructions. More importantly, the skills the agent develops under CE transfer poorly across tasks. We further propose instruction-faithful criteria and show that they better reflect the performance of agent-generated programs.

**comment/C1：** 22 pages, 8 figures, 10 tables. Project page: https://dagroup-pku.github.io/PyRUA-Lean/RSIJudge

</details>

<a id="paper-226"></a>

### 226. [Discovering Learning Rules that Shape Others](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08335v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08335

**作者：** Hong Jun Lee、Ziyan Wang、Jun Wang、Stefanos Leonardos、Yali Du

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08335v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08335v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DUOS，把「元学习发现更新规则」的框架从单智能体扩展到一般和博弈：仅凭自身经验元训练一条与策略分离的更新规则，目标是最大化智能体终身期望回报，并在锦标赛的归一化均分与自博弈中排名第一。

**相关性简评：** 「元学习出一条只读自身经验、独立于策略的更新规则」这一概念形式，可能与 LVR 中如何学习 latent 推理状态的更新规则、以及长程轨迹上的贡献分配与监督相关，可供核查其规则结构与训练目标；但多智能体对手塑造设定距视觉推理较远，迁移属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Discovering Learning Rules that Shape Others

**abstract/A1：** In general-sum games, self-interested learners can reach outcomes that are poor for everyone, which has motivated methods that shape the opponent's learning. Methods like Learning with Opponent-Learning Awareness (LOLA), which differentiates through the opponent's learning process, and Model-Free Opponent Shaping (M-FOS), which uses model-free optimisation methods for long-horizon opponent shaping, have enabled mutual cooperation and exploitation of learners. However, these methods require access to the opponent's parameters, policy, rewards or learning rule during deployment. In single-agent reinforcement learning, update rules discovered by meta-learning have achieved state-of-the-art results (e.g. DiscoRL). These rules are separate from the policy they train and read only the agent's own experience. We hereby extend this framework to general-sum settings by introducing Discovered Update Rule for Opponent Shaping (DUOS). To the best of our knowledge, this is the first method to discover an update rule for an agent in general-sum games. DUOS meta-trains the rule to maximise the agent's expected return over its lifetime, and updates an inner network using the agent's own experience. We show that our discovered rule exhibits behaviour consistent with opponent shaping without reading opponent policies or parameters, and ranks 1st in normalised mean score and 1st in self-play among tournament entrants.

</details>

<a id="paper-227"></a>

### 227. [Enhancing Local and Global Structure in Compressed AI Agent Memory](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08341v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08341

**作者：** Henry Han

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08341v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08341v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究 AI agent 记忆嵌入向量的压缩保真度，用无标签的局部保真度（10 近邻）与全局保真度（30–50% 记忆范围）共同评价，并据此提出面向 OPQ 码本的 locality-aware quantization（LAQ），把远端的秩违规率界转化为码本训练目标。

**相关性简评：** 其“近邻保真”与“远端结构保真”分离的表示几何评价，以及由界导出的量化目标，可能为 LVR 中压缩后的 latent 状态或缓存是否仍保留足够区分度提供可核查的度量与训练线索，但该文不涉及视觉证据或多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Enhancing Local and Global Structure in Compressed AI Agent Memory

**abstract/A1：** AI agents store text as embedding vectors and compress them at scale. A compression is accepted when each item's nearest neighbors survive, but agents also consolidate, delete, and compare memories, tasks that depend on which items lie far apart. We evaluate both ends with two label-free fidelities from our prior work: the local fidelity $L$ at 10 neighbors and the global fidelity $G$ at 30 to 50 percent of the memory. At 96 bytes per passage, rescored binary codes reach an $L$ of 0.97 yet move 10.1 percent of passages to another theme; PCA with 8-bit codes, at an $L$ of 0.77, moves 1.7 percent. Rescoring leaves the stored codes, and thus $G$, unchanged. We prove that across the $G$ band, $1-ν\_k$ is at most 2 to 2.18 times the square root of the near-far rank-violation rate, and that this rate follows the distance noise of the compression (correlation 0.979). Our locality-aware quantization (LAQ) turns the bound into a hinge objective on the codebooks of an OPQ index, keeping its bytes, rotation, and search. To our knowledge, LAQ is the first quantizer whose objective comes from a proven bound on the far end. Reconstruction alone raises $G$ in 0 of 52 held-out runs; the far hinge raises it in all 52. LAQ raises $G$ in all 52 ($p=8.5\\times 10^{-4}$ over 13 memory-encoder groups), including the external FinDER memory, has the highest $G$ of seven equal-size quantizers in all 12 Multi-Doc-2025 runs, scales to a million passages, and keeps evidence recall.

**comment/C1：** 28 pages, 12 figures, 10 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-228"></a>

### 228. [USB Gym: A Gymnasium Testbed for Closed-Loop Ultrasound View Matching](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08352v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08352

**作者：** Thanapong Intharah、Zhichao Gao、Yiliang Liu、Hao Dong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08352v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08352v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建 USB Gym 仿真测试平台，用公开 CT 合成超声图像并搭建可替换模块的闭环探头引导流程（分割器、控制器、奖励），在同一批留出病例上评测 20 种已发表方法组合，发现组合后的引导仍远逊于位姿 oracle。

**相关性简评：** 其闭环视觉反馈、以图像相似度作奖励与「匹配目标视图即停止」的设定，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与 STOP 判据的设计和评价提供参照，但属医疗仿真控制任务，与 latent 推理无直接关联，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** USB Gym: A Gymnasium Testbed for Closed-Loop Ultrasound View Matching

**abstract/A1：** Background and Objective: Our main goal is ultrasound guidance: moving a probe until its image matches a target view. Published methods for each part of a guide are evaluated in separate simulators. We asked whether these methods, combined into one guide, are good enough. Methods: We built USB Gym, a testbed whose standard engine simulates ultrasound from public patient CT, with a network drawing organ textures from CT labels. Its guide has three replaceable parts (slots), an organ segmenter, a controller and a reward, which received 20 published methods, each run on the same 60 episodes of six held-out patients. In each slot, a published method replaced ours only if detectably better on these patients. The better of this combination and our original guide on 20 new CTs is the benchmark baseline. A pose oracle, reading the exact probe pose available only in simulation, set the reference. The results below are exploratory. Results: On these CTs, the probe started on average 16.0 mm from the target view; the pose oracle brought it to 1.5 mm. Of the results known when the combination was fixed, only an image-similarity reward (SSIM) was slightly but detectably better, so the combination was our guide with that reward. This combination was the benchmark baseline, ending at 13.7 mm (16% of the oracle's progress). Conclusions: Combining untuned published methods with ours did not guide the probe near the target view; the task remains under-explored. The testbed accepts new methods; no guide was tested on hardware.

**comment/C1：** 75 pages; supplementary material (62 pages) as an ancillary file. Submitted to Computer Methods and Programs in Biomedicine. Code: https://github.com/tohnperfect/USBGym

</details>

<a id="paper-229"></a>

### 229. [Module-Aware Optimization for Graph Neural Networks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08379v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08379

**作者：** Guy Hadad、Haggai Roitman、Moshe Eliasof、Bracha Shapira

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08379v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08379v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文讨论 GNN 中权重更新的几何问题，指出图传播后的层输入会放大矩阵更新的影响，并提出 GraphMuon：在图传播表示的协方差所定义的度量下进行正交化，并按更新对层表示的实际影响归一化步长。

**相关性简评：** 其核心是「由前向传播表示决定更新度量」，与 LVR 中内部状态表示几何如何塑造更新与信息保留的问题形式上相似，可能为评估 latent 状态条件化的训练信号提供类比线索，但需核查其是否可迁移到 VLM 推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Module-Aware Optimization for Graph Neural Networks

**abstract/A1：** Different optimizers impose different geometries on parameter updates. Adaptive methods such as Adam and AdamW use coordinate-wise updates, while matrix-aware optimizers such as Muon utilize weight-matrix structure through orthogonalized updates. For Graph Neural Networks (GNNs), however, the effective geometry of its weight matrices is shaped not only by its learned channel-mixing weights, but also by the graph-propagated representations it acts on. Thus, even if an update is well normalized as a matrix, its effect on the layer output can be amplified when applied to graph-propagated features. To address this gap, we introduce GraphMuon, a graph-aware optimizer that adapts Muon's matrix updates to the covariance structure of graph-propagated layer inputs. GraphMuon performs orthogonalization in a metric defined by these propagated representations and normalizes step sizes according to the update's induced effect on the layer representation. We show that theoretically, vanilla Muon can suffer from graph-induced update amplification, while our GraphMuon can mitigate this effect. As such, our GraphMuon yields a graph-metric analogue of Muon's polar update step. Empirically, GraphMuon achieves $1.32\\times$-$2.42\\times$ validation-loss time-to-target speedups over AdamW on three large-scale OGB benchmarks. Across 15 dataset-architecture configurations spanning node classification, inductive graph classification, and graph regression, it achieves lower time-to-target in 13 cases, while maintaining competitive or superior predictive performance. Code is available at https://github.com/guy-hadad/GraphMuon.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-230"></a>

### 230. [Learning a patient world model for health system simulation in intensive care](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08392v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08392

**作者：** Yingtai Li、Ruoyan Wang、Yu Li、Shaohua Kevin Zhou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08392v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08392v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文从纵向 EHR 中学习 ICU 患者的「世界模型」，用神经网络给出可跨场景复用的患者轨迹表示，冻结后经场景映射接入床位、护理调度与转出资源，模拟入院需求、护理任务与出院建议。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 无直接关联，但其「紧凑状态是否会丢失轨迹依赖信息」的对比（相同支持状态、不同历史导致约 24 小时恢复差异）可供核查 latent 推理状态应保留多少历史与任务必要信息、以及状态表示能否跨问题复用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning a patient world model for health system simulation in intensive care

**abstract/A1：** Designing healthcare strategies requires anticipating their clinical and operational consequences before implementation, a task that often relies on health system simulation. Such a simulation necessitates modeling how patient states evolve and generate care demands. However, conventional approaches typically tailor compact state transitions to a single policy question and omit history-dependent dynamics, thus requiring the redefinition of states and transitions when there is a new question that entails different clinical information. We propose to learn a patient world model from longitudinal EHRs using a neural network to provide a shared clinical representation of patient trajectories that can serve simulation questions across scenarios. Taking ICU operations as an example, we train the model on MIMIC-IV, freeze it, and couple its time-queryable predictions to shared ICU calendars, resources, and queues via scenario-specific mappings, so that one model renders admission demand, care tasks, and discharge proposals across simulations evaluating ICU bed capacity, care dispatch, and post-ICU transfer capacity. Experiments show that our model matches Markov and semi-Markov comparators in aggregate occupancy and directional responses to capacity and dispatch policies. It also reproduces policy gains from replayed observed trajectories. For patients entering the same support state from different prior trajectories, the learned representation captures a 24-h recovery difference (-6.4 predicted versus -6.7 observed percentage points), a contrast missed by compact state models, which propagates into distinct simulated care demand, queue burden, and bed use. These results indicate that a learned world model of patient dynamics has the potential to replace question-specific state models, allowing new care policies to be evaluated by reconfiguring the scenario without model rebuilding.

</details>

<a id="paper-231"></a>

### 231. [Conformal Prediction for Time-Dependent PDEs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08409v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08409

**作者：** Joshua Stiller、Annika Schneider、Eyke Hüllermeier

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08409v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08409v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究时变 PDE 代理模型上 conformal prediction 覆盖率失效的问题：证明函数空间中任意接近时刻的分布可互相奇异、精确覆盖不可实现，并说明离散化后总变差距离随网格分辨率指数饱和，进而用加权 conformal 方法在增长的时间horizon上恢复有限样本覆盖保证。

**相关性简评：** 「时间推进导致覆盖保证失效」与加权校准的思路，可能为 LVR 多步推理中 STOP 决策的可靠性校准和误差随步数累积问题提供可供核查的概念借鉴，但该文针对物理仿真，与视觉 latent 状态无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Conformal Prediction for Time-Dependent PDEs

**abstract/A1：** Uncertainty quantification is crucial in scientific machine learning, where models inform safety-critical tasks such as flood forecasting, financial risk management, and thermal control in machines. Conformal prediction provides distribution-free coverage guarantees, but in time-dependent settings common to physics and engineering, these guarantees can break down, leading to systematic undercoverage. We study this problem in the context of surrogate models for time-dependent physical systems described by partial differential equations. We prove that in a function space setting, distributions at arbitrarily close times can be mutually singular, making exact coverage guarantees impossible. We further show that in discretized settings, the total variation distance between distributions often saturates exponentially fast with the grid resolution, mandating restrictions on the spatial discretization to ensure coverage. Finally, for certain settings, we show how to use principled weighted conformal prediction to obtain finite-sample coverage guarantees over growing time horizons.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-232"></a>

### 232. [The Gold Rush in AI4Math, II: What are signatures of AI-assisted Proofs?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08412v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08412

**作者：** Yuanchuan Guo、Jiashun Jin、Zheng Tracy Ke、Bingcheng Sui

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、stat.AP

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08412v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08412v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建含 8,281 篇论文的五个队列（含匹配对照与生成式 AI 自产稿件），提出 ProofTrace 将每篇论文表示为「即时证明支持图」并提取可解释的数学组织特征，据此刻画不同 AI 参与程度的梯度、识别 AI 生成证明的结构签名，并在检测实验中区分人类撰写与披露实质 AI 使用的论文。

**相关性简评：** 其证明支持图及「中间结论依赖、外部引用减少、连通性变化」等结构特征，可能可供核查能否用来刻画 VLM/LVR 推理轨迹的结构组织与贡献分配，但属较远领域的迁移设想，需人工判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Gold Rush in AI4Math, II: What are signatures of AI-assisted Proofs?

**abstract/A1：** The growing use of AI in mathematics raises a question: can we detect AI involvement in proofs, separately from AI assistance with writing? Historical arXiv papers, recent papers disclosing substantive AI use, and manuscripts released by OpenAI provide a rich empirical basis for studying this question. However, several challenges remain: lack of matched case-control pairs; potential incompleteness of disclosure; and lack of ready-to-use features designed to capture signals of AI involvement in proofs. To address these challenges, we construct five cohorts comprising 8,281 papers by combining existing manuscripts with those we produced using generative AI. These cohorts span different levels of AI involvement and include matched pairs of papers addressing the same research problems. We then introduce ProofTrace, a framework that represents each paper as a graph of immediate proof support (GIPS) and extracts interpretable features of mathematical organization. Our study yields three discoveries. First, principal-component projections of our features reveal a qualitative gradient of AI involvement, where AI-reworded papers remain close to human papers, the two AI-generated cohorts depart in broadly shared directions, and papers disclosing substantive AI use occupy intermediate positions which shifts toward AI-generated cohorts over time. Second, we identify recurring signatures of AI-generated proofs, such as fewer externally cited results, greater reliance on intermediate conclusions, and altered graph connectivity; many of these signatures persist under controls for research field, manuscript length, or graph size. Finally, in a separate detection experiment, ProofTrace distinguishes human-written papers from those disclosing substantive AI use. Its performance remains stable under AI rewording, whereas the performance of state-of-the-art text-based detectors declines sharply.

**comment/C1：** 31 pages, 15 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-233"></a>

### 233. [Benchmarks as Software: Terminal-Bench and the Case for Continuous Benchmarks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08417v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08417

**作者：** E. Kelly Buchanan、Crystal Zhou、Scott W. Linderman、Christopher Ré

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08417v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08417v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出「benchmark maintenance debt」概念，构建结合社区报告、任务工件、agent traces、自动化 oracle 检查与人工把关修复的持续验证流程，据此审计 Terminal-Bench 2.0 并发布修复版 2.1，同时分析固定 wall-clock 预算如何改变 agent 表现与榜单结论。

**相关性简评：** 该工作不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态，但其「资源预算属于测量规范」及验证器可能误拒有效解的观点，可能可供核查 LVR 中动态计算/停止决策的收益成本核算与评测有效性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Benchmarks as Software: Terminal-Bench and the Case for Continuous Benchmarks

**abstract/A1：** Coding agents are among the most economically consequential deployments of LLMs to date, making reliable evaluation increasingly important. Yet even benchmarks that are carefully validated at release can become stale. As models improve, they can discover valid solutions that existing verifiers reject, while changes to evaluation infrastructure can invalidate assumptions embedded in tasks and tests. We call the resulting accumulation of defects benchmark maintenance debt: a growing gap between benchmark scores and the capabilities they are intended to measure. We introduce a continuous-validation workflow for agentic benchmarks that combines community reports, task artifacts, agent traces, automated oracle checks, and human-gated repairs. We apply this workflow to Terminal-Bench 2.0, a benchmark that underwent extensive human and LM-assisted review and red-teaming before release. Our audit identifies issues in roughly 30% of the benchmark, and we release the resulting repairs as Terminal-Bench 2.1. These repairs change evaluation results: accuracy increased by over 10 percentage points and leaderboard positions move by up to three ranks, even as the overall ranking remains highly correlated (Spearman rho = 0.958). We evaluate how resource allocation shapes benchmark conclusions. We show that fixed wall-clock can materially change agent performance: For example, extending the time limit closes a 20pp harness gap on GPT 5.4 and shrinks a 30 pp gap on GPT 5.4 mini to 5 pp. Benchmark maintenance is therefore not only about repairing task artifacts: resource budgets are part of the measurement specification and must be validated alongside the tasks themselves. More broadly, benchmark maintenance debt is not just accumulated implementation bugs: our results show that as models improve, both evaluation assumptions and resource settings may need to be revalidated.

</details>

<a id="paper-234"></a>

### 234. [On the limitations of molecular representations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08420v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08420

**作者：** Acer Blake、Fergus Imrie、Charlotte M. Deane

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08420v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08420v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文系统评估分子表示（指纹与学习嵌入）的质量，用图结构、3D 形状和 ADMET 相似性等指标检验其是否优于经典描述符，并报告 Enamine REAL 库中大量分子表示不唯一的问题。

**相关性简评：** 其「不依赖下游刷分、直接探查表示是否保留结构与几何相似性、以及表示碰撞」的评估思路，可能可供核查 latent 视觉状态是否保留任务必要细节、是否存在信息坍缩，但领域相隔较远，需较多迁移验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** On the limitations of molecular representations

**abstract/A1：** Molecular representations such as fingerprints and learned embeddings play a central role in cheminformatics and small-molecule machine learning. They serve as the primary input for a diverse array of tasks, including clustering, dataset splitting, and virtual screening, yet their evaluation is often limited to downstream model performance on narrow benchmark tasks. We carried out a systematic investigation of the quality of molecular representations, finding that none of the state-of-the-art molecular embedding models capture graph structure, 3D shape, or ADMET endpoint similarity better than fingerprints or physicochemical descriptors. However, classical fingerprints also revealed their own material limitations. For example, we show that depending on the representation, between 225M and $\\sim$4B molecules have non-unique representations in the 10.8B Enamine REAL database, with implications for virtual screening. Our findings provide an insight into the current limitations of molecular representations, outlining future research directions for the development of more robust encodings of small molecules.

</details>

<a id="paper-235"></a>

### 235. [Data-driven discovery of intrinsic Lie point symmetries of differential equation systems](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08421v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08421

**作者：** Ilker Gurcan、Kadir Ustun、Ramazan Gokberk Cinbis

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.DG、math.DS、math.GR、math.NA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08421v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08421v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文将从微分方程数据中直接发现内在 Lie 点对称生成元表述为采样射流空间上的算子学习问题，用延拓、子空间序列约束与固定非线性字典辨识生成元，并区分冗余、依赖解与几何平凡方向；附秩稳定预检，在已知 11 维对称代数基准上恢复生成元并复现 Lie 括号结构。

**相关性简评：** 其从数据辨识生成元、剔除冗余与平凡方向并检验代数结构稳定的思路，可能为 LVR 中判断 latent 状态哪些方向真正承载不变的任务信息、哪些只是表示冗余提供可供核查的诊断工具，但领域距离较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Data-driven discovery of intrinsic Lie point symmetries of differential equation systems

**abstract/A1：** Discovering continuous symmetries directly from differential-equation data can reveal geometric structure for model reduction and scientific machine learning without symbolic determining equations or a closed-form representation of the governing system. We formulate intrinsic Lie point-symmetry discovery as an operator-theoretic problem on sampled jet-space data. Admissible generators are identified from a sampled determining operator and separated from redundant, solution-dependent, and geometrically trivial directions. The framework combines prolongation, sequential subspace restrictions, and a fixed nonlinear dictionary linear in trainable coefficients. A rank-stabilization preflight test diagnoses systems whose represented intrinsic symmetry algebra does not stabilize under dictionary refinement. On a controlled differential-equation benchmark with an analytically known 11-dimensional represented intrinsic symmetry algebra, the method recovers the complete represented generator set, reproduces its Lie-bracket structure to numerical precision, and approximates a known generator beyond the training domain. Here we show that explicit intrinsic continuous-symmetry generators can be recovered from data obtained from exact or approximate differential systems.

**comment/C1：** 63 pages, 10 figures. Code and data availability statements are included in the manuscript

</details>

<a id="paper-236"></a>

### 236. [The Power of Meta Access in the Software of the Future --- Demonstrated on LLM Chat UIs and Chat AI](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08428v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08428

**作者：** Julian Kunkel、Ali Doosthosseini

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08428v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08428v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出「meta access」概念，主张让聊天助手获得界面自身的结构化、受权限约束的访问能力，并给出从功能教学到完整编排的分级阶梯，以 Meta Chat 原型演示摘要、设置与消息检索、可逆修改和角色推荐等机制。

**相关性简评：** 属于 HCI/接口设计方向，但其「把应用状态结构化暴露给模型并限定控制边界」的思路，可能与 VLM 推理中如何获取、筛选环境证据以及划分 LOOK/STOP 的决策边界有概念类比，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Power of Meta Access in the Software of the Future --- Demonstrated on LLM Chat UIs and Chat AI

**abstract/A1：** Chat remains the default surface for many large language model (LLM) tasks, even as agentic tools such as OpenCode excel at coding and automation. Yet the model sees the current dialogue but not the workspace around it. We explore meta access---giving the assistant a structured, permissioned view of the interface itself and propose a ladder of levels: feature tutoring; metadata, settings, and preference access; retrieval over summaries and messages, confirmed mutation, and full orchestration. Meta access can turn a chat client from many screens into one entry point for finding, navigating, organising, and operating it, while raising risks around grounding, privacy, prompt injection, and trust. General-purpose agentic harnesses are runtimes; because they can change their own settings, meta access becomes reflexive and needs a separate control-plane boundary. We ground this in the Meta Chat prototype of the Chat AI client. It builds bounded context from, e.g., summaries, settings, and relevant messages; links references to conversations, topics, and personas; supports confirmed, reversible changes; offers a browsable tutorial library; and recommends personas as user-started conversations. By exposing application state in structured form, meta access can extend screen readers from widget descriptions to semantic workspace operations. Meta access thus turns chat from a window onto one conversation into a control layer for the whole workspace, extending chat toward agentic capability without surrendering user control. As assistants become embedded in IDEs, dashboards, office suites, and operating systems, AI-era interfaces should expose a structured, permissioned model of their state and capabilities, ideally by opening their existing metamodels through a debugger-like interface.

</details>

<a id="paper-237"></a>

### 237. [Edge-Assisted Crowdsensing for Explainable Cyclist Safety Perception](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08441v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08441

**作者：** Hongsen Cao、Chia-Yen Chiang、Mona Jaber、Meihui Wang、Yasmin Fathy

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、eess.IV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08441v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08441v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作构建边缘辅助众感知系统：车把摄像头提供街景帧，VLM 以单次提示把每帧转成结构化交通属性，再由可解释提升机结合 OSM 道路设施与骑行运动学数据，预测 1-9 的众包安全评分，并给出精确的特征贡献归因。

**相关性简评：** 其「VLM 抽取属性→属性审计→属性误差对下游精度的敏感度分析」可供核查视觉证据是否真正进入决策、显式信息瓶颈是否充分的评价思路，但与 latent 状态生成和推理机制无直接联系，属较远领域的概念启发。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Edge-Assisted Crowdsensing for Explainable Cyclist Safety Perception

**abstract/A1：** Cyclist fatality rates remain high despite policy support for active travel, and autonomous vehicles add complexity to mixed traffic. Neither crash statistics nor multi-model detection pipelines scale to continuous monitoring of perceived safety along a route. We present an edge-assisted crowdsensing system: handlebar cameras supply street-level frames, a vision-language model (VLM) turns each frame into structured traffic attributes through a single prompt, and an explainable boosting machine (EBM) combines them with OpenStreetMap road infrastructure and on-bike kinematics to predict a crowdsourced 1-9 safety score. Extraction belongs at the network edge: the fastest self-hosted model needs 2.49 s per frame and scoring takes 0.4 ms on a CPU. On 1,362 annotated frames from two London routes, the EBM regressor reaches 72.2 percent binary accuracy and a mean absolute error of 1.011 under five-fold cross-validation. It is within one accuracy point of black-box ensembles. Its per-feature shape functions give exact attribution: physical lane separation is worth +0.97 rating points, a painted lane +0.70 and a construction site -0.65. Planners can therefore price an infrastructure change in rating points, and the results enter cost-benefit analysis directly. We also report inter-annotator agreement (alpha = 0.4695), an attribute audit, and a sensitivity analysis linking attribute error to accuracy. With re-encoding, a 20 s sampling interval serves 1,000 riders at 54 Mbit/s at a cost of 1.4 accuracy points, making continuous monitoring practical.

</details>

<a id="paper-238"></a>

### 238. [A Hidden Markov Preference Model for Online Prediction of Adversary Actions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08445v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08445

**作者：** Angel Daruna、Michael Cogswell、Tixiao Shan、Yi Yao、Han-Pang Chiu、Ajay Divakaran

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08445v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08445v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出一个隐马尔可夫偏好模型，用离散隐状态表示攻击者当前行为模式，在带可行性掩码的可执行动作树上建模动作分布，并以同一似然完成下一动作预测与模式信念的在线更新，在 CAGE 2 网络防御基准上评估。

**相关性简评：** 隐状态加在线滤波的结构、模式切换后的快速恢复，以及按路径加权的 KL 信息量度量，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的更新、动态计算与停止决策提供概念参照；但领域与视觉多模态相距较远，迁移价值需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Hidden Markov Preference Model for Online Prediction of Adversary Actions

**abstract/A1：** A defender anticipating an attacker needs a probability distribution over the attacker's next action from information available before that action occurs. Two structures complicate this: the attacker's behavioral regime persists and can switch within an episode, and each action combines an operation with state-dependent arguments whose feasible values change over time. We capture both with a hidden Markov model whose latent state is the active regime and whose regime-conditioned action law is defined on a feasibility-masked tree of executable actions. The same likelihood forms the next-action distribution and updates the regime belief once the action is seen, and a partially observed prefix contributes its resolved local factors. Estimation separates into convex logistic fits that Baum-Welch reuses when regimes are unlabeled. We derive a reach-weighted nodewise KL expression for regime information, a sequence-wise log-loss bound for switching regime paths, and a uniform fallback guarantee. On the CAGE 2 cyber defense benchmark the filtered latent state is worth 0.8 nats per step, and after a controller switch the loss begins to fall in three steps against ten for a recurrent baseline.

</details>

<a id="paper-239"></a>

### 239. [ReproBridge: Building Reproducibility into ML Research Artifacts](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08452v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08452

**作者：** Yubo Wang、Rui Meng、Jaehyun Nam、Tomas Pfister、Jinsung Yoon

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08452v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08452v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 ReproBridge，用编码智能体把论文、代码与辅助材料整理成标准化可复现工件（精炼代码库、环境配置、实验启动脚本、复现指南），并构建含 1029 个任务的 ReproBridge-Bench，另设 Auditor 依据执行证据判定复现是否成功。

**相关性简评：** 其「保留已恢复的流程与决策」以及独立 Auditor 依执行证据核验结果的设计，可能与 LVR 中证据核验、停止决策与中间状态保存的评价方式相关，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ReproBridge: Building Reproducibility into ML Research Artifacts

**abstract/A1：** Reproducing machine learning experiments requires reconstructing procedures from information scattered across papers, released code, and supporting artifacts. For coding agents, reproduction success can therefore depend on their ability to fill in missing details, rather than on the released artifacts alone. We ask whether preserving recovered procedures and decisions improves subsequent independent reproduction. We define a standardized artifact design comprising a refined codebase, environment setup, experiment launchers, and a reproduction guide, and introduce ReproBridge, an agent workflow for constructing these refined reproducible artifacts. ReproBridge-Bench provides 1,029 tasks from 387 experiments across 36 papers, anchored by five human-verified reference scores per task. Agents attempt tasks in fresh sessions with raw code or refined artifacts; a separate Auditor checks execution evidence. Across four evaluated models, refined artifacts raise mean reproduction success from 76.4% to 92.6% and reduce downstream API spending by 19.7% on average. The cross-model success-rate spread narrows from 21.6 to 3.9 percentage points, with all four models achieving mean success rates above 90%. Removing any component reduces success in the ablation study, with the largest losses from code and environment removal. These results suggest that preserving execution knowledge helps even models with lower raw-code success reproduce experiments more reliably and at lower API cost.

</details>

<a id="paper-240"></a>

### 240. [Same Behavior, Different Mechanism: What Finite Experiments Can Certify About Integrated Information](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08469v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08469

**作者：** Linqian Wu、Diaoxuan He

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、q-bio.NC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08469v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08469v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究在有限、部分且含噪的干预下，整合信息 φ 这类机制层面量何时可识别、对机制连续并可被有限数据认证，并在四、五个单元的随机网络中给出行为等价、部分观测盲区、机制边界突变及单侧下界认证的示例与证明。

**相关性简评：** 其「机制量能否从有限干预与部分内部记录中被识别和认证」的框架，可能启发 LVR 中对 latent 内容是否被因果使用、以及内部状态贡献在部分可观测下能否可靠评估的讨论；但 φ 与视觉推理相距较远，可迁移性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Same Behavior, Different Mechanism: What Finite Experiments Can Certify About Integrated Information

**abstract/A1：** Interpretability, AI auditing and assessments of machine consciousness increasingly rest on mechanism-level quantities: functionals of a system's causal transition law that describe how it computes, not what it outputs. Experiments see that law only through finite, partial and noisy interventions. We ask when such a quantity is identifiable from an experiment's access, continuous in the mechanism and certifiable from finite data, and answer exactly for a deliberately demanding case - the integrated information $\\varphi$ of integrated information theory - in stochastic networks of four and five units with known ground truth. Behavior can be silent: a recurrent and a feed-forward network with identical input-output laws at their response delay carry 0.28 and 0 bits, yet internal recordings separate them within 4-53 trials. Partial access can be just as blind: in our examples, units left out of a one-step recording impose precision floors of 0.30-1.02 bits that no sample size lowers, and in all-but-self parity networks, recordings of any length see several hidden units only through the product of their fidelities - a symmetry that one intervention broke in exact checks. The measure has sharp edges: because $\\varphi$ selects a partition, and the cause and effect it specifies, by one criterion and reports another, infinitesimal changes of mechanism that cross an edge change it by 25-100% at 99% of the edges we found in random networks, and about 1% of surrogate models that fit interventional data almost perfectly misreport it by more than 20%. One-sided claims survive: the same data certify a lower bound on both sides of an edge, and we prove $\\varphi&gt;1.004$ bits throughout a 64-dimensional region of mechanisms and certify it from about $10^7$ controlled transitions with false-certificate probability at most $α$. Measurability is thus a design question with computable answers.

**comment/C1：** 30 pages, 7 figures. Code available as ancillary files

</details>

### 241. [EgoGeneralist: Unifying Egocentric Action-Video Tasks through Video-Native Modeling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08477v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08477

**作者：** Yanlin Jin、Bowen Yang、Cheng Zhang、Yifan Yang、Zhe Li、Jianfei Cai、Kai Zhu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08477v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08477v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 EgoGeneralist，将预训练视频生成器适配到第一视角动作视频，用角色自适应动作渲染器与跨流耦合统一动作条件视频生成、姿态估计和联合生成共五项任务，并在未见场景上验证泛化。

**相关性简评：** 其将 metric 3D 动作同时作为空间控制信号与可恢复生成目标，可能为 LVR 中几何/动作信息如何进入内部表征并受监督提供线索；但材料未涉及推理期 latent 状态、证据筛选或停止决策，仅供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EgoGeneralist: Unifying Egocentric Action-Video Tasks through Video-Native Modeling

**abstract/A1：** Modern video models possess strong visual priors, yet do not natively represent human action in metric 3D space. We present EgoGeneralist, which adapts pretrained video generators to unify action-conditioned egocentric video generation, pose estimation, and joint action-video generation. Our role-adaptive action renderer provides video-native representations for multidirectional action-video learning, making metric motion usable as both a spatial control signal and a recoverable generation target. Cross-stream action-video coupling establishes close correspondence between metric motion and visual dynamics through lightweight adaptation while preserving the pretrained visual prior. Together, these designs establish a generalist framework spanning five action-video tasks. Despite adaptation only on controlled laboratory recordings, EgoGeneralist generalizes to diverse unseen scenes and interactions with effective action control and visual quality, bridging pretrained visual knowledge and metric 3D human motion.

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://www.neiljin.site/egogeneralist\_page/

</details>

### 242. [Scientist World: Grow a Research World for AI-Scientists](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08478v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08478

**作者：** Taiming Lu、Sachin Konan、Zhuang Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08478v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08478v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建「Scientist World」：把每篇论文转化为可执行研究环境，自动提取开放问题并复现其方法作基线，让 AI-Scientist 在其中研究并对照人类作者的成果，发布 1000 个已验证环境、6400 小时研究轨迹，并用这些轨迹训练小模型提升研究构思能力。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉/多模态推理无直接关联，但其「可执行环境 + 长程轨迹记录 + 轨迹训练」的评测与监督思路，可能为 LVR 中推理轨迹的训练、贡献分配和过程评价设计提供参照，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Scientist World: Grow a Research World for AI-Scientists

**abstract/A1：** AI-Scientists arrived, but a world for them to grow in did not. Human scientists take theirs for granted $-$ centuries of open problems and shared ideas, waiting in the scientific literature. To AI-Scientists, that world is readable but not runnable, so we build Scientist World for them: every research paper becomes an executable environment. Our autonomous pipeline extracts each paper's open-ended research question and reproduces its proposed method as the baseline; an AI-Scientist entering the environment is evaluated against what the human authors achieved. We release the first 1,000 verified environments. With the world as an evaluation, we found that frontier AI-Scientists can already surpass the human researchers 74% of the time. As a record of how AI does science, it contains 6,400 hours of research traces, with every idea, experiment, failure, and success. As a training ground, we are able to raise (+158%) a small model to match frontier models in research ideation. At last, AI-Scientists' own world has arrived, grown from and growing with science itself.

</details>

### 243. [Learning Noise Optimization for Reward Alignment in Few-Step Generative Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06255v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06255

**作者：** Hongeun Kim、Jeongsol Kim、Jong Chul Ye

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06255v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06255v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 LeNO，用 Local GRPO 与标量奖励学习初始噪声的残差传输，把奖励对齐的噪声优化摊销到训练阶段，使 few-step 扩散/流模型推理时只需一次噪声修正。

**排除原因：** 工作目标是 few-step 生成模型的奖励对齐与生成质量，不涉及 VLM 推理状态、视觉证据筛选或推理过程训练；其中「标量奖励驱动的 latent 噪声修正」至多是很远的类比，缺少可核查的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning Noise Optimization for Reward Alignment in Few-Step Generative Models

**abstract/A1：** Reward alignment for diffusion- and flow-based generative models is often formulated over stochastic trajectories, making it less suitable for few-step deterministic generators. Initial noise optimization provides a trajectory-free alternative, but existing gradient-free methods require costly per-sample search at inference. We introduce LeNO (Learning Noise Optimization), which amortizes reward-guided initial noise optimization into a learned residual transport while keeping the pretrained generator frozen. LeNO learns an anchor-conditioned distribution over noise corrections using Local GRPO and scalar rewards, eliminating iterative test-time optimization. For Gaussian initial noise with norm-preserving projection, we derive a tractable upper bound on the KL divergence between the corrected noise distribution and the original prior, providing a principled regularization objective. We evaluate LeNO on few-step text-to-image and protein structure generation, including expensive black-box designability rewards. Across both domains, LeNO improves target rewards with a single noise correction at inference while largely preserving generation quality and diversity.

</details>

<a id="paper-244"></a>

### 244. [T$^2$-Splat: Adaptive Topology Mesh Splatting with Texture Residuals](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06974v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06974

**作者：** Zhihao Tang、Youjia Zhang、Mingbo Zhao、Wei Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.GR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06974v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06974v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该工作面向三维网格表示与新视角合成的拓扑自适应和纹理残差，属于图形学/重建领域，材料中未见与 VLM 推理中的内部状态、证据筛选或停止决策相关的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** T$^2$-Splat: Adaptive Topology Mesh Splatting with Texture Residuals

**abstract/A1：** Triangle-based splatting connects novel view synthesis with conventional graphics pipelines, but often requires excessive tessellation to preserve appearance details. We present T$^2$-Splat, which decouples topology adaptation from appearance refinement. Safe Restricted Delaunay Triangulation (Safe RDT) initializes mesh connectivity. Dynamic Topology Evolution (DTE) then adapts triangle allocation through edge collapse and vertex split operations. Residual per-face Mip-textures then refine appearance on the fixed mesh using scale-adaptive filtering. Experiments on Mip-NeRF 360, Tanks &amp; Temples, and DTU demonstrate competitive results with compact meshes. T$^2$-Splat improves PSNR by 0.71dB over MeshSplatting on Mip-NeRF 360. Additional compression experiments demonstrate flexible control of mesh complexity, supporting face budgets as low as 10% of the original face count. Project page: https://t2-splat.github.io/.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

### 245. [BlockLock: Reproducible LLM Inference with Fixed Batch Geometry](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06977v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06977

**作者：** Xiuyuan Qi、Pedro Reviriego、Javier Conde、Shanshan Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06977v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06977v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 BlockLock，通过固定受保护请求所见的批量几何（行宽固定或终端锚定打包）使其输出不随同批其他请求变化，从而在保留共享 prefill 的同时实现可复现推理并降低延迟。

**排除原因：** 该工作是推理服务系统的批内数值可复现性与吞吐优化，虽涉及 KV 状态提取，但不讨论视觉信息保留、latent 推理状态生成或证据使用，与 LVR 主线无实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BlockLock: Reproducible LLM Inference with Fixed Batch Geometry

**abstract/A1：** Reproducible LLM inference requires that a request produces the same output regardless of the other requests in the batch. Existing solutions pay for this in different ways: batch-invariant kernels constrain every operator, and isolating prefill with fixed-shape decode ({our CoRun-style baseline}) gives up shared prefill compute and pads decode. We present BlockLock, which keeps shared batched prefill and pins only the numerical path of the protected request: it fixes the geometry that the request sees, with {two modes: BlockLock-Rows uses fixed-width rows and retains batched decode of all requests; BlockLock-Pack uses terminal-anchored packing with an isolated dummy segment, then extracts the protected request's KV state for target-only decode. Under a stated per-deployment contract, both modes preserve the target's output bitwise when its companions change.} Across five {dense} models (Qwen3-8B/14B, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, and Gemma-3-4B/12B-IT) and three single-GPU deployments (NVIDIA A6000, A100, and H100), we evaluate our approach on both tinyBenchmarks and BFCL{; we also include initial tensor-parallel experiments on two NVIDIA A6000 GPUs. Both modes preserve target sequences and Top20-logit bits in the reported tests.} At equal delivered work, {BlockLock-Rows} reduces latency by 53.5--55.0\\% and improves throughput by 115.2--122.2\\% relative to vLLM Batch-Invariant; compared with batch-invariant kernels, it needs no custom kernel implementations and constrains only the protected requests.

</details>

<a id="paper-246"></a>

### 246. [Resource-Efficient Reinforcement Learning Post-Training through Elastic Expert Parallelism](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06984v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06984

**作者：** Jia Wei、Ziyi Qiu、Yi Xu、Xuehai Qian

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.DC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06984v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06984v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 FlexFloor 系统，在 MoE 模型的同步响应生成阶段联合调整响应调度与工作节点活跃组，使完成本地生成的工作节点可提前释放资源，同时保留在途响应所需的专家计算，从而提升 RL 后训练 rollout 吞吐。

**排除原因：** 该工作属于训练基础设施层面的吞吐与显存调度优化，虽涉及计算资源分配，但不涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部状态、证据获取或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺乏具体可核查的联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Resource-Efficient Reinforcement Learning Post-Training through Elastic Expert Parallelism

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement learning post-training improves the capabilities of large language models, but generating training responses is a major computational bottleneck. In mixture-of-experts models, workers that finish their assigned responses may remain occupied because their experts are still needed by workers processing longer responses. Consequently, approximately one-third of occupied worker time in the baseline occurs after local response generation finishes. Here we present FlexFloor, a system that enables earlier resource release during synchronous response generation while preserving the expert computation required by ongoing responses. FlexFloor jointly adapts response scheduling and the active group of workers. It uses historical response lengths and memory constraints to guide batching and prompt placement, then progressively releases workers while maintaining expert availability and correct communication. Compared with the verl baseline, FlexFloor generates approximately 65.40% more response tokens per unit of occupied computing time and increases response generation throughput by 52.74%, with little observed variation in rollout reward. By making reinforcement learning post-training more efficient, FlexFloor supports faster development and iterative improvement of large language models and AI agents.

**comment/C1：** 19 pages including references and appendices, 5 figures

### 247. [Gaussians Can Subtract: Neural Dissolve Fields for Hybrid Scene Rendering](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07049v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07049

**作者：** Yiyan Wang、Haohan Liu、Tetsuo Shibuya

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07049v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07049v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文将 Mesh–Gaussian 混合场景重建表述为多视角不一致问题，提出「先溶解后补填」框架与轻量 Neural Dissolve Field，学习在哪些视角方向上溶解网格内容并让残差 Gaussian 补全，从而以更少 primitives 获得更高视觉保真度与更快渲染。

**排除原因：** 属于三维重建与神经渲染的表示/渲染效率研究，不涉及 VLM 推理状态、视觉证据筛选或 THINK/LOOK/STOP 等决策过程，其「溶解」机制也仅是渲染可见性控制，未提供可迁移到 latent visual reasoning 的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Gaussians Can Subtract: Neural Dissolve Fields for Hybrid Scene Rendering

**abstract/A1：** Hybrid Mesh-Gaussian rendering combines compact textured surfaces with flexible 3D Gaussian primitives, offering an efficient alternative to purely Gaussian scene representations for structured environments. Existing scene-reconstruction hybrids commonly follow an additive formulation, where Gaussian residuals compensate for errors left by the mesh. However, not every Mesh-Gaussian conflict is caused by missing content: residual geometry errors, occlusion mismatch, texture-projection artifacts, and view-dependent appearance can make the same mesh content consistent in one view but inconsistent in another. We therefore formulate hybrid rendering as a multi-view inconsistency problem and present Gaussolve, a subtract-and-refill framework that selectively dissolves inconsistent mesh content before residual Gaussians refill the revealed regions. We introduce a lightweight Neural Dissolve Field, parameterized by scalar spherical-harmonic Gaussians, to learn where and from which viewing directions mesh content should be dissolved. A continuous transmittance fusion rule progressively exposes residual Gaussians behind the dissolved mesh, preserving their reconstruction gradients and avoiding hard visibility discontinuities. Experiments on structured and semi-structured scenes demonstrate higher visual fidelity with fewer Gaussian primitives and faster rendering. Project page: https://ooosn.github.io/gaussians-can-subtract/

**comment/C1：** Accepted to WACV 2027. 16 pages including supplementary material, 11 figures, 8 tables. Project page: https://ooosn.github.io/gaussians-can-subtract/

</details>

### 248. [Regulating Autonomous Mobility-on-Demand Operators with Fixed and Adaptive Penalties: A Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Approach](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07122v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07122

**作者：** Korbinian Kügler、Carolin Schmidt、Florian Dandl、Filipe Rodrigues

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.MA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07122v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07122v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该研究将自动驾驶出行（AMoD）运营商的定价与车辆调度监管建模为固定惩罚的 MDP 与周期性调整区域惩罚的部分可观测随机博弈，并用图结构多目标强化学习求解。

**排除原因：** 研究关注城市出行市场的价格与区域服务监管，不涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部状态、证据使用或停止决策；「外层监管回路」仅是一般性多智能体控制框架，未提供可核查的 LVR 方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Regulating Autonomous Mobility-on-Demand Operators with Fixed and Adaptive Penalties: A Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Approach

**abstract/A1：** Profit-maximizing Autonomous Mobility-on-Demand (AMoD) operators may exploit consumer surplus through excessive prices and underserve less profitable regions by relocating vehicles away from them. We study how penalty-based regulation of an AMoD operator in a multimodal mobility system can limit surge pricing and improve regional service availability. We model regulation as an outer control loop that acts on a learning-based operator. Static regulation with fixed penalties is formulated as a Markov Decision Process for an operator that jointly controls pricing and rebalancing, and dynamic regulation as a Partially Observable Stochastic Game in which a regulator periodically adjusts region-specific penalties in closed loop. Operator and regulator policies are learned with graph-based multi-agent reinforcement learning. In experiments with real-world Manhattan data, fixed surge pricing penalties reduce the share of travelers facing excessive prices from 22% to 1%. For regional service availability, dynamic regulation improves the regulator's reward by up to 15.6% over fixed penalties of equal mean intensity. The gains in service availability are strongest when spare fleet capacity exists, whereas service deficits persist in peak periods with highly utilized fleets. The learned regulator policy responds by reducing penalties where limited fleet capacity restricts compliance. The results identify trade-offs between regulatory targets, operator profitability, and traveler utility.

</details>

### 249. [Machine Learning for Live-Birth Prediction After IVF/ICSI From Structured Clinical Data: A Systematic Review, Meta-Analysis, and Critical Appraisal](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07124v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07124

**作者：** Abdul Raheem Shahzad、Muhammad Zaheer Khan、Umar Ajaib、Asad Ur Rehman、Juan Deng、Ming Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07124v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07124v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 对IVF/ICSI后活产预测的机器学习模型做系统综述与随机效应元分析，并按PROBAST+AI、TRIPOD+AI评估偏倚风险与验证设计。

**排除原因：** 属于结构化临床表格数据的预测建模与证据综合，不涉及视觉表示、latent 推理状态或生成与停止决策，无可具体借鉴的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Machine Learning for Live-Birth Prediction After IVF/ICSI From Structured Clinical Data: A Systematic Review, Meta-Analysis, and Critical Appraisal

**abstract/A1：** Machine-learning models predicting live birth after in vitro fertilisation (IVF) or intracytoplasmic sperm injection (ICSI) from routine clinical data report performance from near chance to near perfect. We systematically reviewed these models under PRISMA 2020, searching OpenAlex, Scopus and Web of Science to 22 October 2026, appraised them with PROBAST+AI and TRIPOD+AI, and pooled their discrimination with random-effects meta-analysis on the logit scale. We define what fixes the meaning of a model's output (decision point, endpoint, unit of analysis, data source) and map the 48 included studies by model family, decision point and validation design. Models evaluated only internally pooled to an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of 0.814, against 0.756 (95% prediction interval 0.546-0.889) for models evaluated on a later period or another centre, and 0.764 for studies not at high risk of bias; neither difference was significant, and heterogeneity was extreme. Models whose decision point was not stated pooled to 0.910, the only significant moderator in meta-regression. Against logistic regression fitted to the same data, the best machine-learning model gained a pooled 0.056 AUC (95% CI 0.017-0.090). Of 48 studies, 47 were at high or unclear risk of bias, only four assessed the calibration slope, and no model has evidence of clinical impact. The certainty of the pooled evidence is low to very low; the gains that survive validation came from staging predictions to decision points and local calibration, not deeper architectures. We provide a TRIPOD+AI-mapped checklist, a benchmark specification and a research agenda.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages, 4 figures, 6 tables; supplementary material (26 pages) in ancillary files. Data and code: https://github.com/abdul-raheem-shahzad/ml-ivf-live-birth-review. Submitted to IEEE Transactions on Pattern Analysis and Machine Intelligence

</details>

### 250. [Data-Driven Modeling of Molten Chloride Corrosion Using Static Test Data](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07125v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07125

**作者：** Md Mahmudul Hasan Pathik

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cond-mat.mtrl-sci

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07125v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07125v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文汇编 291 项文献中的熔融氯化物静态腐蚀实验，构建结构化数据集并训练可解释树集成模型预测腐蚀速率，同时提出 CSPI 指标统一表征盐处理条件，并用 SHAP 与保形预测给出解释和不确定性。

**排除原因：** 这是材料腐蚀领域的表格数据建模工作，不涉及视觉或多模态推理、latent 状态生成与使用，也无可迁移至 LVR 的具体线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Data-Driven Modeling of Molten Chloride Corrosion Using Static Test Data

**abstract/A1：** Molten chloride corrosion has been studied for decades, yet reliable prediction across different alloys, salts, and test conditions remains difficult. In this work, 291 static molten chloride corrosion experiments reported in the literature were compiled into a structured dataset and used to develop an interpretable machine-learning framework for corrosion-rate predictions. To better represent the differences in salt condition across studies, a Chloride Salt Preparation Index (CSPI) was introduced to bring the reported salt-treatment method, atmosphere control, and pre-exposure impurity condition on a common numerical scale. A blended tree-based ensemble provided the most consistent predictive performance, achieving a cross-validated \\(R^2\\) of 0.85 and an MAE of 0.41 mg cm\\(^{-2}\\) day\\(^{-1}\\). The graded CSPI retained more useful information than a simple purified/unpurified classification and emerged as the dominant model descriptor. Global sensitivity and SHAP analyses further identified exposure time, temperature, and alloy chemistry as important contributors to the model predictions, with several observed trends consistent with the established molten-chloride corrosion behavior. The conformal prediction intervals were well calibrated, providing an estimate of prediction uncertainty in addition to the point prediction. Overall, the framework demonstrates that historical corrosion data can support quantitative, interpretable, and uncertainty-aware prediction when experimental context is represented explicitly, while also highlighting where more consistent reporting and targeted experiments are needed to improve future molten-salt corrosion models.

</details>
