### 251. [A Proxy-Based Method for Measuring Racial Disparities in~Text-to-Image~Generators](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07132v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07132

**作者：** Nia Mucher、Ngan Pham、Jason Chin、Aaron Brown、David Evans

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07132v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07132v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出一种自动审计方法，借助美国反歧视法中的种族代理变量，将文本到图像模型生成图像的种族分布与人口基准对比，并结合 CIELAB 肤色度量与组间余弦相似度，分析三个模型在不同代理类别下的表征偏差。

**排除原因：** 主题为图像生成的公平性审计与人口统计偏差度量，不涉及 VLM 推理状态、证据获取或停止决策；组间余弦相似度虽属表示几何，但用于衡量生成图像质量差异，与 latent 推理状态的内容、深度或缓存结构无具体可借鉴联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Proxy-Based Method for Measuring Racial Disparities in~Text-to-Image~Generators

**abstract/A1：** We present an automated method for auditing text-to-image generators for representational bias against ground-truth demographic benchmarks. Our approach adapts the racial proxy variables used in U.S. antidiscrimination law to provide a fine-grained comparative analysis of variations in racial representation in generated images. We classify each generated image by race and then compare the model's distribution to publicly available demographic benchmark data to identify patterns of over- and under-representation. We also measure skin tone using CIELAB values and calculate cosine similarity amongst groups of images in order to capture biases that don't show up in demographic comparisons such as differences in image quality across races and skin tones. We report on experiments testing three models (Imagen 4 Fast, Stable Diffusion 3.5 Large Turbo, and Flux Schnell) using three categories of proxies (geographic, occupational, and surname) and 50 variants within each category. We find notable variation across the models as well as by proxy category. For example, Imagen 4 Fast overrepresents white classified images more than the other models tested and produces images with lighter, more homogeneous skin tones. Imagen 4 Fast also had the largest representational disparity for Black demographics among surname and geographical prompts. Stable Diffusion 3.5 Large Turbo produces results that overall diverge most from census data but yield the most diverse range of skin tones. Our method yields a generalizable and legally-grounded auditing tool that surfaces detailed information about model outputs without relying on implicit normative definitions of fairness.

</details>

### 252. [Backbone-Informed Adaptation after EEG Pretraining: A Capacity-Controlled Study](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07192v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07192

**作者：** Mingyang Jiang、Yamin Li、Fan Ma、Daniel Moyer、Hua Xu、Catie Chang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07192v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07192v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在 EEG 基础模型上固定编码器，比较「骨干结构对齐的 adapter」与参数量近似匹配的 token-wise MLP，在情绪识别、睡眠分期与事件分类任务上发现前者并无一致优势。

**排除原因：** 研究对象是 EEG 信号的下游适配与容量匹配比较，不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据获取或停止决策；「结构感知设计未必优于等容量控制」只是一般性经验，未提供针对 LVR 的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Backbone-Informed Adaptation after EEG Pretraining: A Capacity-Controlled Study

**abstract/A1：** Electroencephalography (EEG) foundation models learn representations that combine information across channels and time. This structure motivates adapters that model the same relationships during downstream learning. However, it is not clear whether such a design outperforms other candidate adapters. Moreover, in order to arrive at a fair comparison between candidate adapters, we also need to consider their trainable parameters. Here, we ask whether a backbone-informed (native) adapter makes better use of a given capacity by comparing it against a token-wise multilayer perceptron control that transforms each feature vector independently. We approximately match their trainable parameter counts and keep the pretrained encoder fixed. Across two EEG foundation models and tasks covering emotion recognition, sleep staging, and EEG event classification, the native adapter shows no consistent advantage. For one backbone, differences between the designs are small relative to variation across runs. For the other, the token-wise control performs better on emotion recognition and EEG event classification. On sleep staging, the native adapter's advantage over the token-wise control is reduced when we also train the component that processes sequences of EEG segments. The relative performance of the two adapters depends on the backbone, task, and downstream training protocol. These findings motivate evaluating backbone-informed adapters against capacity-matched alternatives.

**comment/C1：** 25 pages, 6 figures. Code available at https://github.com/0oJimmyo0/EEGxPlore-TMLR

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07264

**作者：** Clariele Almeida、Renan Beserra、Rafael Moura、Danilo Araújo、Bruno Nogueira、Sérgio Chevtchenko、Ermeson Andrade

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AR、cs.ET

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07264v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07264v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文是对资源受限嵌入式系统中 TinyML 能耗测量与优化研究的系统性映射，梳理了常用模型、能耗评估方法与指标、影响因素，并将优化技术归类为模型压缩、硬件感知设计、系统与通信优化、数据处理优化。

**排除原因：** 材料聚焦微控制器端能耗测量与压缩部署的文献分类与标准化问题，未涉及视觉或多模态推理中的内部状态、证据获取、停止决策或训练监督，也无可直接借鉴的状态更新线索，故不纳入。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Energy Measurement and Optimization of TinyML in Resource-Constrained Embedded Systems: A Systematic Mapping Study

**abstract/A1：** The rapid expansion of the Internet of Things (IoT) has increased the volume of data generated at the network edge, where many devices operate under severe memory, processing, and energy constraints. In this context, Tiny Machine Learning (TinyML) enables machine learning (ML) tasks to be executed on microcontrollers, smart sensors, and other resource-constrained platforms. However, the literature remains heterogeneous regarding the algorithms evaluated, energy-measurement methods, reported metrics, influencing factors, and optimization strategies. This study presents a systematic mapping of energy-aware TinyML research. It identifies the main models and learning approaches, classifies energy-evaluation methodologies and metrics, examines the technical factors affecting energy consumption, and organizes the reported optimization techniques into a structured taxonomy. The findings highlight the frequent use of convolutional and lightweight architectures, direct measurements and analytical estimations, and factors related to model architecture, hardware, communication, numerical precision, and input characteristics. The optimization strategies were grouped into model compression, hardware-aware design, system and communication optimization, and data and processing optimization. The results also reveal limited standardization in experimental platforms, measurement procedures, and reporting practices, limiting reproducibility and direct comparison across studies. This mapping provides an updated overview of the field and guidance for developing and evaluating more energy-efficient TinyML solutions.

**comment/C1：** 40 pages, 10 figures, 8 tables

</details>

### 254. [Householder Motion Embedding: Noise Modulation for Motion-Controllable Video Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07294v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07294

**作者：** Tao Jun Lin、Jinhong Ni、Ruyi Zha、Yifu Wang、Xibin Song、Pan Ji、Hongdong Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07294v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07294v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Householder Motion Embedding，用一串 Householder 反射构成的正交变换把运动信号嵌入每帧噪声的通道向量，在保持帧内高斯结构的同时引入跨帧相关性，用于运动可控视频生成。

**排除原因：** 属于视频扩散生成中的噪声调控与运动控制，不涉及视觉推理状态、证据获取或停止决策；正交保结构变换虽与 latent 更新有形式相似，但材料未提供可核查的 LVR 借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Householder Motion Embedding: Noise Modulation for Motion-Controllable Video Generation

**abstract/A1：** Diffusion models for video generation have achieved remarkable success, yet precise motion control under explicit user guidance remains challenging. Recent noise-warping methods introduce temporal correlations by warping the noise according to the user-provided motion signal. However, standard spatial warping generally disrupts the noise's i.i.d. Gaussian structure, requiring distribution correction, such as Jacobian-based correction, but at a substantial computational overhead. We propose Householder Motion Embedding (HME), which encodes the motion signal into the noise through a sequence of Householder reflections that together form an orthogonal transformation applied to each location's channel vector. HME exactly preserves the spatial Gaussian structure within each frame while introducing cross-frame correlations that promote temporally consistent video. HME is agnostic to the network architecture and integrates directly into standard video diffusion fine-tuning pipelines. Experiments on different scenarios show that HME matches or outperforms the state of the art, while achieving a 2.7x speedup over noise warping. These results demonstrate that channel-space Householder transformations offer a principled and efficient alternative to noise warping for motion-controllable video generation.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026

</details>

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出一个两阶段推荐框架，先依据与预期登陆点的距离筛选可能受热带气旋影响的港口，再用估计的受影响天数对港口排序，并比较三种港口—气旋交互特征表示方式。

**排除原因：** 属于气象灾害下的港口影响排序应用，虽涉及不确定性下的检索与排序，但未触及视觉或多模态推理的内部状态、证据筛选与生成机制，缺乏可核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Robust Ranking of Port Impacts from Impending Tropical Cyclones: A Two-Stage Recommendation System

**abstract/A1：** When a tropical cyclone (TC) threatens several ports, preparedness planning depends on anticipating which ports are likely to be affected and where disruptions may last longest. Limited historical observations at individual ports and uncertainty in landfall location make these relative impacts difficult to estimate. We use a two-stage recommendation framework that first selects candidate ports based on their proximity to the expected landfall location and then ranks them by their estimated number of impact days. Using AIS-derived impact days for 37 ports and 31 TCs in the Gulf of Mexico study region, we compare three ways of representing port--cyclone interactions: pairwise feature interactions, tree-based feature combinations, and explicit feature crosses combined with learned embeddings. In tests on Hurricanes Nicole and Ian, the retrieval stage consistently identifies all observed affected ports under landfall uncertainty. Among the ranking models, Wide &amp; Deep Learning achieves the closest agreement with the observed port rankings. Its mean ranking agreement exceeds that of the distance-based baseline by 85.5% for Nicole and 55.5% for Ian and is less sensitive to uncertainty in the landfall location. These rankings could help port authorities prioritize post-cyclone inspections and allocate recovery resources across ports threatened by the same event.

</details>

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该工作面向 CNC 钻孔的超声声学异常检测与预测性维护，比较自编码器与 XGBoost 的早期卡钻识别，不涉及多模态推理状态、视觉证据使用或推理期决策机制，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Acoustic Anomaly Detection for Early Identification of Drill Jamming

**abstract/A1：** In the context of Industry 4.0, predictive maintenance is essential to minimize unplanned downtime and ensure operational safety. In machining processes, drill jamming is a critical failure that compromises both workpiece quality and tool integrity. This work proposes an acoustic anomaly detection system using ultrasonic sensors to identify pre-failure patterns in Computer Numerical Control (CNC) machining. We release a public dataset of time- and frequency-domain acoustic features from 17 drills, and benchmark semi-supervised autoencoder-based normality modelling against supervised XGBoost classification using Leave-One-Drill-Out validation. The results demonstrate that the Autoencoder is highly effective for industrial safety, achieving a Recall of 1.00 and a consolidated F1-score of 0.81. Furthermore, the threshold-independent analysis shows that the Autoencoder better separates normal and pre-failure acoustic patterns, achieving a consolidated AUC of 0.8294, compared with 0.7559 for the supervised model under inter-tool covariate shift. In contrast, the supervised XGBoost achieved an average F1-score of 0.55 and failed to detect failures in some of the evaluated drills. The findings highlight that the ultrasonic spectrum provides a reliable window for identifying incipient mechanical instabilities, and the AE model offers a robust safety envelope by anticipating all jamming events across the tested drill bits.

**comment/C1：** Accepted for publication at IEEE INDIN 2026. 8 pages, 6 figures. (c) 2026 IEEE. Personal use of this material is permitted

</details>

### 257. [WeLM-Image: A Unified Image Generation Model towards Visual Intelligence](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07392v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07392

**作者：** Canmiao Fu、Chen Li、Guohui Zhang、Hao Lu、Hongyang Wei、Huadong Wu、Huaisong Zhang、Hubery Yin、Liyuan Cui、Qixin Yan、Tong Shao、Xiaoqiang Zhou、Xinhang Leng、Ying Zhang、Zhenhai Liu、Zhipeng Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07392v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07392v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出统一的图像基础模型 WeLM-Image，用 MoT/MoE 骨干支持文生图、指令编辑与多参考合成，并通过数据引擎、分项奖励与在策略蒸馏提升生成质量与少步推理速度。

**排除原因：** 该工作面向图像生成与编辑，其中的 target latent 是生成目标而非推理过程状态，也未见证据获取、停止决策或 latent 内容使用分析，因而与 LVR 主线缺乏实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** WeLM-Image: A Unified Image Generation Model towards Visual Intelligence

**abstract/A1：** We present \\textbf{WeLM-Image}, a unified image foundation model that integrates multimodal understanding into text-to-image generation, instruction-guided editing, and multi-reference synthesis. Designed for single-GPU deployment in WeChat's large-scale consumer applications, the model processes text, reference images, and target latents as one interleaved sequence. A Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) backbone combines shared attention with independent understanding and generation parameters to limit optimization interference. Equipping each path with Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) modules provides sparse capacity, with 31.7B stored parameters and approximately 5.4B generation parameters activated per target latent token. A generation-oriented data engine combines a knowledge-grounded visual vocabulary with large-scale visual clustering to balance semantic coverage and editing tasks, while training-time evaluation guides iterative data refinement. Bilingual annotations and verified editing supervision support progressive training up to native 1.5K resolution. We optimize generation and editing separately with rubric- and checklist-based rewards, then consolidate the specialized policies through noise-aware on-policy distillation. In the reported overall comparisons, the 1536-resolution model ranks first among the evaluated open-source models on PRISM-Bench, Qwen-Image-Bench, and UnicBench. MeanFlow-based few-step distillation yields WeLM-Image-Turbo, which generates a $1024\\times1024$ image in 4.98\\,s on a single NVIDIA H20 GPU using eight guidance-free steps.

</details>

### 258. [FreePath: Neural Mean Free Path for Image Dehazing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07398v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07398

**作者：** Rongrong Gao、Jingxuan Feng、Wenyu Xu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07398v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07398v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 FreePath 神经平均自由程框架，将单图去雾建模为特征空间路径状态复原，用残差模块学习路径与碰撞场并守恒融合状态。

**排除原因：** 该工作属低层图像复原，关注大气散射反演与感知质量，虽出现「状态」术语，但不涉及视觉推理中的 latent 状态生成、证据筛选或停止决策，缺少可迁移的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FreePath: Neural Mean Free Path for Image Dehazing

**abstract/A1：** Single image dehazing requires recovering scene radiance from visual responses that are weakened and veiled by participating media. Existing methods either invert explicit scattering variables, which can be unstable under complex scenes, or learn direct restoration mappings, which often discard the physical organization of haze formation. We present \\emph{FreePath}, a neural mean free path framework for image dehazing. Instead of estimating atmospheric parameters, FreePath represents dehazing as feature space path state restoration, inspired by the expected distance a visual response can propagate before interacting with haze. Within a residual encoder and decoder, the Mean Free Path Residual Block learns path and collision fields to guide haze constrained correction, while FreePath State Conservation Integration preserves restored path states during deep feature aggregation. Experiments on paired CDD-11 haze images and real RTTS scenes show that FreePath consistently improves full reference fidelity, no reference perceptual quality, and visual faithfulness over representative dehazing baselines.

</details>

### 259. [DelegateEval: Evaluating Platforms for Personal External Service Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07508v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07508

**作者：** Jichao Li、Jinrong Lu、Manjiang Yu、Zhixuan Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07508v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07508v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DelegateEval，一个面向「个人对外服务型智能体」的平台级评测框架，从创建便捷性、服务匹配、交付质量、专长继承、服务连续性、学习改进、可靠治理与效率价值八个维度评估平台能力，并给出实验设计。

**排除原因：** 论文侧重智能体平台的商业可行性与服务交付评测，未涉及视觉或多模态内部表示、latent 推理状态生成与使用、THINK/LOOK/STOP 等决策机制，也未给出可供迁移的具体状态或训练方法线索，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DelegateEval: Evaluating Platforms for Personal External Service Agents

**abstract/A1：** AI agents that work for an individual over time may also help that individual serve external clients. We define a personal external service agent as an agent authorized and continuously managed by a specific individual to accept client requests, deliver work, and provide ongoing support using that person's knowledge, experience, professional methods, and service boundaries. Such agents could allow individuals to serve more clients with less direct involvement and generate economic value at scale. We propose DelegateEval, a platform-level evaluation framework for agent systems that may combine large language models, reusable skills, protocols such as MCP, and executable tool environments. It covers eight dimensions: ease of creation, service discovery and matching, delivery quality, expertise inheritance, service continuity, learning and improvement, reliability and governance, and efficiency and value. These dimensions assess whether a platform enables people with different backgrounds to turn their expertise into ongoing services that deliver verifiable work, develop capabilities through experience, and remain economically feasible. We present the framework and an experimental design. Complete datasets, executable environments, a unified scoring protocol, and cross-model results remain to be developed and empirically validated.

**comment/C1：** 28 pages, 7 figures, 6 tables. Evaluation framework and experimental design

</details>

### 260. [FlashOffloading: Low-Latency KV Cache Offloading for Long-Context LLM Serving](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07542v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07542

**作者：** Xuan Wang、Zhuanhao Wu、Tingzhu Fang、Xushi Li、Sicheng Zhang、Zhenyu Yang、Feng Liu、Zhaohui Fan、Qiang Xie、Huayi Zou、Yuncong Liu、Zeyu Song、Ke Zhu、Yanjia Zeng、Jingchun Gao、Yan Shi、Kui Luo、Gezi Li、Liangjun Feng、Cheng Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07542v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07542v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 FlashOffloading，一套把 KV cache 全量卸载到主机 DRAM 的推理架构，通过滚动 HBM 缓存、热缓存、反向稀疏索引与计算–加载重叠内核，在 TPOT 约束下提升长上下文服务的 prefill/decode 吞吐。

**排除原因：** 这是一项面向显存容量与传输延迟的系统层工程优化，其「cache」指硬件存储层级而非模型内部的 latent 推理状态，未涉及视觉证据、latent 内容的使用或推理停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少可迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FlashOffloading: Low-Latency KV Cache Offloading for Long-Context LLM Serving

**abstract/A1：** In large language model (LLM) serving systems, the key-value (KV) cache grows linearly with sequence length and concurrency, so long-context inference easily hits the high-bandwidth memory (HBM) capacity wall and results in low concurrency. Offloading the KV cache to host dynamic random-access memory (DRAM) frees HBM space and raises concurrency, but the additional cache management and host-to-device (H2D) load overhead inflates inference latency, higher concurrency fails to translate into effective throughput. We present FlashOffloading, a host-DRAM-centric inference architecture that fully offloads the KV cache in both the prefill and decode phases. FlashOffloading builds rolling HBM cache for prefill, a hot cache for decode, a reverse sparse index with zero extra miss-lookup overhead, and a compute--load overlapped kernel, optimizing KV offloading latency across the entire pipeline and delivering throughput gains under a time-per-output-token (TPOT) service-level objective (SLO). In experiments deploying GLM-5.2 with multi-token prediction (MTP3) speculative decoding on an Ascend910C prefill--decode (PD)-disaggregated platform, FlashOffloading improves prefill throughput by up to 1.38\\(\\times\\) and decode throughput by up to 1.91\\(\\times\\) under a 30 ms TPOT constraint. FlashOffloading is integrated into an open-source serving framework: https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm-ascend/tree/releases/v0.30.0.

**comment/C1：** 13 pages including references,13 figures

</details>

### 261. [OptIMerge: Optimization-Based Merging of Diffusion Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07543v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07543

**作者：** Viraj Shah、Ashwini Pokle、Peyman Milanfar、Mauricio Delbracio、Hossein Talebi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07543v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07543v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究图像到图像扩散模型（如复原任务专家）的权重合并问题，提出基于梯度的 OptIMerge，用少量图像学习参数高效的合并系数（注意力层低秩特征对齐、卷积层逐核系数与 Cayley 正交变换），以缓解朴素权重平均带来的参数干扰。

**排除原因：** 工作属于扩散模型生成端的任务权重合并，不涉及 VLM 推理中的内部 latent 状态、视觉证据筛选或 THINK/LOOK/STOP 决策，方法线索限于权重空间的专家融合，与 LVR 的表示与推理过程缺乏实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** OptIMerge: Optimization-Based Merging of Diffusion Models

**abstract/A1：** The arithmetic merging of weights from multiple task-specific models fine-tuned from a shared base model, commonly known as model souping, has emerged as a popular strategy for multi-task learning without the computational overhead of joint retraining. However, naive scalar weight averaging frequently leads to severe performance degradation when the constituent models learn structurally divergent feature representations, leading to destructive parameter interference. We study this problem in a largely unexplored setting: merging image-to-image (I2I) diffusion models fine-tuned for dense, pixel-accurate tasks such as image restoration. Unlike the large language models (LLMs) and text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models for which most merging methods were developed, I2I experts encode conflicting spatial priors (e.g., anisotropic motion-blur vs. isotropic defocus-blur) in their convolutional filters, where both naive soup and LLM-style element-wise merging introduce visible artifacts. We introduce OptIMerge, a gradient-based optimization framework: instead of relying on heuristic scalars, OptIMerge learns highly expressive, yet parameter-efficient merger coefficients by minimizing a multi-task diffusion loss over a small set of 100-500 images. OptIMerge parameterizes the merge with architecture-aware transformations: for attention layers, a structured low-rank feature alignment with explicit channel biases; for convolutional layers, per-kernel merger coefficients, and a low-rank Cayley transform that aligns channels by orthogonal rotation without distorting the filters. By keeping the experts frozen and optimizing only these lightweight merger parameters (equivalent to &lt;0.4% of the network footprint), OptIMerge achieves simultaneous multi-task capabilities that rival computationally expensive joint training and decisively outperform zero-shot model soups.

</details>

<a id="paper-262"></a>

### 262. [Training-Free Predictive Spatio-Temporal Token Pruning for Few-Step Autoregressive Video Diffusion](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07551v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07551

**作者：** Jia Li、Xurui Peng、Xing Wang、Hayden Kwok-Hay So

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07551v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07551v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PreSTP，一种无需训练的视频扩散加速框架：利用去噪轨迹早期的干净估计作为在线信号，识别跨帧静态内容与跨步提前收敛这两类 token 冗余，生成统一冗余掩码并跳过后续计算。

**排除原因：** 该工作属视频扩散生成的推理加速（token 剪枝与缓存），冗余来自帧间相似与去噪步收敛，与 LVR 关注的推理状态表示、证据获取、停止决策均无实质关联，其效率机制绑定于扩散去噪轨迹，难以作为 latent 推理方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Training-Free Predictive Spatio-Temporal Token Pruning for Few-Step Autoregressive Video Diffusion

**abstract/A1：** Few-step autoregressive (AR) video diffusion models generate videos chunk by chunk and have made efficient video generation practical. However, further accelerating these models toward real-time generation remains challenging, as few-step distillation leaves little cross-step redundancy for existing training-free caching methods to exploit. We revisit this regime and identify two token-level redundancy patterns: cross-frame redundancy from static content shared across adjacent frames, and cross-step redundancy from tokens that converge early during denoising. Crucially, we find that early clean estimates along the model's own denoising trajectory provide an effective online signal for locating both patterns, requiring no retraining, auxiliary predictor, or reference video. Based on these findings, we propose PreSTP (Predictive Spatio-Temporal Token Pruning), a training-free framework that constructs a unified token-wise redundancy mask from cross-frame similarity and cross-step convergence. Tokens marked as redundant skip subsequent computation under a consistent last-computed-value restoration rule, and a content-adaptive mask reuse strategy further reduces dense probing cost. On 5-second generation, PreSTP achieves 1.52x-3.20x speedups and a better efficiency-fidelity trade-off than coarse-grained caching baselines such as TeaCache and FlowCache. On 30-second generation, it reaches up to 1.96x speedup while improving VBench-Long and suppressing long-horizon drift. Notably, it continues to reduce inference cost even in the extreme one-step regime, where cross-step redundancy vanishes entirely and coarse step-wise caching is no longer applicable.

**comment/C1：** 19 pages, 10 figures, 11 tables, including appendices

</details>

**排除原因：** 该工作是扩散数据增广与检测精度提升，未涉及内部推理状态、视觉证据筛选或 latent 生成与使用的机制，与 LVR 主线缺乏具体可借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Improving Long-Tail Detection via Layout-Instruction Conditioned Diffusion Synthesis

**abstract/A1：** Synthetic data from diffusion models can improve object detection, but long-tail performance is still limited by scarce rare classes and inflexible conditioning (e.g., text-only or layout-only inputs). In this paper, we propose Tail-SD, a layout-instruction conditioned stable diffusion framework that supports text, layouts, or their hybrid, enabling controllable synthesis from boxes, masks, and/or prompts to better cover the long-tail objects. To train a single model across heterogeneous conditions, we introduce a multi-task cross-attention module and multi-task embedding tokens, improving generation quality and diversity. Extensive experiments on COCO and LVIS demonstrate that diffusion synthesis with hybrid conditioning consistently benefits detection, e.g., on LVIS val, it achieves +4.89% / +5.68% rare Box/Mask AP, highlighting strong potential for addressing long-tail challenges.

### 264. [Provable Zeroth-Order Safe Exploration for Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07561v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07561

**作者：** Weiqin Chen、Xinmeng Huang、Dongsheng Ding、Santiago Paternain

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07561v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07561v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究 Safe-RLHF 的训练期安全探索与 log-barrier 策略梯度理论，关注无害性约束下的收敛与样本复杂度，不涉及视觉或多模态状态表示、latent 推理生成、证据使用与停止决策，也无可核查的迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Provable Zeroth-Order Safe Exploration for Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback

**abstract/A1：** Safe reinforcement learning from human feedback (Safe-RLHF) seeks to improve helpfulness while satisfying harmlessness constraints. Existing primal-dual approaches can converge to feasible policies but do not prevent constraint violations during training. We study a stronger requirement, safe exploration for RLHF, under which every policy executed during training, including auxiliary exploration policies, remains feasible. We propose Zeroth-Order Log-Barrier Policy Gradient (ZO-LBPG), a reward-model-free method that optimizes a log-barrier objective using zeroth-order estimates derived directly from human preferences. To ensure safety under noisy feedback, we combine conservative constraint margin estimates with coupled rollouts and an adaptive step size that prevents updates from crossing constraint boundaries. We prove that, with high probability, all iterates and exploration policies remain strictly feasible, and that ZO-LBPG globally converges to an approximately optimal constrained policy. We establish a sample complexity of $\\widetilde{\\mathcal O}(\\varepsilon^{-6}+d^3\\varepsilon^{-5})$ trajectories and $\\widetilde{\\mathcal O}(\\varepsilon^{-8}+d^5\\varepsilon^{-7})$ human queries. Experiments on a synthetic CMDP and a language-model alignment task corroborate the training-time safety guarantees.

</details>

<a id="paper-265"></a>

### 265. [Guidance Is a Field, Not a Scalar: Interval-Integrated Classifier-Free Guidance for One-Step Video Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07566v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07566

**作者：** Zijie Meng、Xixin Cao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07566v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07566v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究一步式视频生成中 CFG 引导强度随噪声、空间与帧位置变化的场式建模，并将其积分进 MeanFlow 目标以省去多步采样；其核心是生成质量与采样加速，不涉及内部推理状态的保留、证据获取或推理停止决策，与 LVR/VLM 推理主线缺少可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Guidance Is a Field, Not a Scalar: Interval-Integrated Classifier-Free Guidance for One-Step Video Generation

**abstract/A1：** One-step generators built on MeanFlow synthesize a video in a single function evaluation, yet inherit a rigid treatment of classifier-free guidance (CFG): the guidance scale is frozen during training or, at best, exposed as a single scalar. This conflicts with mounting evidence that the optimal guidance is not a constant but a field that varies with the noise level, with spatial content, and, for video, with frame position. Every existing remedy presupposes a multi-step sampler along which guidance can be re-scheduled--an assumption one-step models violate by construction. We resolve this tension through a single observation: because the MeanFlow target is an integral over a noise interval, an arbitrary guidance field can be absorbed into the target itself and compiled into one evaluation. We formalize this as the interval-integrated CFG identity, prove that the compiled guidance carries no schedule-discretization error and can thus match or exceed any finite multi-step sampler, and instantiate it as AGAVE, which equips the average-velocity field with a learnable temporal schedule, a free motion-saliency spatial modulation, and a frame-position asymmetry. Adding fewer than ten scalars at zero inference cost, AGAVE surpasses all one-step baselines on VBench, UCF-101 and MSR-VTT while matching multi-step guided sampling. Project repository: https://github.com/YMlinfeng/AGAVE.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 9 figures. Accepted to WACV 2027

</details>

### 266. [ACSeL: Adaptive Configuration Search for Hardware-Constrained LLM Inference](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07570v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07570

**作者：** Bokun Wang、Hung-Yueh Chiang、Diana Marculescu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07570v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07570v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 ACSeL，在内存与延迟约束下联合搜索模型规模、批大小、量化算法与逐层混合精度配置，并用基于 next-token 分布散度的质量代理 RMQD 结合少样本延迟校准与 BandPareto 搜索来筛选可行配置。

**排除原因：** 属部署系统层面的配置搜索与量化，不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据使用或停止决策，质量代理也仅用于约束下的工程选型，缺少可核查的 LVR 方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ACSeL: Adaptive Configuration Search for Hardware-Constrained LLM Inference

**abstract/A1：** Serving large language models (LLMs) under tight memory and latency service-level objectives (SLOs), including on edge devices, requires jointly selecting the model size, batch size, quantization algorithm, and mixed-precision quantization recipes. Exhaustive search over this combinatorially large space is infeasible. Unlike data-center serving, which often prioritizes throughput or generation speed, resource-constrained deployments typically set memory and latency budgets as constraints and then seek the configuration with the highest generation quality among feasible ones. To this end, we propose ACSeL, a framework that identifies configurations predicted to satisfy deployment constraints while achieving high predicted generation quality. To enable efficient quality estimation, we use Relative Model Quality Degradation (RMQD), a proxy based on the divergence between the candidate and reference models' next-token distributions over a small calibration corpus. Across the evaluated model sizes and precisions, RMQD is inexpensive to evaluate and strongly correlated with downstream benchmark performance. ACSeL combines (i) a lightweight quality surrogate that predicts RMQD directly from a configuration; (ii) Few-shot Median Calibration (FMC), which calibrates operator-profile-based latency estimates using a small number of end-to-end profiles; and (iii) a structured search algorithm BandPareto for exploring layerwise mixed-precision configurations. Experiments on an NVIDIA RTX A6000 workstation and an NVIDIA Jetson Thor edge platform show that FMC substantially improves deployment feasibility, while BandPareto + FMC achieves the highest feasibility and the lowest RMQD gaps to the best measured configurations in the evaluation set.

</details>

### 267. [TRACEMARK: Editor-Aware Watermarking for User Attribution in Text-Guided Image Editing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07632v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07632

**作者：** Xiaodong Wu、Xiangman Li、Jianbing Ni、Rongxing Lu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07632v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07632v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 Tracemark，一种面向文本引导图像编辑的用户归因水印框架：在编辑前嵌入用户特定信号，并通过与固定编辑器联合训练编码器和检测器，使水印能在语义编辑后仍被恢复。

**排除原因：** 研究目标是图像水印的鲁棒性与用户归因，虽涉及编辑过程，但未触及视觉推理中的内部表示、latent 状态生成或证据使用等机制，缺少可供核查的联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TRACEMARK: Editor-Aware Watermarking for User Attribution in Text-Guided Image Editing

**abstract/A1：** An image editing service records which user requests each edit, but the edited image itself does not contain this account information. Once the image is downloaded and shared, the service cannot identify the requesting user from the image alone. Embedding a user-specific watermark could enable such attribution, but semantic editing may erase the signal. We propose Tracemark, an editor-aware watermarking framework for identifying users from edited images. A shared encoder embeds a user-specific signal before editing, and a shared detector recovers the user by comparing features of the edited image with learned reference vectors. Both components are trained jointly through an editor with fixed parameters. User identification errors encourage the encoder to produce watermarks that survive editing, while fidelity losses limit changes to the input image and intended edit. Each image domain and editing instruction uses a separately trained model shared across registered users. Experiments on three image domains show that editor-aware training improves user recovery with both reference-vector matching and binary watermark decoding. Separately trained models reach 87.5% mean identification accuracy among 500 users. When tested with a different editor without retraining, the watermark modules still recover user information. These results demonstrate that learning watermarks through the editing process enables user attribution without modifying the editor.

</details>

<a id="paper-268"></a>

### 268. [Redacting Personal Data: A Speed and Accuracy Comparison of Regex, Presidio and Hybrid Designs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07633v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07633

**作者：** Sai Yashwant、Manoj Kumar Gondimalla、Arjun Sharma、Shubham

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07633v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07633v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在简历转结构化资料的场景下，比较 regex、Presidio 及两种混合方案在 PII 脱敏上的召回率与延迟。

**排除原因：** 属文本预处理与合规工程评测，不涉及视觉细节保留、latent 推理状态或证据使用，缺少与 LVR 主线的实质联系或可核查的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Redacting Personal Data: A Speed and Accuracy Comparison of Regex, Presidio and Hybrid Designs

**abstract/A1：** Many enterprise projects now pass free text to large language models (LLMs), and that text often contains personally identifiable information (PII) such as names, phone numbers, email addresses, home addresses and dates of birth. Once a prompt is sent, this data is copied into provider systems, application logs and observability traces. Redacting PII before the call is an obvious control, yet teams seldom have numbers on what it costs in latency or what it lets through. This paper compares four redaction designs that any LLM pipeline can adopt: a pure regular-expression (regex) redactor, Microsoft Presidio running on a spaCy model, a hybrid in which Presidio finds only person names while regex handles structured identifiers, and a fast hybrid that calls spaCy's named-entity component directly. All four share a known-entity rule, which removes the person the system already knows it is handling, and a consistent placeholder scheme, so the LLM can still tell one person from another after redaction. We evaluate the designs on a sample workflow, which we call ProfileAssist, that turns resumes into structured profiles, using 100 synthetic resumes (523 characters on average, with 900 PII values and 600 business terms that must survive). Regex ran at a 0.20 ms median but caught only 88.9% of PII, because it cannot find names that are not known in advance, such as the references listed in a resume. Presidio, hybrid and fast hybrid each caught 100% of PII and kept 100% of business terms, at median latencies of 37.59 ms, 30.23 ms and 13.48 ms. Measured against a 9,120 ms median LLM call, even the slowest option adds only 0.41% to the request. We therefore recommend the fast hybrid as a default for LLM pipelines that handle personal data, with regex kept as a safety net rather than as the only control.

</details>

<a id="paper-269"></a>

### 269. [UniGADD: Universal Generator-Agnostic Deepfake Detector](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07647v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07647

**作者：** Bernard Tomczyk、Agil Aghasanli、Plamen Angelov

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07647v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07647v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 UniGADD，一种通过两阶段优化提升对未知深度伪造生成器泛化能力的检测方法，仅用真实样本做嵌入空间紧致与分离来增强鲁棒性。

**排除原因：** 属于图像取证与伪造检测的二分类泛化工作，不涉及 VLM 推理状态生成、视觉证据使用或停止决策，其对比学习与嵌入几何也缺少可迁移到 LVR 的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** UniGADD: Universal Generator-Agnostic Deepfake Detector

**abstract/A1：** In this paper, we introduce Universal Generator-Agnostic Deepfake Detector (UniGADD), a novel method that employs supervised learning to achieve high accuracy on known deepfake generators, while maintaining robust performance on previously unseen ones. The proposed approach follows a two-stage optimisation process. In the first stage, a contrastive loss encourages the model to learn discriminative feature embeddings from real and known fake images, resulting in strong performance within the training domain. In the second stage, the embedding space is refined by promoting inter-cluster separation and intra-cluster compactness, applied exclusively to real samples. This refinement enhances generalisability, enabling the method to exhibit improved robustness against unseen deepfake generation techniques. UniGADD achieves accuracy on par with state-of-the-art methods for known generators, while significantly outperforming them on unseen cases, demonstrating its scalability and practical applicability for adversarial content detection.

</details>

### 270. [Don't Prompt Your Agents. Equip Them: Skillware as a Modular Standard for Sovereign, Auditable, and Deterministic Capability Execution for Logical Systems](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07652v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07652

**作者：** Vladimiros Peilivanidis

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.SE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07652v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07652v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Skillware 框架，把 agent 的意图与具体执行分离，以版本化、带类型输入契约与单元测试的确定性 Python 能力包替代提示词驱动，并用 23 个模型 / 368 个条件评测其成本与可靠性收益。

**排除原因：** 属 agent 工具调用与工程化执行框架，不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态，也未触及 latent 表示、证据筛选、停止决策或监督分配，缺少与 LVR 主线的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Don't Prompt Your Agents. Equip Them: Skillware as a Modular Standard for Sovereign, Auditable, and Deterministic Capability Execution for Logical Systems

**abstract/A1：** Current autonomous logical systems rely on a fragile assumption: that language models should dynamically write code, guess API payloads, and memorize operational constraints through prompt engineering. In practice, this burns massive context windows, inflates API bills, and produces brittle agents that fail unpredictably in production. In this work, we present Skillware, an open architectural framework and capability registry that formally separates what an agent wants to do from how that task is executed. Instead of prompting models with loose text instructions or markdown files baptized as 'skills', Skillware equips them with modular, versioned software capability bundles. Much like skill hotkeys in video games that execute deterministically under fixed world rules, each bundle combines a typed JSON input contract, deterministic Python code, task directives, bundled datasets, and automated unit tests. In-process adapters translate these capabilities into native tool declarations for any model, from frontier cloud APIs to local open-weight runtimes with zero network latency. Evaluating 23 frontier and open-weight models across 368 benchmark conditions, we show that small local models (3B to 8B parameters) equipped with Skillware consistently outperform unequipped frontier models on complex multi-step workflows. They cut token usage by up to 84.8%, slash inference costs by up to 97.3%, and eliminate parameter errors entirely. True agent reliability does not come from larger models or longer prompts, but from equipping logical systems with verified, deterministic software. Finally, we articulate the trajectory of capability bundles across an invariant continuum of information processing systems: from digital logical systems today, to IoT, drones, and embodied robotics tomorrow, establishing a foundation for auditable, real-time capability execution.

**comment/C1：** 51 pages, 26 figures. Code repository and benchmarks available at https://github.com/arpahls/skillware

</details>

### 271. [ArtCrafter: Feed-Forward Generation of Articulated 3D Object with Analytic Joint Derivation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07658v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07658

**作者：** Minh Canh Tu、Quang-Binh Nguyen、Khoi Nguyen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07658v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07658v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 ArtCrafter，一个前馈生成模型，从「静止态」与「打开态」两张图像生成部件级网格，并用解析方式从成对部件的刚体变换推导 URDF 关节参数。

**排除原因：** 工作属于铰接 3D 资产的几何与运动学生成，其 slot 绑定与跨状态一致性注意力仅服务于部件网格生成和关节推导，未涉及视觉推理中的 latent 状态生成、使用或停止决策，缺乏可核查的实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ArtCrafter: Feed-Forward Generation of Articulated 3D Object with Analytic Joint Derivation

**abstract/A1：** Articulated 3D assets are essential for robotic simulation and training, yet their generation from casual images poses a key challenge: recovering part geometry and kinematics that remain mutually consistent. We present ArtCrafter, a novel feed-forward generative model for controllable generation of articulated 3D objects from a pair of images, one depicting the object in the rest state and the other in the open state. At the core of ArtCrafter is a DiT-based generative model that jointly produces part-level meshes for both articulation states in a single forward pass. Inspired by Slot Attention, its denoising process operates on a $1+2N$ slot layout comprising one shared base slot and $N$ paired rest/open slots, encouraging each pair to bind to the same kinematic part. To ensure geometrically consistent and disjoint part generation, we introduce Triplet Attention to enforce cross-state geometric consistency between paired rest/open slots anchored through the shared base slot, and a repulsion loss $\\mathcal{L}\_{\\mathrm{rep}}$ on decoded part point clouds to prevent inter-part penetration throughout denoising. Given the resulting paired meshes, URDF joint parameters---type, axis, origin, and range---are derived analytically from the rigid transformation between each rest/open pair, providing an interpretable alternative to learned joint-parameter regression. Extensive experiments on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution datasets demonstrate that ArtCrafter outperforms existing method under the same dual-state setting in geometry quality and articulation precision, while remaining competitive with methods operating under different input settings and paradigms.

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07682v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07682v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文面向模拟集成电路版图图像中的器件检测，提出利用同一支持图像构造前景与背景双证据查询，用于少样本检测中的查询选择与区域验证。

**排除原因：** 属于特定领域（版图器件）的少样本目标检测应用，虽使用「证据」「验证」等措辞，但不涉及多模态推理状态、内部表示或证据获取与停止决策，缺少可迁移至 LVR 的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DEQ-Det: Dual Evidence Queries for Few-Shot Analog IC Device Detection

**abstract/A1：** Detecting analog integrated circuit (IC) devices in layout images supports circuit analysis and inspection. With only a few annotated examples, variation in device geometry and visual similarity to dummy or routing structures make reliable detection difficult. Foreground similarity alone can highlight plausible targets without distinguishing them from competing layout patterns. We propose Dual Evidence Queries for Detection (DEQ-Det), which constructs complementary foreground and background references from the same support images without additional annotations. Foreground references capture target morphology, while selected background references characterize surrounding layout patterns. Both sources guide query selection and region verification, combining target appearance with layout context. Across five held-out device categories and three random seeds, DEQ-Det achieves macro AP50 of 39.41 and 53.03 at 10 and 30 shots, exceeding the strongest listed baselines by 3.97 and 9.71 points, respectively. A matched 30-shot ablation further shows that RoI background evidence reduces pooled false positives at comparable recall, supporting its role in interference suppression.

</details>

### 273. [AffineGS: Unlocking Accurate Geometry in 3DGS via Spatially Varying Affine Rectification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07709v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07709

**作者：** Tze Ho Elden Tse、Jizong Peng、Richard Chen、Angela Yao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07709v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07709v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文指出现有单目深度先验的全局尺度-平移对齐假设存在区域相关的分段线性畸变缺陷，并提出空间变化仿射校正场及法向感知正则项，以提升 3DGS 的几何精度。

**排除原因：** 该研究属于 3D 重建与新视角合成中的几何优化问题，不涉及 VLM 推理状态生成、证据筛选、停止决策或 latent 表示使用，缺乏与 LVR 主线的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AffineGS: Unlocking Accurate Geometry in 3DGS via Spatially Varying Affine Rectification

**abstract/A1：** 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become the leading approach for photorealistic novel view synthesis, yet its geometric accuracy lags behind its visual quality. Existing depth-guided methods attempt to resolve this by regularizing the optimization with monocular depth priors, typically using a single global scale-and-shift alignment. Crucially, we discover a fundamental flaw in this assumption: modern monocular depth networks systematically exhibit region-dependent, piecewise-linear distortions. This discovery invalidates standard global alignment strategies, which warp scene geometry by enforcing a single transform. To address this, we introduce a spatially varying affine rectification model that corrects monocular disparity into consistent metric depth using regionally smooth, edge-aware fields. We further derive a normal-aware regularizer that uses spatial gradients to couple the rectified disparity with 3DGS depth and normals. Across diverse experimental settings, our sensor-free method shows strong photometric quality of Gaussian Splatting while substantially improving geometric accuracy and outperforming state-of-the art geometry-focused methods.

**comment/C1：** Accpeted to NeurIPS 2026. Project page: https://eldentse.github.io/AffineGS/

</details>

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究成人 ECG 基础模型向儿童 ECG 的迁移，比较直接应用、儿童数据微调与从零训练，并评估下游分类、年龄回归及临床医生使用效果。

**排除原因：** 属于医学信号领域的模型微调与临床评估，缺少与多模态推理中的视觉状态、证据获取或停止决策相关的具体线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Pediatric ECG foundation model via adult-to-pediatric fine-tuning: downstream tasks and clinical evaluation

**abstract/A1：** Pediatric electrocardiogram (ECG) interpretation is complicated by age-dependent electrophysiological changes that limit direct transfer of adult AI models. We asked whether an adult ECG foundation model could be adapted to pediatric ECGs. Using 339,563 12-lead ECGs from 238,189 children, we compared direct application, pediatric fine-tuning, and training from scratch under an identical architecture. Direct transfer was poor (mean AUC 0.8256). Fine-tuning reached 0.9695, above de novo training (0.9661), and attained 0.9079 using only 1% of training data. On 10,999 external ECGs, fine-tuning achieved a mean AUC of 0.9385 across 30 labels. It generalized to age regression (MAE 1.12 years) and congenital heart disease classification (AUC 0.9562). Across 17 clinicians and 100 cases, diagnostic accuracy rose from 56.8% to 65.4% (p &lt; 0.001) and did not decline when AI erred. Adult ECG foundation models require pediatric adaptation; fine-tuning offers a data-efficient route to scalable pediatric ECG AI.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages,3 figures

</details>

### 275. [EnergyBench: a live, gate-aligned benchmark of day-ahead forecasts for European power markets](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07784v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07784

**作者：** Evgenii Genov、Ander Martinez Alonso、Christoph Bergmeir

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、eess.SY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07784v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07784v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该工作是欧洲电力市场日前预测的实时基准（价格、风光出力与负荷），涉及时间序列基础模型与数值天气预报协变量，与视觉或多模态推理的内部表示、潜在状态生成、证据筛选和停止决策无实质关联，也未提供可核查的方法迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EnergyBench: a live, gate-aligned benchmark of day-ahead forecasts for European power markets

**abstract/A1：** Energy forecasting models are usually compared on backtests and fixed public datasets. Backtests can use data that did not yet exist when the forecast would have been made, and a fixed test set, reused across studies and possibly contained in the pretraining data of foundation models, invites overfitting to the benchmark. EnergyBench evaluates every entry live instead. Each day at 11:30 CET/CEST, thirty minutes before the day-ahead (SDAC) gate closes, every model forecasts the next day's price, wind and solar generation and load on the 15-minute grid for six bidding zones (BE, NL, FR, DE-LU, DK1, DK2). Unlike existing live benchmarks, EnergyBench runs the models itself, on one information set that it freezes at the cut-off and publishes, including three numerical weather prediction models, so that foundation models can be compared with and without the same weather covariates under the actual market deadline. Errors are pooled per series against a seasonal-naive baseline, and models are ranked by a Bradley-Terry rating on the Elo scale with a day-bootstrap interval. The field spans baselines, linear and gradient-boosted models on the frozen weather, eighteen time-series foundation models from eleven families, ensembles, and the grid operators' own forecasts. Over the first thirty-two live days, the covariates reduced the error of each of the ten foundation models run with and without them by 37-51% on matched races (64-73% on wind, 48-61% on solar). Used zero-shot, these models match or outperform a LightGBM model trained on the same covariates. Model size, from 3M to 331M parameters, has a much smaller effect, and median ensembles of the covariate models rank highest. The grid operators' pre-gate forecasts remain ahead on wind and solar, where the best single foundation models have 16-22% more error; on load the gap is 4-6%, within the intervals.

**comment/C1：** 21 pages, 10 figures, 7 tables. Live benchmark at https://energybench.foretec.co

</details>

### 276. [Rain Beyond Streaks: Cascaded Realistic Rain and Wet Surface Synthesis via Scene Priors](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07793v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07793

**作者：** Melissa Tijink、Kirill Tolstobrov、Shunxin Wang、Estefania Talavera、Duc V. Le、Luuk Spreeuwers、Shaodi You、Nicola Strisciuglio

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07793v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07793v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CascadeRS，利用深度与分割先验合成包含雨丝、反射与积水的城市雨天场景，并构建 Cityscapes-CascadeRS 数据集用于评估下游视觉鲁棒性。

**排除原因：** 属于雨天图像合成与鲁棒性数据增强，未涉及 VLM 内部推理状态、证据筛选或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺乏可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Rain Beyond Streaks: Cascaded Realistic Rain and Wet Surface Synthesis via Scene Priors

**abstract/A1：** Realistic rain synthesis is a persistent challenge for vision systems operating in adverse weather. Existing methods primarily focus on generating rain streaks, often neglecting consequent surface effects such as reflections and puddles, which are critical for perceptual realism and robustness of downstream vision tasks. We introduce CascadeRS, a rain synthesis framework that leverages depth and segmentation priors to render scene-consistent rain and surface effects. In addition to rain streaks, CascadeRS produces reflections and puddles aligned with scene semantics through a physics-inspired zero-shot, controllable generative pipeline. It produces photorealistic rainy urban scenes, with high perceptual realism, which we validate qualitatively with an extensive user study and quantitatively. We produce the Cityscapes-CascadeRS dataset, to study downstream task performance under real rainy conditions. Our results establish CascadeRS as a scalable and realistic rain-generation framework for evaluating and enhancing the robustness of vision in adverse rainy weather. Code and dataset are available via https://github.com/nis-research/CascadeRainSynthesis

**comment/C1：** WACV-2027

</details>

### 277. [Self-Supervised Diffusion Models for Handwritten Signature Verification via Ruin Reconstruction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07810v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07810

**作者：** Peirong Zhang、Zhenhua Yang、Jiaxin Zhang、Moises Diaz、Miguel A. Ferrer、Umapada Pal、Wei Zhou、Lianwen Jin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07810v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07810v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SigDiffuser，用扩散模型做自监督笔迹签名表示学习：通过「Reconstruction from Ruins」预任务严重揉毁签名，让视觉编码器从完好区域引导降噪器重建被毁部分，并加金字塔特征聚合模块（PFAM）。

**排除原因：** 属于生物特征认证中的自监督表示学习，扩散过程用于图像重建而非推理状态生成或更新，也没有证据筛选、停止决策或潜在推理机制的具体线索，与 LVR 主线缺乏实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Self-Supervised Diffusion Models for Handwritten Signature Verification via Ruin Reconstruction

**abstract/A1：** Handwritten signature verification (HSV) is a critical biometric technique in legal and forensic authentication, whose efficacy hinges on the discriminability of signature representations. Current HSV methods predominantly adopt supervised learning to enhance representation quality, leaving self-supervised learning largely unexplored. We propose \\emph{SigDiffuser}, a diffusion-based self-supervised framework for signature representation and verification. At its core, \\emph{SigDiffuser} introduces a novel pretext task termed \\emph{Reconstruction from Ruins (RFR)}, which deliberately \`\`ruins'' signatures through severe crumpling, obliterating their structural integrity and writing styles. A visual encoder is employed to capture crucial handwriting patterns from intact signature regions, guiding the denoiser to reconstruct the \`\`ruined'' portions and learning meaningful representations. Additionally, we introduce a \\emph{pyramidal feature aggregation module (PFAM)} to bridge the visual encoder and denoiser, enhancing representation quality via hierarchical aggregation and modulation. Extensive experiments on multilingual signature datasets demonstrate that \\emph{SigDiffuser} significantly outperforms both state-of-the-art SSL methods and HSV models. We further show that increasing the crumpling severity on signatures effectively improves representation quality. Notably, our work demonstrates that extreme destruction, coupled with self-supervised diffusion models, can yield superior signature representation compared to traditional mild deformations, paving new avenues for HSV and other biometric domains. Code will be publicly available.

</details>

### 278. [MV-Sign: Multi-View 3D Motion Integration and Enhanced Text-to-Motion Conditioning for Sign Language Production](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07811v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07811

**作者：** Singyu Muk、Kaustubh Kundu、Kunyuan Xie、Weijie Wang、Kalin Stefanov

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07811v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07811v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作面向手语动作生成，构建多视角 3D 动作数据集并用 ST-VQVAE、伪 gloss 适配与关键帧检索改进文本到动作的对齐。

**排除原因：** 其目标是生成手语人体动作序列，虽涉及多视角融合与离散 token 表示，但不涉及 VLM 内部推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，与 LVR 主线无实质联系且缺具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MV-Sign: Multi-View 3D Motion Integration and Enhanced Text-to-Motion Conditioning for Sign Language Production

**abstract/A1：** Sign language production (SLP) aims to generate appropriate sign language motion from spoken language text. However, current 3D SLP methods suffer from noisy supervision and weak text conditioning. Specifically, these methods typically rely on pseudo-3D motion data fitted from single-view frontal videos, which introduces severe self-occlusion and loses fine-grained hand details, ultimately degrading downstream production. To overcome these limitations, we propose a comprehensive framework that jointly integrates multi-view 3D motion data and optimizes the text-conditioning pathway. First, we introduce How2Sign-MV3D, a high-fidelity dataset constructed via a novel cross-view curation pipeline that fuses multi-view estimates to resolve occlusions and filter non-signing segments. Second, we design a robust SLP model incorporating a sign-aware spatio-temporal VQ-VAE (ST-VQVAE) for joint body-hand tokenization, a pseudo-gloss adapter, and keyframe-aware retrieval to enhance text-to-motion alignment. Evaluated on the How2Sign benchmark, our framework reduces body and hand DTW-PA-JPE by 32.8% and 20.9% respectively compared to the state-of-the-art. Finally, we release two new high-quality 3D datasets, ASLCitizen-3D and MM-WAuslan-MV3D, alongside SGNify-MV as a multi-view video benchmark to support broader community research.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the IEEE/CVF Winter Conference on Applications of Computer Vision (WACV 2027)

</details>

### 279. [Enhanced MRI brain tumor detection using deep learning in conjunction with explainable AI SHAP based diverse and multi feature analysis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07843v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07843

**作者：** Asif Rahman、Maqsood Hayat、Nadeem Iqbal、Fawaz Khaled Alarfaj、Salem Alkhalaf、Fahad Alturise

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07843v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07843v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文比较 LBP、Gabor、小波、FFT、CNN 等多种特征表示与传统分类器组合在 MRI 脑肿瘤识别上的表现，并用 SHAP 与统计检验分析特征贡献。

**排除原因：** 属于医学图像特征工程加分类器刷分的应用研究，没有 latent 推理状态、视觉证据筛选或多步决策机制，仅有的特征重要性分析也不涉及内部状态如何使用，缺少可迁移到 LVR 的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Enhanced MRI brain tumor detection using deep learning in conjunction with explainable AI SHAP based diverse and multi feature analysis

**abstract/A1：** Recent innovations in medical imaging have markedly improved brain tumor identification, surpassing conventional diagnostic approaches that suffer from low resolution, radiation exposure, and limited contrast. Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) is pivotal in precise and accurate tumor characterization owing to its high-resolution, non-invasive nature. This study investigates the synergy among multiple feature representation schemes such as local Binary Patterns (LBP), Gabor filters, Discrete Wavelet Transform, Fast Fourier Transform, Convolutional Neural Networks (CNN), and Gray-Level Run Length Matrix alongside five learning algorithms namely: k-nearest Neighbor, Random Forest, Support Vector Classifier (SVC), and probabilistic neural network (PNN), and CNN. Empirical findings indicate that LBP in conjunction with SVC and CNN obtained high specificity and accuracy, rendering it a promising method for MRI-based tumor diagnosis. Further to investigate the contribution of LBP, Statistical analysis chi-square and p-value tests are used to confirm the significant impact of LBP feature space for identification of brain Tumor. In addition, The SHAP analysis was used to identify the most important features in classification. In a small dataset, CNN obtained 97.8% accuracy while SVC yielded 98.06% accuracy. In subsequent analysis, a large benchmark dataset is also utilized to evaluate the performance of learning algorithms in order to investigate the generalization power of the proposed model. CNN achieves the highest accuracy of 98.9%, followed by SVC at 96.7%. These results highlight CNN's effectiveness in automated, high-precision tumor diagnosis. Code: https://github.com/asifrahman557/BrainTumorDetection

**comment/C1：** 15 pages. Published in Scientific Reports 15, 29411 (2025). Open access under CC BY 4.0

### 280. [Responsible GenAI Use During Active Armed Conflict: Evidence from Sudan's Emergency Response Rooms](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07874v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07874

**作者：** Dalia Ali、Yearin Lee、Cheng Yu、Ro Encarnación、Alsanosi Adam、Danaé Metaxa、Ingmar Weber、Orestis Papakyriakopoulos

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.HC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07874v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07874v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该研究通过问卷与访谈考察苏丹冲突地区应急响应网络志愿者使用生成式 AI 的情况，讨论可及性、核验困难与安全风险等负责任使用条件。

**排除原因：** 属人道主义场景下 GenAI 负责任使用研究，不涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部状态、证据获取与决策机制，缺少可借鉴的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Responsible GenAI Use During Active Armed Conflict: Evidence from Sudan's Emergency Response Rooms

**abstract/A1：** Armed conflict can increase the value of Generative AI (GenAI) while undermining the conditions required for its responsible use. Yet the perspectives of community-based humanitarian responders working under these conditions remain underrepresented in responsible AI research. We examine GenAI use in Sudan's Emergency Response Rooms (ERRs), a community-led humanitarian network, through an exploratory survey of 89 respondents and 15 semi-structured interviews with GenAI users and non-users. War reconfigures GenAI's role, making it most useful where its outputs are hardest to verify. ERR responders turned to GenAI when conflict made human expertise difficult or dangerous to reach. However, participants cautioned that GenAI could not independently establish rapidly changing local conditions, and that relying on its outputs could lead to harmful decisions when human verification was difficult. Risks arose before GenAI was used. For some volunteers, carrying a phone or accessing the internet could expose them to danger. Others were concerned that sharing sensitive beneficiary or operational information with GenAI could put people at risk. Participants feared that AI-assisted reports could undermine their credibility with donors, whose funding sustains the food and medical aid ERRs provide. Access to GenAI was uneven, and volunteers using it sometimes relied on colleagues with relevant expertise or local knowledge to verify its results. Our findings show that responsible use of GenAI during conflict depends not only on model performance or individual adoption, but also on whether responders can safely access these tools, verify their outputs against local conditions, and recognize when limiting or avoiding GenAI is the more responsible choice.

</details>

### 281. [XM4D: 4D Reconstruction through Certifiable Geometric Optimization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07902v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07902

**作者：** Haoyu Han、Jack Benarroch Jedlicki、Jiahui Lei、Heng Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.RO、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07902v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07902v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 XM4D，将相机运动与非刚性形变分开，两阶段都化为仅含旋转的 QCQP，用 SDP 松弛与可验证全局最优性证书，并以 GPU 加速的 Burer-Monteiro 求解，最后用动态 Gaussian splatting 做光度精修。

**排除原因：** 论文聚焦 4D 几何重建与全局最优优化求解，不涉及 VLM 的内部推理状态、证据获取或停止决策；虽含「可验证最优性」概念，但与 latent 推理状态的验证缺少具体可迁移线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** XM4D: 4D Reconstruction through Certifiable Geometric Optimization

**abstract/A1：** Accurate 4D reconstruction remains challenging: jointly estimating camera motion, scene geometry, and non-rigid deformation entails highly nonconvex optimization, making global optimality difficult to guarantee. We present XM4D, a framework for accurate and efficient 4D reconstruction built upon certifiably globally optimal geometric optimization. By separating the estimation of camera motion from non-rigid deformation, we formulate both stages as structured quadratically constrained quadratic programs (QCQP) that admit semidefinite programming (SDP) relaxations and verifiable global optimality certificates. For camera motion estimation, we lift static point tracks into 3D using foundational depth prediction models and formulate a scaled bundle adjustment problem that jointly estimates camera poses, depth scales, and static landmarks. By analytically eliminating translations and landmarks, we reduce the problem to a QCQP over scaled rotations. For non-rigid reconstruction, we represent dynamic motion using an embedded deformation graph (EDG) and show that its tracking, rigidity, and temporal regularization objectives similarly reduce to a rotation-only QCQP. Both problems admit SDP relaxations that we solve efficiently with a GPU-accelerated Burer-Monteiro solver based on Riemannian optimization. The recovered geometry further initializes dynamic Gaussian splatting for photometric refinement. Extensive experiments across seven benchmarks demonstrate accurate camera motion estimation on both static and dynamic scenes, efficient optimization that scales to thousands of frames, and state-of-the-art novel-view rendering quality.

</details>

<a id="paper-282"></a>

### 282. [Flux as a Codec: Zero-Shot Lossy Compression with Flux](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07929v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07929

**作者：** Fuma Kimishima、Jinjia Zhou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07929v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07929v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 FluxC，将预训练 Flux 扩散模型作为零样本有损编解码器，分析编码路径、重建方式（ancestral sampling 与 ODE）对率-失真-感知的影响。

**排除原因：** 该工作面向图像压缩的率失真与重建质量，不涉及 VLM 推理中的 latent 状态生成、证据使用或停止决策，缺乏可迁移的具体线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Flux as a Codec: Zero-Shot Lossy Compression with Flux

**abstract/A1：** We propose FluxC, a zero-shot codec that uses Flux as a compression prior. Although DiffC has shown that pretrained diffusion models can serve as zero-shot codecs, we ask how the choice of coding path and reconstruction method affects rate--distortion--perception behavior. We analyze FluxC along three axes: coding cost and bit allocation, reconstruction method, and low rate distortion. At matched signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), we show that the path choice does not affect the ideal information rate, but changes how bits are allocated across time steps. For reconstruction, we compare ancestral sampling, one-step ODE reconstruction, and many-step ODE reconstruction. Our analysis shows that ODE-based reconstruction can achieve lower distortion than ancestral sampling, especially in the low rate regime. Experiments on Gaussian sources and natural images support this prediction. Moreover, FluxC performs competitively with, and in some cases outperforms, recent neural image compression methods, suggesting that Flux can serve as a principled zero-shot codec.

</details>

### 283. [Towards Scalable Diagnostic-Driven Whole-Slide Image Retrieval for Pathology Archives](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07935v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.07935

**作者：** Jingsong Liu、Youssef Wally Han Li、Weiwei Zhou、Jing Dai、Elisabeth Wetzer、Peter J. Schüffler

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07935v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.07935v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CLEAR-WSI，用固定长度切片嵌入做病理全切片图像的诊断驱动检索，并通过诊断标签重排候选、增量扩充记忆库，在公开与临床数据上评估检索精度与延迟。

**排除原因：** 属于病理图像检索的工程应用，未涉及多模态推理状态、视觉证据筛选机制或 latent 轨迹的训练与使用，压缩嵌入与记忆库仅服务于检索排序，与 LVR 主线缺乏具体可核查的状态或决策联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Towards Scalable Diagnostic-Driven Whole-Slide Image Retrieval for Pathology Archives

**abstract/A1：** The growing adoption of digital pathology has rapidly expanded collections of hematoxylin-and-eosin whole-slide images, yet efficient navigation through these archives remains challenging, as metadata is often inconsistent or unavailable. Content-based image retrieval addresses this challenge by identifying similar cases directly from visual representations instead of manual annotations or metadata. Existing retrieval systems have demonstrated promising performance but largely rely on handcrafted indexing pipelines and predefined sampling strategies, constraining scalability and generalization. Here, we present CLEAR-WSI (Constant-Length Embedding for Automatic Retrieval of Whole-Slide Images) as a scalable, diagnostic-driven slide-retrieval framework, validated on both public and clinical data. Using a fixed-length slide embedding, CLEAR-WSI refines retrieved candidates with either a predicted diagnostic label or the ground-truth label, depending on the retrieval setting. Newly processed cases are subsequently added to the expanding memory bank, allowing the retrieval archive to grow over time. On a comprehensive benchmark consisting of two public and one private clinical datasets representing four retrieval tasks, CLEAR-WSI improves Precision@5 over the strongest evaluated baseline, with gains on CAMELYON16 (3.10), BRACS-1 (2.75), BRACS-2 (1.95), and X-HER2 (3.75). Additional ablation and latency studies further examine the contribution of each component and highlight its potential for scalable deployment.

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文构建了一个开源化学工程 agent 平台，让 LLM agent 调用催化、流程模拟、HAZOP、生命周期评估等工具，并在 Git 中版本化管理工程文件、为每个数值记录来源，用规则化检查报告不一致与过期结果，并以氨案例说明其与人工审查的分工。

**排除原因：** 内容是化学工程工作流的工具编排与工程文件管理，不涉及视觉细节表示、latent 推理状态或 VLM 训练与决策；其数值溯源与规则检查属工程合规校验，未见可供 LVR 核查的具体方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ChemE-as-Code: An Open-Source Platform for Agentic AI in Chemical Engineering

**abstract/A1：** ChemE-as-Code provides an open-source environment in which AI agents built on large language models (LLMs) can carry out chemical engineering work and engineers can check it. Its calculation tools range from catalysis through process simulation to plant layout, HAZOP and life cycle assessment, and skills tell a general-purpose agent how to use them. Engineering files are linked, machine-readable and versioned in Git; calculations run outside the LLM, each value records its origin, and automated checks report inconsistencies and out-of-date results by file and line. In an illustrative ammonia case, agents developed a written task into a preliminary basic-engineering package (simulation files, P&amp;IDs, layout, HAZOP, datasheets, relief) for USD 224 of API cost in the design sessions. Planted faults and engineering changes show that the checks find what their rules cover and miss the rest, so they guide human review rather than replace it. No independent engineer has reviewed the design.

**comment/C1：** 28 pages, 4 figures, 5 tables; Supporting Information (28 pages) appended. Submitted to AIChE Journal. Code and data: https://github.com/ChemE-as-Code

</details>

### 285. [When GeoFMs Meet VHR: Benchmarking Foundation Models on Very High Resolution Earth Observation Imagery](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08003v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08003

**作者：** Kshitij Raj Sharma、Pierre Adorni、Minh-Tan Pham、Dirk Tiede、Sébastien Lefèvre

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08003v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08003v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文构建 VHR-Bench（10 个 1.5m 以下 GSD 数据集、5 类任务），在冻结骨干、两种标签比例下比较 12 个编码器的三类预训练方案，并进一步在百万级 VHR 切片上继续自监督预训练 DINOv3 ViT-L，发现「先通用再特化」可带来增益。

**排除原因：** 研究焦点是遥感高分辨率场景下编码器的预训练迁移与下游精度，未涉及 latent 推理状态生成、证据筛选、动态计算或停止决策；高分辨率细节只体现在输入尺度与领域预训练层面，未分析内部表示如何使用，故缺少可核查的 LVR 借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When GeoFMs Meet VHR: Benchmarking Foundation Models on Very High Resolution Earth Observation Imagery

**abstract/A1：** Geospatial foundation models are a widely adopted starting point for remote sensing tasks. Most are pretrained on Sentinel-1/2 and Landsat imagery at a ground sampling distance (GSD) of 10m or coarser. Many applications instead rely on very high resolution (VHR) imagery, where individual buildings, roads, and amenities are resolved. We study which pretraining regimes transfer well to this scale. We introduce VHR-Bench, a benchmark suite of ten datasets at 1.5m GSD or finer spanning five task families, eight application domains, and both satellite and aerial platforms. We evaluate twelve encoders in three pretraining tiers with frozen backbones at two training-data fractions. We find that both pretraining on large natural-image collections and VHR-specific pretraining transfer effectively to VHR tasks, with complementary strengths. SkySense leads when labels are scarce; at the full label fraction the web-pretrained DINOv3 ViT-L edges ahead, with the two close together there. Encoders pretrained only on Sentinel-scale imagery transfer least well under RGB input. Guided by these complementary results, we continue the self-supervised pretraining of DINOv3 ViT-L on 1.02 million held-out VHR tiles and evaluate the resulting encoder without changing the downstream protocol. The continued model gives a clear improvement over the released backbone when labels are scarce and a modest one at the full fraction, at no increase in inference parameters or compute. These findings support a generalize-then-specialize strategy: already strong, broad natural-image pretraining can be further improved by a short VHR continuation stage while preserving its general visual features.

</details>

### 286. [GTAFL: Addressing Test-Agnostic Long-Tailed Federated Learning in Medical Image](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08021v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08021

**作者：** Guangyu Chen、Jingyun Zeng、Hui Jiang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08021v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08021v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究联邦学习下医疗图像的长尾分布与未知测试分布问题，属于分类器去偏与训练/推理分布偏移，未涉及 latent 推理状态、视觉证据筛选或停止决策；推理期的专家分类器集成也仅是输出层聚合，缺乏可迁移至 LVR 的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GTAFL: Addressing Test-Agnostic Long-Tailed Federated Learning in Medical Image

**abstract/A1：** In healthcare, protecting patient privacy is crucial due to the sensitivity of medical data and its extensive accessibility. Federated Learning (FL) offers a decentralized and privacy-preserving training paradigm, making it an ideal solution for healthcare applications. A critical challenge in healthcare is that real-world medical data often exhibits long-tailed distributions in local and global views. Existing methods addressing long-tailed FL problem typically assume that the model will be evaluated on uniform test data distribution. However, practical test data in healthcare systems is often agnostic and unpredictable, leading to potential model failures in real-world scenarios. In this paper, we introduce a novel task termed Test-Agnostic Long-Tailed Federated Learning and propose GTAFL, a comprehensive framework to address this challenge. During the training stage, GTAFL employs adaptive re-sampling, expert classifier retraining, and self-supervised learning to correct biased classifiers and distorted feature spaces caused by long-tailed training distributions. During the inference stage, an ensemble mechanism combines retrained expert classifiers to handle test data with unknown distributions. Extensive experiments on CIFAR10 and two medical datasets manifest that our framework outperforms other state-of-the-art methods. The code is available at https://github.com/cgy-lavine/GTAFL

**comment/C1：** Accepted for publication in IEEE Journal of Biomedical and Health Informatics (JBHI). 13 pages. Code: https://github.com/cgy-lavine/GTAFL

</details>
