**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08042v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08042v1>)

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 Secure Formal Raising，用保留「未知/不一致」的四值证据刻画 raising 环境中的策略完整性、授权与机密数据流，并证明六条安全不变量、给出 C 参考实现。

**排除原因：** 论述对象是 AI 开发环境的安全准入与工具调用溯源，未涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部状态、证据筛选与停止决策；四值证据仅服务于访问控制判定，与 LVR 的相关性过于间接，缺少可核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Secure Formal Raising: Protecting the Development of AI through Executable Properties

**abstract/A1：** Model raising revisits Turing's developmental view of machine intelligence. Formal Raising (FR) has recently been proposed to complement this view with explicit behavioral properties. This paper extends that proposal to the security of the raising environment. Secure Formal Raising (SFR) models policy integrity, authorization, and confidential data flow using four-valued evidence that preserves unknown and inconsistent assessments. We prove six security invariants and show that they force admission to accept only confirmed evidence. A plain-C reference implementation satisfies the invariants in a bounded exhaustive exploration, which also exposes a confused-deputy gap for tool calls that a provenance requirement closes.

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08081

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建面向地球观测的大规模开放词汇分割数据集 TerraMesh-Masks（1600 万个二值掩码及专家审核评测集），并据此训练开放词汇版 TerraMind，报告分割 IoU 提升。

**排除原因：** 属遥感分割数据集与基准刷分工作，未涉及 latent 推理状态、证据筛选或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TerraMesh-Masks: Open-Vocabulary Segmentation for Earth Observation

**abstract/A1：** Open-vocabulary segmentation (OVS) enables models to segment a variety of concepts specified in natural language, removing the need for fixed label sets and fine-tuning. While recent OVS approaches have shown strong performance on natural images, they transfer poorly to Earth observation (EO) data due to differing semantics, scales, sensing modality, and geographic context. To date, large-scale EO-specific datasets and benchmarks for OVS remain scarce. We, therefore, introduce TerraMesh-Masks, a large-scale open-vocabulary segmentation dataset for EO, comprising 16 million binary masks aligned with nine million multimodal TerraMesh samples and annotated using OpenStreetMap and Overture-derived semantics. In addition, we provide an expert-reviewed evaluation set of 523 samples covering over 200 classes to enable robust evaluation. To demonstrate the impact of TerraMesh-Masks, we build an open-vocabulary version of TerraMind. Our results demonstrate significantly improved performance over general-purpose state-of-the-art models reaching 35.18% IoU versus 16.29% for DINOv3.txt and 11.33% for SAM-3. We release the dataset publicly under a permissive license at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ibm-esa-geospatial/TerraMesh-Masks.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

### 289. [DictLLM: Post-training Compression of Large Language Model with Dictionary Kernels](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08082v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08082

**作者：** Jinho Park、Se Young Chun、Mingoo Seok

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08082v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08082v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 DictLLM，一种无需再训练的 LLM 权重压缩方案：把成组层的权重矩阵分解为共享字典与逐层稀疏系数，并用 Hessian 信息指导稀疏化、字典细化与量化。

**排除原因：** 属于纯部署侧权重压缩，未涉及视觉细节保留、推理状态生成或证据使用与停止决策，其字典共享、稀疏分解与 LVR 的表示或动态计算无可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DictLLM: Post-training Compression of Large Language Model with Dictionary Kernels

**abstract/A1：** Efficient deployment of large language models (LLMs) on mobile devices requires balancing performance with compute and memory constraints. Post-training compression is an effective approach to managing this trade-off, while requiring much less computation than in-training counterparts. We propose DictLLM, a dictionary-based compression scheme in which weight matrices within grouped layers are decomposed into a shared dictionary and layer-specific sparse coefficients, directly obtained from a pre-trained model without further training. Specifically, we cast DictLLM as a four-step post-training optimization. First, we obtain shared dictionaries via singular value decomposition (SVD) of input-scaled weight matrices, amortizing rank across grouped layers; second, we sparsify the layer-specific coefficients to the target compression ratio using a dictionary-aware Hessian; and third, we refine the dictionaries given the resulting sparse coefficients. Finally, we aggressively quantize both components using factor-specific Hessian information: input covariance for the dictionaries and the dictionary-aware Hessian for the coefficients. The resulting representation reduces memory footprint by storing a single shared dictionary per layer group, along with sparse layer-specific coefficients. On language modeling and zero-shot reasoning benchmarks, DictLLM consistently outperforms existing post-training low-rank compression methods and pushes the Pareto frontier between model size and performance. DictLLM compresses the original LLaMA-7B model by 5.1x with only a 2.9-point increase in WikiText-2 perplexity, achieving a 3.6x higher compression ratio than the state-of-the-art low-rank method.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026. Project page: https://jp4327.github.io/dictllm/

</details>

### 290. [MUSS: A Multi-scale and Sequence-based Model for Single-cell Gene Regulation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08086v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08086

**作者：** Kangjie Zheng、Amirhossein Vahidi、Liying Jin、Joseph Clarke、Vijaya Baskar MS、Connor Rogerson、Mengji Zhang、Muzlifah Haniffa、Anshul Kundaje、Mo Lotfollahi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、q-bio.GN

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08086v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08086v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 MUSS，用冻结的 DNA 序列编码器并结合 peak–gene 注意力，构建单细胞基因调控的多尺度表示，用于基因表达预测与调控元件分析。

**排除原因：** 研究对象为单细胞基因组调控与表达预测，不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，其多尺度表示与注意力也不针对 LVR 问题，缺乏可迁移的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MUSS: A Multi-scale and Sequence-based Model for Single-cell Gene Regulation

**abstract/A1：** Modeling gene regulation in single cells requires jointly representing DNA sequence, chromatin accessibility, and gene expression across regulatory scales. Sequence-based models capture DNA-level regulatory grammar but often lack cell-level resolution, whereas many single-cell omics models represent genes and peaks as fixed-vocabulary tokens without their underlying sequence. Here, we present MUSS, a sequence-based multi-scale model for single-cell gene regulation that preserves both DNA-level and cell-level resolution. MUSS derives peak- and gene-level representations from DNA using a frozen pre-trained sequence encoder and integrates them through peak-peak self-attention and distance-biased peak-gene interactions. Peak masked language modeling followed by training on paired scATAC-seq and scRNA-seq aligns these representations with cell-specific regulatory states. Across gene expression prediction, enhancer-gene linking, motif-guided regulatory prioritization, and zero-shot cell clustering, MUSS achieves strong performance against sequence-based and single-cell omics baselines. It further predicts expression for genes and peaks unseen during task training. These results support sequence-grounded, multi-scale representations for decoding cell-type-specific regulatory programs. Code, datasets, and model checkpoints are available at https://github.com/Lotfollahi-lab/MUSS\_neurips26.

**comment/C1：** 30 pages, 5 figures; includes references, appendices, and the NeurIPS checklist

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08091v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08091v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出中世纪阿拉伯语科学手稿的转写标注规范与人工标注数据集 ATHAAR，并用现有 VLM 测试跨书写风格、章节与论著的迁移表现。

**排除原因：** 工作核心是手写文本识别数据集与跨域迁移评测，不涉及内部推理状态、视觉证据筛选、动态计算或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ATHAAR: A Medieval Scientific Arabic Manuscript Dataset for Handwritten Text Recognition

**abstract/A1：** Despite recent progress in handwritten text recognition, medieval Arabic scientific manuscripts remain an open challenge for current vision-language models (VLMs) due to cursive ligatures, variable diacritization, and more importantly the lack of training data. This paper aims to address this gap, by proposing the first annotation guidelines dedicated to the transcription of scientific Arabic manuscripts inspired by textual criticism, a well-established approach to historical text editing. We then propose, ATHAAR, a new dataset manually annotated following this guideline. Unlike previous resources in the field, ATHAAR varies both witnesses of the same text, calligraphic styles, and content to deal with texts but also numerals. Relying on state-of-the-art VLMs, we finally design a set of experiments to study models transfer across scripts, chapters, and treatises. Our findings highlight the limitations of current systems when faced with the specificities of ancient scientific texts.

</details>

### 292. [National-scale mapping of tree species composition in Switzerland from high-resolution aerial imagery and multimodal data](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08095v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08095

**作者：** Yixin Zhou、Luca Ferrari、Verena C. Griess、Nataliia Rehush、Konrad Schindler、Mirela Beloiu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08095v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08095v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文构建深度学习模型，融合高分辨率航拍 RGB、ALS、卫星嵌入与气候变量，在瑞士全国 30 m 分辨率上估计十种主要树种的断面积占比。

**排除原因：** 属于遥感地物组分制图与生态应用，虽使用多模态视觉输入，但不涉及内部推理状态、latent 表示、证据获取或停止决策等 LVR 关注点，缺少可迁移的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** National-scale mapping of tree species composition in Switzerland from high-resolution aerial imagery and multimodal data

**abstract/A1：** Tree-species composition shapes forest productivity and how forests respond to climate disturbances such as drought, windthrow and insect outbreaks. Yet most national forest maps assign each pixel a single dominant species and cannot show the share of accompanying species. We mapped the basal-area shares of ten main tree species and a residual Other class across Switzerland at 30 m resolution. A deep-learning model combining 0.1 m aerial RGB imagery, airborne laser scanning (ALS), annual AlphaEarth satellite embeddings, and climate variables was trained on basal-area observations from 3,992 Swiss National Forest Inventory (NFI) sites. In spatial cross-validation, the model reached a macro-$R^2$ of 0.485 across the 11 classes. On an independent forest-reserve dataset, the map reached a macro-$R^2$ of 0.552 for individual 30 m pixels and 0.733 for plots, and identified the dominant class in 73.2% of pixels and 81.1% of plots. The map shows maple and ash as widespread minor components that dominant-class maps omit. It also reproduces the elevational sequence from broadleaved lowland stands through spruce to larch and Arolla pine near the treeline. By resolving the species mixture within each 30 m pixel, the map complements field inventory statistics with spatially continuous information that can support forest monitoring, the management of mixed stands, and climate-adaptation planning across Switzerland.

**comment/C1：** 39 pages, 17 figures (11 in the main text, 6 in the supplementary material). Submitted to Remote Sensing of Environment

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08116v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08116v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文报告一家小型软件公司自建 AI 推理栈的部署经验：本地模型无法胜任复杂编码任务，于是采用商业模型负责复杂任务、本地模型负责协调类工作的混合分工，并记录了成本估算与因 UX 摩擦导致的首次落地失败。

**排除原因：** 属工程部署与成本运维经验报告，讨论的是模型选型、工具集成与组织采纳，不涉及内部表示、推理状态生成或证据使用；「按能力与成本分配任务」与 LVR 的动态计算预算仅是宽泛类比，缺少可核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Create Your Own Work Stack: Deploying Self-hosted AI and Tooling in a Tech Startup

**abstract/A1：** Self-hosting AI is often presented as a way to reduce inference costs and increase control, but deployment introduces its own costs. We report our experience deploying a hybrid AI stack at Grayhat Developers, a small software company with an AI-intensive development workflow. Our initial motivation was to counter the growing cost of commercial AI usage: Claude/Codex incurred approximately \\$325 per month, with more than 25.93 billion recorded tokens. We experimented with local open-source models as replacements for commercial coding agents, but after learning that their performance and inference speed were insufficient, we adopted a hybrid approach in which commercial agents remained responsible for complex tasks while local models handled lower-complexity tasks, organizational information retrieval, and synchronization with self-hosted tools including Outline, Plane, and ERPNext. We estimated that approximately 30--40\\% of our commercial AI workload was associated with context transfer, documentation synchronization, task management, and related operational coordination. By moving this work to local models, we estimate that approximately 90\\% of this coordination workload could be handled without commercial inference, corresponding to an estimated 27--36\\% reduction in overall commercial-AI workload or cost-equivalent usage. Our first internal deployment nevertheless failed to achieve adoption because it introduced UX friction. Replacing it with a centrally configured deployment substantially improved feedback and adoption. We conclude that local inference is most useful not as a wholesale replacement for commercial AI, but as part of a hybrid architecture that assigns tasks according to their capability requirements, cost, context demands, and operational sensitivity.

**comment/C1：** Accepted for poster presentation at the Thirty-Ninth Annual Conference on Innovative Applications of Artificial Intelligence (IAAI-27), February 18-20, 2027

</details>

<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Describe Your Facial Expressions by Linking Image Encoders and Large Language Models

**abstract/A1：** This paper presents a novel task of describing human facial expressions of a facial image in natural language, which captures the nuances of facial actions and emotional states beyond traditional emotion categories or facial action units (AUs). To achieve the facial expression captioning model, we propose a three-stage training framework that trains a vision-to-language model using synthesized image-text pairs and the BLIP-2 pre-training techniques. To overcome the challenge of missing training image-text pairs for facial expression captioning, we propose a strategy that involves synthesizing and combining captions using GPT-3.5 and existing annotations on either emotion categories or AUs. Experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method in generating captions that describe details of facial actions and emotions, as well as the inferential relationship between them, even when those emotions are not present in the training data. It is also demonstrated that the vision-to-language task enhances the performance of the intermediate visual features on both AU detection and emotion classification tasks.

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 PACE-NAS，一种面向微控制器（MCU）的光流网络宏到微观架构搜索框架，通过解耦峰值 SRAM 与延迟，在 Grove Vision AI V2 上把 EdgeFlowNet 改造并搜索出 MCUFlowNet 系列模型，兼顾速度、精度与无人机避障成功率。

**排除原因：** 研究内容是嵌入式光流网络的硬件感知 NAS 与实时效率优化，不涉及 latent reasoning 状态、视觉证据使用、推理停止或动态计算等主线问题，也未提供可迁移的状态表示或监督线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MCUFlowNet: Hardware-Aware Neural Architecture Search for Optical Flow on Microcontrollers

**abstract/A1：** Dense optical flow allows small drones to avoid obstacles. However, few dense optical flow networks are designed for microcontrollers (MCUs) with neural accelerators, whose on-chip SRAM limits the input resolution. We present PACE-NAS, a macro-to-micro neural architecture search framework that decouples memory from latency: a macro design fixes the backbone and its peak SRAM, and a micro design searches operators and depths to trade accuracy against compiler-estimated latency. We apply it to EdgeFlowNet on a Grove Vision AI V2 board (Ethos-U55), where profiling locates the peak SRAM at the full-resolution output head. Replacing transposed convolutions with bilinear upsampling and convolutions lowers the peak SRAM by 18%, and two lightweight context modules lower the endpoint error (EPE) without raising the peak. All searched subnets share this peak, so the search needs no memory constraint. We name the Pareto-optimal models MCUFlowNet; the fastest variant, MCUFlowNet-S, runs 1.76x faster than a retrained EdgeFlowNet at a 1.6% higher EPE and, in simulated forest flights at 2 m/s, reaches the goal without collision in 51.5% of trials, against 41.8%. PACE-NAS also applies to semantic segmentation, where a searched PIDNet-S variant runs 1.17x faster at similar accuracy. Code is available at https://gitlab.kuleuven.be/emedia-research-lab/esat-stadius/mcuflownet/mcuflownet.

**comment/C1：** Submitted to IEEE ISCAS 2027 (under review). 5 pages

</details>

### 296. [Interpretable Multimodal MRI-Clinical Fusion for Functional Outcome Prediction After Acute Ischemic Stroke](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08154v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08154

**作者：** Sina Raeisadigh、Henning Müller、Abderrahmane Hedjoudje

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CV、eess.IV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08154v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08154v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该研究在66例急性缺血性脑卒中患者上，用预训练3D ResNet提取ADC、DWI、FLAIR嵌入并与临床变量融合，以线性SVM预测三个月功能结局，并比较不同模态组合与特征重要性。

**排除原因：** 属于医学影像预后建模与可解释性分析，没有推理过程中的内部状态生成、证据获取或停止决策，也未涉及VLM推理机制，缺少可迁移的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Interpretable Multimodal MRI-Clinical Fusion for Functional Outcome Prediction After Acute Ischemic Stroke

**abstract/A1：** This study evaluates the added prognostic value of day-1 multimodal MRI for predicting three-month functional outcome after acute ischemic stroke (AIS). Sixty-six AIS patients with ADC, DWI, FLAIR, and clinical data were analyzed. Three-dimensional pretrained ResNet embeddings were extracted from each MRI modality, reduced using fold-isolated principal component analysis, and combined with structured clinical variables and ADC-derived lesion biomarkers. Linear support vector machines were evaluated using eight-fold stratified group cross-validation across MRI modality combinations. Clinical-only modeling achieved strong performance (AUC = $0.899 \\pm 0.151$), while MRI-only models showed limited discrimination (AUC $\\approx$ $0.67$-$0.73$). Among multimodal configurations, FLAIR-clinical fusion achieved the highest mean cross-validated performance (AUC = $0.948 \\pm 0.061$), followed by ADC-clinical fusion (AUC = $0.941 \\pm 0.094$). Full ADC-DWI-FLAIR fusion did not consistently outperform single-modality clinical fusion. Feature-importance analysis identified age, NIHSS subscores, ADC lesion burden, and learned FLAIR representations as key contributors, while qualitative FLAIR activation maps showed responses over lesion-like regions. These findings suggest that compact day-1 FLAIR and ADC representations provide complementary prognostic information beyond clinical variables and support interpretable, data-efficient outcome prediction in AIS.

**comment/C1：** 5 pages, 2 figures, 1 table

</details>

### 297. [SplatAnything: Gaussian Spacing Normalization for Feed-forward 3DGS across Arbitrary Scenes](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08264v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08264

**作者：** Hao Yu、Zhenwei Wang、Yongzhen Hu、Yuning Gong、Junta Wu、Xiaowei Zhou、Hujun Bao、Tengfei Wang、Chunchao Guo、Sida Peng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08264v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08264v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SplatAnything，用高斯间距归一化（GSNorm）统一前馈 3DGS 中的尺度监督参考，并建立跨数据集、稀疏输入与外推视角的基准。

**排除原因：** 属于三维重建与渲染中的属性归一化与几何尺度问题，不涉及 VLM 推理状态、视觉证据使用或停止决策，也未提供可迁移的 latent 推理线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SplatAnything: Gaussian Spacing Normalization for Feed-forward 3DGS across Arbitrary Scenes

**abstract/A1：** We identify a key limitation of existing feed-forward 3DGS methods: Gaussian attributes are typically predicted in a radial-distance-normalized space that canonicalizes a global radial statistic of the reconstructed geometry, while the spacing between neighboring Gaussian centers can still vary substantially across scene scales and camera configurations. This mismatch induces systematic shifts in Gaussian-scale targets, leading to ambiguous supervision. To address this, we propose SplatAnything with Gaussian Spacing Normalization (GSNorm), which canonicalizes a robust scene-level statistic of Gaussian spacing to provide a more consistent spatial reference for Gaussian prediction. To rigorously assess our method, we further establish a systematic benchmark spanning diverse datasets, input sparsity levels, and challenging extrapolated viewpoints. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SplatAnything achieves state-of-the-art performance, with particularly strong gains under substantial variations in scene scale and viewpoint.

</details>

### 298. [DART: Domain-Agnostic Residual Transfer for Generalist Anomaly Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08271v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08271

**作者：** Muhammad Aqeel、Maham Nazir、Marco Cristani、Francesco Setti

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08271v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08271v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DART，将 GAD 中的图像残差分解为异常相关与域相关成分，并配合跨域元训练提升未见域上的异常检测迁移。

**排除原因：** 文中的「residual」指图像层面的异常残差，与 LVR 的 latent 推理状态无关；去域化与元训练服务于异常检测刷分，未涉及推理状态生成、证据筛选或停止决策，缺少可核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DART: Domain-Agnostic Residual Transfer for Generalist Anomaly Detection

**abstract/A1：** Generalist Anomaly Detection (GAD) aims to detect anomalies in unseen domains using only a few reference samples. Recent normal-abnormal guided methods improve this setting by exploiting abnormal references, but their residuals often mix transferable anomaly cues with domain-specific appearance factors such as texture, geometry, and acquisition conditions. This residual domain entanglement limits cross-domain transfer and may produce spurious activations on novel domains. We propose DART, a plug-in framework for normal-abnormal guided GAD that learns to separate residuals into anomaly-relevant and domain-specific components. DART combines a Residual Disentanglement Module, which preserves anomaly-discriminative information while suppressing domain information, with Cross-Domain Meta-Training, which forms episodes across source domains to make the separation effective. The method is architecture-agnostic and can be inserted between residual computation and the detection head of existing GAD pipelines. Experiments on MVTec AD, VisA, and BraTS show consistent improvements over multiple base methods, with the largest gains under stronger domain shifts. https://github.com/aqeeelmirza/DART

**comment/C1：** Accepted to NeurIPS 2026

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文构建「语境完整性」的生成式模型：由两个 LLM 扮演信息持有者与索取者进行对话，考察情境、持有者决策逻辑与模型差异如何影响信息披露与拒绝。

**排除原因：** 研究的是社交语境下信息流动的规范适当性，不涉及视觉或多模态内部表示、推理状态生成与视觉证据使用，也未提供可核查的状态更新、贡献分配或停止决策线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Generative Model of Contextual Integrity: Appropriate vs. Inappropriate Information Sharing

**abstract/A1：** Contextual integrity (CI) holds that appropriate information flow depends on context-specific norms. The same disclosure may be appropriate in one context and inappropriate in another. We build a generative model of contextual integrity to study information sharing through simulated conversations. Two actors played by large language models exchange messages, with one holding private information and the other seeking it. We examine how sharing and refusal vary with the situation, the holder's decision logic, disposition, and the underlying language model. Actors shared information with the rightful recipients when it was needed, but also disclosed it to people who were not entitled to receive it. The model provides a way to study how disclosure and refusal develop through conversation as the situations and the actors vary.

**comment/C1：** Accepted as a main conference paper at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

### 300. [AutoCompress: Automated Hardware-Guided Model Compression](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08290v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08290

**作者：** Sheldon Liang、Ali Taghibakhshi、Saurav Muralidharan、Sharath Turuvekere Sreenivas、Ashwath Aithal、Pavlo Molchanov、Nima Tajbakhsh

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08290v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08290v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 AutoCompress，将部署时的延迟、吞吐与显存约束纳入可微架构搜索，联合优化全局与逐层结构，得到异构剪枝架构。

**排除原因：** 工作聚焦 LLM 剪枝与硬件成本建模，未涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据使用或停止决策，也无具体可借助的 latent 推理线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AutoCompress: Automated Hardware-Guided Model Compression

**abstract/A1：** LLM pruning must preserve quality while meeting hardware- and workload-specific constraints on latency, throughput, and memory. Existing pruning workflows often target parameter counts, requiring practitioners to manually select model sizes that meet latency, throughput, and GPU memory limits. However, parameter count alone does not predict these metrics, which depend on the model architecture, workload, hardware, and serving engine. We introduce AutoCompress, a hardware-guided compression framework that directly incorporates such constraints into differentiable architecture search. AutoCompress jointly optimizes global and per-layer structure to produce heterogeneous architectures, guided by serving-engine cost models validated against end-to-end latency, throughput, and memory measurements. We evaluate AutoCompress on long-context local coding, batched datacenter serving, and latency-sensitive in-game interaction. Compared to the state-of-the-art Minitron pruning framework, AutoCompress improves average accuracy across eight benchmarks by 6.57, 2.35, and 0.57 percentage points on RTX 5070, RTX 5080, and H100, respectively. AutoCompress also compresses Nemotron 3.5 Super from 120B to 74B parameters for single-B200 BF16 serving at 128K context, retaining 95% of its mean benchmark score.

</details>

### 301. [4D Point Splatting for mmWave Radar Micro-Doppler Differentiable Rendering](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08342v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08342

**作者：** Adnan Armouti、Guanyue Qian、Rajalakshmi Nandakumar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.GR、cs.LG、eess.SP

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08342v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08342v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 4D Point Splatting（4DPS），一个物理驱动的可微渲染器，用带材质、法向与速度的定向点表示运动场景，将点映射到距离与多普勒 bin，从而拟合真实毫米波雷达采集并生成可用于下游人体感知模型训练的原始复信号。

**排除原因：** 工作面向雷达 micro-Doppler 渲染与 sim2real 数据合成，属于传感仿真与信号处理，不涉及 VLM 推理状态、latent 生成或证据使用决策，也无可直接核查的方法借鉴线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** 4D Point Splatting for mmWave Radar Micro-Doppler Differentiable Rendering

**abstract/A1：** Millimeter-wave radar resolves the micrometer displacements of a moving body as micro-Doppler, enabling gait, gesture, activity, pose and vital-sign sensing. Each task relies on a downstream model trained on real data, yet radar datasets lag orders of magnitude behind vision and language. Brute-force collection will not close that gap, and existing synthesis methods (forward-only simulators, learned generators and static-scene inverse renderers) cannot fit a real capture of a moving body, leaving a sim2real gap. We present 4D Point Splatting (4DPS), a physics-based differentiable renderer that fits an explicit moving scene to a real capture. Using the synchronized camera to initialize the room and body, we represent the scene as oriented points carrying material, normal and velocity, and splat each point into range and Doppler bins. Our output is the raw complex return a real radar records, so we apply the same signal processing on the rendered data to obtain any representation for training any SOTA downstream model. We fit 235 sequences on HuPR and 100 on RT-Pose, two datasets with different radars. Our reconstructions set the state of the art, with median correlations of 0.940 and 0.830 on range-azimuth, 0.829 and 0.695 on range-Doppler, and 0.988 and 0.966 on micro-Doppler. Our renders also set the state of the art in downstream sim2real gap. On RT-Pose, a pose model trained on them reaches 21.9 cm MPJPE on real data, against 16.7 cm for real training and 127.6 cm for the RF-Genesis baseline, where both the rendered- and real-trained models learn nearly the same features. Our custom CUDA kernels fit a sequence in 5.7 minutes on HuPR and 12.9 on RT-Pose on one H100, making dataset-scale inverse rendering of mmWave radar possible for the first time. This work can enable synthetic data generation for any human-centered downstream task and model, and scene editing for data augmentation.

**comment/C1：** Under Review

</details>

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Adaptive Low-Light Enhancement for Road Anomaly Detection: A Multi-Architecture Benchmark and Edge Deployment Study

**abstract/A1：** Road anomaly detection systems on autonomous vehicles must function reliably under degraded visual conditions including darkness, rain, and Harmattan dust haze. This paper benchmarks four object detection architectures, YOLOv8n, YOLOv12n, RT-DETR, and Faster R-CNN, under physically grounded lighting and weather degradation, and shows that uniform application of the SCI low-light enhancement network is actively harmful at dusk-level lighting (-16.1% to -30.7% mAP@50). We propose a lightweight, training-free luminance-adaptive router that selects between no enhancement, moderate SCI, and denoising plus strong SCI based on per-frame Rec. 601 luminance, recovering most of the dusk penalty while preserving low-light gains, and we validate the full pipeline on NVIDIA Jetson Nano edge hardware.

**comment/C1：** 5 pages

</details>

### 303. [Synthesis of Ovarian Cancer Multi-Omics Datasets: A Biology-Informed Evaluation Framework](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08394v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08394

**作者：** Déborah Boyenval、Alina Malyutina、Jaana Oikkonen、Sammeli Liikkanen、Sampsa Hautaniemi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08394v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08394v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 研究目标是合成卵巢癌配对 RNA/CNV 多组学数据的生成与生物学导向评测，属于医学数据生成与评估基准，不涉及视觉或多模态推理中的内部状态、证据筛选或停止决策，也未提供可核查的状态更新或训练监督线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Synthesis of Ovarian Cancer Multi-Omics Datasets: A Biology-Informed Evaluation Framework

**abstract/A1：** Paired multi-omic cancer cohorts are costly to assemble and difficult to share, motivating synthetic cohorts as a complementary resource. As generative model development accelerates, we shift the focus from proposing yet another architecture to defining evaluation criteria that determine whether synthetic data preserve biologically relevant structure. We introduce a biology-informed and patient-disjoint framework for evaluating the generation of paired RNA expression and copy-number variation (CNV) profiles, and apply it to the longitudinal DECIDER high-grade serous ovarian cancer cohort. The framework assesses modality-specific fidelity and coherence between RNA and CNV pairs using agreement between real training and held-out data as a reference, alongside complementary training-data proximity diagnostics. We adapt three multi-omic integration architectures based on variational autoencoders to generate paired profiles. The model leading several fidelity and coherence measures also shows the strongest training-data proximity. The protocol and case study offer the community a reference for comparing future multi-omic generators.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the GenAI4Health Workshop at NeurIPS 2026 and in the Findings track of ML4H 2026. Both acceptances are non-archival

### 304. [SM4RT: Cascaded Feed-forward Model for 4D Reconstruction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08436v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08436

**作者：** Pang-Chi Lo、Jhao-Syun Lai、Tsung-Wei Ke

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08436v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08436v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** SM4RT 是一个级联前馈模型，先从 RGB 视频重建 3D 几何，再以预测结构引导逐像素 3D 运动估计，并用弱监督损失缓解运动标签稀疏。

**排除原因：** 该工作面向 4D 动态场景重建，虽涉及几何与运动表示，但没有 latent 推理状态、证据获取或停止决策等环节，其弱监督针对点运动标签而非推理轨迹监督，与 LVR 主线缺少具体可核查的联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SM4RT: Cascaded Feed-forward Model for 4D Reconstruction

**abstract/A1：** We introduce SM4RT, a multi-task cascade feed-forward model for dynamic scene reconstruction from RGB videos. While existing feed-forward approaches typically address geometry reconstruction and motion estimation separately--and often predict motions in a single coordinate frame that must account for camera movement--SM4RT solves these tasks sequentially by first reconstructing 3D geometry and then bootstrapping motion estimation from the predicted structure. By expressing each pixel's 3D motion in its corresponding camera frame, SM4RT reduces ambiguity arising from camera motion. To address the sparsity of real-world motion labels, SM4RT further incorporates a weakly supervised loss that regularizes unlabeled point motions using labeled point positions at the other time step. We evaluate motion estimation on three dynamic-scene datasets--Kubric, Waymo, and Point Odyssey--and SM4RT achieves the best performance on most metrics compared with prior baselines. We additionally benchmark geometry reconstruction on five dynamic scene datasets and show that SM4RT achieves competitive performance with state-of-the-art methods. Overall, our unified framework demonstrates that combining geometry reconstruction and motion estimation in a cascade not only avoids performance trade-offs but also enhances motion estimation through improved geometric reasoning.

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://sm4rt-eailab.github.io/

</details>

### 305. [Adversarial Training with Large Step Sizes: Implicit Bias, Optimization, and Sharpness](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08457v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08457

**作者：** Yi Feng、Andrea Paudice

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08457v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08457v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文从理论上分析对抗训练中使用大常数步长梯度下降的动力学，包括线性逻辑回归的隐式偏差、优化收敛速度以及神经网络训练中损失尖锐度的演化。

**排除原因：** 研究主题是对抗训练与大步长优化的理论性质，未涉及视觉或多模态推理中的表征、证据获取、latent 状态或停止决策，也未提供可核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Adversarial Training with Large Step Sizes: Implicit Bias, Optimization, and Sharpness

**abstract/A1：** Adversarial Training (AT) is an important method for improving model robustness. We study the dynamics of deterministic Gradient Descent (GD) in AT. Most existing works in this direction focus on the small or vanishing step sizes regime, where GD resembles a continuous-time flow. However, such step sizes can make training inefficient and are rarely used in practice. In this paper, we study the AT dynamics of GD with large constant step sizes. We first study GD in adversarial linear logistic regression, focusing on two aspects: where the model parameters converge (implicit bias) and how quickly the losses decrease (optimization). We then turn to neural networks training and study how the sharpness of losses evolve during AT. Our results advance the understanding of the behaviors of GD with large step sizes in AT and highlight three key differences from its behavior in Standard Training (ST): (i) the implicit bias gap between AT and ST, previously established only for small step sizes, persists for arbitrarily large constant step sizes, (ii) different from ST, there is no uniform rate of convergence for the optimization error in AT, and (iii) sharpness can exhibit two-stage dynamics that differ from the Edge-of-Stability behavior observed in ST.

</details>

<a id="paper-306"></a>

### 306. [Do You Miss the Nights We Spent Crafting Figures? Rethinking Teaser and Framework Figures in the Age of Generative AI](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08486v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08486

**作者：** Xiaoqi Zhao、Youwei Pang、Kailai Zhou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08486v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08486v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文对 200 篇论文中的 371 幅动机图与框架图做视觉回溯分析，用多模态评审模型按六个维度打分，并尝试做受源约束的 AI 改写，考察清晰度与美学一致性的变化。

**排除原因：** 研究对象是科学论文插图的表达与设计改写，只把多模态模型当作评分工具，未涉及 VLM 内部视觉状态、证据筛选或推理决策，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Do You Miss the Nights We Spent Crafting Figures? Rethinking Teaser and Framework Figures in the Age of Generative AI

**abstract/A1：** Scientific figures give research ideas a visual form through choices of abstraction, composition, and emphasis. Generative AI offers new ways to realize these choices, raising questions about explanatory clarity and purposeful visual expression. We examine these questions through a visual retrospective of 371 motivation and framework figures from 200 papers. The corpus comprises a quota-based main sample of 160 papers spanning four temporal periods and a separate purposive style-contrast sample of 40 papers. A single multimodal judge evaluates six dimensions of communication and design, with three repeated judgments per figure. We attempt source-constrained revisions of all 371 figures, of which 369 pass the recorded fidelity checks. Original-figure ratings show no uniform decline across periods. Revisions increase mean clarity ratings by 0.428 points and decrease aesthetic coherence ratings by 0.111 points on a five-point scale. Gains in the six-dimension average are larger for main-sample motivation figures than for frameworks. Case analyses show that added explanation can clarify comparisons while leaving typographic hierarchy and visual economy unresolved. These model-based findings support researcher-led design, with AI assisting exploration and authors retaining responsibility for explanatory purpose, composition, and scientific relationships. The accompanying atlas and evaluation records are available at https://ntu-ai4x.github.io/Rethinking\_Figures/index\_en.html.

</details>
